查尔斯·奥伯特（Charles Obert）著　·　Almuten Press，Minneapolis，2019

全书 210 条译名契约。正文、引文、注释一律照此渲染——点右侧按钮展开查阅。

本表是全书译文的契约：正文、引文、注释一律照此渲染，不得中途改用同义词。
基准：实践者体系（占星学／占星师／星盘／星座／宫位／守护星）。技术名词尽量沿用《天宫图与公共领域》旧表以保跨项目可查；与旧表分歧 4 处（astrology、astrologer、sign、chart）均在「简注」栏说明理由。
A. 学科、传统与从业者
英文中译简注
astrology占星学全书基准译法。本书是执业占星师写给同行的书，非科学史论著，故不用旧表的「星占学」。旧表的选择是为了对抗「术」字的贬抑，本书语境无此压力，且「占星」与你 AstrologyHall 笔记、与中文占星社群通行说法一致。跨项目查检时请记：本表「占星学」＝旧表「星占学」。
astrologer占星师旧表作「星占家」（学者兼从业者）。本书所论者从瓦伦斯到福里斯特皆为执业者，故统一作「占星师」。
traditional astrology传统占星学本书核心对立项之一，指希腊化—波斯阿拉伯—中世纪拉丁—利利一线。
modern astrology现代占星学本书核心对立项之二，指二十世纪以降心理化、人本化的一线。
Hellenistic astrology希腊化占星学公元前二世纪至公元七世纪的希腊语占星传统。
working astrologer执业占星师Obert 用以强调瓦伦斯是真正接活断盘的人，非纯理论家。译时须保住这层「手上有活」的意味。
practice (of astrology)占星实践作动词「to practice」→「执业 / 断盘」。
signification征象行星／宫位所「表示」的事物清单。不译「象征」——象征偏文学，征象是技术清单。动词 signify →「表征 / 主」。
attribution归属含义Obert 常用 “meanings attributed to Saturn”→「归给土星的含义」。
delineation / interpretation释盘 / 解释由盘面推出人事的过程。
B. 盘面构件与技术名词（多沿用旧表）
英文中译简注
chart星盘Obert 用 chart 指个人出生盘。旧表 horoscope→「天宫图」为史学语境；本书作「星盘」。
nativity本命盘沿用旧表。瓦伦斯 “noble nativities”→「显赫高贵的本命盘」，此处兼指盘与命格。
native命主沿用旧表。盘所属之人。
planet行星传统占星学中日、月亦在「七行星」之列，译文不作「星体」以免松动。
the lights / luminaries二曜沿用旧表。日月合称。Obert “opposite the lights”→「与二曜相对」。
(zodiac) sign星座与旧表分歧。旧表作「黄道十二座」以严防与 house 相混；本书 sign 与 house 同页高频出现但语境清晰，且实践者一律说「摩羯座」「狮子座」，故取「星座」。house 一律作「宫」，绝不作「座」，分辨即靠此。
rising sign上升星座出生时从东方地平线升起的星座；其守护星即上升守护星。与 Ascendant「上升点」相关但不混作同一词。
house宫泛指用「宫位」，序数用「第 N 宫」。此条写死，不得同页摇摆。
whole sign (houses)整宫制以上升所在整座为第一宫的宫位制。Obert 自述用此制。
Ascendant (ASC)上升点沿用旧表。
Midheaven (MC)天顶本书未出现，但与你 AstrologyHall 笔记对齐，预先写定。旧表史学语境作「中天」。
angle角宫指上升、天底、下降、天顶四条主轴所落的第一、第四、第七、第十宫。依中文占星学通行用法，不作泛义的「角度」。
ruler / ruling planet守护星与你既有笔记一致。旧表史学语境多作「主星」。「ruler of the Ascendant」→「上升守护星」。
rule (over)主管动词。「it rules lead, wood, and stone」→「它主管铅、木与石」。
exaltation / exalted擢升 / 擢升于沿用旧表。不用「入旺」——「旺」是现代简化说法，与「庙」「陷」一套混用易乱；「擢升」保住 exaltatio 的「被抬举」原义，正合本书土星主题。
fall落陷与擢升相对的必然弱势；不并入泛称的「落于弱势」。
dignity / essential dignity尊贵 / 必然尊贵沿用旧表体系。
debilitated落于弱势与「尊贵」相对。
well / ill dignified尊贵良好 / 尊贵不良指行星获得本宫、擢升、三方等尊贵条件的程度，不是人的举止是否有尊严。
aspect相位沿用旧表。动词 to aspect →「与……成相位」。
square四分相沿用旧表（quartile / square 同译）。
opposition对分相沿用旧表。本书此词分量极重——土星几乎一切「阻挡、妨碍、敌对」之义皆由此出，凡出现处不得代以「对冲」。
conjunction合相沿用旧表。
triplicity三方组由同一元素的三个星座组成；须与三分相 trine 区分。
retrograde / station / direct motion逆行 / 留 / 顺行station 指视运动转向前看似停驻；利利章的 Direction 仅指恢复顺行，不是定向推运。
oriental / occidental晨出 / 昏出相对太阳而言先于太阳升起／后于太阳升起；不按方位直译「东方／西方」。
orb容许度相位在精确成相前后开始与结束作用的度数范围。
benefic / malefic吉星 / 凶星木、金为吉，土、火为凶。Obert 在第三部专章辨析 malefic 与「道德之恶 evil」不是一回事，此处译名须为该辨析预留余地，故不作「恶星」。
the greater / lesser malefic大凶星 / 小凶星土星／火星的传统成对称号。「大／小」指传统所判的致害范围与强度，不是道德邪恶程度；核心词仍依契约作「凶星」。
sect (αἵρεσις / secta)宗派希腊 αἵρεσις 原义为「所选定的一方、所属的学派党派」（哲学各派即称 hairesis，英语 heresy 亦出于此）；拉丁 secta 出自 sequi「跟从」，即「所追随的那一党」。故此词的本义是阵营归属，不是「差别」。中文占星圈另见「区分」「区间」二译，各有问题，详见首见处校注。本表定「宗派」，并锁定下列派生译法，各章不得自造。
the day / night sect昼派 / 夜派日、木、土属昼派；月、金、火属夜派；水星两属（晨出属昼，昏出属夜）。「Saturn is of the day sect」→「土星属昼派」。
diurnal / nocturnal (chart)昼盘 / 夜盘太阳在地平之上为昼盘，之下为夜盘。修饰行星时作「昼间行星／夜间行星」。
in sect / out of sect得派（常用：得时） / 失派（常用：失时）正文统一用「得派／失派」，以保持 sect 的派生词族清楚可见；中文业内也常称「得时／失时」，故在表中并列标注，便于查检。行星所属之派与盘的昼夜相合为「得派（得时）」，不合为「失派（失时）」。得派则性情转和——凶星减其害，吉星增其益。不作「入区分／出区分」：那读起来像在说位置，而此处说的是「当值与否」。
contrary to sect逆派尤指「逆派之凶星」（the malefic contrary to sect），全盘最难处理的一颗。
sect light宗派光体昼盘取太阳，夜盘取月亮，为该盘所属宗派的领头之曜。不作「派主星」——「主星」一词已为 hyleg（命主星）、dominus geniturae（生辰主星）等占用，须避让。
halb / ḥayyizhalb / ḥayyiz（保留原词）依工程所有者决定保留原词，不另造中译。halb 指行星依其昼夜属性处在合宜半球；ḥayyiz 则在此基础上再要求行星与所在星座的阴阳属性相合。二者都不是 sect 的译名，且与 sect 处于不同层级，切不可混译。中文「区间」一译疑受 ḥayyiz「区域、领地」的字面义影响；若再用它译 sect，会把本须分辨的术语并成一个。
Thema Mundi世界本命盘沿用旧表。传说中世界受造时的盘，日狮土瓶相对分即出于此图。
first visibility初见月亮离日后首度可见（希腊 φάσις），此后渐盈。瓦伦斯据此判月之强弱。
humors体液沿用旧表。
melancholic忧郁质四体液之一，冷而干，土星所属。
intemperate (quality)失衡的（性质）四体液理论中指某种性质过盛、超出均衡状态；不按一般性情语境译作「放纵」。
Chaldean order迦勒底序列传统地心宇宙中由外向内的行星次序：土、木、火、日、金、水、月。博纳蒂章据此分配妊娠月份与宫位。
Firmament恒星天传统地心宇宙中七行星天之外的恒星球，被视为固定不变；土星守在游动行星与恒星天的边界。
C. 本书自身的概念词（Obert 的论证骨架）
英文中译简注
between time and eternity在时间与永恒之间副书名，全书总纲。
border planet边界行星Obert 的核心提法：土星立于时间与永恒的交界。
eternal law永恒法则第二部标题。兼指自然律与道德律，故不作「永恒律法」（偏法学）。
eternal moral law永恒道德律「律」字在此保留，因其确指有约束力的规范。
Law and Judgment法则与审判作者归纳的土星核心含义簇之一：土星既表征维持秩序的法则，也表征越界之后的判断与惩罚。
eternal order and pattern永恒的秩序与格局pattern 不作「模式」——太工程化。
worldview世界观全书论证中的背景框架：人据以理解宇宙、人性、因果与价值的一整组基本预设。
assumptions预设不作「假设」——assumption 在此是「不自觉地默认」，非「待检验的假说」。
context脉络Obert 反复说 “meaning relies on its context”。不作「语境」（偏语言学）或「背景」（太松）。
living / to make living sense活的 / 讲得通且是活的Obert 高频用 living 与 alive，意在「不是博物馆里的死知识」。译时须逐处保住这层温度。
recover找回不作「恢复」——recover 在此是把丢掉的东西重新捡起来，非把坏了的修好。
the Chesterton Test切斯特顿检验Obert 自造的检验法：把一段话改用短词重说，看它是否还剩下东西。
cluster of meanings含义簇Obert 归纳征象的单位。
austerity / ascetic清苦自持 / 苦修者前者指主动限制欲望、生活俭朴严峻的品格；后者指把这种自我限制落实为宗教生活的人。不可按经济政策语境把 austerity 译作「紧缩」。
humility谦卑土星德性之一：承认自身限度、放下自我夸示，并在不求被看见的情况下服务；不得与 humiliation「屈辱」混译。
Theosophy神智学十九世纪后期兴起的西方秘学运动；把东方灵性观念与现代演化、进步叙事结合，对现代占星学影响极大。
archetype原型荣格心理学与原型占星学用语；指集体无意识中的基本意象／结构，不与柏拉图 Forms「理型」混译。
spiritual astrology灵性占星学把行星等因素解释为灵魂觉悟旅程阶段的现代混合流派；不泛译作「精神占星学」。
12 Letter Alphabet十二字母体系多宾斯编定的现代对应体系，把十二星座、十二宫与行星依序视为同一组可互换含义；其别称 Zip Code 译作「邮政编码」并保留原文。
fate / fortune / providence命定 / 命运 / 天意锁定的三分：fate 是不可更易的定分；fortune 是起落无常、可好可坏的外部遭逢；providence 是具有意图与安排的神学性天意。三词不得归并。
Ascended Masters扬升大师神智学用语，指经过多世演化、达到高阶觉悟并被认为能以意念支配现实的超凡人类存有。
archetypal psychology原型心理学由詹姆斯·希尔曼发展；与一般荣格心理学相承又有自身取向。
senex / puer老者原型 / 少年原型原型心理学的成对拉丁术语，分别强调年老、秩序、结构与青春、开放、冲动；首见处保留原词。
Transpersonal Psychology超个人心理学研究超越日常个体自我边界的意识经验；本书所引巴特勒体系把它与荣格原型结合。
hylotropic / holotropic趋物 / 趋整格罗夫意识模型的成对用语：hylē「物质」，holos「整体」，分别指朝向物质分立现实与朝向整体意识。
Uranian astrology天王星占星学二十世纪二十年代起源于德国的汉堡学派体系，重数学、几何、刻度盘、行星图式与硬相位。
Harmonic Astrology谐波占星学按整数分割星盘圆周，研究不同谐波及其相位含义。
vibrational astrology振动占星学大卫·科克伦在谐波占星学基础上发展的体系，把行星互动视为特定能量／振动模式。
Tao道本章一处为东方思想中的「道」；C. S. 路易斯又借此词总称跨文化的自然法／客观价值传统，须依上下文理解。
Maya摩耶（幻相）源于印度思想的概念；本书用以指经验世界作为表象或幻相，与其背后不变的永恒实在相对。首见处保留原词。
progressions / transits次限推运 / 行运progressions 指以象征时间推进本命盘的次限类技术，不与 profection「小限推运」混同；transits 指实际天体运行相对于本命盘的位置。
Ouranos乌拉诺斯希腊天空神之名，字面即「天／天空」；本章用此拼法区别现代行星 Uranus「天王星」。
Unitive Knowing / All-At-Once knowing整全之知 / 一举而知作者对 Intellect「智性」的白话释义；沿 Unitive Thought「整全之思」词族，保留 knowing 与 thought 的差别。
first Saturn return第一次土星回归土星运行约二十九年后首次回到本命位置；作者把它视为人生进入成熟责任阶段的重要界标。
golden mean中道亚里士多德伦理学中的适度原则：德性位于过度与不足之间；不是统计学平均数。
Sacred Seven神圣七曜传统占星宇宙中的日、月与水、金、火、木、土七颗游星；不包括现代外行星。
moral evil道德之恶具有自由意志与伤害意图的恶；与疾病、灾害等仅在客观上造成伤害的 harmful evil 分开。
evil / harmful evil恶 / 有害之恶evil 泛指「恶」；作者进一步用 harmful evil 指疾病、灾害等客观造成伤害、却不包含恶意选择的事件，与 moral evil「道德之恶」严格分开。
suffering well善处苦难不是歌颂苦难或「很会吃苦」，而是以合宜、有德性的方式回应不可避免的苦难，避免再增加第二层苦难。
second level suffering第二层苦难「对于苦难的苦难」：抗拒、自责、羞耻等反应在原初痛苦之上叠加的苦难。
redemptive suffering救赎性苦难为帮助他人而自愿承担、奉献的苦难；作者主要在基督宗教尤其天主教传统中讨论。
Caritas爱德拉丁基督宗教术语，指无私、慈悲并联结众生的神圣仁爱；不缩减为现代狭义慈善施舍。
communion of Saints诸圣相通天主教传统中生者、亡者与诸圣构成跨越时间的共同体，能够彼此代祷、扶助。
Evolutionary astrology演化占星学以轮回、多世与灵魂今生目的为核心的现代占星流派；本书尝试用柏拉图—毕达哥拉斯传统重新提供语境。
science (older sense)学问 / 知识体系附录 pp. 215–222 讨论英语 science 的旧义。现代经验义仍译「科学」；涉及本原、原因与系统知识的旧义依上下文译「学问／知识体系」或保留原词。
C2. 柏拉图传统词汇（第二部；与塔罗冥想项目共用，须两边合一）
英文中译简注
the Forms / Ideas理型 / 理念不作「理念」单用——汉语「理念」已被「企业理念」一类用法占满，带主观色彩，与柏拉图「独立于人心而实存」相反。取「理型」：理＝结构原理，型＝可生成无数个例的范型，正合 Obert 自注的 “structural principles”。Ideas 与 Forms 原书互换使用，分译「理念」「理型」以存其别，此后统一用「理型」。学界另有「相」（陈康）「型相」二译。
Intellect (νοῦς)智性全书最要紧的一对之一。一举全览、不经步骤的整全之知。
Reason (διάνοια)理性逐步推演、比较判断的过程之知。「理智」一词全书禁用——它对 Intellect 与 Reason 皆可，一旦用上这对区分在中文里即消失，而第二部的论证全架在这对区分上。
Unitive Thought整全之思Obert 自造词，即 Intellect 的白话说法。
the One / the Good太一 / 至善τὸ ἕν / τὸ ἀγαθόν。不作「一者」「唯一」——后者会与一神教「独一之神」混同，而 Obert 此处正要区分二者。
the Source (of All)（万有之）源头不作「本源」（偏中哲）。
Demiurge工匠神（德穆革）δημιουργός 本义「为公众做工的人」。取意译「工匠神」，首见处并注音译——「工匠」正是论证所依赖的那层意思（照图纸加工，非从无创造）。勿以诺斯替义（无知或恶意的低等造物者）读之。
daemon代蒙δαίμων，介于神人之间的中层灵性存有，本身无善恶。绝不作「恶魔」（那是 demon），亦不作「精灵」（滑向童话）。与希尔曼《灵魂密码》中译一脉相承。
Nous智性希腊语 νοῦς；在《厄尔神话》附录中指人的不朽、能思考判断并作伦理选择的灵魂面向。与逐步推演的 Reason「理性」仍须区分。
genius (daimōn)代蒙乔伊特《理想国》英译在厄尔神话中用 genius 译 daimōn；依全书契约仍作「代蒙」，即灵魂自选的一生守护神灵。
Spindle of Necessity必然之纺锤厄尔神话的地心宇宙图式：恒星天与古典七曜如八个相套锤轮绕天极轴旋转，灵魂投生时进入其中。
the Three Fates命运三女神拉刻西斯、克洛托、阿特洛波斯；必然女神之女，分别歌唱过去、现在、未来，并使灵魂选择的生命之签成为不可逆的命定。
Great Chain of Being存在巨链拉丁 scala naturae。名从洛夫乔伊同名专著中译本。
sublunary world月下世界月亮轨道以内、四元素构成的生灭世界。不作「月球以下」。
justice正义δικαιοσύνη，柏拉图理型。不作「公义」——那是圣经汉语的词面。首轮试译瓦伦斯章曾误作「公义」，已回改统一。
fairness / respect公平 / 尊重与正义并列的三个道德理型，逐一分译。
structural principle结构原理Obert 对 Forms 的自注，为「理型」译名之根据。
contemplation / meditation沉思 / 冥想与塔罗冥想项目对齐。
Ephemeris Time / Delta T历书时 / ΔT天文学定译。原书对 ΔT 的描述有误（把累积量说成年增量），照译出注，不代作者修正。
table of houses宫位表据地球自转编算的宫位起始度数表。
D. 人名（首见处中译＋原名，其后用中译）
英文中译简注
Charles Obert查尔斯·奥伯特本书作者，Almuten Press 主人，studentofastrology.com。
Vettius Valens维提乌斯·瓦伦斯安条克人，约 120—175 年。
G. K. ChestertonG. K. 切斯特顿1874—1936，英国作家、记者。
William Lilly威廉·利利1602—1681。中文亦见「莉莉」「里利」等写法；本表取新华社译名规范的「利利」，全书统一。
Guido Bonatti圭多·博纳蒂十三世纪意大利占星师。
Firmicus Maternus菲尔米库斯·马特尔努斯约 285—360。
Abu Ma'shar阿布·马沙尔约 787—886，阿拉伯占星师、天文学家；本章据本·戴克斯英译选本转引。
Ben Dykes本·戴克斯当代传统占星文献译者；本书阿布·马沙尔引文据其英译选本转引。
Al-Biruni比鲁尼约 973—1048；本章引文取自其占星学教本。
Avraham Ibn-Ezra ben Meir亚伯拉罕·伊本·以斯拉·本·梅厄约 1089—1164，中世纪犹太学者；简称伊本·以斯拉。原书所称晚年迁往以色列地一事存疑，见本章校记。
Raphael拉斐尔十九世纪英国占星出版界由多人承接使用的笔名；《占星指南》初版于 1877 年。
Sepharial / Walter Gorn Old塞法里尔 / 沃尔特·戈恩·奥尔德1864—1929，英国占星师、神智学者；原书误排 Walter Orn Gold，已校正。
Madame Blavatsky布拉瓦茨基夫人海伦娜·布拉瓦茨基，神智学运动主要奠基者。
Alan Leo艾伦·利奥现代占星学早期重要作者；其从预测转向性格分析部分出于应对反算命法律的压力。
Liz Greene丽兹·格林心理占星学代表人物。
Steven Forrest史蒂芬·福里斯特演化占星学代表人物。
Nemesis涅墨西斯希腊神话中掌报应的女神。
Max Heindel马克斯·海因德尔1865—1919，玫瑰十字会创立者；其基督教秘学吸收神智学的轮回、业力与灵性演化观。
Llewellyn George卢埃林·乔治1876—1954，卢埃林出版社创立者；保留传统征象清单，同时引入神智学与正向思考。
Charles Ernest Owen Carter查尔斯·欧内斯特·欧文·卡特1887—1968，英国占星师、神智学者；正文简称查尔斯·E. O. 卡特。
Marion March / Joan McEvers玛丽昂·马奇 / 琼·麦克埃弗斯《学习占星学的唯一途径》系列合著者。
Ronald C Davison罗纳德·C. 戴维森1914—1985，英国占星师，以简明实用的占星教材著称。
Isabel Hickey伊莎贝尔·希基1903—1980，美国占星师；二十世纪灵性占星学代表作者。
James Holden詹姆斯·霍尔登占星史研究者；本书引其对伊莎贝尔·希基的简传。
Zipporah Pottenger Dobyns齐波拉·波廷杰·多宾斯1921—2003，美国现代占星师，编定「十二字母体系」；正文简称齐波拉·多宾斯。
Mark Pottenger马克·波廷杰齐波拉·多宾斯之子，以《美国星历》闻名。
James Hillman詹姆斯·希尔曼1926—2011，荣格派出身的心理学家，原型心理学的主要发展者。
Richard Tarnas理查德·塔纳斯文化史与原型宇宙论作者；本书转引其论希尔曼的导言。
Sir Edward Bulwer-Lytton爱德华·布尔沃-利顿爵士1803—1873，英国小说家；1842 年小说《扎诺尼》创出「门槛上的居者」一语。
Renn Butler伦恩·巴特勒当代原型占星学作者，将超个人心理学与原型占星学结合。
Stanislav Grof斯坦尼斯拉夫·格罗夫超个人心理学家；原书姓名误作 Stanislaus Grof。
Timothy Leary蒂莫西·利里二十世纪六十年代美国反文化中的迷幻药与意识探索代表人物。
Alfred Witte阿尔弗雷德·维特1878—1941，德国占星师，天王星占星学／汉堡学派奠基者。
Reinhold Ebertin莱因霍尔德·埃伯廷1901—1988，德国占星师，宇宙生物学体系代表人物。
David Cochrane大卫·科克伦当代振动占星学体系的主要发展者。
John Addey约翰·阿迪1920—1982，英国占星师，现代谐波占星学的重要奠基者。
Thomas Taylor托马斯·泰勒1758—1835，英国柏拉图主义译者；本书《法篇》引文取其英译并经作者参照现代译本调整。
Dane Rudhyar丹恩·鲁伊尔1895—1985，人本占星学的重要奠基者；强调个体潜能、周期与人格展开。
Alexander Ruperti亚历山大·鲁佩蒂1913—1998，鲁伊尔的学生，以人本占星学的周期研究著称。
Laurie Efrein劳里·埃弗林占星师，《如何校正本命盘》作者；本书借其章题说明一生预测周期在出生时已内含。
Dante Alighieri但丁·阿利吉耶里约 1265—1321，意大利诗人；《飨宴》把七艺分别配给七行星天。
Aristotle亚里士多德公元前 384—322；本书用其四因论、德性习惯与中道学说说明传统宇宙中的形式、目的与伦理。
C. S. LewisC. S. 刘易斯1898—1963，英国作家与学者；本书引《被弃的意象》说明但丁笔下命运作为地球智性的宇宙职分。
Gabrielle Giffords / Mark Kelly加布里埃尔·吉福兹 / 马克·凯利吉福兹 2011 年遭枪击；原书 p. 185 把丈夫 Mark 误作 Mike，中文已校正并出注。
Viktor Frankl维克多·弗兰克尔1905—1997，心理学家、纳粹集中营幸存者，《活出意义来：从集中营说到存在主义》作者；原书拼作 Victor。
Fyodor Dostoyevsky费奥多尔·陀思妥耶夫斯基1821—1881，俄国作家；本书以其死刑缓期经历讨论直面道德之恶与信仰转化。
Aleksandr Solzhenitsyn亚历山大·索尔仁尼琴1918—2008，俄国作家、《古拉格群岛》作者；原书英文拼写有误，中文按通行名。
Anwar Sadat安瓦尔·萨达特1918—1981，埃及总统；本书以其监禁后的信仰转变与和平工作为例。
Jordan Peterson乔丹·彼得森当代加拿大心理学家；本书以其直面道德之恶后强调终极善为例。
Anne Rice安妮·赖斯1941—2021，美国小说家；本书借其吸血鬼故事说明不朽者也可能与时代脱节。
Marcus Aurelius / Epictetus / Seneca马可·奥勒留 / 爱比克泰德 / 塞涅卡三位斯多葛传统代表人物；作者推荐其著作以学习善处苦难。
Dorothy Day多萝西·戴1897—1980，美国天主教社会活动家；本书以其减轻他人苦难的服务为例。
Teresa of Ávila阿维拉的德肋撒1515—1582，西班牙加尔默罗会神秘家；原书拼作 Theresa，本书采用天主教通行译名。
Noah Webster诺亚·韦伯斯特1758—1843，美国词典编纂家；附录借 1828、1913 年《韦氏词典》追溯 science 的旧义。
Proclus普罗克洛约 412—485，晚期柏拉图主义哲学家；以几何式公理结构写作神学与形而上学。
Benjamin Jowett / F. M. Cornford本杰明·乔伊特 / F. M. 康福德《厄尔神话》附录主要依据乔伊特公版英译，并参考康福德译注调整少数段落。
Odysseus奥德修斯《奥德赛》英雄；厄尔神话中，他抽到最后一签，却因厌弃野心而选择安分守己的普通人生。
E. 书名（首见处给原文，其后用中译简称）
英文中译简注
Saturn Through the Ages: Between Time and Eternity《纵观土星：在时间与永恒之间》本书。Through the Ages 取「纵观」——中文「纵观」本身即含「贯通古今地看」之意，一词兼摄 through 与 ages，比直译「穿越诸时代」自然，也更合本书历史巡礼的体裁。
Vettius Valens, Anthology瓦伦斯《占星选集》希腊原题 Anthologiae（Ἀνθολογίαι），字面即「采集起来的花束」。定为《占星选集》（不作《选集》）——「选集」二字在中文里太泛，冠以「占星」方能一望而知其类，且与中文占星社群通行说法一致。首见处给原文。Obert 所据当为 Mark Riley 英译本。
William Lilly, Christian Astrology利利《基督教占星学》1647 年，英语占星学的里程碑。
Firmicus Maternus, Mathesis《星占八书》依工程所有者决定保留《星占八书》。中文资料中另见《数学八卷》《星占学》等常见译法；三名在校注中互见，以兼顾正文统一与资料查检。拉丁原题为 Matheseos libri VIII。
Abu Ma'shar, The Great Introduction阿布·马沙尔《大导论》阿拉伯占星学的重要总论著作；本章据本·戴克斯英译选本转引。
Ben Dykes, Introductions to Traditional Astrology本·戴克斯《传统占星学导论集》收入阿布·马沙尔及其他重要阿拉伯占星师的导论性文献英译。
Al-Biruni, Book of Instruction in the Elements of the Art of Astrology比鲁尼《占星术基础教程》依工程所有者决定定为《占星术基础教程》。「教程」保留 Book of Instruction 的教学性质，「基础」概括 Elements 所指的基础要素。
Avraham Ibn-Ezra, The Beginning of Wisdom伊本·以斯拉《智慧之始》希伯来文占星学著作；本章节录其土星征象。
Guido Bonatti, Book of Astronomy博纳蒂《天文书》全题作《星辰判断导论》；大型拉丁文汇编，承接阿拉伯占星传统。
Raphael, Guide to Astrology拉斐尔《占星指南》1877 年初版；以宫位性格描述为主，处于传统向现代占星学过渡阶段。
Sepharial, The Manual of Astrology塞法里尔《占星手册》把传统征象压缩成按主题排列的简表，并加强个人品质与人生经验。
G. K. Chesterton, Orthodoxy切斯特顿《正统》1908 年。中译本亦作《回到正统》。
Max Heindel, Simplified Scientific Astrology海因德尔《简明科学占星学》二十世纪中期流行的星盘绘制入门教材。
Max Heindel, The Message of the Stars海因德尔《群星的讯息》本书海因德尔一节土星引文的出处。
Llewellyn George, A to Z Horoscope Maker and Delineator乔治《星盘制作与释盘大全（A 至 Z）》1910 年初版；书名突出从制盘到解释的全流程工具书性质。
Marion March and Joan McEvers, The Only Way to Learn Astrology马奇、麦克埃弗斯《学习占星学的唯一途径》二十世纪七十年代起流行的心理化、关键词式占星教材系列。
Isabel Hickey, Astrology: A Cosmic Science希基《占星学：一门宇宙科学》二十世纪灵性占星学的代表性教材。
Liz Greene, Saturn: A New Look at an Old Devil格林《土星：新观点与老恶魔》心理占星学代表作；把土星经验解释为通往心理完整与自由的过程。
Steven Forrest, The Inner Sky福里斯特《内在的天空》1982 年初版，现代演化／心理占星学的流行入门教材。
Edward Bulwer-Lytton, Zanoni布尔沃-利顿《扎诺尼》1842 年秘学小说；「门槛上的居者」一语的出处。
Renn Butler, Pathways to Wholeness巴特勒《通往完整之路：原型占星学与超个人旅程》把原型占星学置于超个人心理学与趋整意识模型中。
Alfred Witte, Rules for Planetary Pictures维特《行星图式规则》天王星占星学的主要参考书，以两颗、三颗行星或点位的几何组合释义。
Reinhold Ebertin, The Combination of Stellar Influences埃伯廷《星体影响组合》宇宙生物学主要参考著作，重相位、中点与三因素组合。
Plato, Laws柏拉图《法篇》本书第 135 页引第四卷克洛诺斯时代的政治神话；所据泰勒英译把部分希腊神名改作 Saturn。
Dane Rudhyar, The Practice of Astrology鲁伊尔《占星学实践》本书引其行运章，讨论本命个体与变化宇宙之间的张力。
Alexander Ruperti, Cycles of Becoming鲁佩蒂《生成的周期：人本进路》以次限推运象征内在展开、行运象征外界压力的周期论著作。
Laurie Efrein, How to Rectify a Birth Chart埃弗林《如何校正本命盘》本书借其〈一生的占星结构如何在出生时内置〉章题阐述完整周期。
Dante, Convivio但丁《飨宴》把七艺配给七行星天，并以运行缓慢、位置最高、所需学科与经验繁多说明占星学对应土星。
C. S. Lewis, The Discarded Image刘易斯《被弃的意象：中世纪与文艺复兴文学导论》本书〈命运〉章长引其对但丁宇宙模型中 Fortune 的说明。
Aristotle, Nicomachean Ethics亚里士多德《尼各马可伦理学》本书〈平衡〉章据其中道学说讨论德性位于过度与不足之间。
Viktor Frankl, Man's Search for Meaning弗兰克尔《活出意义来：从集中营说到存在主义》以集中营经验论人类精神、意义与回应苦难的自由。
Aleksandr Solzhenitsyn, The Gulag Archipelago索尔仁尼琴《古拉格群岛》本书〈恶〉章列为直面道德之恶后仍见证终极善的作品。
Proclus, Elements of Theology普罗克洛《神学原理》仿照欧几里得公理式结构展开的晚期柏拉图主义著作；附录借此说明 science 的旧义。
Proclus, The Theology of Plato普罗克洛《柏拉图神学》以紧密推理论述存在层级与诸神位阶；作者用以说明神学在旧义下也可称 science。
Plato, Republic / Myth of Er柏拉图《理想国》／厄尔神话全书结尾的附录考察第十卷 614a–621d，以多世旅程说明正义、命定与灵魂选择。

导论Introduction

占星师astrologer大多有自己偏爱的行星——最能与之共鸣的那一颗，也是他们觉得最有意思的那一颗。通常，这颗行星在他们自己的星盘chart里格外有力，或格外显著。原书 p.10
Many astrologers have their favorite planets, the ones they resonate to the most, the ones they find the most interesting. Typically it is a planet that is particularly strong or prominent in their own chart.
我的是土星。
Mine is Saturn.
我的上升星座rising sign是摩羯座。其守护星ruling planet土星擢升exalted于天秤座，按整宫制whole sign落在第十宫，与上升点Ascendant构成一个紧密的四分相square。传统占星学traditional astrology里，上升守护星代表这个人本身。就传统占星学而言，我就是土星。关于土星，我写下的文字比其余六颗传统行星加起来还多。
My rising sign is Capricorn. The ruling planet, Saturn, is exalted in Libra in my tenth house by whole sign, aspecting the Ascendant by a tight square. In traditional astrology the ruler of the Ascendant represents the person. In traditional astrology, I am Saturn. I have written more about Saturn than all of the other six traditional planets put together.
术语擢升与整宫制
exalted → 擢升，不作现代常见的「入旺」。exaltatio 的字面义是「被抬举、被高举」，在本书里承担实义：土星擢升于天秤，正是这颗最低、最暗的星被抬到最高处的一次反转，与全书「边界／对分」的主线呼应；译作「旺」则这层意思全失。whole sign → 整宫制：以上升所在的整个星座为第一宫，不切分度数——希腊化占星学的原始宫位制，二十世纪末随传统占星学复兴而重新通行。奥伯特特意点明自己用此制，是在向读者亮明技术立场。

本书之旨Purpose

本书的目的，是把土星完整的传统含义找回recover来，同时找回一个活的脉络living context，让这份完整的传统含义在其中重新讲得通。土星的含义，是依脉络而立的。
The purpose of this book is to recover the full traditional meaning of Saturn, and to recover a living context within which that full traditional meaning makes living sense. The meaning of Saturn relies on its context.
土星最关键的那层含义——其余一切都系于其上——来自它是最外围的行星，立在时间之世界与永恒之世界的交界上。这是打开土星全部意义之丰饶的钥匙；本书要找回的，正是这层含义及其脉络。
The pivotal meaning of Saturn, that which everything else hinges on, comes from it being the outermost planet, on the border between the worlds of time and eternity. That is the key to the full riches of Saturn's meanings, and I want to recover that meaning and context in this book.
第一部循着历史，探究土星这颗行星的完整含义。第二部给出一个活的脉络，让这层含义在我们的现代世界里讲得通。第三部则就土星含义的若干具体面向作进一步的探讨与展开。
Part One explores the full meaning of the planet Saturn through history. Part Two gives a living context for that meaning to make sense in our modern world. Part Three explores and develops some specific aspects of Saturn's meaning.
术语recover / living / context 三个词
这三个词是奥伯特的口头禅，几乎每页都来，译法必须一次定死。recover →「找回」：不是「恢复」——recover 在他笔下是把丢掉的东西重新捡起来，不是把坏掉的东西修好；全书论旨正是「现代占星学把土星的一半含义弄丢了」。living →「活的」：他反复用 living、alive，针对的是「博物馆里的死知识」，译成「有生命力的」会稀释掉这股火气。context →「脉络」：不作「语境」（偏语言学）、不作「背景」（太松散）——他要说的是含义生长于其中的那套世界图景。

全书纲目Outline

第一部：历史巡礼。第一部分即历史巡礼，用意是把西方占星学史上归给土星的种种含义一一记录下来。你会发现，从二世纪的维提乌斯·瓦伦斯Vettius Valens到十七世纪的威廉·利利William Lilly，历代传统作者归给土星的含义始终相当一致，稳定得令人意外。原书 p.11
Part One: Historical Survey. The purpose of the first section, the historical survey, is to document the diifferent meanings attributed to Saturn throughout the history of Western astrology. You will find that through all of the traditional writers, from Vettius Valens in the second century to William Lilly in the seventeenth century, the meanings attributed to Saturn stay quite remarkably consistent.
直到现代，尤其是二十世纪初以后，归给土星的含义才出现重大变化。土星含义上的这些差异，与整体世界观worldview的一次重大转移相伴而生——所谓世界观，就是我们向外打量世界、作出判断时所依据的那些预设assumptions。第一部里有一章，我在其中勾勒了现代世界观的这些重大变化。
It is not until the modern era, especially from the early twentieth century and later, that we see significant changes in the meanings attributed to Saturn. The differences in meaning of Saturn go together with a significant shift in overall worldview, the assumptions we make when we look out at the world and make judgments. I have a chapter in part one where I sketch out the significant changes in worldview in the modern era.
现代占星学modern astrology那一部分里，我对现代人打量宇宙时所持的预设作了一些批评；这些预设我们自己看不见，因为它们就是我们身在其中的水。批评现代占星师，用意是把他们关于世界的预设摆到意识层面、说得明白，连同这些预设的后果一并挑明。我考察占星师实际写下的字句，按其通常的意思去理解。同时我也点出现代占星学的长处与重要贡献——我认为，把现代与传统两边的长处合起来，各以己长补对方之短，我们是能得益的。
In the modern astrology section I include critiques of the assumptions that our modern world makes when we look out at the cosmos, assumptions that are invisible to us since they are the water we swim in. The purpose of the critiques of the modern astrologers is to bring to awareness and make explicit the assumptions they make about the world, and the implications of those assumptions. I examine the actual words the astrologers wrote, and interpret them in their normal meanings. I also highlight the strengths and significant contributions that modern astrology makes, as I think we can benefit by combining the strengths of both modern and traditional astrology, and use each one's strengths to balance and complement the other's weak points.
第二部：永恒法则the Eternal Law。这一部分是全书的核心与枢轴。土星的传统含义要讲得通，就需要有一套托得住它的世界观、一种托得住它的思维方式。第二部分的用意，正是提供这一脉络与支撑。
Part Two: The Eternal Law. This section is the core and pivot of the entire book. For the traditional meaning of Saturn to make sense you need to have a supporting worldview, a way of thinking, that supports its meaning. The purpose of the second section is to provide that context and support.
土星的传统含义，建立在这样一个预设之上：宇宙有一种永恒的秩序与格局eternal order and pattern——它不是我们人类造出来、再从心里投射出去的东西，而是宇宙自身的一部分，既在我们心中，也在整个宇宙的底层结构里。土星的完整含义，包含一条嵌入宇宙底层结构的永恒道德律eternal moral law。原书 p.12
The traditional meanings of Saturn are based on the assumption that there is an eternal order and pattern to the cosmos - not something that we humans have created and projected out from our minds, but an order that is part of the cosmos itself, both within our minds and in the underlying structure of the universe as a whole. Saturn's full meaning includes an eternal moral law integrated into the underlying structure of the cosmos.
这一部分里，我回到柏拉图传统的根本处，论证确有这样一种永恒秩序，且它在我们的现代世界里依然活着、依然有意义。在现代世界，无论就科学的意义还是就道德伦理的意义而言，以永恒法则来思考仍有十分充分而鲜活的理由。
In this section I look back to the fundamentals of the Platonic tradition to argue that there is such an eternal order, and that it is still alive and meaningful in our modern world. In our modern world there are still very good living reasons for thinking in terms of an eternal law in both the scientific sense and the moral and ethical sense.
所以，第一部是历史巡礼，第二部则通过铺陈土星含义的哲学与伦理根基，提供一个参照框架，使土星的传统含义今天仍能讲得通。
So, while Part One is the historical survey, Part Two provides the frame of reference for the traditional meanings of Saturn to still make sense by laying out the philosophical and ethical underpinnings of Saturn's meaning.
第三部：土星主题诸论。第三部分收的是若干与土星相关的主题论文，我认为这些题目在现代流行文化的处境下需要重新审视。我着力于土星与人类生存中艰难的、负面的那些面向——在现代世界，这些是我们巴不得不必去面对的东西。这与现代通行的做法恰成对照：现代人极力主张把土星、乃至占星学中的一切都往正面去看。我则接纳土星这些艰难的维度——受苦、衰老、死亡、人之恶的难题——并主张它们是完整人类经验的一部分；承认并接纳它们，我们的生命反而更丰盛。
Part Three: Essays on Saturn Themes. In the third section are essays on Saturn related themes that I think need to be revisited in the context of our modern popular culture. I am concentrating on the difficult or negative aspects of Saturn and human existence that we would just as soon not have to deal with in our modern world. This is in contrast to the common modern approach to heavily emphasize viewing Saturn, and everything else in astrology, in a positive light. I embrace these difficult dimensions of Saturn - suffering, aging, death, the problem of human evil - and argue that they are part of the full human experience, and that our lives are richer when we acknowledge and embrace them.
附记：熟悉我博客的读者，也许读过我写的几篇文章，论土星象征的阴性一面。那些文章未收入本书。此题日后另书处理。
Note: Those of you who are familiar with my online blog may have seen articles which I wrote on the topic of Saturn's symbolism being feminine. Those essays are not included in this book. That topic will be addressed in a separate book in the future.

切斯特顿检验The Chesterton Test

本书好几处会用到我称之为「切斯特顿检验the Chesterton Test」的东西。这名字取自英国伟大的记者兼作家 G. K. 切斯特顿G. K. Chesterton。下面这段引文出自他那本书名极不合时宜的著作——《正统》Orthodoxy。原书 p.13
At various points in this book I will be referring to what I call the Chesterton Test. I name it after the great British journalist and writer G K Chesterton. This quote is from his book with the very unfashionable title, Orthodoxy.
长词从我们身边隆隆驶过，像一列列长长的火车。我们知道，车上载着成千上万的人，他们太累了，或者太懒了，懒得自己走路，也懒得自己思想。
Long words go rattling by us like long railway trains. We know they are carrying thousands who are too tired or too indolent to walk and think for themselves.
不妨偶尔做一次这样的练习：把自己所持的任何一个看法，全用单音节的词说出来。
It is a good exercise to try for once in a way to express any opinion one holds in words of one syllable.
你若是说：「不定期刑之社会效用，已为一切犯罪学家所公认，乃我们朝着更人道、更科学的刑罚观演进之社会学进程的一环」——这样的话你可以一连讲上几个钟头，而颅内的灰质几乎不必动一动。
If you say "The social utility of the indeterminate sentence is recognized by all criminologists as a part of our sociological evolution towards a more humane and scientific view of punishment," you can go on talking like that for hours with hardly a movement of the gray matter inside your skull.
可你若是这样开口：「我要琼斯去坐牢，要布朗来定琼斯什么时候出来」——你会悚然发现：你不得不思考了。
But if you begin "I wish Jones to go to gaol and Brown to say when Jones shall come out," you will discover, with a thrill of horror, that you are obliged to think.
难的不是长词；难的是短词。
The long words are not the hard words; it is the short words that are hard.
校记「单音节词」在汉语中失效
切斯特顿的检验建立在英语「长词＝拉丁—希腊借词＝抽象、可空转」与「短词＝盎格鲁—撒克逊本土词＝具体、逼人思考」的对立上。汉语没有这个对立：汉语的字本来就是单音节，抽象与具体的分野落在双音节抽象名词（社会效用、演进、进程）与单音节动词（要、去、坐、出）之间。译文因此把第三段刻意堆成文言腔的名词链，第四段压成大白话短句，用汉语自身的对立复制原文的对立——这是意译，但保住了检验本身能不能成立。若照字面译作「用单音节的词」，中文读者会一头雾水：汉语哪个词不是单音节起家的？
我会把切斯特顿检验用在我们所考察的一些占星师的引文上。用意是帮我们把话里究竟说了什么想清楚，并弄明白其中的含意。与土星相配的那种思考，讲的是简朴与清晰——剥去多余的绒毛与噪音，让明白的意思显露出来。
I will apply the Chesterton Test to quotes from some of the astrologers we review. The purpose is to help us think clearly about what is being said, and help us understand the implications. The sort of thinking that goes with Saturn has to do with simplicity and clarity, stripping away excess fluff and noise to let the clear meaning be visible.
但愿这本书既短，又难。
Hopefully this book will be both short, and hard.
校记「short and hard」的双关
hard 在此一身三任：接切斯特顿末句的「短词才难」（difficult）；又指土星本身的质地——坚硬、冷、拒人（第 45 页作者把 hard 与 physical 并置论土星与土地）；还带「读来不好受」的意思。中译取「难」以接住引文的直接呼应，「硬」的一层只能靠此注补出。若译「既短又硬」，则与引文断了线，得不偿失。
PART ONE

第一部　历史巡礼

Part One: Historical Survey
我们先从一次巡礼开始：看看西方占星学史上归给土星的种种含义。这里有一些颇有意思的规律。若从希腊化时代最早的占星师之一维提乌斯·瓦伦斯起步，一路穿过波斯与阿拉伯时期，进入博纳蒂Bonatti的汇编，再到英语世界的里程碑之作、利利的《基督教占星学》Christian Astrology，就会〔看到〕：在这整段时期里，含义的一致性高得惊人。我们眼前分明是同一个传统，横跨二世纪到十七世纪。原书 p.14
We are going to start with a survey of the meanings attributed to Saturn throughout the history of Western astrology. There are some interesting patterns here. If we start with Vettius Valens, one of the earliest Hellenistic era astrologers, and go right through the Persian and Arabic periods, into Bonatti's compilation and the landmark English work, William Lilly's Christian Astrology, we will that there is a remarkable consistency of meaning throughout this entire era. We are recognizably looking at a single tradition spanning from the second to the seventeenth century.
越靠近现代，变动就越大。看两位十九世纪占星师的引文，已能察觉重心的移动。一进入二十世纪、现代占星学诞生，土星的含义就变得相当剧烈。我们打交道的仍然认得出是同一颗行星，但笔调不同了，重心大不相同，有些旧有的含义丢失了。从传统到现代的这一转变，同时牵涉一种很不一样的世界观。我为此专辟一章，比较传统与现代的世界观，好让读者对这一变化有个体感。
As we get closer to our modern era there are some major shifts. Looking at quotes from two astrologers from the nineteenth century, we can begin to see a shift of emphasis. Once we hit the twentieth century and the birth of modern astrology the meanings of Saturn shift quite dramatically. We are still recognizably dealing with the same planet, but the style is different, the emphases are very different, and some old meanings are lost. This shift, from traditional to modern astrology, also involves a very different view of the world. I devote a chapter here to comparing traditional and modern worldviews to give a feel for that change.
本部分余下的篇幅，我考察一批现代占星师，从二十世纪初直到今天，包括丽兹·格林Liz Greene、史蒂芬·福里斯特Steven Forrest这样广受欢迎且影响深远的人物。这一部分里我对他们的描述作了一些批评，用意是把那些为其解释染上底色的现代预设揭示出来。我们是现代的鱼，游在另一种水里；这一部分我试图让这水变得可见。最后我以一章作结，谈现代占星学独有的贡献。二十世纪有若干重要发展丰富了我们的占星实践，我要给它们应有的承认。
In the remainder of this section I examine a series of modern astrologers, from early twentieth century up to today, including popular and influential astrologers like Liz Greene and Steven Forrest. In this section I am doing some critiquing of the descriptions, and the purpose of that critique is to bring to light the kinds of modern assumptions we make that color their interpretation. We are modern fish swimming in a different kind of water, and in this section I am trying to make the water visible. I then conclude with a chapter on the unique contributions of modern astrology. There are some important developments in the twentieth century that enrich our practice of astrology, and I want to recognize them.
传统作者：二至十七世纪

维提乌斯·瓦伦斯《占星选集》Vettius Valens, Anthology

安条克的维提乌斯·瓦伦斯（约公元 120—175 年）著有《占星选集》Anthology，这是我们手上最早的一部执业占星师working astrologer手册，记录了一位真正在接活的占星师如何描述土星。当代对希腊化占星学的兴趣复兴，此书正是其核心文本之一。原书 p.15
Vettius Valens of Antioch (c. 120- 175 AD) is the author of the Anthology, the earliest working astrologer's handbook we have, the record of how a working astrologer describes Saturn. It is one of the books at the center of a contemporary revival of interest in Hellenistic astrology.
出处《占星选集》与其英译
希腊原题 Anthologiae（Ἀνθολογίαι），字面即「采集起来的花束」，中文亦见《选集》《占星文选》诸译；本表定为《占星选集》——「选集」二字太泛，冠以「占星」方能一望而知其类，且与中文占星社群通行说法一致。首见处给原文。此书之为「最早的执业手册」，关键在瓦伦斯书中保存了上百张真实盘例，多可由现代天文软件回推验证生年，是希腊化占星学断代的硬证据。奥伯特所引英译，行文与 Mark Riley 的免费英译本一致（Riley 未正式出版，长期在网络流通），本译本据其英文译出；凡涉希腊原文的判断，本注一律标明「据英译推测」，不代 Riley 立论。
下面第一组引文，出自逐一交代七颗行星含义的那一节。
This first series of quotes are from a section on the meanings of each of the seven planets in turn.
土星使生于其下者卑琐、恶毒、忧劳、自贬、孤独、诡诈、行诈而隐秘、严苛、消沉，带一副伪善的神气，形容污秽，衣着黑色，纠缠不休，面带愁苦，境遇困顿，性好航海，操水边之业。
Saturn makes those born under him petty, malignant, care-worn, self-depreciating, solitary, deceitful, secretive in their trickery, strict, downcast, with a hypocritical air, squalid, black-clad, importunate, sad-looking, miserable, with a nautical bent, plying waterside trades.
请注意这里与水的关联，也注意那些让一个人变小、变负面、受限、不可信的特质。此处的「自贬」意思是自我设限、自我削减。也请注意与黑色的关联，以及与恶运的关联。另有一簇含义关乎欺骗、隐藏、行诈与伪善。若把处在光中视为诚实，那么处在土星的黑暗中就是欺瞒、隐藏、不诚。
Note the connection with water, and also with traits that make a person small, or negative, or limited, or untrustworthy. Self-depreciating here means limiting or cutting down oneself. Also note the connection with the color black, and with bad fortune. Another cluster of meanings relates to deceiving, hiding, tricking or being hypocritical. If you think of being in the light as honest, then being in the darkness of Saturn is deceiving, hiding, dishonest.
校记征象清单的译法
古典征象清单（signification list）在原文中是一串无连词的形容词／名词，功能近于中医的「主治」条目，不是文学描写。译文因此取二字与四字相间的短促节奏，不加连词、不作铺陈，并让「形容污秽，衣着黑色」这类由介词短语（squalid, black-clad）转出的项落在四字上，以维持清单的均齐。凡清单一律用此体例，与奥伯特自己的按语（现代白话）在语感上区分开——这也是本译本判读「哪句是瓦伦斯、哪句是作者」的一道内部标记。
土星又致人受辱、迟滞、失业、事业受阻、讼事无尽、生意倾覆、隐秘之事、囚禁、镣铐、悲伤、控告、涕泣、丧亲、被俘，以及弃婴。
Saturn also causes humblings, sluggishness, unemployment, obstacles in business, interminable lawsuits, subversion of business, secrets, imprisonment, chains, griefs, accusations, tears, bereavement, capture, exposures of children.
术语exposures of children ＝ 弃婴
不是「孩子被曝光」。exposure 在古典语境中是专名，指希腊罗马世界合法的弃婴习俗——将新生儿置于野外或市集听其生死，多因残疾、家贫或性别。瓦伦斯的清单里它与「被俘」并列，同属「人被弃置、被夺走」的一类。此条是本试译中最容易被机器译反的一处，也说明为何古典占星文本必须由懂古代史的人过一遍。
这一节包含难题、恶运、死亡，以及那些起阻挡或设限作用的事件。其中有与「耗时长久、拖延不绝」之事的关联，也有与隐秘、与藏在暗处之物的关联。
This section includes problems, bad fortunes, death and events that block or limit. There are connections with things that take a long time or stretch on, and connections with secrecy, things that are hidden or in the dark.
因其主管土地，土星造就农奴与农人；又使人成为物业的租赁者、包税人，且行事暴烈。原书 p.16
Saturn makes serfs and farmers because of its rule over the land, and it causes men to be renters of property, tax farmers, and violent in action.
术语tax farmers ＝ 包税人
与农业无关。罗马的 publicani 向国家承包某地某税的征收权，先垫付定额、再向民间超额索取，差额自留——《新约》里的「税吏」正是此辈，故其名声与「暴烈」「受憎」相连，与土星的严苛、榨取一路。英文 farm 此处是「承包」义（to farm out），译作「包税人」为定译。此条与上条「弃婴」同属一类陷阱：字面可解，解出来全错。
这里可以看到土星与土地的关联，与坚硬之物、物质之物的关联。这也包括与土地打交道的人——租地者、佣工，或地主。
Here we see the connection of Saturn with land, with what is hard and physical. This includes people who work with the land, as renters, as servants or as owner.
它把高位显职交到人手里，使人得以监理事务、经管他人产业，并为他人之子作父。
It puts into one's hands great ranks and distinguished positions, supervisions, management of others' property, and the fathership of others' children.
土星与凌驾他人的权力与威权相关。这与土星身为最外围的行星、位居众星之上有关。与此相连的是主宰、管理与法律这几个主题。年岁也与土星相关，因为年岁本身是威权的一部分。
Saturn is associated with power and authority over others. This relates to Saturn being the outermost planet, above all the others. Associated with this we see themes of rulership, management, and law. Saturn is also related to age as part of authority.
于材质，它主管铅、木与石。
Of materials, it rules lead, wood, and stone.
于身体肢节，它主管腿、膝、腱、淋巴、黏液、膀胱、肾，以及内部隐藏的脏器。
Of the limbs of the body, it rules the legs, the knees, the tendons, the lymph, the phlegm, the bladder, the kidneys, and the internal, hidden organs.
坚硬之物，或隐藏之物。土星所属的星座中，摩羯座主膝，水瓶座主胫与踝，天秤座主肾。
Things hard or things hidden. Of Saturn's Signs, Capricorn rules knees, Aquarius rules the shins and ankles, Libra rules the kidneys.
校记此段归属存疑，兼论「土星所属的星座」
（一）归属。原书此段排作引文（缩进同瓦伦斯诸条），但首句「坚硬之物，或隐藏之物」是对上一条身体部位表的归纳，语气与奥伯特自己的按语一致，不像瓦伦斯清单的句法。疑为排版时误将作者按语并入引文块。译文照原书排版处理，出此注志疑；待核 Riley 英译本相应段落即可判定。（二）「土星所属的星座」。摩羯、水瓶是土星的两个本宫（domicile），天秤则是土星的擢升之座——三者性质不同，原文以 “Saturn's Signs” 一语笼统带过。这不是错，是希腊化文本的惯常笼统；但中文读者若不知此别，会误以为天秤也归土星主管。
土星表征因寒与湿而起的伤患，如水肿、神经痛、痛风、咳嗽、痢疾、疝气、痉挛。
Saturn is indicative of injuries arising from cold and moisture, such as dropsy, neuralgia, gout, cough, dysentery, hernia, spasms.
我们又一次看到土星在早期与寒、湿的关联。在后面的引文里，土星将与身体干枯、失去柔韧一类的病症相关，例如关节炎。随着时间推移，寒与干的关联会越来越占上风。寒／干的关联与四体液humors学说有关：在该体系中土星被归为忧郁质melancholic，是众行星中最寒、最干的一颗。行星一旦被系于某套分类体系，凡与该分类不合的含义，往往就此凋落。
Again we see the early connection of Saturn with cold and wet. In later quotes we will see Saturn associated with diseases related to the body drying up or becoming inflexible, like arthritis. The cold and dry associations with become more dominant as we move through time. The cold/dry association relates to the system of humors where Saturn is classified as melancholic, the most cold and dry of the planets. Where planets are associated with a system of classification, the meanings that don't fit within the classification tend to fall away.
提示一句话说清「寒湿 → 寒干」这场位移
四体液学说把万物按「冷／热 × 干／湿」四格分派：血液（热湿）、黄胆（热干）、黏液（冷湿）、黑胆（冷干，即忧郁质 melancholic）。土星一旦被钉进「黑胆＝冷干」这一格，它早期那些与水相连的征象——航海、水边行业、溺毙、水肿——就与格子对不上了，于是逐代脱落。奥伯特这句「与分类不合的含义往往就此凋落」是全书方法论的一次小型示范：不是占星师发现了新事实，而是一套分类表把旧事实挤了出去。这正是他要「找回」的那一半。
它表征以下诸症：附身、同性恋，与堕落。原书 p.17
It is indicative of these syndromes: possession, homosexuality, and depravity.
背景此条须作历史文本读
这是一份二世纪的清单，反映的是古代地中海世界把性角色、宗教附身与「道德败坏」并置的分类习惯，既非奥伯特的判断，也不构成任何现代意义上的断言。英译作 homosexuality，但希腊原文所指多与古代对性行为中主动／被动角色的评价体系有关，与现代「性取向」概念并不对应；以现代词对译，本身就是一次时代错置（此点据英译推测，待核希腊原文）。译文照录以存原貌，读者当以读古代医书、古代法典的方式读它。
这些是土星与邪恶、腐败、罪与悖离常轨之物的关联。
These are associations of Saturn with things evil, or corrupt, sinful or deviant.
土星造就独身者与寡居者，造就丧亲与无嗣。
Saturn makes bachelors and widows, bereavements, and childlessness.
这把土星与因死亡而致的失去、或因不能生育而致的失去联系起来。
This connects Saturn with losses by death, or from an inability to give birth.
它致人横死于水、于绞勒、于囚禁，或于痢疾。
It causes violent deaths by water, strangulation, imprisonment, or dysentery.
土星的这些关联全都是与死亡相关的恶运，其中又一次出现与水的关联。
All of these Saturn associations are bad fortune related to death, and again the association with water.
它又致人仆面而倒。它是涅墨西斯之星；属昼派。
It also causes falling on the face. It is the star of Nemesis; it is of the day sect.
背景涅墨西斯
涅墨西斯（Νέμεσις）掌的不是泄愤式的复仇，而是「份额的归还」：谁僭越了自己应得的份额，她就把多出来的那部分讨回去。这一层「应得、必至、可迟不可免」的意味，正是土星作为审判者的原型——也是奥伯特第二部要接的「永恒道德律」的伏笔。
术语sect 何以译「宗派」，不译「区分」「区间」
一、这个词本身是什么。希腊 αἵρεσις（hairesis）字面是「所选定的一方」，古典时期专指哲学、医学的学派党派（斯多亚派、伊壁鸠鲁派各称一个 hairesis；英语 heresy「异端」即出于此）。拉丁 secta 出自 sequi「跟从」，即「所追随的那一党」。所以这个词的本义是「阵营归属」，不是「差别」。

二、它在技术上管什么。昼夜二分同时落在三个层面：
· 盘：太阳在地平之上为昼盘，之下为夜盘。
· 行星：固定党籍。日、木、土属昼派；月、金、火属夜派；水星两属（晨出于日前属昼，昏没于日后属夜）。
· 状态：行星的党与盘的昼夜相合，即得派（业内常称「得时」），其性情转和——凶星减其害，吉星增其益；不合则失派（业内常称「失时」）。
古人的排法有其道理：把冷的土星编进热的昼派，把热的火星编进冷的夜派，各以对方之性相济。这正是瓦伦斯此处「属昼派」一语的分量，也解释了菲尔米库斯为何说土星在昼盘中的判词远比夜盘正面。

三、三个中译的比较。
· 「区间」——建议弃用。汉语「区间」指数轴上的一段、或路线的一节，毫无「阵营」义，反而强烈暗示「刻度上的范围」，与原义正相反。此译疑由阿拉伯 ḥayyiz（本义「区域、领地」）辗转而来；但 halb 与 ḥayyiz 都不是 sect 的译名。本表保留原词：halb 指行星依其昼夜属性处在合宜半球；ḥayyiz 则在此基础上再要求行星与所在星座的阴阳属性相合。二者与 sect 不同层级。用「区间」译 sect，等于把传统文献中本须分辨的术语并成一个。
· 「区分」（你说台湾多用此译）——可作话题标签，不宜作术语。它译的是功能（「这里作了一道区分」），不是事物（「一个阵营」）。原文 “Saturn is of the day sect” 是归属判断：土星属于哪一队。译成「土星属于日间区分」不成话。更要命的是派生词：in sect / out of sect / contrary to sect / sect light 一整套，在「区分」上全都长不出来（「合于区分」？「区分之光」？）。且「区分」是极常用的动词，译文里无法与日常用法分辨，检索也失效。
· 「宗派」——本表所取。合于 αἵρεσις／secta 的党派本义；是名词，能长出完整的派生族：昼派／夜派、得派／失派、逆派、宗派光体。代价是「宗」字带佛教宗门的联想，略重；但行文中多用单字「派」即可化解（「土星属昼派」「此盘火星失派」），「宗派」只在需要正式称名时出现。

四、本表锁定的派生译法（各章不得自造）：sect →宗派；the day/night sect →昼派／夜派；diurnal/nocturnal chart →昼盘／夜盘；in sect →得派（常用：得时）；out of sect →失派（常用：失时）；contrary to sect →逆派；sect light →宗派光体。
sect light 不作「派主星」——「主星」已被 hyleg（命主星）、dominus geniturae（生辰主星）、lord of the millennium（千年主星）占满，须避让。
涅墨西斯是希腊神话中掌复仇、掌报应之正义的女神；她所施的惩罚是应得的，尽管可能迟来。这凸显出土星与正义、与审判、与法律之刑罚一面的关联。前面我们已见过土星与法律的关联，那是就威权一面而言的。
Nemesis is the Greek goddess of vengeance or retributive justice, a punishment that is deserved, though one that could be delayed. This highlights a connection of Saturn with justice, judgment, the punitive side of the law. Earlier we saw an association of Saturn with law as related to authority.
其色如海狸香，其味涩。
It is like castor in color and astringent in taste.
术语castor ＝ 海狸香，非蓖麻
castor 在此指 castoreum（海狸香）——河狸腺囊分泌的膏状物，色深褐近黑，古代入药亦作香料。不是蓖麻（castor oil plant）：蓖麻油近乎无色，与「土星之色」的整条脉络（黑、暗褐）对不上。译作「海狸香」既保住颜色，也保住那股「腺体、陈旧、气味浓重」的土星气质。
涩味使口中发紧、收缩、发干，因此与土星之寒且干相关。
Astringent tastes make the mouth shrivel or shrink or dry up, so it is related to Saturn being cold and dry.
下面这一组引文出自后面一节，瓦伦斯在其中给出土星与其余各星两两相配时的含义。
This next series gives quotes is from a later section where Valens gives the meanings of Saturn paired with each of the other planets.
土星与木星同处时，二者彼此相协，带来来自遗产与收养的好处，并使人成为田产之主、监护人、他人产业的经管者、管家与收税人。
When Saturn and Jupiter are together, they are in agreement with each other, and they bring about benefits from legacies and adoptions, and they cause men to be masters of property consisting of land, to be guardians, managers of others' property, stewards, and tax gatherers.
这些含义几乎全是正面的，兼有威权与掌控。所谓「遗产」是因人亡故而来的继承。这里又一次出现土星与土地的关联——也就是掌握土地的财富。原书 p.18
These meanings are almost all positive, and combine authority and control. Legacies are inheritances that come from deaths. We also have the connection again of Saturn with land, so controlling the wealth of land.
土星与火星彼此敌对，生出逆转与毁败。它们带来家中争吵、失和与仇恨，连同背叛、阴谋、恶意与诉讼。然而，若这两颗星不在自己的星座、也不在起作用的星座，且有吉星与之成相位，则会造就显赫高贵的本命盘，只是其福乐不稳，且易遭意外的凶险与背叛。
Saturn and Mars are hostile, productive of reversals and ruin. They bring family quarrels, disharmony, and hatred, along with treachery, plots, malevolence, and trials. However, if these stars are not in their own or in operative signs, and if they have benefics in aspect, they produce distinguished and noble nativities, although unsteady in their happiness and prone to unexpected dangers and treachery.
两颗凶星malefic凑在一起，是暴烈而具破坏性的。文中提到的「高贵」，指的是这两颗凶星若居于显要位置时所能施展的力量。无论有力与否，这两颗行星合起来的作用总带着不稳与不可信。你不会愿意去得罪一个有这些特质的人。
The two malefics together are violent and destructive. The references to nobility refers to the power that these malefics could exert if in a prominent position. Whether powerful or not there is an instability and lack of trustworthiness to the action of these two planets combined. You would not want to be on the bad side of a person with these traits.
术语malefic 何以不译「恶星」
本表定 benefic／malefic 为吉星／凶星，不用「善星／恶星」。理由在书内：奥伯特第三部专辟〈Benefic and Malefic〉与〈Evil〉两章，要点正是「凶」（造成损害）与「恶」（道德上的败坏）不是一回事——土星作为 malefic 是说它带来艰难，不是说它道德上邪恶。中译若用「恶星」，这场辨析在第三部会整个塌掉。此条是「术语契约必须为后文预留余地」的样板：译名要对的不只是本页，是全书。
土星与水星互为盟友，生出作为与职业。不过它们也会带来关乎宗教的毁谤、讼事与债务，以及文书与钱财方面的纷扰。另一方面，这两颗星造就的人不乏资财、不乏才智，阅历与觉察都深，好奇而有远见，是学者，是神秘学问的追寻者，敬畏神明，但心上负担甚重。
Saturn and Mercury are allies and productive of activities/employment. They do, however, bring slanders about religion, lawsuits, and debts, as well as disturbances about written matters and money. On the other hand, these stars make men who are not without resources and not unintelligent, with much experience and awareness, and who are curious, far-seeing scholars, seekers after mystic lore, revering the gods, but with much on their consciences.
术语activities/employment ＝ 作为与职业
英译此处用斜杠并置两词，是译者对希腊 πρᾶξις（praxis）的处理：该词兼指「所行之事」与「赖以谋生之业」，英语无单一对应词，故并列。中译取「作为与职业」，保留其双重性，并在下文土星—金星条中沿用同一译法。凡英译出现斜杠并置，本译本一律译成并列的两个中文词，不择一舍一——斜杠本身是译者留下的「此处不可单译」的记号，不该在中译里消失。
把土星与水星对商贸的主管权合起来，就得到对钱财的掌控。水星又与学问相关，与土星相合便给出深度、深邃、神秘的一面，以及一个探究隐秘真理的研究者。总体而言，土星与水星的组合被视为正面。原书 p.19
Combining Saturn with Mercury's rulership of commerce and we get control of money. Mercury is also associated with learning, which combines with Saturn to give depth, profundity, a mystic side, a researcher into hidden truths. Overall Saturn and Mercury together is seen as a positive combination.
就作为与职业而言，土星与金星的作用是和谐的：它们在情感纠葛与婚姻上促成顺遂，但这份相协与益处只维持一时，不能到底。事实上它们招致虐待、离异、变心与死亡，常使人与出身卑微者、地位低下者纠缠不清，从而陷入伤害与讼事。
Saturn and Venus act harmoniously with respect to activities/employment: they promote success with respect to entanglements and marriage, agreeing and beneficial only for a time, not to the end. Indeed they cause abuse, divorces, inconstancy, and death, often entangling men with the base-born and the lowly, and causing them to fall into harm and lawsuits.
这些大多与金星和关系、婚姻的关联有关。土星—金星的连结在稳定时是好的，但一旦落于弱势debilitated，就满是难题与争斗。这里同样有土星与死亡的关联。
Most of these relate to Venus as associated with relationships and marriage. The Saturn Venus connection is good when it is stable, but filled with problems and strife when debilitated. You also have the connection of Saturn with death.
土星与月亮是有益的，生出钱财、田产、船舶之主权，以及来自亡者的利益——尤其当月亮恰好行至初见之后的那一段轨道上，且有吉星与之成相位时。此时它带来与显贵者的往来、馈赠，以及仇敌的挫败。然而这一组合在财物上并不稳固；就女性而言则不安而痛苦，因有分离、憎恨与悲伤。它还生出身体的苦楚、猝发的痉挛、主司官能与神经的疼痛，以及要人的死亡。
Saturn and the Moon are beneficial, productive of money, estates, ship ownership, and profits from the deceased, especially if the moon happens to be in the part of its orbit just following first visibility and has benefics in aspect. Then it causes association with the great, gifts, and the discomfiture of enemies. This combination, however, is unsteady with respect to possession, and with respect to women it is insecure and painful because of separations, hatred, and grief. It also produces bodily suffering, sudden fits, pains of the governing faculties and nerves, as well as the deaths of important figures.
背景「初见之后的那一段轨道」
初见first visibility（希腊 φάσις）指月亮离开太阳光辉、在日落后西方低空首度重现的那一刻——约合朔后一两日。此后月亮渐盈、光增，希腊化占星学以之为月亮「增光」的开端，判为得力。英译 “the part of its orbit just following first visibility” 说的正是这一段渐盈期。此处不可译作「初见」的现代天文义（首次观测），也不宜简化为「新月后」——新月（合朔）不可见，初见才是可见的起点，二者差一两天，在古代判断中是实打实的差别。
这是月亮主管起落无常的命运；我们又一次看到土星与死亡、与亡者、与遗产的关联。月亮又与女性相关，因而与分离相关。月亮关乎身体健康，与土星相合便给出肉身的苦楚。
This is the Moon ruling changing fortunes, and again we see the connection of Saturn with death, the deceased, and legacies. The Moon is also related to women and thus to separations. The Moon relates to bodily health, which combined with Saturn gives physical suffering.
土星与太阳彼此不合，怀着恶意地给出、又夺走财物与友谊。因此生于这一相会之下的人，遭受暗中的仇视、显贵者的威胁，被人设计陷害，终身为人所憎。若能把自己的角色扮好，他们会比大多数仇敌活得更久。他们并非不乏资财，只是心绪不宁、久历苦楚。在这一连串逆转的冲击中，他们能自持。
Saturn and the Sun are at odds, giving and taking away possessions and friendships maliciously. Therefore those born under such a juncture suffer secret enmities and threats from great persons and are plotted against by some and live hated to the end. Playing their part well, they outlive most of their enemies. They are, however, not without resources, but are disturbed and long-suffering. They are self-controlled in this onslaught of reversals.
土星—太阳的这些含义，大多源自一个事实：这两颗行星在各自主管的黄道星座上正相对分opposition——土星在水瓶座，太阳在狮子座。最热最亮的行星，对着最冷最暗的行星。土星总体上代表对分相，因而也代表一切阻挡与妨碍之物。原书 p.20
Most of these Saturn-Sun meanings come from the fact that the two planets are in opposition in the Zodiac signs they rule, Saturn in Aquarius opposite Sun in Leo. We have the hottest and brightest planet opposite the coldest and darkest. Saturn stands for the opposition aspect in general, and thus anything that blocks or hinders.
提示这一句是全书的枢轴
日狮／土瓶的对分，出自世界本命盘Thema Mundi——传说中世界受造时的星盘：月置巨蟹、日置狮子，其余五星依次外排，土星遂落水瓶，与日正对。奥伯特在第 43—48 页把这一句展开成一张对照表（热干／冷湿、光／暗、昼／夜、夏／冬、显／隐、高／低），并由此推出土星的全部含义簇。换言之：土星不是「凶」，土星是「太阳的对面」。一切阻挡、延迟、敌对、隐藏、寒冷、低下，都是从这一个几何事实上长出来的。试译到此为止，但这一句已足以看出全书的推演方式——他要证明的是传统含义有几何与宇宙论的根据，不是历代占星师的经验累加。
校记原书文字讹误四处
本试译范围内，原书有四处显系笔误或校对疏漏，译文径改并于此出注，不照录讹字：
① p. 11「the diifferent meanings」——d 后衍一 i，当作 different。
② p. 14「we will that there is a remarkable consistency」——脱动词，当作 we will see that…；译文补「〔看到〕」，加方括号志补。
③ p. 16「The cold and dry associations with become more dominant」——衍一 with，当作 will become 或径删。
④ p. 17「This next series gives quotes is from a later section」——句法两存，当作 This next series of quotes is from…。
体例说明：原书观点上可商榷之处（如 p. 16「土星所属的星座」笼统合并本宫与擢升）一律照译出注，不代作者修正；文字上的手民之误则径改出注。两者处理方向相反，这是全书通例，后续各章沿用。

菲尔米库斯《星占八书》Firmicus Maternus, Mathesis

尤利乌斯·菲尔米库斯·马特尔努斯Julius Firmicus Maternus（约 285—360）是罗马律师、占星师astrologer兼作家。他的《星占八书》Mathesis是我们现存古典时代早期篇幅最长、内容最为广博的占星学论著。菲尔米库斯极其重视宗派sect：一张星盘究竟是昼盘还是夜盘，也就是属昼还是属夜。土星被视为昼间行星，所以土星在昼盘diurnal chart中的征象，远比在夜盘nocturnal chart中正面。原书 p.21
Julius Firmicus Maternus (c. 285-360) was a Roman lawyer, astrologer and writer. His book Mathesis is the longest and most extensive astrology treatise that we have from this early classical era. Firmicus places a very strong importance on sect, whether a chart is diurnal or nocturnal, day or night. Saturn is considered a day planet, so the meanings for Saturn in a day chart are much more positive than for a night chart.
出处《星占八书》的其他常见译法
本书依工程术语契约保留《星占八书》。中文资料中另常见《数学八卷》与《星占学》；前者贴近拉丁原题 Matheseos libri VIII 的卷数与旧义，后者突出全书的占星内容。为便于资料查检，三种译名在此互见，正文仍统一用《星占八书》。
术语宗派决定土星如何显现
这里的 sect 不是思想学说上的「宗派」，而是行星按昼夜分成的两个阵营：日、木、土属昼派，月、金、火属夜派，水星随晨昏而变。太阳在地平线上为昼盘，在地平线下为夜盘；行星所属之派与星盘昼夜相合，便是「得派」（业内常称「得时」）。

所以「土星是昼间行星」并不等于土星只在白天出现，而是说它在昼盘里较能依本性有序行事。到了夜盘，寒冷、阻滞与损失的一面便更难约束。本章四组判断的正负翻转，都由这一条控制。
这里我从若干宫位中选取一些归给土星的含义，摘录如下。你会看到，它们沿用了其他传统文献提到的同一批主题，再把这些主题落到具体宫位的脉络中；依星盘的宗派不同，其表现也就更正面或更负面。
I include excerpts here from the meanings attributed to Saturn in a sample of the houses. You will see that they pick up on the same themes that are mentioned in other traditional texts and applies them to the house context, making them more positive or negative depending on the sect of the chart.
校记“they … applies” 主谓不一致
原书 p. 21 作 they pick up … and applies them，并列谓语前一项用复数 pick，后一项却误用单数 applies。依主语 they 当作 apply。此为显然的文字讹误，中文径改为「它们沿用……再把……落到」，不照录其语法错误。
土星若居第四宫，且在昼盘中占据此宫，便使命主贪求钱财，成为金银的保管者。但若它在夜间处于这一星座，便会耗散父业，并使父亲早亡。
Saturn posited in the 4th house, will if it holds this house by day, make those greedy for money, and custodians of gold and silver. But if it was in this sign by night, it dissipates the paternal inheritance, and will cause the quick death of the father.
这里，土星与父亲、年岁和财富相关。
Here Saturn is associated with the father, with age, and with wealth.
土星若从上升点数起居第九宫，便会造就著名的术士、声名卓著的哲学家或神庙祭司，其魔法声望远播。依星座性质而定，它也会造就占卜者、先知和占星师；他们总是勤于作出真实的解释，给出的答复仿佛出自某种神圣权威。
Saturn posited in the 9th house from the ASC will make famous mages or renowned philosophers, or priests of temples, always famous in their reputation for magic. According to the quality of the sign, it also makes diviners, prophets and astrologers always industrious for their true interpretation, and whose responses are such as if they were brought forth by a kind of divine authority.
背景第九宫的术士、祭司与占卜者
古典占星学的第九宫关乎神、宗教仪式、预言与高深学问，因此这份名单把今日看来分属不同领域的人并列：mages 作「术士」，指掌握祭仪、占卜或秘传知识者；神庙祭司执行公共祭祀；占卜者、先知与占星师则以不同方式传达神意。

菲尔米库斯在意的不是现代意义上的职业分类，而是这些人都能接近隐秘而高深的知识，并以超越个人意见的权威作答。这正好让第九宫的宗教属性与土星的深邃、年长和权威连在一起。
土星与智慧、深邃以及一切深藏之物相关，所以这里也涉及对隐秘、深奥及秘传之事的探究。思想的深度与隐秘之事，从最早期的文献起就是土星主题；在这里，它们与掌管宗教的第九宫结合，便显露出来。
Saturn is associated with wisdom and profundity, things that are deep, so this also relates to exploring hidden and deep and occult matters. Depth of thought and hidden matters are Saturn themes since the earliest texts, and the association here with the ninth house of religion brings those out.
但若土星在夜盘中居于此宫，便使命主承受诸神之怒与帝王之不悦。原书 p.22
But if Saturn is posited in this house by night, it will make the native to suffer from the wrath of the Gods, and the displeasure of Emperors.
这里出现的是来自权威者的负面裁断，无论权威者是诸神还是帝王。土星要么与诸神协力，要么在诸神震怒中受审。须注意，原文并未说受土星影响的人会去审判别人；它说的是这个人会承受审判。
Here we have negative judgments from those in authority, whether Gods or Emperors. Saturn is either working with the gods or judged in wrath by the gods. Notice that it does not say anything about the person influenced by Saturn doing the judging, but rather suffering the judgment.
提示土星在这里不是审判者
现代读法容易把「土星＝审判」直接理解成一个严厉、爱评判别人的人格特征。奥伯特在这里特意把方向倒回来：原典说的是命主受到更高权威的裁断，而不是命主掌握裁断权。

昼盘里，土星与神圣秩序协力，于是表现为哲学、祭司职分与可信的预言；夜盘里，同一套高于个人的秩序转成从上而下的震怒与惩处。改变的不是「有没有法则」，而是命主与法则站在怎样的关系中。
土星若从上升点数起居第十宫，便会造就帝王、军队统帅、禁卫军长官。若土星以这种方式居于其擢升exalted之处，并在昼盘中位于天顶，便会造就品行端正、习俗淳厚而且富有的农人；其产业总在海边、河畔或沼泽近旁。它也会带来极丰厚的资财、盛大的荣耀，以及来自显贵之人的遗产。
Saturn posited in the tenth house from the ASC will make Emperors, military commanders, prefects of the Imperial Bodyguard. If, with Saturn posited thus in those places in which it is exalted, in the MC by day, it will make honest farmers with honest customs, but wealthy ones, and those whose possessions are always adjacent to the sea or rivers or to swamps. It will also give the greatest assets, great glory, and inheritances from greater persons.
这里，土星依自身状态不同，或与权威和控制的职位相关，或与其完全相反的一端相关。作为最外围的行星，土星若与控制相联，就是从最高处实施统治，是一种权力登峰造极的位置。走到相反的极端，它也可以是最底层：奴隶与农奴，以及受严酷控制的人。我们在这里还再次看到，土星始终把土地与水联系在一起。
Here Saturn is associated with positions of authority and control, or their exact opposite, depending on its condition. As the outermost planet, when Saturn is associated with control it is rulership from the top, a position of extreme power. At the opposite extreme it can be the bottom, slaves and serfs, those subjected to harsh control. We also see the persistent connection of Saturn with land and water together.
但若土星在夜盘中居于天顶，便主不幸：使人丧失遗产，不得婚姻，不得子女……尤其是，土星若在夜盘中居于任何角宫angle，便给本命盘带来最大的凶害：它杀害妻子，毁掉子女，并总是预示丧亲的惨痛哀伤。
But if it was found by night in the MC, it denotes misfortunes. It will cause the loss of his inheritance, it will deny marriage, it will deny children...But in particular, the star of Saturn posited by night in any angle denotes the greatest evils for the nativity, for it kills the wives, ruins the children, and always indicates the bitter pains of bereavement.
术语angle 译「角宫」
这里的 angle 不是几何学上泛指的「角度」，而是星盘四条主轴落入的四个宫：第一宫（上升）、第四宫（天底）、第七宫（下降）、第十宫（天顶）。这四宫合称角宫，力量直接而显著。

这里依中文占星学通行用法定作「角宫」；它指宫位类别，不是相位的角度。菲尔米库斯的意思是：夜盘中的土星一旦占据这四个强势位置之一，其凶害就会特别鲜明。
土星是昼间行星，所以夜间的土星处于最坏的状态，并与死亡、损失和厄运相关。
Saturn is a day planet, so Saturn at night is at its worst and is associated with death, loss and bad fortune.

阿布·马沙尔《大导论》Abu Ma'shar, The Great Introduction

阿布·马沙尔Abu Ma'shar（约 787—886）是阿拉伯占星师、天文学家，也是一位著述极丰的作家。他的许多著作以拉丁译本或阿拉伯文原本流传至今。下面这段文字出自他的《大导论》，本·戴克斯Ben Dykes编译的《传统占星学导论集》Introductions to Traditional Astrology收录了它，并收有其他重要阿拉伯占星师宗旨相近的引文。原书 p.23
Abu Ma'shar (c. 787-886) was an Arab astrologer and astronomer and a very prolific writer, and many of his books have come down to us, either in Latin or in the original Arabic. The following text is from his Great Introduction and is included in Introductions to Traditional Astrology by Ben Dykes, along with quotes from other important Arabic astrologers that are along the same lines.
这段文字在本·戴克斯的书里原是一整个长段落。我把它拆成了几个较短的片段，因为它并非一脉相承的连续论述，而是一组归属含义的汇集。
This text appears in the book by Ben Dykes as a single long paragraph. I have broken it up into smaller pieces since it is not really a continuous train of thought, but rather a collection of attributes.
出处本段底本与奥伯特的分段
奥伯特并非直接从阿拉伯文翻译，而是转引本·戴克斯的英译选本 Introductions to Traditional Astrology。他还明确说明：下列段落划分是他为便于讲解而加的，戴克斯书中原是一整段。

因此，译文照录奥伯特的分段以维持中英对位，但不把这些断点误当作阿布·马沙尔原作的论证层次。
所以，土星之性为冷、干、忧郁质melancholic、暗，沉重而严酷。它或许也冷而湿，沉重而有臭气；它多食，并受人真心敬重。
Therefore, the nature of Saturn is cold, dry, melancholic, dark, of heavy harshness. And perhaps he will be cold and moist, heavy, of stinking odor, and he is of much eating and true esteem.
这里首先出现的是土星与黑暗、寒冷的关联。土星有时冷而干，有时冷而湿；这里先强调它与忧郁质、与冷干的联系。土星有臭气，指的是令人不快、有毒或正在腐烂之物的气味。腐烂、朽坏之物正处在死亡过程中。我们还看到土星与真正的敬重相关，也就是靠自己赢得、配得的尊敬。我只在这一处见过土星与多食相关。
Here we have the primary association of Saturn with dark and cold. Sometimes Saturn is cold and dry, sometimes cold and moist. Here the connection of Saturn with melancholy and cold-dry is emphasized first. Saturn is stinking as in something unpleasant or poisonous or rotting. Things which are rotting or decay are in the process of dying. We also see an association with true esteem, an earned or deserved respect. This is the only place I have seen Saturn associated with much eating.
它表征与水湿有关的劳作、土地耕种、农民、乡里同伴、土地开垦，也表征建筑、水与河流，物事的数量或尺度、土地的划分，还表征富足、众多资财、手工技艺、贪婪、赤贫与穷人。
And he signifies works of moisture and the cultivation of land, and peasants, and village companions, and the settlement of lands, also buildings and waters and rivers, and the quantities or measures of things, and the divisions of the earth, also affluence and a multitude of assets, and masteries which are done by hand, greed and the greatest poverty and the poor.
土星与土地、耕作者以及水边的土地相关，所以我们在这里再次看到它与水湿的联系。测量之所以与土星相关，是因为测量牵涉限定、分割、划界，或设立边界与框架。
Saturn relates to earth, and people who work the earth, and earth near water, so again we see the connection with moisture. The connection with measuring associates with limiting, dividing, delimiting or setting boundaries or frameworks.
提示测量为什么属于土星
测量看似是水星式的计算，奥伯特却抓住另一层：量一个东西，就是给它定下界限。一块土地丈量完毕，才有边线；一件事物被分成若干份，才有数量；一座建筑有了框架，才有确定形状。

所以这里的共同动作不是「算得快」，而是把连续之物截成有边界、有尺度的部分。这正是土星作为边界、结构与限制的功能。
土星也与财富的两个极端相连：掌控甚多，便是富足；地位卑下、遭贬抑，便是贫穷。它还与作为道德败坏的贪婪相关；贪婪就是想把超过自己公平份额的东西抓住并控制在手。原书 p.24
Saturn also is connected to extremes related to wealth - affluence from controlling much, or poverty from being lowly, cast down. There is an association with greed as a moral corruption, and greed as wanting to clutch and control more than one's fair share.
提示富足与赤贫为何同属一星
清单把「富足」和「赤贫」紧挨着列出，并不是自相矛盾。奥伯特把两者还原为同一根轴线：土星主管控制与等级；站在控制的顶端，便拥有许多，落在被控制的底端，便一无所有。

贪婪也在这条轴线上：它不是单纯喜欢钱，而是拒绝放手、力图把超过公平份额的东西锁在自己掌中。土星的含义常以这种「同一原则的两个极端」出现。
它表征海上旅行、遥远漫长而艰苦的异域旅行；也表征机巧、妒忌、才智、诱骗，临险之大胆，阻碍、犹疑、孤僻、少与人交往，骄傲、气度恢宏、虚张声势、自吹自擂、使人臣服；还表征王国的执政者，以及一切凭强力、恶行与伤害而成的事业，易怒之性，乃至战士、镣铐与牢狱；
And he signifies travel by sea, and foreign travel that is far away and of great length, and bad. And cleverness, envy and wits and seductions, and boldness in dangers and impediment, and hesitation, and being singular, and a scarcity of association with men, and pride and magnanimity and bluffing and bragging and the subjection of men, also the managers of a kingdom and of every work which comes to be with force and with evil and injuries and a tendency to anger, even warriors and fettering and prison,
这里又出现土星的机敏、智慧与狡黠：它的行动不公开、不直截，而是在黑暗中隐蔽行事。
Here we see more connections with Saturn as clever, wise, crafty, action that is not overt and blunt but covert, in the darkness, hidden.
也表征言语真实、受人敬重、审慎、理解、经验、冒犯、执拗、思虑繁多、谋划深远，以及坚持己见、行事固执。
also truth in words, and esteem, and prudence and understanding, and experience and offense, and obstinacy and a multitude of thoughts and depth of counsel, and insistence, and stubbornness in his method.
这些正面特质来自年岁、坚忍与经验。我们在这里看到随年岁而来的智慧。土星代表深邃，是深而非浅；它在这个词的多重意义上都是深的。土星可靠时是真实，不可靠时则是虚假、狡计与欺骗。
These are positive traits from age, perseverance and experience. We have the wisdom that comes with age. This is Saturn as profundity, deep rather than shallow. Saturn is deep in multiple senses of the term. Saturn when reliable is truth, Saturn when unreliable is falsehood, craftiness and deception.
他不轻易动怒；一旦动怒，便不能自己拿定主意。
He does not easily get angry, and if he were angry he would not be able to make up his own mind.
校记“make up his own mind” 语义可疑
照现有英译，make up his own mind 通常只能解作「自己拿定主意」。但这与前半句「不轻易动怒」衔接突兀，也不像古典征象清单常见的固定搭配。此处可能是英译把「恢复心神／息怒」一类原义处理得过于现代，但未核阿拉伯原文，不宜径改。

译文因此按英语字面照译并出注志疑，不擅自替换成更顺的「一旦动怒便不易平息」。
他不愿任何人得好。
He wishes good to no one.
这是土星作为凶星malefic的一面：愿他人受害。
This is Saturn as a malefic, wishing harm on others.
它表征老人、沉稳之人、重担、恐惧、悲痛、哀愁与心思纠结；原书 p.25
And he signifies old men and weighty men and burdens and fear, griefs and sorrow and complication of the mind.
年老与哀愁是土星常见的主题。
Age and sorrow are common Saturn themes.
也表征欺诈、苦难、艰困与损失；
And fraud and affliction and difficulty and loss;
这里再次出现作为凶星的土星，也再次出现它与欺诈、说谎和隐藏的关联。
Again we have Saturn as malefic, and again the association with fraud, lying, hiding.
还表征祖先、亡者遗留之物、服丧、失怙与古旧之物；乃至祖父、父亲、兄弟、年长者、奴隶、骡夫、受斥责之人、盗贼、掘墓者以及盗取死者衣物之人。
also ancestors and what is left behind by the dead, mourning, and being orphaned, and old things. Even grandfathers and fathers and brothers, and senior people and slaves and mule drivers and men who are blamed, and robbers and those who dig up graves and who rob the garments of the dead.
这些征象或与年岁相关，或与男性亲属相关。我们还看到卑下职业、失去自由、与死亡相关的职业，以及掘土之事。
These are associations with age, or male relatives. We also see lowly trades, or loss of freedom, trades associated with death, and digging into the earth.
背景骡夫、掘墓者与盗尸衣者
这串人物并非随意罗列：奴隶对应失去自由、受人控制；骡夫从事负重与运输，工作粗重且地位低；掘墓者进入地下、接触死者；盗取尸衣者又把死亡、贫穷、隐秘与犯罪叠在一起。

古典征象清单常用具体职业把一颗行星的数层性质压在同一个人身上。若只把它们当作过时职业名录，就会错过背后的组合规则：卑下＋重负＋地下＋死亡＋被排斥，每一项都沿着土星的主轴。
它表征思虑绵长、少言、知晓秘密；旁人不知他心中所想，智者也不会把每一件隐秘之事都向他披露。它还表征清苦自持austerity与各宗教的苦修者ascetics。
And he signifies a great length of thought and a scarcity of speaking and the knowledge of secrets, and one does not know what is in his mind, nor does a wise person make disclosures to him about every obscure matter. And he signifies austerity and the ascetics of religions.
术语austerity / ascetics 分译
austerity 在这里不是经济政策中的「紧缩」，而是主动限制欲望、生活俭朴严峻的品格，译作「清苦自持」；ascetics 指把这种自我限制落实为宗教生活的人，译作「苦修者」。

两词不可合并：前者是生活方式与德性，后者是践行者。它们把土星的限制、孤独和严峻转为宗教修持，并不只是消极的贫困或受苦。
这里出现的是思想的深邃，它来自长时间持续不断的用功。我们还看到土星的隐藏、寡言、退隐、安静与守秘。土星式的宗教特质严峻、自我限制，并带有修道生活的性质。
Here the we have he association with profundity of thought, which comes from work over a sustained time. We also have Saturn as hidden, taciturn, withdrawn, quiet, secretive. Saturnian religious traits are severe, self-limiting, and monastic.
校记“Here the we have he association” 衍脱
原书 p. 25 作 Here the we have he association with profundity of thought，显系排校时混入两个残词：the 衍入，he 当为 the。依句法当作 Here we have the association…。中文径改为「这里出现的是思想的深邃」，不照录讹字。

比鲁尼Al-Biruni

比鲁尼Al-Biruni（约 973—1048）是阿拉伯学者、科学家兼作家。以下引文取自他的《占星术基础教程》Book of Instruction in the Elements of the Art of Astrology。该书按主题分成一系列章节，逐一论述各行星；我在这里把几个主题门类中有关土星的资料汇集到一起。方括号内是原书的章节标题。与其他传统文献一样，这里也有一些简短却丰富复杂的关联清单。它们不是现代占星学那种经过精简的关键词，而是具有开放隐喻延展力的性质，同时又扎根于实际存在之物的物理属性。原书 p.26
Al-Biruni (c.973-1048) was an Arab scholar, scientist and author. These quotes are taken from his Book of Instruction in the Elements of the Art of Astrology. This book covers the planets in a series of sections according to the subject areas. I am here drawing together the data on Saturn from several of the topic areas. The bracketed text gives the section titles. As with other traditional sources there are brief lists of associations that are very rich and complex. These aren't streamlined keywords as in modern astrology, they are qualities that have an open-ended metaphoric quality, and they are also rooted in qualities of physically existing things.
出处《占星术基础教程》书名说明
原题英译为 Book of Instruction in the Elements of the Art of Astrology。依工程所有者决定定为《占星术基础教程》：「教程」保留 Book of Instruction 的教学性质，「基础」概括 Elements 所指的基础要素。
提示清单不是现代关键词表
奥伯特在这里预先规定了读法：不要把每个名词当作彼此孤立、只能死记的关键词。例如「铅色、地窖、重载牲畜、奴隶、坟墓」表面毫不相干，底下却共享冷、暗、重、低、受限等物理性质。

所谓「开放的隐喻」，就是从这些可触摸的性质继续推衍：重可以是物体沉重，也可以是重担、沉稳或心情沉重；低可以是地下，也可以是社会地位卑下。传统征象因此能延伸，却不是任意联想。
〔行星的本性及其征象〕土星极冷、极干。大凶星greater malefic。雄性。昼间。难受而涩，酸气冲鼻，有臭气；乌黑，也有黑中杂黄、铅色、漆黑。
[NATURES OF THE PLANETS AND THEIR INDICATORS] Saturn is extremely cold and dry. The greater malefic. Male. Diurnal. Disagreeable and astringent, offensively acid, stinking, jet-black also black mixed with yellow, lead colour, pitch-dark.
土星：万物中最冷、最硬、最臭、最有力者。短、干、硬、重。荒瘠之山。
Saturn: Coldest, hardest, most stinking and most powerful of things. Shortness, dryness, hardness, heaviness. Barren mountains.
术语greater malefic 译「大凶星」
the greater malefic 是与火星 the lesser malefic 成对的传统称号，分别作大凶星／小凶星。这里的「大／小」不是道德邪恶程度，而是传统上所判的致害范围与强度；核心词仍遵守契约，malefic 一律译「凶星」，不作「恶星」。
这些关联主要来自冷干体液的性质，以及与黑暗、朽烂的联系。
These associations are mainly from the cold dry humors, and associations with darkness and rotting.
〔建筑与地域〕土星：地下水道与地窖、水井、古旧建筑、荒僻道路、野兽巢穴、野兽遍布的荒漠，以及马、驴、骆驼的厩舍与象舍。
[BUILDINGS AND COUNTRIES] Saturn: Underground canals and vaults, wells, old buildings, desolate roads, lairs of wild beasts, deserts full of them, stables for horses, asses, and camels, and elephant's houses.
土星与黑暗之地、隔绝之地、地位卑下的动物，或背负沉重载荷的牲畜相配。
Saturn goes with dark places, isolated places, lowly animals or animals that are beasts carrying heavy burdens.
〔亲属与关系、体态与面相〕土星：父亲、祖父、兄长与奴隶。丑陋、高大、干瘪，面色阴沉；头大，连眉，眼小，嘴阔，唇厚；目光低垂，黑发浓密；颈短，手粗，指短；身形笨拙，腿弯，脚大。
[RELATIONS AND CONNECTIONS, FIGURE, AND FACE] Saturn: Fathers, grandfathers, older brothers and slaves. Ugly, tall, wizened, sour face, large head, eyebrows joined, small eyes, wide mouth, thick lips, downcast look, much black hair, short neck, coarse hand, short fingers, awkward figure, legs crooked, big feet.
〔性情与举止〕土星：恐惧、胆怯、焦虑、多疑、吝啬，心怀恶意的谋算者；阴郁而骄傲，忧愁，说真话，庄重，可靠，不肯相信任何人的善意；专注于自己的事务，因而主不和；或愚昧，或聪慧，但其愚昧藏而不露。原书 p.27
[DISPOSITION AND MANNERS] Saturn: Fearful, timid, anxious, suspicious, miserly, a malevolent plotter, sullen and proud, melancholy, truth-telling, grave, trusty, unwilling to believe good of anyone, engrossed in his own affairs and consequently indicates discord, and either ignorance or intelligence, but the ignorance is concealed.
这里的土星孤立离群。我们又看到它与隐藏、间接、身在黑暗、不公开坦诚之物相关，因此也就多疑、自私。
Saturn here is isolated, and again we see the association with things hidden, indirect, in the darkness, not open and honest, hence also suspicious, selfish.
〔活动、禀性与品行〕土星：流亡与贫穷，或以自己和他人的诡计取得财富；生意失败，猛烈，混乱，寻求独处，以暴力或背叛奴役他人，欺诈，哭泣、号啕与哀悼。
[ACTIVITIES, INSTINCTS AND MORALS] Saturn: Exile and poverty, or wealth acquired by his own trickery and that of others, failure in business, vehemence, confusion, seeking solitariness, enslaving people by violence or treachery, fraud, weeping and wailing and lamentation.
这里列出的土星获取财富与控制的方式都是负面的：欺骗、暴力、背叛。
The ways listed here that Saturn gains wealth and control are negative - deception, violence, treachery.
〔宗教、行星图像〕土星：犹太人及身着黑衣者。老人坐于狼背，右手提一颗人头，左手持一只人手；另一幅图像则是他骑一匹鲜亮的枣红马，头戴盔，左手执盾，右手持剑。
[RELIGIONS, PICTURES OF PLANETS] Saturn: Jews and those who dress in black. Old man seated on a wolf, in his right hand the head of a man and in the left a man's hand; or according to another picture, mounted on a bright bay horse, on his head a helmet, in the left hand a shield and in the right a sword.
行星曾与不同宗教相配；土星配犹太人，因为他们是《妥拉》之民、律法之民，正如土星通常与法则相关。这也与犹太教是非常古老的宗教有关：它是基督教的先驱或祖先，而基督教与下一层的木星相配。土星是法则之星，正如木星是仁慈之星。
Planets were associated with religions, and Saturn was associated with the Jews as the people of the Torah, the Law, just as Saturn is associated with law in general. This also relates to Judaism as being a very old religion, a fore-runner or ancestor of Christianity which was associated with Jupiter, the next planet down. Saturn is the planet of Law as Jupiter is the planet of Mercy.
背景宗教归属与两幅土星图像
这里记录的是前现代占星文献的对应体系，不是对当代宗教群体的性格判断。犹太教与土星相配，抓住的是古老、律法与黑衣等传统关联；奥伯特再用古典宇宙的行星次序，把下一层木星与基督教的仁慈相连。

老人骑狼、手持人头与断手，或武装骑马的形象，则属于行星图像传统：把冷酷、死亡、战争、权威等征象压缩成可辨认的图像，常用于图像学或护符语境。奥伯特关于犹太教是基督教「祖先」的说法较为简化，译文照录其论述方向，不把它改写成现代宗教史判断。
〔行业与职业〕土星：营造、发饷官、农耕、垦地与分水、分配钱财与遗产、掘墓；售卖铁、铅、骨、毛发、铜所制之物，贩卖黑奴；将知识用于恶行，以及导致邪恶压迫、震怒、监禁、酷刑的政务。原书 p.28
[TRADES, PROFESSIONS] Saturn: Building, paymaster, farming, reclaiming land and distribution of water, apportioning money and heritages, grave-digging; selling things made of iron, lead, bone, hair, copper, black slaves; knowledge used for bad purposes, such acts of government as lead to evil oppression, wrath, captivity, torture.
背景这份职业清单的组合规则
「营造、垦地、分水、分钱、分遗产」共享划界与分配；铁、铅、骨、毛发、铜是坚硬、沉重、来自身体或色泽幽暗的材料；掘墓把土地与死亡接在一起；发饷官则掌管有限财物的计量和发放。

原文还直列奴隶买卖、压迫、监禁与酷刑，反映这套古代征象把社会中卑下、受控制或施行严酷控制的角色都归给土星。译文保留其历史措辞，并不把这种制度正常化。
这里有与土地相关的征象，也再次出现土地与水合在一起的征象，还有死亡与卑下之人。我们又看到腐败动机的关联：土星是腐败、压迫或邪恶的统治者，带来痛苦与恶意。
Here are associations with land, or again land and water together, with death, and with lowly people. Again we see the connection with corrupt motives; Saturn is a corrupt or oppressive or evil ruler who causes pain and malice.

亚伯拉罕·伊本·以斯拉《智慧之始》Avraham Ibn-Ezra, The Beginning of Wisdom

亚伯拉罕·伊本·以斯拉·本·梅厄Avraham Ibn-Ezra ben Meir（约 1089—1164）生于中世纪的西班牙，晚年迁往以色列地。伊本·以斯拉是犹太人，著作用希伯来文写成。其占星学著作与阿拉伯占星师大体属于同一传统，而这些著作的根源都可上溯至最初的希腊传统。原书 p.29
Avraham Ibn-Ezra ben Meir (c 1089-1164) was born in Spain in the middle ages and moved to Israel in the latter part of his life. Ibn-Ezra was Jewish and his writings are in Hebrew. The astrology writings of Ibn-Ezra are in the same general tradition as the Arabic astrologers, which all have their roots in the original Greek tradition.
校记「晚年迁往以色列地」存疑
原书明确写作 moved to Israel in the latter part of his life。此处把 Israel 译作地理意义上的「以色列地」，不读成二十世纪成立的现代国家。

但常见现代传记所列的晚年行迹是离开西班牙后辗转意大利、法国与英格兰，死亡地点亦未定，并无一致证据支持他「迁往以色列地」。这是事实层面的可商榷之处，故照译并志疑，不代作者修正。
土星冷而干，其本性为恶而有害。它表征毁灭、败坏、死亡、悲痛、服丧、哭泣、号啕，以及古旧之物。
Saturn is cold and dry and his nature is evil and harmful. He indicates destruction, and ruin, and death, and grief, and mourning and weeping and crying, and ancient things.
术语此处 evil 照译「恶」
原文这里确用 evil and harmful，不是契约中的技术词 malefic。因此分别译作「恶而有害」，不为了维持「凶星」译名而把 evil 偷换成「凶」。

这一区分正为第三部的论证保留材料：malefic 是占星学上「致害」的分类，作「凶」；evil 涉及道德或本性判断，作「恶」。原典作者有时会把两者连在一起，奥伯特后文则会专门拆开辨析。
在人之精神中，它所分得的是思想之力。
His share of the human spirit is the power of thought.
请注意这个反复出现的主题：土星是思想，是深度，是智慧，是深邃。我认为这与瓦伦斯所说的土星—水星关系有关，两颗行星都与思想相关。这也关乎土星与法则的联系。
Note the recurring theme of Saturn as thought, as depth, as wisdom, as profundity. I think this is related to the relation of Saturn and Mercury that Valens talked about, with both having connections with thought. This also relates to the connection of Saturn and the Law.
……总括而言，所有老人、农人、营造者、鞣皮匠、洁厕者、奴隶、卑下之人、盗贼、掘沟者、掘墓者与殡葬者。在地上诸处所中，分属它的有洞穴、水井、坑窟、监狱、一切黑暗无人之地，以及墓园。
...in general all old people, and farmers, and builders, and tanners, and those who clean lavatories, slaves, the inferior, robbers, ditch and grave diggers, and undertakers. In his share of places of the earth are the caves and wells, and pits, and prisons, and every dark and uninhabited place, and the cemeteries.
背景鞣皮匠、洁厕者与殡葬者
这几种职业都在前现代社会里承担必要却遭嫌弃的工作：鞣皮要处理腐败、发臭的动物皮；洁厕者接触污秽；掘墓者与殡葬者接触尸体和地下。它们与奴隶、盗贼、卑下之人并列，显示土星同时主管污秽、死亡、地下、社会排斥与低下地位。

紧接着的处所清单把同一组性质空间化：洞穴、井、坑、牢狱和墓园全都低、暗、封闭，或与死亡相连。
土星与老年、耕作者、一般的卑下职业以及与死亡相关的职业相连。我们还看到与死亡、禁闭、地下、隐蔽或黑暗相关的处所。
Saturn is connected with old age, with people who work with earth, lowly trades in general, and trades connected to death. We also see places connected to death, or confinement, or underground, or hidden, or dark.
其本性冷而干；其味为涩，以及一切味道与气味令人不快之物。
His nature is cold and dry, and his flavor is the astringent and everything that has disagreeable taste and smell.
这里的土星冷而干，而非冷而湿。后期文献似乎较少强调土星冷湿的性质，可能是因为四体液或四气质体系逐渐系统化；不合体液体系的关联，往往不再受到强调，或被舍弃。
This is Saturn as cold and dry rather than cold and wet. The cold and wet qualities of Saturn seem to be emphasized less in later texts, probably because of the systematizing of the four humors or temperaments. Associations that did not fit in with the humors tend to be emphasized less or to be dropped.
在人性中，分属它的有沉思contemplation、少言、敏锐、离群；控制他人、取胜与征税；动怒、守信、深思、知晓秘密与敬拜上帝；卑下之人、抵牾、恐惧、忧虑、贪求；总括而言，是惯于说谎，少有益处，多有败坏。原书 p.30
In his share of the human nature is the contemplation, little talk, astuteness, isolation from people, controlling them and winning and levying taxes, and getting angry, keeping one's word, deep thought, knowledge of secrets and the worship of God, inferior people, contrariness, fear, worry, coveting, and in general habitually lying with little usefulness and much ruin.
我们又看到彼此矛盾的归属含义混在一起：深邃、安静、隐秘知识或敬拜，同时又有欺骗、说谎、掩藏真相。从最早期的文献开始，这两组主题便都与土星相关。
Again we see the mix of contradictory attributions - profundity, quietness, hidden knowledge or worship, but also deceit, lying, hiding the truth. Both themes are associated with Saturn from the earliest texts.
提示两组矛盾征象共享一个根
深邃与欺瞒、少言与隐瞒、秘传知识与掩藏真相，看起来一正一负，底层动作却相同：不把内里的东西直接显露在外。经过长久内省，它可以成为沉思、守秘与深知；出于恶意，它便成为欺骗、说谎和遮蔽真相。

传统清单之所以保留矛盾项，是因为行星先表示一种作用方式，再依处境和品格显为好坏；它不是把某颗星简化成一组单向人格标签。
土星表征耕地、营造、采炼金属、寻觅秘藏、发掘并检视死者之物，以及一切延续多年的事物。
Saturn indicates tilling the land, building, mining metals, seeking hidden treasures, digging and examining things of the dead, and everything that lasts for many years.
这是土星与土地、长久延续和死者的关联。
This is Saturn associated with earth, with long duration, and the dead.
它表征父亲与祖父、亡者、哭泣与离别、流离、贫穷、受辱、危机潜伏的遥远恶路，以及诸事不成。
He indicates fathers and grandfathers, the deceased, crying and separation, wandering, poverty, humiliation, distant bad roads where danger lurks and he has no success in all undertaking.
土星是一颗古老而运行迟缓的行星，因此与父亲、祖父相关。其余归属含义则关乎厄运：它使人落入卑下境况。
Saturn as an old and slow moving planet is associated with fathers and grandfathers. The other attributions are related to bad fortune that puts one in lowly conditions.

圭多·博纳蒂《天文书》Bonatti, Book of Astronomy

圭多·博纳蒂Guido Bonatti（1210—1296）是中世纪意大利占星师。他以拉丁文写成的鸿篇巨制原名《星辰判断导论》The Book of Introduction to the Judgments of the Stars，通称《天文书》The Book of Astronomy。这部巨型汇编收集了阿拉伯传统中各位大占星师的材料；把这一传统的大部分内容传给后来欧洲乃至西方世界的占星师，它很可能是影响最大的一部文集。原书 p.31
Guido Bonatti (1210-1296) was a medieval Italian astrologer. His magnum opus written in Latin is The Book of Introduction to the Judgments of the Stars, better known as The Book of Astronomy. It is a massive compendium of material from the great astrologers of the Arabic tradition, and is likely the single most influential collection transmitting much of that tradition to later astrologers in Europe and the Western world.
出处《天文书》的全题与性质
本书契约已锁定通称 Book of Astronomy 为《天文书》。奥伯特在这里同时给出较长的全题 The Book of Introduction to the Judgments of the Stars，译作《星辰判断导论》。

它不是博纳蒂个人观点的短论，而是汇集阿拉伯占星传统的大型拉丁文总集；因此本章清单中许多说法会与阿布·马沙尔、比鲁尼高度重合。重复本身正是奥伯特要展示的传统连续性。
土星是雄性、昼间行星，作用于失衡的酷寒与燥烈。它表征父亲、祖父与一切祖先。
Saturn is a masculine, diurnal planet, and he works at intemperate coldness and dryness. And he is a significator of fathers and grandfathers and all ancestors.
在体液体系中，土星所代表的是失衡或过度的冷与干。冷湿征象大多已被舍弃。
In the system of humors Saturn is intemperate or excessive cold and dry. Cold and wet significations have mostly been dropped.
术语intemperate 译「失衡」
intemperate 不是一般所谓脾气「放纵」，而是四体液理论中的比例失调：某种性质过盛，超出均衡状态。所以正文先译「失衡的酷寒与燥烈」，奥伯特按语再用「失衡或过度」释明。

这也解释了为什么寒与干在这里不是中性的分类，而会导向收缩、僵硬、衰老和忧郁等问题。
……因为它天然表征命主其人或其身体；一个人首先发生之事，便是获有肉身，存在由此赋予他。
... for he naturally signifies the person or body of the native, on account of the fact that the first thing which happens to a man is the physical person through which being is given to him.
土星与身体相关，因为身体是一道边界、一重限制、一个容器或一种结构；它也与身体的物质性、属地性相关。
Saturn relates to the body as a boundary or limit or container or structure. It also relates to body as being physical, of the earth.
土星居于行星的第一重天圈，在其序列中居首，其他行星无不随后。精种落入母腹之后，它甚至是首先对所孕胎儿施加作用的行星：它把形成胎儿的物质束缚、聚合在一起。
And Saturn is in the first circle of the planets, and is the first planet in their order, and whom all others follow, and is even the first one who exercises his operation in a conceived child after the falling of the seed into the womb, by binding and uniting together the matter from which the conceived child is formed.
这里出现了土星与第一宫、第八宫——出生与死亡之宫——的关联。土星立在物质变易世界的边线上，因而与物质性相关，并为时间与物质中的事物赋予形体。妊娠九个月按{T("迦勒底序列","Chaldean order")}依次分给七颗行星：第一月从土星开始，第二月为木星，依次轮转，到第八月又回到土星。宫位也按同一序列与行星相配：第一宫为土星，第二宫为木星，一直到第七宫为月亮；第八宫再从土星重复，如此类推。土星是最外围的行星，所以我们下降入世时，首先经过它；死亡离世时，最后经过的也是它。它因此与开端、终结和边界相连。
There is an association here of Saturn with the first and eighth houses, the houses of birth and death. We see Saturn associated with the physical, at the borderline of the physical mutable world, hence the giver of form to things of matter and time. The nine months of gestation were given to the seven Chaldean planets in order, starting with Saturn in month one, Jupiter in month two and so on, then around to Saturn again month eight. The houses were associated with the planets in the same order, with house one Saturn, house two Jupiter, and on to house seven with the Moon, then the order repeats with house eight Saturn and so on. As the outermost planet Saturn is the first planet we pass as we descend and enter the world, and the last we pass as we exit the world at death. It is connected with beginnings and endings, and with borders.
背景迦勒底序列，以及“进入／离开时间”的出处
迦勒底序列按传统地心宇宙中行星由外向内的次序排列：土、木、火、日、金、水、月。这里把妊娠月份和十二宫都从土星起按此序循环，于是第一宫与第八宫同归土星，分别接上出生与死亡。

本书 p. 44 曾说「如博纳蒂所指出的，土星标记我们进入时间，也标记我们离开时间」。现在可以精确定位其根据：博纳蒂原话只说土星居行星序列之首，并首先作用于胎儿；下降入世时首先经过土星、死亡离世时最后经过土星，是奥伯特结合迦勒底序列与第一／第八宫所作的推演。因此 p. 44 的「如博纳蒂所指出」是概括性归因，不是逐字引文。
土星表征父亲、古旧之物，也表征负重之物；这是鉴于它运行迟缓而艰涩，且性质沉重。因此，先贤令它表征年长的双亲、古代之物与负重之物。原书 p.32
Saturn is a significator of fathers, and of old things, and the burdensome things they have in light of his slow and burdened motion, and his heaviness; and therefore they made him the significator of older parents and of ancient things and burdensome things.
土星表征失衡的寒与干之一切严酷性质。在诸体液中，它表征忧郁质……也使命主肢体迟缓而沉重。
And Saturn is everything which signifies the severity of intemperate cold and dryness. And of the humors hie signifies melancholy... and with slowness and heaviness of limbs of the native's body.
校记hie 当作 he
原书 p. 32 作 of the humors hie signifies melancholy，hie 在此不成句，显系 he 多衍一个 i。中文径按「它表征忧郁质」译出，并在此志明，不照录讹字。
寒与干合在一起，往往使事物收缩；这与严酷相关，也与衰老、沉重和抑郁带来的问题相关。
Coldness and dryness together tend to shrink things, and this is related to severity, and to problems from age, heaviness, and depression.
若土星状态良好，便表征学识深邃、谋划良善而深远，旁人几乎或绝无可能知道怎样加以改进。
And if he were of good condition, it signifies profoundness of knowledge, and good and deep counsel, such that another will hardly or never know how to improve on it.
我们再次看到土星与渊博的知识、深度、智慧和良策相连。
Again we see Saturn connected to profound knowledge, depth, wisdom, good counsel.
在职业方面，它表征古老之事与劳苦繁重之事，也表征水上作业或水边作业（如磨坊、桥梁、船只之类）、引水、耕地（如田作、植树、营造房屋，尤其是营造黑衣修道者的居所）——若它得吉助且状态良好。
And of professions he signifies ancient things and those full of labor, and aquatic works or those which take place near water (like mills, bridges, ships and the like), and the bringing forth of waters, and the cultivation of the earth (like fields, planting trees, the building of houses - and especially of the houses of religious men wearing black clothing - if he were made fortunate and of good condition.
校记职业引文第二层括号未闭合
原书 p. 32 在 the cultivation of the earth 后开启括号，列举田作、植树和营造房屋，到 religious men wearing black clothing 后却未见右括号，直接以破折号接条件从句。从句法看，右括号显然应落在 clothing 之后。中文不补造原文符号，改用破折号把「若它得吉助且状态良好」与前面的职业清单分开，并在此志明。
这里有土地、邻水之地以及土地劳作的关联。宗教与黑色也在其中，因而有修士；他们同样严峻而出离尘俗。原书 p.33
Here we have the connection of land, and land near water, and also work with the land. There are connections with religion and the color black, hence monks, who would also be severe and renunciate.
背景黑衣修士为何属于土星
中世纪修道服常用未染或深色粗布；在征象层面，黑衣直接接上土星之黑。修道生活又强调清贫、独身、服从、克己与长期规律，进一步接上土星的限制、严峻和持久。

这里的 renunciate 不是单纯「消极退缩」，而是主动舍弃世俗财产与欲望，故译作「出离尘俗」。与上一章的「清苦自持／苦修者」属于同一概念族，但原词不同，不强行归并。
但若土星受凶扰且状态不良，便表征古旧而卑下之事，如挥锄劳作、挖掘污坑、在恶臭之地工作；用颈肩背负石块与灰浆送到墙边，尤其是地下墙体、城墙或坑边墙体；并制作许多砖造之物等等。这类人往往终身劳苦、困顿、贫穷，所食也是恶劣腐臭之物。
If however he were made unfortunate and of bad condition, he signifies old and low class things, like working with hoes, the digging of base pits, and in stinking places, and the carrying of stones and cement to walls by the neck, and especially to underground walls, or those of cities, and which are next to pits, and the making of many things which come to be of brick, and the like. And often such people live in labor and distress and poverty, and they eat bad and stinking foods.
这是多重意义上的「低」土星：黑暗、贫穷、状态不良，物理位置低下而又不卫生的处所，低收入或贫困，还有低劣食物——正在腐坏、走向死亡的食物。
This is Saturn as low in several senses - dark, poor, bad condition, physically low and unhealthy places, and low income or poverty, also low quality foods, foods that are rotting and dying.
提示同一个 low 贯穿空间、阶层与品质
奥伯特特意反复使用 low：地下墙体和坑是位置低，劳工和收入是社会经济地位低，腐臭食物是品质低。三个看似不同的清单门类，被同一个方向词串了起来。

这正是传统征象的隐喻扩展法：先有可见的空间方向，再映射到阶层与价值判断。译文保留「低／卑下／低劣」这一同根词群，使这条推导线在中文里仍看得见。
若土星状态良好，便表征杰出而富有的水手；命主会受人真心敬重，宽裕且耐心、能够忍耐。
And if he were in good condition, he signifies great and wealthy sailors, and he will be of true esteem, and ample and patient or enduring.
这里仍可看到土星与水的关联；但有时也有经水上事业或不动产而来的财富。
This is a place where we still see the association with water, but also sometimes wealth that comes from ventures over water or from real estate.
若土星状态不良，命主便默默无闻、悲伤、哀痛、心怀恶疑，动辄疑人作恶，并以耳语和恶意煽动挑起人心。
And if he were of bad condition, the native will be undistinguished, sad, grieving, of bad suspicion, eager to suspect every evil, and in rousing men by whispers and evil incitements.
这一组关联很复杂：既有卑下、品质低劣之物，也有隐秘、多疑、犬儒的性质——一切都藏着，于是出现耳语、诽谤，以及避开光明说出的话。这是土星作为凶星malefic的一面，以煽动恶行为乐。土星既可以像腐烂之物一样腐坏，也可以是动机不纯、道德败坏意义上的腐败。
This group of associations is complex - things low and poor quality, but also a secretive, suspicious and cynical quality - hidden, so whispers and slanders, words spoken out of the view of the light. This is Saturn as malefic, rejoicing in inciting evil. Saturn is corrupt as in rotting but also corrupt as in impure or corrupt motives.
提示corrupt 从腐坏转到腐败
英文 corrupt 同时覆盖物质朽坏与道德腐败，奥伯特正利用这个一词双义：食物在黑暗潮湿处腐坏，人的动机也可能在隐蔽处腐败。

中文不能用一个词自然包办两义，所以正文分译，再用注释把桥补回来。土星之「恶」在此不是凭空贴上的道德标签，而是从它原有的腐朽、隐藏征象继续隐喻延伸。
若土星状态良好，便表征古旧而耐久之物，例如来自任何来源的遗产（尤其是来自亡者的遗产），以及命主以合法手段而非邪恶手段取得的不动产。原书 p.34
And if he were of good condition, he signifies old and durable things, as are inheritances which come from any source (and especially from the dead), and estates which are acquired by him by lawful means more so than by wicked means.
土星与遗产、因死亡而来的钱财相关，也与祖先、老者、亡者、先行之人及过去相关。土星还在「耐久」这层意义上与年岁相连：它表征延续长久之物。
Saturn is connected to inheritances, money from death. There is the association with ancestors, the aged and dead, those who have gone before, the past. Saturn also relates to age in the sense of durable, things that last for a long time.

威廉·利利《基督教占星学》William Lilly: Christian Astrology

威廉·利利William Lilly（1602—1681）是最著名的英国占星师；他的经典教本《基督教占星学》堪称英语世界最伟大、最有影响力的占星学著作。本节文字取自第一卷占星学基础部分。此书影响极大，所以我会大量引用并加以评论。这里使用的各节标题，见于原版印刷本的页边。原书 p.35
William Lilly (1602-1681) is the most famous of all English astrologers, and his classic textbook, Christian Astrology, is arguably the greatest and most influential astrology text in the English language. The text of this section is from book One covering the basic elements of astrology. Since this book is so very influential I will quote and comment on it extensively. The section headings I use here show up in the margins of the original printed edition.
名称——通常称它为土星，但有些作者称它克洛诺斯、法厄农、法尔基费尔。
Names - He is called usually Saturn, but in some Authors Chronos, Phoenon, Falcifer.
克洛诺斯或 Kronos 是与土星相连的希腊名称；Saturn 则是罗马名称。克洛诺斯是瑞亚之夫，Chronos 是与时间相关之神。两者原是不同的神与概念，后来逐渐合而为一，如今都与土星相连。法厄农是土星的另一个希腊名称，我没有找到别的含义。我所能找到的法尔基费尔资料，只说它意为「黑暗之主」，是堕落天使或恶魔之名。利利列出这些传统名称，却没有援引或展开神话；其解释也没有一项系于这些名称。重度依赖神话来解释，是现代的发展。
Chronus or Kronos are Greek names associated with Saturn. The name Saturn is Roman rather than Greek. Chronus or Kronus is Saturn, husband of Rhea, and Chronos is a god associated with time. They are originally two different gods and concepts, and the two have been combined into one over time, and both are now associated with Saturn. Phoenon is another Greek name for Saturn, and I was unable to find any other meaning. The only reference I could find to Falcifer is a name meaning ‘Lord of Darkness’, a fallen angel or demon. Lilly lists these traditional names of Saturn, but does not refer to or develop the mythology. None of the meaning or interpretation is tied to these names. The heavy emphasis on mythological interpretation is a modern development.
校记Phaenon 与 Falcifer 的词义
奥伯特所写 Phoenon 通常作 Phaenon / Phainon，希腊语意为「发光者」，是土星的古称。Falcifer 则由拉丁语 falx「镰刀」与 -fer「持有者」构成，意为「持镰者」，正是持镰的农神萨图尔努斯之称。奥伯特把它解释成「黑暗之主、堕天使或恶魔」，应是误把另一路资料当成词源。

正文仍照译奥伯特的叙述；此注只校正名物事实，不代他重写论证。
颜色——它是所有行星中最为高上的，位于木星与恒星天之间；它不甚明亮辉煌，也不闪烁发光，而呈苍白、惨淡、铅灰或灰烬之色，运行迟缓。
Color - He is the supremest or highest of all Planets; is placed betwixt Jupiter and the Firmament, he is not very bright or glorious, nor doth he twinkle or sparkle, but is of a Pale, Wan or Leaden, Ashy colour slow in motion.
苍白、铅灰、灰烬色、灰色、迟缓，都是典型的土星性质。「最高」指土星是游动行星中距太阳最远者。恒星天Firmament是不变的恒星球，被认为固定、永恒不易。请注意，土星正处在动与不动、时间与永恒、变化与不变的边界。
Pale, leaden, ashy, grey, slow, are all typical Saturn qualities. By supremest he means furthest from the Sun of the moving planets. The Firmament is the unchanging sphere of the fixed stars, which was considered to be fixed, immutable, unchanging. Note that Saturn is the planet at the border of moving and unmoving, temporal and eternal, changing and unchanging.
背景恒星天与「最高的行星」
传统地心宇宙由一重重天球套叠而成。七行星天之外是恒星天；恒星彼此位置不变，故象征不变与永恒。土星不是绝对意义上的最高天，而是会移动的七行星中最外、最高的一层，恰守在变动世界与不动天界的交界。
还记得土星与「黑暗之主」的关联。土星位于永恒与时间、天与地之间的外环，是这个堕落、黑暗、变易世界的守门者。原书 p.36
Recall the association of Saturn with Lord of Darkness. Saturn is at the outer ring between eternal and temporal, heaven and earth, the guardian of this fallen, dark, mutable world.
运行——它走完黄道十二星座约需 29 年 157 日；平均运动为二分一秒；每日有时行三、四、五或六分，极少更多。
Motion - Finishing his Course through the twelve signs of the Zodiac in 29 years, 157 days, or thereabouts; his middle motion is two minutes and one second; his diurnal motion sometimes is three, four, five, or six minutes, or seldom more.
土星是传统行星中运行最慢的一颗。迟缓与漫长时段都与土星相关。
Saturn is the slowest moving of the traditional planets. Slowness, and long periods of time, are both associated with Saturn.
纬度——它在黄道以北的最大纬度为 2°48′，在黄道以南为 2°49′，不会超过此数。
Latitude - His greatest North Latitude from the Ecliptic is two degrees 48 minutes; his South Latitude from the Ecliptic is two degrees 49 minutes; and more than this he hath not.
本宫——在黄道十二星座中，它有两座为本宫：摩羯座是夜间本宫，水瓶座是昼间本宫；它擢升exaltation于天秤座，落陷fall于白羊座，并喜乐于水瓶座。
