Houses - In the Zodiac he hath two of the twelve Signs for his Houses, viz. Capricorn his Night house, Aquarius his Day house; he has his Exaltation in Libra, he receives his Fall in Aries; he rejoiceth in the sign Aquarius.
土星主管的摩羯、水瓶二座，离日月二曜主管的星座最远。二曜主管夏季星座，土星则主管隆冬的两座——极寒极暗之时。
The two signs ruled by Saturn, Capricorn and Aquarius, are the two that are furthest from the two Lights, Sun and Moon. As the Lights rule summer signs, Saturn rules the two signs of the dead of winter, the time of extreme cold and darkness.
三方组——昼间它主管风象三方组triplicity：双子座、天秤座、水瓶座。
Triplicity - He governeth the Airy Triplicity by day, which is composed of these Signs; Gemini, Libra, Aquarius.
它持续逆行retrograde 140 日；逆行前在第一次留station停五日，恢复顺行前在第二次留也停五日。
He continueth Retrograde 140 dayes. He is five days in his first station before Retrogradation, and so many in his second station before Direction.
术语Direction 在此是「恢复顺行」
利利此处的 Direction 与推运术 primary direction 无关，只是与逆行相对的顺行运动。故译「恢复顺行」，不作契约里的「定向推运」。station 定为「留」：行星视运动转向前看似停驻。
本性——它是昼间行星，冷而干（因远离太阳之热）又有湿气，忧郁质，属地，雄性，大凶星greater Infortune，孤独之主，心怀恶意，等等。
Nature - He is a Diurnal Planet, Cold and Dry (being far removed from the heat of the Sun) and moist Vapours, Melancholic, Earthly, Masculine, the greater Infortune, author of Solitariness, Malevolent, &c.
土星既被描述为冷干，也被描述为冷湿；共同点是使万物枯萎的极寒。孤立的寒冷处所确实容易潮湿：空气容纳不了多少水汽，水汽便凝结出来。那里黑暗、令人不适、孤独、隔绝、停滞、有害健康。想想隆冬深夜之寒，或黑暗而没有暖气的地下室。原书 p.37
Saturn is described both as cold and dry, and as cold and moist. The common thread is extreme cold, that makes things wither. Isolated cold places do tend to get damp, as the air can’t hold very much moisture so it condenses out. The places are dark, unpleasant, solitary, isolated, stagnant, unhealthy. Think of the cold of the dead of winter at night, or the cold of a dark and unheated basement.
讨论土星为何是昼间行星时，值得注意：其他昼间行星——太阳、木星、有时还有水星——都是温暖的。土星极冷、暗而干，被归入昼派，是为了调和这些性质。另一凶星火星热而干，则归入凉爽的夜派，以调和极热。
When we consider Saturn as a diurnal or day planet, it is worth noting that the other day planets, Sun, Jupiter and sometimes Mercury, are warm. Saturn is extreme cold, dark and dry, so it is grouped with the day planets in order to moderate those properties. The other malefic, hot and dry Mars, is grouped with the cool night planets to moderate that extreme heat.
提示宗派像一套温度调节系统
昼派不是把相似行星堆在一起，反而把极冷的土星放进温暖一组；夜派则把极热的火星放进凉爽一组。阵营的作用是调和极端：土星在昼盘得派，寒性受日间温暖节制；火星在夜盘得派，热性受夜凉节制。
举止与行动：尊贵良好well dignified时——想象深邃，行事严厉，言语谨慎，少言少施；劳作耐心，论辩庄重；勤勉操心以取得今生财物；一切行动皆严峻自持。
Manners & Actions, when well dignified - Then he is profound in Imagination, in his Acts severe, in words reserved, in speaking and giving very spare, in labour patient, in arguing or disputing grave, in obtaining the goods of this life studious and solicitous, in all manner of actions austere.
这些是年岁与经验之德，也是随之而来的智慧；与耐心、迟缓和深度相关。
These are the virtues of age and experience, and the wisdom that comes with that. They are related to patience, slowness and depth.
尊贵不良ill dignified时——妒忌、贪求、猜忌而不信任；胆怯、卑污，外表装假；迟钝、多疑、顽固，蔑视女人，暗中说谎，恶毒，抱怨，从不满足，永远怨尤。
When ill dignified - Then he is envious, covetous, jealous and mistrustful, timorous, sordid, outwardly dissembling, sluggish, suspicious, stubborn, a contemner of women, a close liar, malicious, murmuring, never contented, ever repining.
这类恶习来自内里干瘪枯缩：退缩、防御、隐蔽、鬼祟、不诚实、偷偷摸摸，是藏在黑暗里的恶习。想想人们躲在黑色面罩与群体匿名性之后威胁、攻击他人。
These are the sort of vices that come from becoming shriveled and dried up inside. They are shrinking, defensive, covert, furtive, dishonest, sneaky. These are the vices of hiding in darkness. Think of people hiding behind the anonymity of black masks and in a group, threatening and attacking.
术语well / ill dignified
dignified 指行星是否获得本宫、擢升、三方等尊贵条件，不是人的举止是否「有尊严」。本书依契约译为「尊贵」；标题分别作「尊贵良好时／尊贵不良时」，以保留程度差别。
体貌——其身体多冷而干，中等身材；肤色苍白、黝黑或泥土色；
Corporature - Most part his Body more cold and dry, of a middle stature; his complexion pale, swarthish or muddy,
眼小而黑、目光下垂，额宽；头发黑或色暗，质硬粗糙；耳大；眉毛下垂低压，唇鼻厚，胡须稀薄；面容笨重不悦，头向前伸或躬身；肩宽大且常弯曲；腹部略短瘦，腿细瘦而不长；膝足不雅，走路常拖曳或相互碰撞，等等。原书 p.38
his Eyes little and black, looking downward, a broad Forehead, black or sad Hair, and it hard or rugged, great Ears; hanging, lowring Eyebrows, thick Lips and Nose, a rare or thin Beard, a lumpish, unpleasant Countenance, either holding his Head forward or stooping, his Shoulders broad and large, and many times crooked, his Belly somewhat short and lank, his Thighs spare; lean and not long; his Knees and Feet indecent, many times shoveling or hitting one against another, &c.
这是土星与比例失调、粗陋、畸形、丑陋的关联。
This is Saturn associated with being ill-proportioned, crude, misshapen, ugly.
土星晨出Oriental——须注意，若土星晨出于太阳之前，身材较矮，却匀称端正。
Saturn Oriental - You must observe, if Saturn be Oriental of the Sun, the stature is more short, but decent and well composed.
昏出Occidental——其人肤色更黑、身体更瘦、毛发更少。若土星没有纬度，身体较瘦；纬度大则较胖多肉；纬度在南则较多肉但行动敏捷；纬度在北则多毛多肉。
Occidental - The man is more black and lean, and fewer Hairs; and again, if he want latitude, the body is more lean, if he have great latitude, the body is more fat or fleshy; if the latitude be Meridional or South, more fleshy; if the latitude be Meridional or South, more fleshy, but quick in motion. If the latitude be North, hairy and much flesh.
校记南纬条件重复一遍
原书连续两次写 if the latitude be Meridional or South, more fleshy，第二次才续以「但行动敏捷」。两句条件完全相同，显为衍复；中文合并为一次，不重复照录。
土星在第一次留时略胖；第二次留时肥胖、身体难看而虚弱。对其他行星也应一贯如此观察。
Saturn in his first station, a little fat. In his second station, fat, ill favoured Bodies, and weak; and this observe constantly in all the other Planets.
所主之人——总括而言，它表征农夫、乡野粗人、乞丐、日工、老人、父亲、祖父、修士、耶稣会士、严苛教派信徒。
Quality of Men - In generall he signifieth Husbandmen, Clowns, Beggars, Day labourers, Old-men, Fathers, Grandfathers, Monks, Jesuits, Sectarists.
行星先于太阳升起称为晨出，后于太阳升起称为昏出。这里的 clown 指笨拙、粗鄙、无教养的下层乡野人；清单包含不受尊重的下层群体，也有年岁与严肃的关联。宗教关联则严峻、隔绝、悔罪，并与黑色相连。Sectarist 是严厉而偏执、游离主流、以异样眼光看人的教派成员。
A planet is considered oriental when it rises before the Sun, and occidental when it rises after. The word clown here means awkward, ill-bred, crude, oafish, lower class, and the list includes lower class groups getting little respect. We also have associations with age and seriousness. The religious associations are severe, isolated, penitential, and associated with the color black. A Sectarist is a person of a severe and prejudiced group, related to heretic, outside of the mainstream, that looks askance on others.
职业——鞣皮匠、夜粪清运工、地下矿工、锡匠、陶工、制帚工、铅管工、制砖工、制麦芽者、扫烟囱者、教堂司事、抬尸者、清道夫、马厩工、煤工、车夫、园丁、挖沟者、蜡烛匠、黑布染工、牧人、牧羊人或牧牛人。原书 p.39
Profession - Curriers, Night-farmers, Miners under ground, Tinners, Potters, Broom men, Plumbers, Brick-makers, Malsters, Chimney sweepers, Sextons of Churches, Bearers of dead corpses, Scavengers, Hostlers, Colliers, Carters, Gardeners, Ditchers, Chandlers, Diers of Black cloth, an Herdsman, Shepherd or Cow-keeper.
Currier 是处理鞣制皮革的工人；Malster 制作麦芽；Hostler 是马夫；Collier 是煤矿工；Carter 推小车或运走垃圾。这些职业多属下层、低薪的非熟练体力劳动，有些与污垢、腐坏、死亡、夜晚或地下相关；牧人与牧羊人则离群独处。说到地下，传统上地狱位于地下；拉丁文 Infernum 基本义就是较低、地下、在下面。
A Currier works with tanning hides. A Malster is a maker of malt - ie, a brewer. A Hostler is a groom or stable worker. A Collier is a coal miner. A Carter is someone who pushes small carts, or hauls away garbage. Most of these professions are lower class, low paying, and many are unskilled manual labor. Some are associated with dirt, or decay, or death, or night, or underground. Some are done in isolation, away from other people, like herdsmen and shepherds. Regarding the association with things underground, Hell is traditionally below the earth - the Latin word for Hell, Infernum, basically means inferior or underground or below.
背景Night-farmer 不是夜间耕田者
night-farmer 是早期现代英语职业名，指夜间清掏并搬运粪坑污物的人，故译「夜粪清运工」。它与清道夫、扫烟囱者、抬尸者同属接触污秽、黑暗与社会排斥的职业。
疾病——右耳与牙齿的一切障碍；由寒、干、忧郁质失调所生的一切四日疟；麻风、流注、消耗病、黑疸、瘫痪、震颤、妄惧、幻想、水肿、手足痛风、中风、犬饥症、痔血过多；若在天蝎座或狮子座并与金星成任何不良相位，则主疝气。
Sicknesses - All Impediments in the right Ears, Teeth, all quartan Agues proceeding of cold, dry and melancholy Distempers, Leprosies, Rheumes, Consumptions, black Jaundies, Palsies, Tremblings, vain Fears, Fantasies, Dropsy, the Hand and Footgout, Apoplexies, Dog-hunger, too much flux of the Hemoroids, Ruptures if in Scorpio or Leo, in any ill aspect with Venus.
四日疟是一种发热、出汗、抽搐的疾病，因每四日复发而得名。想想待在寒湿地下室里会患上的疾病：身体消耗、腐坏，还活着便开始朽烂。妄惧与幻想则像想象力的腐烂，使人向不健康、邪魔性的力量敞开。天主教夜祷圣歌常祈求免受夜间恐惧、幻想与虚假污秽梦境——即魔鬼为诱捕人而送来的梦；这里说的正是这类东西。
Quartan ague is a disease like influenza that has a fever, sweating and convulsions, called Quartan for a fever that recurred every 4 days. Think of the sort of diseases you would get hanging out in a cold wet basement. These are diseases that waste or decay the body, where it starts to rot while it is still alive. Vain fears and fantasies are like a rotting of the imagination, opening up to unhealthy and demonic forces. There is a very common Catholic night hour hymn that prays to be protected from nightly fears and fantasies and from false and polluting dreams, dreams sent by the devil to ensnare people. That is the sort of thing we are looking at here.
背景疾病名称按历史医学语境理解
这份清单属于十七世纪体液医学，不宜直接拿来作现代诊断。quartan ague 多指隔日发作、每第四日计次的疟热；consumption 是使身体日渐消瘦的消耗性疾病总称；dog-hunger 指异常强烈、难以满足的饥饿。译文保留历史病名，不把它们擅自对应成单一现代疾病。
味——酸、苦、辛；在人体中它主要主管脾。原书 p.40
Savors - Sour, Bitter, Sharp, in mans body he principally ruleth the Spleen.
草药——熊掌草、紫菀、乌头、毒芹、蕨、白藜芦与黑藜芦、天仙子、药室铁角蕨或指蕨、苍耳或牛蒡、欧洲防风、龙草、豆类、马鞭草、曼德拉草、罂粟、苔藓、龙葵、旋花、当归、鼠尾草、黄杨、土三草、滨藜或黄金草、菠菜、荠菜、孜然、木贼、烟堇。
Herbs - He governeth Beirsfoot, Starwort, Wolfbane, Hemlock, Ferne, Hellebor the white and black, Henbane, Ceterach or Finger ferne, Clotbur or Burdock, Parsnip, Dragon, Pulse, Vervine, Mandrake, Poppy, Mosse, Nightshade, Bythwind, Angelica, Sage, Box, Tutfan, Orage or golden Hearb, Spinach, Shepherds Purse, Cumin, Horitaile, Fumitory.
这里有几个共同主题：许多植物含致命毒性；有些生长在黑暗、荫蔽或多苔之地；另一些以辛锐、苦涩或令人不快的味道著称。
There are a couple of common themes here. Many of these plants are deadly poisons. Some of them grow in dark or shady or mossy places. Others are noted from having a sharp or bitter or unpleasant taste.
校记十七世纪草药俗名的译法
利利使用的若干俗名拼写古旧，同一名称在不同地区可能指不同植物；例如 Beirsfoot, Dragon, Tutfan, Orage, Bythwind 并非都能无歧义地还原到现代植物分类。正文按最常见对应暂译，并保留英文原文供互查。涉及药用或毒性时，不可把本清单当作现代植物鉴定或用药依据。
植物与树木——柽柳、沙地柏、番泻、刺山柑、芸香或恩典草、水龙骨、柳树、紫杉、柏树、大麻、松树。
Plants and Trees - Tamarisk, Savine, Sene, Capers, Rue or Hearbgrice, Polipody, Willow or Sallow Tree, Yew-tree, Cypress tree, Hemp, Pine-tree.
走兽等——驴、猫、野兔、鼠、鼹鼠、象、熊、犬、狼、蛇怪、鳄鱼、蝎、蟾蜍、蛇、蝰蛇、猪；以及一切由腐败物在土中、水中或房屋废墟里孳生的爬行生物。
Beasts etc. - The Ass, Cat Hare, Mouse, Mole, Elephant, Bear, Dog, Wolf, Basilisk, Crocodile, Scorpion, Toad, Serpent, Adder, Hog, all manner of creeping Creatures breeding of putrification, either in the Earth, Water or Ruins of Houses.
这些生物或夜间活动，或隐秘鬼祟，或有毒危险，并生活在腐败、朽坏、不健康之地。
These are creatures of the night; sneaky, furtive, hidden; venomous, dangerous; living in corrupt or decaying or unhealthy places.
鱼、鸟等——鳗、龟、贝类；蝙蝠或 Blood-black〔名物待考〕、乌鸦、凤头麦鸡、猫头鹰、蚋、鹤、孔雀、蚱蜢、鸫、乌鸫、鸵鸟、杜鹃。
Fishes, Birds etc. - The Eel, Tortoise, Shellfishes. The Bat or Blood-black, Crow, Lapwing, Owl, Gnat, Crane, Peacock, Grasshopper, Thrush, Blackbird, Ostritch, Cuckoo.
处所——它喜爱荒漠、树林、幽暗山谷、洞穴、兽窟、孔洞、山地，或埋葬过人的地方、教堂墓地等；残破建筑、煤矿、污水坑、肮脏恶臭的泥泞地、水井与厕所等。
Places - He delights in Deserts, Woods, obscure Valleys, Caves, Dens, Holes, Mountains, or where men have been buried, Churchyards, &c. Ruinous Buildings, Coal mines, Sinks, Dirty or Stinking Muddy Places, Wells and Houses of Offices, &c.
我们看到的仍是同一批主题：隐藏、隔绝、地下的处所，与死亡或朽坏相关、有害健康的处所。原书 p.41
We’re seeing the same sorts of themes here, places that are hidden, or isolated, or underground, places associated with death or decay, unhealthy places.
矿物——它主管铅、铅矿石、各种金属的渣滓，以及一切事物的尘土与废屑。
Minerals - He ruleth over Lead, the Leadstone, the Dross of all Metals, as also, the Dust and Rubbish of every thing.
铅不仅色泽暗灰近黑，而且有毒。
Along with being a dull greyish black color, lead is also poisonous.
宝石——蓝宝石、青金石；一切黑色、丑陋、无法抛光的乡野石块，以及色泽阴沉、灰烬色或黑色之石。
Stones - Sapphire, Lapis Lazuli, all black, ugly Country Stones not polishable, and of a sad ashy or black color.
天气——它造成多云、黑暗、幽晦、寒冷有害的空气，以及厚重、漆黑、浓密的云；另有专论详述。
Weather - He causeth Cloudy, Dark, obscure Air, cold and hurtful, thick, black and cadense Clouds: but of this more particularly in a Treatise by itself.
这描写的是以土星方式危害健康的空气：黑暗、厚重、浓密、寒冷。
This describes air that is unhealthy in a Saturn sort of way - dark, thick, dense, cold.
风——它喜爱天之东方，造成东风。采集任何归属土星的植物时，古人会在土星时面向东方；若可能，还要让土星位于角宫Angle，即上升点、第十宫或第十一宫，并让月亮以入相的三分相或六分相趋近土星。
Winds - He delighteth in the East quarter of Heaven, and causeth Eastern Winds, at the time of gathering any Planet belonging to him, the Ancients did observe to turn their faces towards the East in his hour, and he, if possible, in an Angle, either in the Ascendant, or tenth, or eleventh house, the Moon applying by a Trine or Sextile to him.
容许度orb——土星前后各九度；也就是说，当土星趋近相位，或任何行星趋近土星，进入相位前九度时，其影响便开始发生；越过精确相位后，直至分离九度才告终止。
Orb - His Orb is nine degrees before and after; that is, his influence begins to work, when either he applies, or any Planet applies to him, and is within nine degrees of his aspect, and from that aspect.
校记Plant、角宫与容许度句
对照利利原版可见三点：① 奥伯特本页的 gathering any Planet 应为 Plant「采集植物」；② 原文说「位于角宫」，却列上升点、第十宫和第十一宫，而第十一宫通常不是角宫；译文照录此技术不一致；③ 奥伯特的容许度引文在 and from that aspect 处残断，利利原文还说作用持续到出相位九度。中文据原版补足后一层意思，并在此出注。
年岁——就年岁而言，它关乎衰朽老人、父亲、祖父；植物、树木和一切生物中同类年老者亦然。
Age - As to Age, he relates to decrepit old men; Fathers, Grandfathers, the like in Plants, Trees, and all living Creatures.
它的朋友是木星、太阳与水星，敌人是火星与金星。星期六称为土星日，因为土星自日出开始主管，并主管该日第一与第八时。原书 p.42
His friends are Jupiter, Sun and Mercury, his enemies Mars and Venus. We call Saturday his day, for then he begins to rule at Sun rise, and ruleth the first hour and eighth of that day.
土星是昼间行星；木星、太阳、有时还有水星也属昼派。金星与火星属夜派。我还会把另一夜间行星月亮列为土星之敌，或至少把土星视为月亮之敌；土星与月亮的困难相位，对月亮而言不是好消息。
Saturn is a day planet, and Jupiter, Sun and sometimes Mercury are also day planets. Venus and Mars are two of the night planets. I would add the other night planet, the Moon, as an enemy of Saturn, or at least Saturn as the enemy of the Moon. Hard Saturn to Moon aspects are bad news for the Moon.
提示「朋友／敌人」在这里主要沿宗派分组
利利列出的朋友几乎就是土星的昼派同伴，敌人则是夜派行星。奥伯特补入月亮，使这条阵营线更加完整。这里说的是行星关系与相位对月亮功能的影响，不宜直接人格化成现实人际中的朋友和仇敌。
归纳

传统占星学中的土星含义The Meanings of Saturn in Traditional Astrology

我们已经巡览过几位传统作者，可以看出他们之间含义的一致性相当高。这里我想把其中几簇cluster共通的含义抽出来，看看它们可能是从哪里来的。原书 p.43
Now that we have surveyed several traditional authors you can see that there is a great deal of consistency of meaning across them. Here I want to draw out some of the common clusters of meaning, and look at where they may have come from.
前面提过，我有一个直觉：土星「冷而湿」是比「冷而干」更早的归属。在四体液humors体系里，土星被归为忧郁质melancholic，冷而干。这一归类取代了更早的与水、与湿的关联。
As I have mentioned, I have a hunch that Saturn as cold and damp is an earlier attribution than cold and dry. In the system of the 4 humors Saturn is categorized as melancholic, cold and dry. This superseded the earlier association with water and damp.
土星的许多含义来自它与二曜the lights的对分——主要是太阳，也包括月亮。这一簇含义源出于土星在世界本命盘Thema Mundi中的位置，以及该图中最主要的那个对分相opposition。这颗行星的含义有几何上的根据。若把对立项列成一张表，让太阳一栏正对土星一栏，土星意义中几个最重要的维度就显出来了。
A lot of the meanings of Saturn come from its opposition to the lights, primarily the Sun, but also the Moon. The cluster of meanings for Saturn come from its position in the Thema Mundi and the primary aspect of opposition in that diagram. There is a geometrical underpinning to the meanings of the planet. If we take a table of opposites and put the Sun in one column across from Saturn, we see several of the most important dimensions of Saturn's meaning.
日 · 土对立项表
太阳 Sun土星 Saturn
热而干 hot and dry冷而湿 cold and damp
光 light暗 dark
昼 day夜 night
夏 summer冬 winter
显 revealed隐 concealed
高 high低 low
高处 height深处 depth

原书 p. 43 表格，逐格照译。首行为表头，原书以粗体标出。

提示这张表是全书的发动机
奥伯特整本书的推理法，就浓缩在这七行里。他不是说「历代占星师觉得土星凶」，而是说：土星在世界本命盘里正对太阳，于是凡太阳是什么，土星就是它的反面——太阳热，土星就冷；太阳显，土星就隐；太阳高，土星就低。一条几何关系，推出一整套含义。

这就是他要「找回」的东西：传统含义不是经验的堆积，是从一个图形长出来的。后面每一簇含义（时间、边界、法则、对分）都能回溯到这张表的某一行。读全书时不妨把这七行按在手边。
校记height / depth 一行的译法
前一行 high／low 已作「高／低」，此行 height／depth 若再作「高／低」就重复了。英文这里从形容词换成名词，是有意的递进：从位置的高低，转到幅度的高深。译文作「高处／深处」以显出这层名词性。

更要紧的是：depth 这一格是后文「深邃、思想之深度」（profundity and depth of thought，原书 p. 46）的来源——土星之「深」先是空间的深（地下、洞穴、地窖），再转为思想的深。译名保住「深」字，这条线才接得上。
日与月都关乎肉身的生命力，而土星与两者皆相对。有些含义直接来自对分相本身——凡阻挡、拖延、对抗之物。原书 p.44
Both Sun and Moon are related to physical vitality, and Saturn is opposite both. Some meanings are from the opposition aspect itself - whatever blocks, delays, opposes.

土星在传统宇宙中的位置Saturn's Place in the Traditional Cosmos

另有几条核心原理，是从这颗行星的物理特征、以及它在传统宇宙中的位置推出来的。有些含义来自土星是离太阳之热最远的行星，因而是冷与暗的极端。土星身为最冷、最暗、最远的行星，这一点与对分相、与它在世界本命盘中正对二曜的位置相关。
There are several other core principles that are derived from the physical characteristics of the planet and its location in the traditional cosmos. Some of the meanings come from Saturn being the planet furthest from the heat of the Sun, thus the extreme of cold and of dark. Saturn being the coldest, darkest and most distant planet is related to the opposition aspect and its place opposite the lights in Thema Mundi.
背景世界本命盘（Thema Mundi）
传说中「世界受造那一刻」的星盘，见于菲尔米库斯《星占八书》等古典文献，是一张示意用的理想盘，非天文实测：月置巨蟹 15°，日置狮子，其余五星按行星次序依次向外排开——水星处女、金星天秤、火星天蝎、木星射手、土星摩羯。

由此，土星的两个本宫（摩羯、水瓶）恰好正对太阳与月亮的本宫（狮子、巨蟹）。本命宫的对分是被「设计」出来的，不是被观测出来的——这正是奥伯特说「有几何上的根据」的意思。本书凡说「土星与二曜相对」，指的都是这张图里的关系。
土星是一颗边界行星border planet，立在时间与永恒的边缘上，立在不动者与动者的边缘上。于是有了土星作为边界、墙垣、结构、边缘、容器、皮肤。立在永恒边缘上的土星，同时是中介者：把天界永恒的法则与秩序传递到底下这个变动的世界里。这就是作为审判的土星——与现代的业力观念相关；也是作为智慧的土星——智慧在此指对那些法则的认识与理解。法则加上年岁，就得出年岁的智慧。土星身为最外围的行星，很大程度上是整全周期的行星，也是由认识整全周期而来的智慧的行星。
Saturn is a border planet at the edge of time and eternity, the unmoving and the moving. You have Saturn as borders, walls, structures, edges, containers, skin. Saturn on the edge of eternity also is the mediator of the eternal law and order of the heavens down into the mutable world. This is Saturn as judgment, related to the modern notion of karma, but also Saturn as wisdom meaning knowledge and understanding of those laws - combine law plus age and you get the wisdom of age. Saturn as the outermost planet is very much the planet of entire cycles, and the wisdom that comes from knowledge of the entire cycles.
校记「与现代的业力观念相关」是一处让步，不是溯源
karma → 业力（梵语 कर्म，字面「行为」）。注意奥伯特的措辞是 related to the modern notion of karma——「与现代的业力观念相关」，不是「即业力」。希腊化与中世纪的土星审判观出自柏拉图—斯多亚的宇宙法则传统，与印度业报轮回并无历史渊源；两者相像，是因为都在处理「行为必有后果」这同一个问题。

他在这里用一个现代读者熟悉的词作桥，同时用 modern 一字划清界线。译文须把「现代」二字坐实，否则中文读者极易读成「土星即业力」，而这恰是全书第一部一直在批评的那类现代嫁接。
作为动与不动、时间与永恒之间的边界或门户，土星就是死亡——而且，如博纳蒂Bonatti所指出的，土星同时标记着进入时间与离开时间这两道口。这把土星与死后的清算联系起来：在那场审判中，我们的一生被拿去与永恒法则相量。土星于是也用来代表时间本身、代表整体的进程，因而也代表整体的周期，以及时间流逝所带来的后果。
As border or gateway between moving and unmoving, time and eternity, Saturn is death - and, as Bonatti pointed out, Saturn marks both the entry into time and the exit from time. This relates Saturn to the after death reckoning, the judgment where our lives are measured over against the eternal law. Saturn also comes to represent Time itself, the overall process, and thus also overall cycles, and the consequences of the passage of time.
出处博纳蒂「进入与离开时间」
圭多·博纳蒂Guido Bonatti（约 1210—1296），福尔利人，十三世纪拉丁世界最重要的占星师，所著 Liber Astronomiae（《天文书》，中译界亦作《占星书》）是中世纪拉丁占星学的集大成之作，本书第一部另有专章处理。

现已在本书博纳蒂专章（原书 pp. 31–32）核到这项归因的根据：博纳蒂原话说土星居行星序列之首，并首先作用于受孕后的胎儿；奥伯特继而说明，妊娠月份和宫位按迦勒底序列从土星起算，第一宫与第八宫遂同归土星。

「下降入世时首先经过土星、死亡离世时最后经过土星」是奥伯特据此作出的宇宙论推演，不是博纳蒂的逐字原句。所以此处 “as Bonatti pointed out” 应读作概括性归因；其文本根据已明，但不可加引号冒充直接引文。
把年岁、寒、湿三者合起来，就得出与腐坏、朽烂、不健康、患病、垂死的关联。
Combine age plus cold plus damp and you get associations with decaying, rotting, unhealthy, diseased, dying.
另有一些含义来自土星是运行最慢的行星，因而与耗时长久之物、行动迟缓之物、或与年岁相关之物挂钩。原书 p.45
Some of the other meanings come from Saturn being the slowest moving planet, hence associated with things that take a long time, move slowly, or are associated with age.
把上面列出的这些基本含义作各种组合，就能推出土星几簇主要的归属含义。
We can derive some main clusters of attributions of Saturn from various combinations of the basic meanings we have listed here.
时间——许多含义来自土星立于永恒与时间的边界上。因此土星与时间的流逝相关。由此又生出其他几个相关主题。
Time - Many of the meanings come from Saturn's being on the border of eternity and time. Hence Saturn is associated with the passage of time. This has other related themes.
老年：它是时间的效果，也是一个人对时间格外有感的年纪。相应地，土星也与长者、老人、与前代人相关。
Old age, which is the effect of time, and an age where you are very aware of time. In turn, Saturn is also associated with the elders, the aged, and with previous generations.
老年的智慧：此时你已获得由时间流逝而来的、对周期的整体眼光。
The wisdom of old age, when you have the perspective on cycles that comes from time passage.
腐坏、散架，作为时间流逝的一部分；因而也是败坏、朽烂、疾病。土星似乎格外与这几类病症相关：寒性的、使身体干枯的、或形如朽烂腐坏的；因此麻风这样的皮肤病便属土星。
Decaying, falling apart, as part of the passage of time; thus also corruption, decay, disease. Saturn seems to be especially related to diseases that are cold, or dry things up, or diseases that are like rotting or decay, so a skin disease like leprosy would be Saturnian.
土星因此也与死亡相关——那是在时间中生活的必然后果。
Saturn is thus also related to death, the inevitable consequence of life in time.
土星是传统，即在时间中被传递下来的东西。
Saturn is tradition, that which is passed down in time.
土星关乎受苦——时间、腐坏、拖延等等作用在人身上的效果。
Saturn relates to suffering, the effect of time, decay, delay etc. on people.
土星与受苦可以关联到道德律，于是有了「善处苦难」，或为他人担受苦楚。
Saturn and suffering can relate to moral law, and then you get suffering well, or enduring suffering for others.
校记“suffering well” 译「善处苦难」
英文 suffering well 是刻意的拗口：well 在此不是「顺利」，是「合乎德性地」——与古典伦理学的 eu zên（善好地生活）同构。意思是受苦这件事本身有高下之分，可以受得好，也可以受得坏。

结合第三部〈苦难〉专章（原书 pp. 202–205）的完整论述，定译为「善处苦难」：「处」强调回应与应对，既避开「很会吃苦」的歧义，也不把苦难本身说成善。
边界与结构——这层含义同样来自土星是介于时间与无时间之间的边界行星。土星与容器相关，如身体、身体的皮肤、结构、骨与齿。
Borders and Structures - This meaning also comes from being a border planet between time and timeless. Saturn is associated with containers like bodies, the skin of the body, the structure, the bones and teeth.
边界把我们划开、分开，所以土星是孤单、是隔绝、是被囚限在某道边界或某个结构之内。在时间中生活，其中一部分就是碎裂为一个个分离的实体。我们不再单单是「一」的一部分，我们是各自分离的存在。原书 p.46
Borders delineate and separate us, so Saturn is aloneness, isolation, being confined in a border or structure. Part of life in time includes fragmentation into separate entities. We are no longer simply part of oneness, we are separate beings.
土星关乎习惯——习惯是由重复行动在时间中筑起的结构，像一道有了自身惯性的沟槽。
Saturn relates to habit, which is a structure that is built from repeated action over time, an action like a groove that has its own momentum.
在亚里士多德那里，诸德性被描述为我们须靠重复行动筑成的习惯；所以土星也可以是自律，是好习惯的养成。土星同样可以是积久恶习的惰性，以及这些恶习带来的后果。
In Aristotle the virtues are described as habits we need to build by repeated actions, so Saturn can also be discipline, creating good habits. Saturn can also be the inertia of long standing bad habits, and their consequences.
背景亚里士多德的「习惯即德性」
出自《尼各马可伦理学》第二卷：德性aretē不是天生的，也不是学来的知识，而是hexis——靠反复实践养成的稳定品质（希腊文 ethos「习惯」与 ēthos「品格」仅一字之差，亚氏明言二者同源）。「我们由做公正的事而成为公正的人。」

奥伯特借此把土星的两面接成一条线：同一个「重复筑成结构」的机制，做对了是自律，做错了是恶习的惯性。土星本身不判好坏，它只负责「重复会固化」这件事——这也是他反对把土星简单读成「坏行星」的一处论据。
冷与湿——这是一项早期归属，很可能与土星正对太阳、与土星和冬天的联系有关。它涵盖朽烂与腐坏，那是冷与湿再加上时间的结果。土星系于冬，而老年与死亡正是人一生的冬天。
Cold and Damp - This is an early attribution that is likely related to being opposite the Sun, and to Saturn being connected with Winter. This includes rotting, decaying, which combines cold and damp with time. Saturn is linked to Winter, and old age and dying are the winter of a human life.
法则与审判Law and Judgment——这是一簇丰厚的关联，与作为结构的土星相关，也与立在时间与永恒交界上的土星相关。土星因此把永恒的法则与结构映照进时间之中。
Law and Judgment - This is a rich association, related to Saturn as structure, and to Saturn as at the border of time and eternal. Hence Saturn mirrors the eternal laws and structures into time.
由此关联到审判，即行动的后果。
This relates to judgment, the consequences of action.
道德与宗教上的智慧，关乎对法则的认识与理解。于是我们也得到了与深邃、与思想之深度的关联。
Moral and religious wisdom relate to knowledge and understanding of the laws. Thus we also get the connection with profundity and depth of thought.
土星系于义务——即学习并遵循道德律的责任。
Saturn ties to duty, which is the obligation to learn and follow the moral laws.
法则加上年岁，得出被传递下来的传统。举例来说，土星在第九宫而有力，可以表示对传统哲学与占星学的兴趣。
Law plus age gives tradition passed down. Thus, for instance, Saturn strong in the ninth house can indicate interest in traditional philosophy and astrology.
土星关乎谦卑与无私服事这两种德性。若太阳是对自我的肯定，土星就是否定；若太阳是自我中心，土星就是谦卑。再与对法则的义务相合，就得出无私的服事。
Saturn relates to the virtues of humility and selfless service. If the Sun is affirmation of the self then Saturn is the negation. If Sun is egotism then Saturn is humility. Combine with duty to law and you get selfless service.
由此可关联到内疚、羞耻、恐惧之感——它们都与某条道德律、以及「自知已经违犯了它」这一认识相关。原书 p.47
Relate this to feelings of guilt, shame, fear, related to a moral law and the knowledge one has broken it.
后果与审判，把法则的概念与时间的概念接了起来。
Consequences and judgment connect the concepts of law with time.
就更大的意义而言，土星是命定、是命运、是天意——那些因法则在时间中运行而落到你身上的事。
In a larger sense Saturn is fate, fortune, providence, those things that happen to you because of the laws playing out over time.
土星给出眼界与客观——那是时间、法则与思想三者的合成。
Saturn gives perspective, objectivity, which combines time with law with thought.
有了时间，就有了历史感，有了对时间流逝的感知。
With time you get a sense of history, of passage of time.
术语fate / fortune / providence 三词必须分开译
奥伯特把三个词并列，中文极易含混地都译成「命运」，那样这句话就废了。三者层次不同：
· fate（fatum，希腊 εἱμαρμένη）→ 命定：已被定死、不可更易的那一份。
· fortune（fortuna，希腊 τύχη）→ 命运：起落无常、可好可坏的那一份，中性偏「运气」。
· providence（providentia，希腊 πρόνοια）→ 天意：有意图、有安排的那一份，神学词。

三词沿用《天宫图与公共领域》旧表，跨项目一致。此条已并入 §0 表 C 组。
对分——土星的许多含义都与世界本命盘中这个基本相位有关：土星与太阳成对分。于是我们得到作为敌手、作为对抗者的土星，得到那阻挡者、妨碍者。拖延，就是在时间中展开的对分。
Opposition - Many of the meanings of Saturn are related to this basic aspect from the Thema Mundi where Saturn is in opposition to the Sun. Hence we get Saturn as adversary, opposer, that which blocks, hinders. Delay is opposition over time.
提示「拖延＝在时间中展开的对分」
这半句是全章最漂亮的一次推导，值得单独拆开看。

对分相在空间上是「两星相隔 180°，互相顶住」。把这个「顶住」放进时间里展开，就是：事情本该发生，被顶住了，于是往后推——那就是拖延。

同一个几何关系，在空间里读出「对抗」，在时间里读出「延迟」。土星最叫人头疼的那个日常表现（什么都慢、什么都被推迟），就这样从一个纯粹的角度关系里长了出来。
下面是土星的另一些含义，附上我对它们如何从前述核心含义推衍而来的一点想法。
Here are other meanings of Saturn, with some thoughts as to how they were derived from the core meanings we looked at.
卑下、贫穷、受轻贱——土星之「低」，对着太阳之「高」。这也可以指物理上的低，因而是地下的处所，如洞穴，或建筑物的地窖。这里把「低」与「暗而隐」合在一起，也可能再加上「冷而湿」。
Lowly, Poor, Despised - Saturn as low as opposed to Sun as high. This also can mean physically low, hence places underground like caves, or basements in buildings. This combines being low with being dark and hidden, and possibly cold and damp.
欺瞒、犬儒——想想土星正对太阳：太阳敞开、可见、诚实。土星反过来便可以是隐蔽的，是刻意的藏匿或欺瞒。
Deceitful, Cynical - Think of Saturn opposite Sun, which is wide open, visible and honest. Saturn in turn can be hidden, deliberately hiding or deceiving.
恶、凶、有害——这与土星正对二曜、与作为对分的土星相关：于是「恶」就是对「善」的对抗，是从善那里被隔开、或对善的悖逆。「凶malefic」与道德上的「恶evil」并不完全是一回事，这两个概念我们会在后面的一篇文章里细看。原书 p.48
Evil, Malefic, Harmful - This is related to Saturn opposite the lights, and Saturn as opposition, so we have evil as opposition to good, separated off or in rebellion from the good. Malefic and moral evil aren't quite the same thing, and we will look at those concepts in more detail in a later essay.
术语这一句兑现了 §0 表的预留
第一次试译时，§0 表把 malefic 定为「凶星」而非「恶星」，理由是「奥伯特后文要辨析『凶』与『恶』不是一回事，用『恶星』则该辨析会塌掉」。这句话就是那个后文。

原文 Malefic and moral evil aren't quite the same thing——若前面一路把 malefic 译作「恶星」，此处就成了「恶与道德上的恶不完全是一回事」，中文读者只会觉得作者在绕口令。

译作「凶／恶」，这句话立刻站得住：凶是「造成损害」，恶是「道德上的败坏」；土星带来艰难，不等于土星道德上邪恶。这是术语契约「要对的不只是本页，是全书」的一次实证。详细辨析见第三部〈Benefic and Malefic〉与〈Evil〉两章（原书 pp. 179–188）。
土星的所有这些含义簇，归根到底只关联到寥寥两三个起点：土星与太阳相对、且离太阳最远；以及土星身为最外围的行星，立在时间与永恒的边界上。这些看来就是土星意义的主要钥匙。
All of these clusters of meaning of Saturn relate to just a couple of main starting points - Saturn as opposite the Sun and furthest from the Sun, and Saturn as the outermost planet on the border of time and eternity. These seem to be the main keys to Saturn's meaning.
提示一句话总结这一章
土星的全部含义，是从两个事实推出来的：（一）它正对太阳；（二）它在最外圈。

由（一）得出所有「反面」的含义：冷、暗、夜、冬、隐、低、阻挡、敌对、拖延、卑贱、欺瞒。
由（二）得出所有「边界」的含义：时间与永恒的交界、死亡、结构、皮肤、骨、法则、审判、周期、年岁、智慧。

其余一切——受苦、传统、自律、谦卑、麻风、地窖、业力、天意——都是这两条主干上的枝节，或两者的组合（年岁＋寒＋湿＝腐朽）。

这一章之所以是全书枢轴，就在于它证明了：传统占星学的土星不是一张需要背诵的清单，是一个可以推导的系统。第一部前面那些征象长清单，到这里才显出结构。
过渡期：十九世纪

过渡占星师Transition Astrologers

接下来引用的两位占星师来自十九世纪。他们尚未完全进入现代，但我们已经能看出，传统文献丰富的含义正在大量流失。我们也看到，重心开始离开物理、具体的含义，转向以心理与性格描述为主。原书 p.49
These next two astrologers I quote are from the 1800's. They are not yet quite part of the modern era, but at the same time we can see that a lot of the richness of meaning of the traditional texts is being lost. We also see the beginning of the move away from physical and concrete meanings towards primarily psychology and character description.
须留意，本节考察的两位占星师都处在十九世纪后期——达尔文与演化论的时代，也是招魂术与神智学的时代。我们正站在转向现代占星学的边界上。
Take into account that both of the astrologers I look at in this section are late 18th century, the era of Darwin and evolution, the era of spiritualism and theosophy. We are right at the border of the change to modern astrology here.
校记late 18th century 当作十九世纪后期
原书先说两人来自 the 1800s，拉斐尔这部书首版于 1877 年，塞法里尔则生于 1864 年；下一段却误写 late 18th century「十八世纪后期」。依上下文与年代，显然应为 late 19th century，中文径改作「十九世纪后期」。
提示这里的“过渡”具体丢掉了什么
早期清单同时谈颜色、金属、气味、动物、职业、疾病、地形和人的品格；到这两位作者手里，外部世界仍在，但篇幅迅速收缩，人物性格开始占据中心。改变不是突然换了一套关键词，而是同一套传统中某些门类逐渐失声。

拉斐尔《占星指南》Raphael, Guide to Astrology

本书初版于 1877 年。拉斐尔是一位英国占星师——更准确地说，是先后使用这一笔名写作的一系列占星师。原书 p.50
This book was first published in 1877. Raphael is a British astrologer, or rather a series of astrologers who wrote with this pseudonym.
背景“拉斐尔”是一项传承笔名
Raphael 不是一位作者的本名，而是十九世纪英国占星出版界由多人承接的笔名；天使拉斐尔传统上与水星相连，因而适合作为占星写作者署名。本章所引《占星指南》通常归于罗伯特·托马斯·克罗斯一系。
非常值得注意的是，这种描述与最早期希腊化材料本质上属于同一类型；总体关联和主题一眼可辨。传统占星学在其历史大部分时期都极为保守、一致。直到二十世纪以后，含义才开始显著改变。
It is very noteworthy that this is essentially the same sort of description that we see in the very earliest Hellenistic material. The general associations and themes are recognizably the same. Traditional astrology stayed remarkably conservative and consistent throughout most of its history. It is only in the twentieth century and later than we start seeing significant changes in meaning.
与前面的征象清单相比，这里几乎全是性格特质，不过在土星经过各宫的部分，仍能看到外部事物与事件。行星按所居宫位来描述。我先给出上升点的描述，因为它最长；然后再抽取其他宫位的一些含义。
As compared to the previous lists of significations, these are almost all character traits, although we do see attributions to external things and events in the section on Saturn through the houses. Description of planets is by house position. I will first give the description for the Ascendant since it is the longest, and then a sample of meanings for other houses.
土星出生时位于上升点，使命主胆怯、谨慎、不信任、恐惧；沉默、深思、恶毒、报复；贪婪、妒忌。
This planet in the Ascendant at birth makes the native timid, cautious, mistrustful, and fearful: reserved, thoughtful, malicious and revengeful; covetous and envious.
在火象星座中，这些性质有所改善；命主行事较为坦荡、直率、诚实，却鲁莽冲动，喜爱争辩与吵架。
In the fiery signs he is improved; he is more free, straight, and honest in his actions, but rash and impetuous, fond of argument and quarreling.
在土象星座中，尤其金牛座，命主痴愚、迟钝、沉重、恶毒；在处女座则极爱追问、多疑、烦躁；在摩羯座则好辩、狡猾、自私、勤劳、吝啬。在风象星座中，命主深思、好学、沉思；对钱财与自身事务守口如瓶；宗教上真诚虔敬——只要关系到「自己」。
In the earthy signs, especially Taurus, the native is sottish, dull, heavy and malicious; in Virgo very inquisitive, suspicious and fretful; in Capricorn argumentative, crafty, selfish, hard-working and miserly. In the airy signs, thoughtful, studious, contemplative, close over money matters and his own affairs, sincere and devout in religion so far as it concerns "self".
双子座与水瓶座优于天秤座；在天秤座，命主固执己见、自私、吝啬、骄傲，简直给每个人添麻烦。原书 p.51
Gemini and Aquarius are better signs than Libra, for in the latter he is simply a nuisance to every one on account of his opinionative, selfish, miserly, and proud ways.
尤其在巨蟹座或双鱼座，命主迟钝痴愚、沮丧、堕落而猥亵，有时狂热于宗教；在天蝎座则极其狡猾恶毒，不可信任。
In Cancer or Pisces especially, he is dull, sottish, and dejected, depraved, and obscene; sometimes an enthusiast in religion; in Scorpio very crafty and malicious and not to be trusted.
总的说来，土星型命主羞怯，厌弃自己的目标；吝啬，眷恋住所；坚持不移，对宗教信仰好奇，耽于幻想而偏执；常有言语障碍（除非土星在风象星座）；谨慎料理事务和家庭，严峻而厌恶改变。
Generally the native of Saturn is shy, and has aversion to his own ends; miserly, fond of his habitation; constant, curious in religious beliefs, fanciful and bigoted, frequently an impediment in speech (except Saturn be in the airy signs), careful of his affairs and family, austere and averse to changes.
提示外部征象开始收缩成人格描述
这些段落仍保留传统的冷、重、隐、固执、孤立等主轴，却主要把它们写成「怎样的人」。即使谈四元素星座，重点也不是土地、水域、材料或职业，而是性格怎样变化。这正是上一页所说的过渡。
土星在第二宫——钱财事务多有烦恼忧虑；损财；贸易或生意不顺。即使土星有必然尊贵essentially fortified，命主通常仍须极辛苦地挣钱；若全无尊贵，困顿与贫穷往往或多或少终身紧随其后。
Saturn in the 2nd - Much trouble and worry in money matters; loss of money; ill success in trade or business; the native has usually to work very hard for his money, even when Saturn is essentially fortified; when out of dignities, distress and poverty usually dog the native's footsteps, more or less through life.
土星在第九宫——宗教上坚定不移；固执、深思、沉默、沉思；好学；喜爱占星学、秘学、非常之事、魔法等；水上旅行有危险；因姻亲受损。除非土星因位置或相位极强，命主应避免异国。
Saturn in the 9th - Firm and steadfast in religion; headstrong, thoughtful, reserved, and contemplative; studious; fond of astrology, occultism, and things out of the common track, magic, &c., danger in traveling by water; loss through relations by marriage. The native should avoid foreign lands unless Saturn be very strong by position or aspect.
土星在第十宫——人生成功，最终却失势受辱、陷入麻烦；事业上常失信誉；母亲有难，财产受损。
Saturn in the 10th - Success in life, with an ultimate fall to disgrace and trouble; frequent discredit in business; trouble to the mother and loss of substance.

塞法里尔《占星手册》Sepharial, The Manual of Astrology

塞法里尔Sepharial，即沃尔特·戈恩·奥尔德Walter Gorn Old，是另一位以笔名写作、影响深远的英国占星师。本书行星部分按主题编成参考表，类似比鲁尼，但清单大为缩短。到了宫位含义，个人品质与经验开始受到更多强调。它与早期清单一致，却高度缩略；冷、重、暗、干的联系保持不变，与水的联系则几乎消失。读来像前代文献的浓缩摘要。原书 p.52
Sepharial, or Walter Orn Gold, is another influential British astrologer who wrote under a pseudonym. The section on the planets in this book consists of reference tables by topic, similar to Al-Biruni. The lists are much more abbreviated. When you get to the house meanings we are starting to see more of an emphasis on personal qualities and experience. It is consistent with the earlier lists, but much abbreviated. The connection with cold, heavy, dark and dry is constant, the connection with water is pretty much gone. This reads like a condensed digest of previous texts.
校记Walter Orn Gold 当作 Walter Gorn Old
原书把塞法里尔姓名排作 Walter Orn Gold；通行传记与书目均作 Walter Gorn Old（亦连写 Gornold），本名 Walter Richard Old。此处显为字序转录错误，中文径按通名「沃尔特·戈恩·奥尔德」译出。
〔气质〕土星：神经紧张、守秘、防御、束缚、恒定、冷、干、硬、不育。
[TEMPERAMENT] Saturn is nervous, secretive, defensive, binding, constant, cold, dry, hard and barren.
〔味〕土星：冷、涩、酸。
[FLAVOURS] Saturn is cold, astringent and sour.
〔形态〕土星给出坚硬、轮廓分明的外形，短直线与局促形态。
[FORMS] Saturn gives hard, clear-cut outlines, straight short lines, and cramped forms.
〔颜色〕土星：黑、深褐、靛青。
[COLOURS] Saturn: black, dark brown, and indigo.
〔金属〕土星主管铅。
[METALS] Saturn governs lead.
〔人体部位〕土星主管骨与关节、肝、脾、左耳、小腿与膝，以及总体的分泌系统。
[PARTS OF HUMAN BODY] Saturn rules over bones and articulations, liver, spleen, left ear, the calves and knees; the secretive system generally.
校记secretive system 疑为 secretory system
人体系统中没有通常所谓 secretive system「守秘系统」；结合生理语境，此处几乎必为 secretory system「分泌系统」的转录误差。中文径改，并保留英文原貌供核。
〔职业〕土星：土地与房产商、矿工、煤商、铅及其他土星商品的商人、铅管工、狱卒、教堂司事、掘墓者、守夜人等；从事繁重工作者，以及夜间或地下工作者。
[OCCUPATIONS] Saturn - Land and property dealers, miners, coal merchants, dealers in lead and other Saturnine commodities, plumbers, jailers, sextons, grave-diggers, watchmen etc.; such as follow laborious employments, and those that work at night or underground.
下面举几个土星落入各宫的含义。
Here are some examples of the meanings of Saturn in the houses.
第一宫：土星——心性忧郁，习惯独处；举止羞怯、紧张；使命主易患感冒；头部受挫伤；一路逆势而上；性情耐心。原书 p.53
First House: Saturn - Melancholy mind, solitary habits; shy, nervous manners; subjects the native to colds; causes bruises to the head; an uphill struggle; patient disposition.
第二宫：土星——生意损失；性情节俭；劳苦而所得甚少；有时贫穷。
Second House: Saturn - Business losses; a thrifty nature; hard work for little gain; sometimes poverty.
第九宫：土星——爱好哲学；有宗教精神；异国有麻烦；航程危险；因诉讼受损；姻亲中有欺骗；性情好学深思。
Ninth House: Saturn - Taste for philosophy; religious spirit; troubles in foreign lands; dangerous voyages; loss through legal suits; deceit among relatives by marriage; studious and thoughtful nature.
第十宫：土星——人生上升，继而坠落。耐心与目标坚定标记命主一生。经商则主财务毁灭；从事专业则主失誉失败；从政则主败北。命主生来便有一种宿命悬于头顶：早年有败落或失去双亲之一的危险；公共事务失败，或带来损失与失信。
Tenth House: Saturn - Rise in life, followed by a downfall. Patience and firmness of purpose mark the life of the native. In business, financial ruin is shown; in professional life, dishonour and failure; in government, defeat. A fatality hangs over the native from his birth; danger of ruin or loss of a parent in early life; public affairs fail or bring to loss and discredit.
提示从“宇宙清单”到“人生摘要”
塞法里尔仍保留颜色、金属、形态、身体与职业，但每类只剩一两行；宫位判断则已像现代读者熟悉的人生主题摘要。传统结构尚在，注意力却明显从宇宙万物转向「命主会经历什么、性格如何」。
世界观的转变

旧宇宙与新宇宙The Old and New Cosmos

要理解二十世纪赋予土星的含义，我们必须认识到：现代宇宙模型与传统模型大不相同。我们已经看到，从希腊化时代到十七世纪威廉·利利时期，整个传统时代归给土星的含义显著一致。同一时代的世界观也有显著连续性：希腊罗马文明、犹太—基督教文明与伊斯兰文明共享一份遗产。尽管各有差异与发展，他们世界的形状仍有若干稳定不变之处；他们视为当然的东西，今天的我们反而很难设想。原书 p.54
In order to understand the meanings given to Saturn in the twentieth century we need to realize that the modern model of the universe is very different from the traditional model. We have seen that that there is a notable consistency in the meanings attributed to Saturn throughout the traditional era, from the Hellenistic period up through the 17th century and the era of William Lilly. That same era shared a notable consistency in world view, including the periods of the Greek and Roman civilizations, the Judeo-Christian civilization, and the Islamic civilization, all of which have a common heritage. For all the differences and developments there are notable stable consistencies in the shape of their world, things they took for granted that we have a hard time conceiving today.
今天我们心中世界的形状已经不同。二十一世纪初的我们向外看，不再看见祖先所见的那类世界。这影响我们的思考、感受、言语与行动，也影响我们怎样理解占星学、怎样赋予其构件以含义。
We have a differently shaped world in our minds today; when we look out at the world in the early 21st century we do not see the same kind of world that our ancestors saw. This affects how we think, how we feel, how we speak and act - and, how we conceive of astrology, and the meanings we give to the elements of astrology.
这不是说宇宙改变了，而是我们对宇宙的构想改变了。这也不是说现代构想更进步、更真实或更演化；它只是不同。尤其绝不意味着我们比祖先更聪明或更进化。
This does not mean that the Universe has changed, it means that our conception of the Universe has changed. This does not mean that our modern conception is improved, or truer, or more evolved. It means that it is different. And, it emphatically does not mean that we are smarter or more evolved than our ancestors.
提示“世界的形状”不是天文学图纸而已
奥伯特所谓世界的「形状」，同时包括空间结构、价值来源、时间方向和人类在其中的位置。宇宙有没有边界、真理在上方还是从物质中长出、过去是否比未来更有权威——这些预设会决定同一个「土星」还能不能表示永恒边界。
过去几个世纪的一系列发现与发展，彻底改变了我们观看所居世界的方式。下面简要回顾其中一些，以及它们如何促成改变后的世界。
Over the past few centuries there have been a series of discoveries and developments that have completely changed how we view the world we live in. We will briefly review some of these and their contribution to our changed world.
校记本页两处衍词
原书 p. 54 有 we have seen that that there is，第二个 that 衍入；中文径删。同章 p. 55 末句 we are considering here here 亦重复一个 here，中文只译一次。

太阳系The Solar System

我们关于太阳系与银河系的构想发生了重大变化。在旧模型中，地球是固定中心，行星围绕地球运行；行星天球之外，则是固定、永恒的恒星天。原书 p.55
There is a significant change in our conception of the solar system and galaxy. In the old model the earth is a fixed center, with the planets revolving around the earth, and then the fixed and eternal firmament of heaven with the fixed stars outside of the spheres of the planets.
十五、十六世纪，哥白尼与伽利略的工作开始改变这一图景：在数学上，把地球与其他行星看作绕太阳运行，比把地球当作宇宙静止中心更讲得通。现代人很难想象这次转变有多么震撼。稳定不变的世界秩序被撞歪，原本固定的中心也动了起来。
This began to change in the 15th and 16th centuries, with the work of Copernicus and Galileo demonstrating that it makes more sense mathematically to view the Earth and other planets as revolving around the Sun, rather than having the Earth be the static center of the cosmos. In our modern world it is hard to conceive what a traumatic shift this must have been. The stable and unchanging order of the world was knocked askew, and what was once the fixed center was set in motion.
更近的时代又有一次转变。地球不仅绕太阳运行；我们如今还有更大的参照框架：整个太阳系本身也在银河中运动，环绕所谓银河中心的点运行。
More recently there has been a further shift. Along with the earth revolving around the Sun, we now have a larger frame of reference, where our entire solar system is itself in motion moving through the galaxy, revolving around a point referred to as the galactic center.
祖先仰望天空，看见固定而永恒的秩序——天之恒星天，如宏伟教堂般封闭的穹顶或拱顶；宏大、稳定、不动、永恒的秩序映照在天上。现代世界的我们仰头——更准确说，向外看——却看见运动着、无穷无尽的无限空间。这是视角的深刻变化。我们生活在一个不断运动、永远流变、外无他物的世界。现代世界失去固定而永恒的尺度；它几乎会贯穿这里考察的所有世界观变化。
Our ancestors looked up at the sky and saw a fixed and eternal order, the firmament of heaven, an enclosed dome or arch like a grand cathedral. It is a grand, stable, unmoving and eternal order mirrored in the heavens. By contrast, in our modern world we look up - or, rather, we look out - and see moving, endless infinite space. That is a profound change in perspective. We live in a world in movement and in perpetual flux, with nothing outside of it. This loss of any fixed and eternal standard in our modern world will resonate through almost all of the changes in our worldview that we are considering here here.
提示从“向上看”到“向外看”
旧宇宙有垂直方向：越往上越接近不动与永恒；新宇宙则主要是横向延伸的空间，没有最后一层天。一个小小的介词变化——向上／向外——拿掉了土星作为最外边界的宇宙论位置。

新行星New Planets

与宇宙图景的变化并行，现代土星也不再被视为最外围行星。新行星陆续被发现：1781 年天王星，1846 年海王星；1930 年冥王星初被发现时也被视为行星。
Parallel with this change in the cosmos, Saturn in the modern era is no longer considered to be the outermost planet. There have been new planets discovered - first Uranus in 1781, then Neptune in 1846. Pluto was considered to be a planet when it was first discovered in 1930.
虽然冥王星已失去现代科学中的正式行星地位，在大多数现代占星学中仍实际被当作行星，并在一些流派中居显要位置。原书 p.56
Even though Pluto has lost its official status as a planet in modern science, it is still effectively a planet in most of modern astrology, and it plays a prominent role in some schools.
在现代占星学中，土星不再是最外围行星，不再坐在游动行星的外边界、恒星天边缘。旧秩序里它立于时间与永恒之间；新宇宙里，它只是其他运动物体之间又一个运动物体。
Saturn is no longer the outermost planet in modern astrology, and it no longer sits at the outer border of the moving planets, at the edge of the sphere of the fixed stars. In the old order Saturn stood at the border between time and eternity, and in the new cosmos it is one more moving body in the midst of other moving bodies.
考察更多现代占星师时，我们会看到这如何深刻改变土星的含义。传统占星师（例如我）对三颗现代外行星的理解，与多数现代占星师大不相同；对土星与其他古典七行星含义的处理方式也往往不同。
We will see, as we consider more modern astrologers, that this has a very profound effect on changing concepts of the meaning of the planet Saturn. Traditional astrologers (like myself) have a very different conception of the three modern outer planets than do most modern astrologers. Traditional astrologers also typically approach the meaning of Saturn and the other classical seven planets in a different way from many modern astrologers.

古代科学与现代科学Ancient and Modern Science

与宇宙变化并行的，是所谓现代科学世界观的兴起。它是唯物论的：认为宇宙基本由物质构成，其他一切——不同生命形态，最终包括人类——都由此生长。人的意识在这一模型中位置颇不稳固。
Parallel with this change in the cosmos is the rise of what we call the modern scientific worldview. It is materialist, viewing the universe as basically created from matter, and all other elements growing from that, including different forms of life, and eventually humans. Human consciousness has a somewhat precarious place in this model.
为了勾勒图景，我在简化，但大体笔触是准确的。旧世界观中有永恒秩序，通常也有永恒的创造者与秩序统治者；我们在地上所居的变动世界位于该秩序之内。传统秩序自上而下，从永恒开始、以物质告终；新秩序自下而上，从物质开始、向上生长。
I am simplifying here to paint a picture, but I think the broad strokes are accurate. In the old concept of the world there is an eternal order, and usually the concept of an eternal Creator and ruler of that order, and the changing world we live in here on earth is within that eternal order. The traditional order is top down, starting with the eternal and concluding with matter, and the new order is bottom-up starting with matter and growing upward.
关于人如何认识传统秩序，曾有不同看法。追随亚里士多德者认为我们从感觉经验学习，再从中抽象、提取秩序原理；追随柏拉图及其学派者则认为秩序原理本具于心，感觉经验只是令它们进入意识。
There were different notions as to how humans could learn that traditional order. Following Aristotle, some thought that we learn from sense experience, and then we abstract or draw out principles of order from that. Others, following Plato and his school, thought that we have these principles of order inherent in our minds, and our sense experience is needed to bring those to awareness.
无论哪一种，都预设有一套基本秩序确实存在、真实存在，而且人的心智能把握它。上帝或神性、外部世界与人的心智世界，同属一个整体，彼此对应。原书 p.57
Either way there is an assumption that there is a basic order that is there and that is real, and that our minds can apprehend that order. God or the Divine, the external world, and the world of the human mind, are all of a piece and all correspond with each other.
旧宇宙秩序固定、永恒、有序，自上而下——起初如何，今日亦然，永世皆然，世世无尽。新秩序则变化、生长、演化，自下而上，没有边界，也没有固定秩序。
The old order of the cosmos is fixed, eternal and ordered, from the top down - as it was in the beginning, is now, and ever shall be, world without end. The new order is changing, growing, evolving from the bottom up, with no boundaries and no fixed order.
提示自上而下与自下而上
旧模型先有永恒秩序，物质依它成形；新模型先有物质，生命、意识和价值随后涌现。两者争的不只是宇宙起点，更是尺度从哪里来：是事物向一个先在标准靠拢，还是标准由发展过程本身生成。

科学：旧义与新义Science Old and New

多数人没有意识到，「科学」一词的意义与涵义在过去几百年发生了巨大变化。旧义上的科学主要建立在永恒不变的法则与原理上。我们可以完全脱离感觉经验来思考这些法则；它们也独立于外部感觉经验而存在。因为这些法则映照在心中，我们能够直接思考真实与真理。请注意，这个内在抽象秩序还包含伦理维度；当我们思考正义等概念时，它对应某种真实存在。现代意义的科学则严格建立在观察与经验证据上。
Most people don't realize that the meaning and connotations of the term Science have changed greatly in the past few hundred years. In the older meaning of the term, Science is primarily based on eternal and unchanging laws and principles. We can think of these laws completely separate from sense experience, and these laws exist independent of external sense experience. We can think directly about what is real and true since these are mirrored in our minds. Note that this internal abstract order includes an ethical dimension, so that when we think of concepts like Justice, this corresponds to something real. By contrast, in the modern meaning of the term, Science is now based strictly on observation and empirical evidence.
「科学」词义的转变非常晚近，大约只在过去 150 年间。翻阅十九世纪初韦氏词典原版，旧义仍占主导；现代词典已完全不见这个定义，严格经验性的意义才符合今日通常用法。附录有一篇文章考察旧词典定义与实例。
This shift in the meaning of the term Science is very recent, within about the last 150 years. If you go back and look at the original Webster's dictionary from the early 1800's the older meaning of the term still dominates. That old definition has completely disappeared from more modern dictionaries, and the strictly empirical sense fits how we usually use the term today. I have included an essay in the Appendix where I examine older dictionary definitions and examples to illustrate that.
校记这是奥伯特为本书建立的 science 两义框架
作者把旧义概括为关于永恒原理的系统知识，把现代义概括为严格经验科学。实际词义史更复杂，旧时代也有经验观察，现代数学与理论科学也不只等于直接经验。译文照录其二分，因为后文论证建立在这套框架上；附录 pp. 215–222 会提供他的词典证据。
你读到上一段「科学」的旧义时，很可能根本没留意，或觉得有点古怪，或不太确定它是什么意思。你多半会想：「天哪，谁都知道科学就是经验证据！」这正是我所谓我们视为当然、甚至根本察觉不到的预设。今天 science 对我们就是这个意思，所以我们假定它历来如此。原书 p.58
There is a very good chance that, when you read the paragraph on the old meaning of the term science, that either it did not register, or it sounded kind of odd, or perhaps you weren't quite sure what it meant. You very likely thought something like, "Well goodness, everyone knows that Science means empirical evidence!" That is exactly what I mean when I talk about assumptions we take for granted and likely do not even notice. This is what the word Science means to us today, so we assume that it always meant that.

演化与进步Evolution and Progress

这两种现代观念大约同时成形，并与变化中的世界模型结合。宇宙模型变了，对科学的理解变了；与之并行的，是我们对人类起源及其在新秩序中位置的看法发生巨变。
These are other modern concepts that took shape around the same time to combine with this changing model of the world. Our model of the cosmos has changed, how we think of Science has changed, and parallel to that is a huge shift in how we think of the origin and place of the human race in this new order.
查尔斯·达尔文Charles Darwin于 1859 年出版《物种起源》。自此，从简单要素走向复杂形态的演化观念，成为塑造世界理解的主导隐喻，远远超出达尔文原本的科学主张。
Charles Darwin published his On the Origin of Species in 1859, and since then his concept of evolution, starting with simpler elements and moving towards more complex, has become a dominant metaphor shaping how we think of the world, a metaphor that goes far beyond Darwin's original scientific claims.
同一时期，技术发明与进步飞速发展，主要由地球化石燃料驱动：先是煤，继而石油与天然气。它提供了「进步」隐喻；与演化隐喻结合后，便形成一幅无尽改善的图景，从过去向未来不断前进、上升。
During this same time period we have an era of rapid technological invention and progress, largely driven by the use of our planet's fossil fuels, first coal, and then oil and gas. This provided a metaphor of progress that combines with the metaphor of evolution to give a picture of an endless march of improvement moving ever forward and upward from the past to the future.
这又与欧洲、英国殖民扩张和全球统治的高峰并行。当时人把这种扩张视为文明的成长与传播，仿佛带着全人类一同前进。演化与进步于是又与无尽扩张相连：既适用于如今无边无限的宇宙，也适用于现代文明遍布全球的增长。
This in turn parallels the peak of the period of European and British colonial expansion and domination across the entire earth, a growth which was viewed at the time as the growth and spread of civilization, moving the entire human race forward with it. The concepts of evolution and progress are now linked with the concept of endless expansion. This applies to our now endless and infinite solar system, and it applies to the growth of modern civilization across the planet.
进步＝演化＝增长＝扩张——这些概念如今在心中彼此相连，已深深成为世界观的一部分，以至我们再也看不见它们。它们是预设、是知觉滤镜，像呼吸的空气一样属于环境。祖先仰望天空，看见固定永恒秩序；我们抬头则看见潜能、无边无际的可能，以及无尽增长扩张。借一部流行电视剧的口号说，我们准备「勇踏前人未至之境」。原书 p.59
Progress equals evolution equals growth equals expansion - all of these concepts are now linked in our minds. They are now so much a part of our view of the world that we don't see them anymore. They are assumptions, perceptual filters, as much a part of our environment as the air we breathe. Our ancestors looked at the sky and saw a fixed eternal order. We look up and see potential, boundless and limitless possibility for endless growth and expansion. To quote a slogan from a popular TV show, we are ready to Boldly Go Where No Man Has Gone Before.
背景“勇踏前人未至之境”
这是《星际迷航》的著名开场口号。作者借它把无限宇宙、技术进步、殖民扩张与「未来必然更好」的想象绑在一起；重点不是批评太空探索本身，而是揭出「扩张天然等于进步」这一未明说的价值判断。

过去与未来Past and Future

这一新隐喻的重要效果，是抬高新而贬低旧，抬高未来而贬低过去。若演化与改善持续不断，过去必然过时，未来则许诺新荣光。过去被贬值，新与现代受到高度评价。
An important effect of this new metaphor is to place a great value on the new over the old, the future over the past. If there is constant evolution and improvement then the past must be outmoded, and the future promises new glories. The past is devalued, the new and modern is highly valued.
旧秩序高度珍视传统与过去的智慧，认为它们反映永恒不变秩序。新世界里，过去被现在取代，旧式被现代取代，原始迷信的过去被开明优越的未来取代。我们许多价值判断都在这一模型中作出；二十世纪以后的占星写作也深受浸透。
In the old order a high value was placed on tradition, the wisdom of the past, which was thought to reflect an eternal unchanging order. In the new world the past is superseded by the present, the old fashioned by the modern, the primitive and superstitious past by the enlightened and superior future. We make many of our value judgments in the context of this model, and it permeates much of astrology writing from the twentieth century on forward.
这一点无论怎样强调都不为过。
I cannot emphasize this point enough.
新优于旧、未来优于过去的底层预设，深嵌于今日观看世界的方式；它无处不在，以至极难进入意识。考察二十世纪占星师的著作时，我们会反复看到这一预设。
This underlying assumption that new is better than old and future is better than past is deeply embedded in how we look at the world today, so much so that it is very difficult to become conscious of its all-pervasive scope. We will see that assumption repeatedly as we look at the writings of twentieth century astrologers.
提示作者真正要反对的是“时间自动赋值”
奥伯特并未在这里证明旧必定优于新；他要求先撤掉另一条同样未经证明的公式：只因更晚出现，所以就更真实、更文明。后面评价现代占星学时，他会不断追问：某个新解释究竟有论据，还是只借了「未来＝进步」的光环。

变化的真理观The Concept of Changing Truth

若宇宙不断演化改善，我们关于宇宙的观念似乎也必须不断演化改善。于是产生一个有趣想法：真理本身可以塑形，会随时代改变。例如，中世纪哲学家托马斯·阿奎那在其时代或许是真理思考的顶峰，如今思想却已过时老旧。它是旧的，所以不再可能为真。许多现代人很难想象：既然变化如此巨大，阿奎那怎会仍与现代相关？真理本身变得可塑、会变；新真理仅仅因为新，就优于旧真理。原书 p.60
If the universe is constantly evolving and improving, this means that our ideas about the universe must also be evolving and improving. You get the interesting notion that truth itself is malleable, and changes with the time. So, for instance, the Medieval philosopher Thomas Aquinas may have been the pinnacle of thinking about the truth for his time, but his thought is now outmoded, old-fashioned. It is old, therefore it can no longer be true. Many modern people have a hard time conceiving how Aquinas could be relevant to our modern age when so much has changed. Truth itself has become plastic, changing, and New Truth is superior to Old Truth by the very fact that it is New.
这个底层预设也弥漫在现代占星写作中。它有时不说出口，有时明说：新占星学更优越、更前沿，取代旧占星师古怪迷信的做法。现代占星师因此常觉得自己必须提出新东西、给出新视角；许多过时旧法可以丢弃，以更优越的新法取代。创新与原创性价值极高，所以占星含义在过去一百年的创新，比此前两千年还多。
This underlying assumption is also very pervasive in much modern astrology writing. There is the unspoken, and sometimes spoken, assumption that our new astrology is superior, cutting-edge, and that it supersedes the quaint and superstitious ways of the old astrologers. This also means that modern astrologers often approach their work with the idea that they have to come up with something new - that they need a fresh way of looking at things - that they can discard many of the outmoded old ways and replace them with superior new ways. There is a high value placed on innovation and originality, so you see more innovation in the meanings of astrology in the last one hundred years than in the previous two thousand.
这种变化是好事吗？若你生活在一个预设永恒进步的世界里，答案自然是肯定的。但那是一项预设，不是真理；值得察觉它，并对它提出质疑。
Is this change a positive thing? If you live in a world that assumes perpetual progress, the answer is yes. That is an assumption and not a truth, so it is worth becoming aware of and questioning that assumption.
提示“真理会进步”与“知识会增长”不是一回事
新证据可以增长，理论可以修正；作者质疑的是更强的断言：某一命题只因年代较新，就自动比旧命题更真。若不拆开这两层，对传统的评价就会变成年代排序，而不是论证比较。

神智学与东方哲学Theosophy and Eastern Philosophy

大约在达尔文的演化隐喻站稳脚跟的同时，十九世纪后期的神智学Theosophy运动兴起，主要由布拉瓦茨基夫人Madame Blavatsky开创。神智学对现代占星学影响巨大，对二十世纪占星学的「复兴」——更准确说，重新发明——尤其重要。
At around the same time that Darwin's metaphor of evolution was taking hold, we see the growth of the Theosophical movement in the late 19th century, largely pioneered by Madame Blavatsky. Theosophy has had an enormous influence on modern astrology and it was very influential in the astrology "revival", or rather the re-invention of astrology in the 20th century.
布拉瓦茨基与神智学汲取东方灵性模型；正是经由神智学，轮回、多生与业力进入我们文化的一般灵性词汇，尤其进入占星学。原书 p.61
Blavatsky and Theosophy drew on Eastern spiritual models, and it is through Theosophy that the concepts of reincarnation, multiple lives and karma became part of the spiritual vocabulary of our culture in general and astrology specifically.
布拉瓦茨基深受时代塑造，把印度教关于业力与无穷劫多生的观念，同演化、进步观念结合。单独看，印度教周期就是周期，在无尽轮中旋转；今天的观念却是混合物：周期无尽，同时还在进步、前行、扩张。神智学把印度宇宙论与达尔文结合，造出一种宗教：灵魂经历无数生命不断进步、无限扩张，最终与无限神性合一。
Blavatsky was very much a child of her times, and the Hindu concepts of karma and multiple lives across endless aeons was combined with the notion of evolution and progress. Taken by itself the Hindu concepts of cycles were just that, cyclical, and revolved in an endless wheel. What we have today is a hybrid notion of endless cycles that are also progressing, moving forward and expanding. Theosophy combined Hindu cosmology with Darwin to give us a kind of religion of ongoing spiritual progress through an endless series of lives, a process of limitless expansion which culminates in union with the Infinite Divine.
校记作者说的是现代混合观念，不是等同两套传统
奥伯特的论点是谱系性的：现代西方「灵魂逐世升级」把印度周期观与十九世纪进步论接在一起。译文保留「混合」一词，避免把业力、轮回本身说成达尔文式演化，也避免误读为所有印度传统都共享神智学版本。

正向思考与新思想运动Positive Thinking and New Thought

就在十九世纪后期同一时期——达尔文演化论撼动思想与灵性世界、神智学开始影响现代文化之时——另一现代流派也开花，强烈影响整个二十世纪直到今天。它原称新思想运动，也称基督教科学派或心灵科学；今天我们更可能把它理解为「正向思考」。该运动相信思想能影响现实——不只影响情绪或心境，还影响身体健康与福祉，乃至外部环境。
Right around this same time period of the late 1800's, when Darwin's concept of evolution was turning the intellectual and spiritual worlds on their heads, and Theosophy was beginning exert its influence on modern culture, we have the flowering of another modern school that strongly influences all of twentieth century thought up to the present day. It was originally known as New Thought, or as Christian Science or Science of Mind, and today we are more likely to conceive of it as Positive Thinking. This is the movement that is based on the idea that our thinking can effect our reality - not just our mood or state of mind, but our physical health and well-being, and even our external environment.
校记effect 当作 affect
原书 p. 61 作 our thinking can effect our reality。若取 effect 作动词，意思会是「创造出整个现实」；但后面列举影响情绪、健康与环境，句意首先是 affect「影响」。中文据上下文译「影响」，而更强的「创造现实」命题留到下一页口号处再明说。
当心智以科学技术征服世界，以演化获得更强力量与能力，又借神智学扩展到新的灵性层次，人们自然也会发现：人类思想具有塑造现实的力量。
While the mind was conquering the world technologically through science, and the mind was evolving to greater power and capability through evolution, and expanding to new spiritual levels with Theosophy, it only makes sense that there should be a parallel sense of discovery of the power of human thought to shape reality.
这表现在基督教科学派等心灵治疗运动中；表现在拿破仑·希尔《思考致富》那类主张以正确思想获得富足的运动中；表现在诺曼·文森特·皮尔《积极思考的力量》的一般正向态度中；最后走到逻辑终点，成为新时代运动的口号与核心信念：「你创造自己的现实。」原书 p.62
This was expressed through mind healing movements like Christian Science; in movements espousing prosperity through right thinking like Napoleon Hill's Think and Grow Rich; through the general positive attitude of Norman Vincent Peale's The Power of Positive Thinking, and finally through to its logical conclusion in the New Age slogan and core belief, You Create Your Own Reality.
可以看出，相信心智力量如何与演化、科学及其他进步、打破旧障碍的无限扩张、向外太空扩张，以及跨越无数生命的灵性扩张演化接合。这种对人类心智无限力量的感受，带着强烈迷恋，甚至有一种醉醺醺的眩晕。
You can see how this belief in the power of mind can tie in with evolution, with progress (scientific and otherwise), through limitless expansion smashing through previous barriers, through expansion into outer space - and through limitless spiritual expansion and evolution across a limitless series of lives. There is a great sense of infatuation, even a kind of drunken giddiness, to this sense of the limitless power of the human mind.
无论好坏，正向思考力量这一核心信念几乎渗透现代文化各方面。现代占星学也以此为显著标志：强烈强调把星盘一切作正面解释、寻找潜能；并认为个体凭努力与正向心智训练，几乎拥有无限力量，可任意发展本命盘、次限推运或行运所示的潜能。星盘只是潜能，归我们发展，任我们处置。
This core belief in the power of positive thinking pervades almost all aspects of our modern culture, for good or for ill. Not surprisingly it also is a hallmark of much modern astrology, which has a very strong emphasis on interpreting everything in the chart in a positive way, on looking for potential, and on giving virtually unlimited power to the individual human, through effort and positive mental discipline, to make what they will of the potential of the natal chart, or of progressions, or of transits. Our astrology chart is potential, and it is ours to develop and do with as we will.
提示“星盘只是潜能”背后的强命题
这句常见安慰话在作者看来并不中性。它预设：外部命运与限制最终都能被个体心智加工，星盘没有真正不可控之事。传统占星学则把天气、疾病、他人、社会地位与死亡等外部因素都纳入判断；两者分歧在于人的控制力边界。

价值与伦理Values and Ethics

若上帝已死，若再无可仰望的固定永恒天界，若真理本身能够变化演化，那么按逻辑也不再有固定永恒的道德标准。道德成为主观感受、意见与判断之事；标准也能演化、生长、改变，昨日道德律不再规范今日世界。十诫石版已经粉碎。
If God is dead, if there is no eternal fixed heaven to look up to, if truth itself can change and evolve, then logically there are no fixed and eternal moral standards either. Morality becomes a matter of subjective feeling, opinion and judgment. Moral standards can also evolve, grow and change, and yesterday's moral laws are no longer normative for today's world. The tablets of the Ten Commandments have been shattered.
价值如今被视为相对，取决于观点。再没有「真理」，只有「我的真理」和「你的真理」——我是谁、任何人又是谁，能说我的真理比你的优越？乍看这非常解放：不必再遵守过时道德标准，不再有内疚与罪；每个人都能自由选择自己的现实，成为想成为的人。原书 p.63
Values are now considered relative, and depend on your point of view. There is no longer The Truth, there is now My Truth, and Your Truth - and who am I, who is anyone to say that My Truth is superior to yours? At first glance this can seem to be very liberating. There is no more abiding by old outmoded moral standards, no more guilt and sin, and each of us is free to choose our reality for ourselves, to become who we want to be.
再说一次，乍看这宽厚而解放；但我主张，若真正想透这项信念的后果，就会发现它是现代文化最危险、最具破坏力的运动之一——危险到若推至逻辑结论，足以摧毁西方文明。此事非常重要，后文将另辟一章讨论。
Again, this can sound liberating and generous at first glance, but I maintain that, if you really think through the implications of this belief, that this is one of the most dangerous and destructive movements in modern culture - dangerous enough that, pushed to its logical conclusion, it will destroy western civilization. This is important enough that I will devote a separate chapter to this topic later in the book.
校记此处是作者的规范性判断
「足以摧毁西方文明」是奥伯特的强烈价值判断，不是译者代为认可的历史事实。依工程原则照译，不弱化语气；同时注明其论证将在第二部永恒道德律及第三部相关章节展开，读者应按全书论证评估。

思考与感受Thinking and Feeling

人的理性reason是作判断的能力：衡量、比较、对照不同观念与证据，得出结论。它是鲜明的人类特质，使我们有别于已知其他生物。在人类历史大部分时期，绝大多数人都预设有固定永恒的现实——物质、道德、灵性皆然——提供永恒指引与判断尺度。外面有一个世界，人的心智必须与之相合。
Human reason is our ability to make judgments, to weigh, compare and contrast different ideas and evidence to come to a conclusion. It is a distinctly human trait, one that sets us aside from any other known living creatures. For most of human history the overwhelming majority of humanity assumed that there is a fixed and eternal reality, physical, moral and spiritual, that provides an eternal guideline, an eternal standard to judge by. There is a World Out There that our minds have to conform to.
永恒尺度消失后，理性必须另找判断依据。理性本身无法衡量任何东西，除非有一把尺度；外部标准既无，理性便转向内在——实际判断依据成为感受与情绪。判断要紧的是「感觉正确」。这几乎颠倒传统人性模型：传统上，理性的任务是塑造、引导、培养情感生活，使之符合道德标准；标准消失后，理性反被感受塑造。世界在变，理性没有坚实地基，只剩主观感受作向导。
When there no longer is an eternal standard, then human reason needs to look somewhere for a basis to make judgments. Reason all by itself cannot measure anything unless it has something to measure by. With no external standard to look to, reason turns within - and the effective basis of judgment is now feeling, emotion. The important thing about a judgment is that it feels right. This is very nearly the inverse of the traditional model of the human, where the task of human reason is to shape, direct and grow the human emotional life to conform to moral standards. With no standards, reason is now shaped by feeling rather than the other way around. The world is changing, our reason no longer has a solid base to build on, so our only guide is our subjective feelings.
提示理性与感受的箭头倒转
作者用的是方向图：传统模型中，外在标准 → 理性 → 情感；现代模型中，情感 → 理性 → 判断。争点不是应否有情感，而是谁校准谁：「感觉对」是判断后的体验，还是判断本身的最高依据。

现代心理学Modern Psychology

前面谈过十九世纪末汇聚的几股运动：神智学、达尔文演化论、科学技术进步，以及西方文明向全球扩张。同一时期，西格蒙德·弗洛伊德的工作、无意识心灵概念与精神分析出现。弗洛伊德一度的学生兼同事卡尔·荣格进一步发展这些工作；约从二十世纪中叶起，荣格对占星学发展影响极大。原书 p.64
We already talked about some important movements all converging around the end of the 1800's - Theosophy, Darwin's concept of evolution, scientific and technological progress, and the expansion of western civilization across the globe. During this same period we have the emergence of the work of Sigmund Freud, of the concept of the unconscious mind, and of psychoanalysis. Freud's one-time student and colleague Carl Jung took this work further, and Jung's work has had an enormous influence on the development of 20th century astrology from about mid-century onwards.
对许多现代人而言，上帝死了，诸天消失了；取而代之的是人类内在心灵的无限领域。探索扩张内在空间，与向外太空扩张并行。上帝、天使与灵性世界，被集体无意识的原型archetypes取代；对上帝的崇拜被对人及人类潜能的崇拜取代。人如今是万物尺度，人的心智不承认更高力量；没有什么外部限制不能被人击碎以解放自己。现实就是人凭塑造世界之力决定它应当成为的样子。
To many in the modern world God was dead, the Heavens were gone, and they were replaced by the limitless reaches of the human mind within. The exploration and expansion of inner space paralleled the expansion into outer space. God, the angels and the spiritual world were replaced by the archetypes of the collective unconscious. The worship of God has been replaced with worship of the human and of human potential. The human being is now the measure of all things, and the human mind recognizes no superior power. There are no external restrictions that the human cannot smash through to free itself. Reality is whatever the human decides it should be by its own power to shape the world.
校记“取代”是作者的批判性谱系
荣格本人并不简单否定宗教，也常把原型用于解释宗教经验。奥伯特这里用「取代」勾勒现代文化大势，是有意尖锐的概括。译文保留其批判语气，但不把它误标为荣格本人的自我陈述。

内外断裂Inner-Outer Disconnect

现代世界观还有一个重要元素，至少可追溯到十六、十七世纪的勒内·笛卡尔——与利利、哥白尼、伽利略同一时代。传统世界中，人的心智与外部实存宇宙同属一体；我们思考世界时，思想与某个实存之物真实对应。现代世界观则使人的主观意识世界与外部物质世界断裂。
There is one more important element in the modern world view, an element that goes at least as far back as Rene Descartes in the 16th and 17th centuries, the same era as William Lilly, and of Copernicus and Galileo. In the traditional world the human mind and the outer existing universe are all of a piece, and when we think of the world, our thought has a real correspondence with something existing. In the modern worldview there is a disconnect between the subjective world of human consciousness and the external material world.
一旦断裂，一切价值判断、伦理判断便严格属于主观，只存在于人的心智。现代科学研究的外部物质世界完全没有价值；对我们而言只是感觉资料，它拥有的任何价值都是人心投射上去的。原书 p.65
With this disconnect, it now means that all value judgments and all ethical judgments are strictly subjective and exist only within the human mind. The external world of matter, the world studied by our modern science, is strictly value-free. It is mere sense data to us, and any value it has it projected onto it by the human mind.
价值只存在于人的意识，没有真实的外在所指。
Values exist only within human consciousness. They have no real external referent.
这指向传统文献与现代写作描述土星乃至整个占星学时，最醒目、最普遍的单一差别。我们早已彻底习惯这场变化，反而容易错过。
This points to the single most striking and pervasive difference in the descriptions of the meaning of Saturn, or of astrology in general, in the traditional texts compared to the modern writing. We are so thoroughly used to this change that it is easy to miss it.
传统文献的大多数归属含义，是存在于人心之外的事物——处所、动物、树木、草药、天气等等。多数现代文献的绝大多数描述，则是人的意识属性。
In the traditional texts the majority of the attributions are of things existing outside the human mind - places, animals, trees, herbs, weather, and so on. In most modern texts the overwhelming majority of the descriptions are of attributes of human consciousness.
提示全章的核心对照
前面几十页清单看似杂乱，到这里才显出方法意义：它们证明传统土星遍布外部世界。现代土星若主要变成恐惧、界限感、阴影等心理内容，改变的不只是词表，而是占星学还在不在描述一个独立于人心的世界。
现代占星师有时把旧占星学称为宿命论。我怀疑他们指的部分正是这场变化。旧占星学包括整个世界，包括各种不受我们控制的因素；许多新式现代占星学几乎完全位于心内，只谈我们可以控制之事。
You will sometimes see modern astrologers describe the old astrology as fatalistic. I suspect this change is part of what they are getting at. The old astrology includes the whole world, including all kinds of factors out of our control. Much of the new modern astrology is almost exclusively within our minds and refers only to things we can control.
现代占星学强调性格分析而非外部预测，还有法律背景。多数国家曾严禁算命；二十世纪早期最著名、最有影响力的占星思想家之一艾伦·利奥Alan Leo，就多次因违反此类法律出庭。他的应对方式之一，是把写作重心从预测转向心理与性格；这对二十世纪占星学后续发展影响巨大。
The emphasis in modern astrology on character analysis rather than external prediction also has a legal background. Most countries had strict laws against fortune telling, and Alan Leo, one of the most prominent and influential early 20th century philosophers, was brought to court multiple times for violating these laws. Part of the way Leo dealt with this was to switch the emphasis in his writing from prediction to psychology and character. That has had an enormous influence on the further development of astrology in the 20th century.
背景心理化也曾是一种法律生存策略
把星盘解释成性格倾向，不只是思想潮流，也能避开「预言具体事件」所触犯的算命禁令。心理化因此同时是理论转向、出版策略与执业策略；后来习以为常的现代风格，有一部分由外部法律压力塑成。

总结：新世界Summing Up - The New World

这份现代世界观变化的简短巡览有两个主要目的。第一，展示现代观看世界的方式在多少方面有别于传统占星学的世界；第二，也是更重要的，让现代人毫无觉察便视为当然的一些重要预设浮现到意识。原书 p.66
This brief survey of the changes in our modern way of looking at the world has a couple of main purposes. The first is to to show the many ways that our modern way of looking at the world is very different from the world of traditional astrology. Second, and more important, is to bring to mind and make conscious some of the very important assumptions that we in the modern world take for granted without being aware of them at all.
接下来考察二十世纪及以后占星师关于土星的写作时，我会集中评论作者所作的各类预设——他们很可能没有完全意识到，那些只是预设。
In the following sections, where we examine the writing about Saturn by astrologers of the twentieth century and later, I will be focusing in my comments on the kinds of assumptions the authors are making, likely without being completely aware that they are just that, assumptions.
以下现代占星师各节的主要目的不是挑错，而是把预设带进有意识的觉察。只有当我认为现代占星师不公平地把传统占星学漫画化，以便贬低或摒弃它时，我才批评他们。
The main purpose of the following sections on modern astrologers is not to find fault, it is to bring assumptions to conscious awareness. The only time I criticize modern astrologers is when I think they unfairly caricature traditional astrology in order to belittle or dismiss it.
我只收录自己钦佩的占星师；他们从各自立场出发，已经或正在做良好而有价值的工作。我不会批评或评论自己并不首先尊重的人。我也努力纳入不同流派、采用大不相同方法的例子。现代占星学的多样性极大，远超西方传统占星学此前任何时期。请注意，这里只处理西方占星学；我对所谓吠陀占星学或 Jyotish 不够熟悉，无法明智评论该传统。
I will note that I am only including astrologers that I admire, who have done or are doing good and worthwhile work from their particular point of view. I never criticize or comment on anyone who I do not first respect. Also, I am trying to include examples of astrologers from different schools, using very different approaches. There is a great deal of variety in modern astrology, far more than in any of the previous history of western traditional astrology. Note that I am dealing only with Western astrology here. I do not have enough familiarity with what is called Vedic astrology or Jyotish to comment intelligently on that tradition.
本部分将始终应用切斯特顿检验：把使用含混或听似专业之词的陈述，改用朴素、直截、日常语言重说，帮助我们看清实际说了什么，从而思考、检验。
Throughout this section I will be applying the Chesterton Test, taking statements that use vague or technical sounding words and restating them in plain, blunt ordinary language. This is to help us see what is actually being said so that we can think about it and examine it.
校记本段三处小讹
原书另有三处显然的语法或排校疏漏，中文均径改：
① p. 61 was beginning exert its influence 脱 to，当作 beginning to exert；
② p. 65 any value it has it projected 的第二个 it 当为 is；
③ p. 66 The first is to to show 衍一个 to。
提示后文的批评应怎样读
作者给出两条阅读规则：只批评自己尊重的对象；批评目标是无意识预设，不是人格。切斯特顿检验则负责把抽象好听的话降回普通语言。因此后文若语气尖锐，应先问他改写后的命题是否准确，再判断其批评是否成立。
现代占星师：二十世纪至今

马克斯·海因德尔Max Heindel

马克斯·海因德尔Max Heindel（1865—1919）是一位占星师，也是加利福尼亚州欧申赛德玫瑰十字会的创立者。海因德尔的学派是神智学基督教化之后的支脉。他讲解如何绘制星盘的著作《简明科学占星学》，在二十世纪中期广为流传，曾是许多占星师的入门教材，我也包括在内。以下引文出自他阐释占星学基本含义的《群星的讯息》。原书 p.67
Max Heindel (1865-1919) was an astrologer and the founder of the Rosicrucian Fellowship at Oceanside, California. Heindel's school was a Christianized descendant of Theosophy. His text on drawing up a chart, Simplified Scientific Astrology, was very popular in the middle 20th century and served as a starting textbook for many astrologers, including me. The quotes here are from his basic text on astrology's meaning, The Message of the Stars.
背景玫瑰十字会与神智学
海因德尔于 1909 年创立玫瑰十字会（Rosicrucian Fellowship）。这里的「玫瑰十字」不是泛指所有早期近代玫瑰十字传统，而是指他建立的现代基督教秘学组织；其教义吸收轮回、业力与灵性演化等神智学要素。
第一组文字把土星与太阳相对照，从一组对立面来理解它。请留意其中极强的道德训诫口吻，以及它如何把这颗行星置于灵魂走向无私的整体演化框架中。我们还会看到它极其强调业力与多生轮回，以至于发生在我们身上的任何坏事都成了自己的过错与所受的惩罚。神智学与二十世纪以前的土星含义中，几乎见不到这条线索；如今它却支配着现代占星学的很大一部分。这种说法还暗示：凡是发生的坏事，都源于我们内在的某种东西——这也是渗透现代占星学的另一主题。
In this first section Heindel is focusing on Saturn as opposed to the Sun, looking at opposites. Note the very strong moralistic tone, and how it puts the planet in the overall frame of the evolution of our souls towards unselfishness. We also see a very heavy emphasis on karma and multiple lives, so that anything bad that happens to us is our own fault and is punishment. We really don't see that thread in the meanings of Saturn prior to Theosophy and the twentieth century, and it now dominates much of modern astrology. This also implies that whatever bad happens is because of something within us, another theme that permeates much of modern astrology.
最后请注意，大量具体细节，也就是一项项征象归属，已经差不多消失了。解释不再那么具体，而变得更抽象、更概念化。
Finally, notice that much of the concrete detail, the listings of attributions, has pretty much dropped away. The interpretation is less concrete, more abstract, more conceptual.
生命显现为运动；但土星的关键词是「阻碍」，所以它是衰老与腐朽之星。因此，当太阳位于天秤座——土星的擢升exaltation星座——时，正值秋分，自然已经疲倦，准备进入冬眠。
Life manifests as motion; but the keynote of Saturn is Obstruction, therefore that is the planet of decrepitude and decay, and consequently when the Sun is in Libra, the sign of Saturn's exaltation, at the fall equinox, Nature is tired and ready for its wintry sleep.
年轻时，太阳的力量奔涌全身，同化与排泄保持平衡；但随着时间推移，「克罗诺斯」或土星在分泌器官中积聚阻塞，排除废物的过程逐渐受限，生命的通道被堵塞，衰老与腐朽便使生命的天平（天秤）倒向死亡之域。
In youth, when the Sun's forces surge through the frame, assimilation and excretion balance, but as time goes on, "Chronos" or Saturn accumulates obstructions in the organs of secretion, and elimination is gradually restricted, the avenues of life are dammed up, and decrepitude and decay turn the scales of life (Libra) towards the realm of death.
提示从身体阻塞到道德阻碍
这段先把衰老解释为身体通道逐渐堵塞，再把同一套「阻碍—抵抗—成熟」结构移到社会生活与灵魂成长上。它不是现代医学说明，而是以当时生理语言搭起的象征类比；天秤的「天平」又把秋分、衰老与死亡连成一幅图。
人生的其他领域也一样：太阳赋予人乐观和阳光般灿烂的微笑，使他成为社交宠儿；土星却造就隐居者，以愁容与悲观使生活变得酸涩……原书 p.68
Similarly in other departments of life; where the Sun makes the social favorite, by imbuing him with optimism and a bright sunny smile, Saturn makes recluses and sours existence with frowns and pessimism...
土星的缰绳并不舒服；被这样拴住时，我们有时会恼怒、焦躁、大发脾气。但与此同时，我们也在成熟；等阻碍解除，便更有能力拥有或运用土星所延迟之物。正如克服身体障碍能锻炼肌肉，我们也借土星在灵性上造成的阻力，培养灵魂的力量。
The leash of Saturn is not pleasant; we sometimes chafe, fret and fume while being thus held in leash; but meanwhile we are ripened and are more fitted, when the obstruction is removed, to have or use that which Saturn delayed, for as we develop physical muscle by overcoming physical obstacles, so we cultivate soul power by the resistance spiritually engendered by Saturn.
当我们记得，本命盘所显示的命定，是我们在过去诸世亲手造成的，便会明白：土星只是在本命盘上标出我们的弱点，也就是我们脆弱、容易走错的地方。弱点一旦受到诱惑而显露出来，我们若屈服，惩罚便会自动随之而来，成为自然且合乎逻辑的后果；因为每一个错误行为自身都带着惩罚的种子，会把我们犯下的错误带回意识之中。我们必须在心中清楚把握：虽然行星使人倾向某条行动路线，但作为不断演化的自我，我们理应辨别善恶，只选择善。若在这方面失败、向诱惑屈服，过犯便依自然法则应得公正报偿；土星所象征的正是这些事。
When we remember that the destiny shown by our horoscope is of our own making in past existences then we shall understand that Saturn only marks the weak spots in our horoscope, where we are vulnerable and liable to go wrong. When the weak points have been brought out through temptation, and we have yielded, the punishment follows automatically as a natural and logical sequence, for every wrong act carries in itself the seed of punishment which brings home to our consciousness the mistakes we have made. We must hold clearly before our mind's eye that though the planets incline to a certain line of action we, as evolving Egos, are supposed to discriminate between good and evil and choose only that which is good. If we fail in this respect and yield to the temptation the transgression merits a just recompense under the laws of nature and these are the things signified by Saturn.
提示“业力”在这里成了道德因果论
这不只是说行动会有后果，而是把受苦解释为过去诸世犯错所应得的惩罚。作者随后反复提醒：这种框架很容易把不幸归咎于承受不幸的人；它是海因德尔这段话的隐含伦理后果，并非传统土星征象清单中的必然结论。
机敏与圆融、方法与体系、耐心与坚忍、荣誉与贞洁、勤奋与机械才能、正义justice与公允，皆来自与土星形成良好相位之时。只有在我们违背它所代表的原则时，它才会借不利相位所生的影响惩罚我们，直到让我们双膝跪地，向天父祈求宽恕，并求得克服低等本性的力量。
Tact and diplomacy, method and system, patience and perseverance, honor and chastity, industry and mechanical ability, justice and fair-mindedness all come from Saturn when he is well-aspected and it is only when we transgress the principles for which he stands that under the influences generated by his adverse aspects he punishes us until he has brought us to our knees to pray to our Father in Heaven for forgiveness and strength to overcome our lower nature.

卢埃林·乔治Llewellyn George

卢埃林·乔治Llewellyn George（1876—1954）是卢埃林出版社的创立者。他那部卷帙浩繁的《星盘制作与释盘大全（A 至 Z）》在二十世纪大部分时间里，都是内容最全面、最实用的占星学通论教材之一。以下引文取自初版；依我看，它远胜于目前可以买到的大幅修订版。原书 p.70
Llewellyn George (1876-1954) was the founder of Llewellyn Publications. His massive textbook, A to Z Horoscope Maker and Delineator, was one of the most comprehensive and useful overall astrology texts for much of the century. The quotes here are from the original edition, which I think is much superior to the greatly revised edition currently available.
本书初版于 1910 年。卢埃林·乔治是位有趣的桥梁人物：他保留了许多旧有征象归属，同时又把它们同神智学及正向思考的取向结合起来。
This book was first published in 1910. Llewellyn George is an interesting bridge figure, and he keeps a lot of the old attributions while combining them with some of the Theosophical and positive thinking approach.
土星——有时以时间老人象征。古时它名为萨特尔（Sater），或撒但（Satan），象征邪恶、黑暗、隐秘、损失与不幸。其性质寒、干、黏液质、属地而阳性。土星若落位良好或相位良好，其人严肃、深沉、审慎、谨慎，并具卓越的组织与行政才能。但若土星弱或受克，命主容易偏执、贪得、易怒、不满而多抱怨。它主管牙齿、骨骼、脾脏、膝盖、右耳与听觉。主管砌砖、制陶、石工、水管安装，以及其他费力或不合心意的职业。它象征老人、瘦削者、神经质者、肤色深者与离群者；也象征农民、矿工、煤炭商、旧货商或农产品商，以及产业所有者。它主管土地、产业、矿山、铅和房地产交易。
Saturn - Sometimes symbolized as Father Time. In olden days Sater, or Satan was its name and it signified evil, darkness, secrecy, loss and misfortune. Its nature is cold, dry, phlegmatic, earthly and masculine. When Saturn is well placed or aspected, the person, is grave, profound, prudent, cautious and of excellent organizing and executive ability. But when he is weak or afflicted, the native is apt to be bigoted, acquisitive, irritable, discontented and complaining. He governs the teeth, bones, spleen, knees, right ear, and sense of hearing. Rules bricklaying, pottery, masonry, plumbing and other laborious or uncongenial employment. Denotes aged persons, thin, nervous, dark, seclusive. Also farmers, miners; coal, junk or produce dealers, property owners. It rules land, property, mines, lead and dealings in real estate.
这是我找到的最早一处把土星与撒但、也就是魔鬼联系起来的文字。此前诸书中，我们没有见到这种明说的联系；但它在现代土星著作中十分常见，后面几章还会反复遇到。该段其余大部分内容，仍是我们在传统文献中一再见过的那类征象归属清单。
Here we have the earliest reference I have found associating Saturn with Satan, the devil. We did not see this explicit association the earlier texts, but it is very common in modern works on Saturn, and we will note it repeatedly in the following chapters. Most of the rest of the paragraph is the sort of list of attributes we have seen repeatedly in traditional texts.
校记Saturn 并不源自 Satan
引文所说古名「Sater, or Satan」是二十世纪秘学式联想，不是可信的词源关系。拉丁 Saturnus、英语 Saturn 与希伯来语经希腊文传入的 Satan「对手、控告者」分属不同词源。此处照译乔治的说法，同时把词源判断与象征联想分开。
下面一组引文取自论土星落十二星座的一节。原书 p.71
This next set of quotes is from a section on Saturn in the 12 signs.
土星的影响通常被称作邪恶，但它在这方面大受诬蔑，因为事实上并不存在邪恶：万事最终都相互作用而成就善。土星起阻止作用；因为它给某些人生带来拒绝与不得不然，所以被视为压迫者或撒但。「凡使人充满骄傲者，必使他跌倒」；因为土星会使他屈膝，令其本性谦卑，并借限制、界限与逆境，使人思索、研究，寻找苦难的根源，好让它日后得以克服。因此，土星虽是毁灭者（毁掉虚假的理想），也是救赎者：它使心智转入内省，并激起趋向完美与胜利的努力。
The influence of Saturn is commonly called evil, and in this respect he is much maligned as in reality there is no evil, since all things work together for good ultimately. Saturn acts as a deterrent and because he brings denial and necessity into some lives, has been considered an oppressor or Satan. "He that filleth with pride will suffer a fall," for Saturn will bring him to his knees, humble his nature, and by means of restrictions, limitations and adversities, will cause the individual to ponder, study and seek to find the source of woe, that in future it may be overcome. Thus, while Saturn is a destroyer (of false ideals), he is also a redeemer, in that he brings the mind to a state of introspection and stimulates effort toward perfection and victory.
本命盘中土星落位良好且相位良好者，性情严肃务实；他们善于合理节俭，审慎、保守，具有行政才干且思想深刻，是优秀的组织者与管理者。
Persons born with Saturn well placed and aspected in their horoscope have a serious and practical nature; they are wisely economical, prudent, conservative, executive and profound, being good organizers and managers.
这一节里的卢埃林·乔治听来与刚才读到的马克斯·海因德尔非常相似。神智学那种道德化的多生轮回观点占据了主导。
In this section Llewellyn George sounds very similar to the quotes we looked at from Max Heindel. The moralistic, multiple lifetime viewpoint of Theosophy dominates.
提示旧清单与新救赎论并列
第 70 页仍按身体部位、职业、人物与物产罗列征象；第 71 页却把土星统一解释为促人成长的「毁灭者兼救赎者」。乔治的桥梁性正在这里：传统清单尚未消失，现代的灵魂进步叙事已经覆盖在它上面。

查尔斯·E. O. 卡特Charles E O Carter

查尔斯·欧内斯特·欧文·卡特Charles Ernest Owen Carter（1887—1968）是一位卓有影响的占星师与神智学者，写过一系列占星学基础著作，在二十世纪中叶影响很大。以下卡特引文与前述传统含义仍然一致；但也请注意，描述已经精简，并更多从道德与灵性目的以及心理特质的角度来组织。加粗文字出自卡特原文，属于以少数关键词概括行星含义的做法。这种进路在现代著作中非常流行。原书 p.72
Charles Ernest Owen Carter (1887-1968) was a prominent and influential astrologer and Theosophist who wrote a series of basic astrology texts that were very influential in the middle 20th century. In these quotes from Carter there is still a consistency with the previous traditional meanings, but also note that the description is streamlined, and is framed more in terms of both moral and spiritual purpose and of psychological traits. The highlighted words are Carter's, and they are along the lines of the approach that sums up the meaning of a planet in a few keywords. This approach is very popular in modern texts.
