这一天体守护固定风象的水瓶座与基本土象的摩羯座。它擢升exalted于天秤座，在那里变得更温和、更仁慈；因为土星本身是严正正义之星，凡它给予之物，必要求全数偿还。它是具体而实际的行星，使人对物质价值与所谓硬事实有鲜明感受。它强旺时，赋予人大部分可称为「自助型」的美德，如耐心、坚忍、勤奋、节俭、专注、稳固与可靠。
This body rules Aquarius, fixed air, and Capricorn, cardinal earth. It is exalted in Libra, wherein it becomes gentler and more merciful, for of itself Saturn is the planet of strict justice that demands full payment for all it bestows. It is the planet of the concrete and practical, and gives a vivid sense of material values and what are called hard facts. When strong it gives most of the virtues of what may be called the "self-help" type, such as patience, perseverance, hard work, thrift, concentration, solidity, and reliability.
它是限制之星；即便在本命盘中落位良好，深受其光线影响的命主，其见解与同情心仍容易受到限制和拘束，显得保守、谨慎而狭隘。
It is the planet of limitation, and even when well placed in the horoscope the native who comes much under its ray is apt to be limited and circumscribed in his views and sympathies, conservative, cautious, and narrow.
提示关键词化正在改写说明方式
卡特并未完全放弃传统征象，却把它们收束到「严正正义、硬事实、自助、限制」几个轴心。这样更便于记忆，也会使彼此矛盾、无法由单一心理原则推出的旧征象逐渐退场。
若按星座与相位而言落位不良，却因宫位而突出，它容易表现为吝啬、自私、责任沉重之感，以及沮丧悲观的人生观。在这一点上，可把它与三颗火性行星（我的注：太阳、火星、木星）及其典型的乐观和信念相对照——人会依自己的道德演化阶段，信上帝、好运或自己。真正的土星型人物通常相信，不经艰苦劳动便一无所得；这种信念的美德与弊病，他都兼而有之。土星代表有别于表象与虚饰的真正价值，并赋予万物长久而持恒的性质。另一方面，它限制火向外发散的活动，造成阻滞、延迟与失望，因而成为划定我们努力之明确边界的命定之星。
When ill-placed as regards sign and aspect, but prominent by house, it is apt to show meanness, selfishness, a sense of heavy responsibilities, and a despondent, sad outlook. In this respect it may be contrasted with the three fiery planets (my note - Sun, Mars, Jupiter) and their typical optimism and faith - in God, luck or themselves, according to their stage of moral evolution. The true Saturnian usually believes that nothing can be won without hard work, and he has both the virtues and vices of this belief. It is the planet of real worth, as apart from show and make-believe, and gives to all things their permanent and lasting qualities. On the other hand, by limiting the outgoing activities of fire, it causes checks, delays, and disappointments, and so becomes the planet of fate, which sets definite bounds to our efforts.
它的作用缓慢、彻底而不可避免。原书 p.73
Its action is slow, thorough, and inevitable.
术语fate 译作“命定”
这里的 fate 不是泛指人生遭遇，而是「为努力划定明确边界」的既定限度，依本书 fate／fortune／providence 的区分译作「命定」。卡特把传统外部限制转写成性格与道德成长语言，却仍保留了土星作用「不可避免」这一较强判断。

《学习占星学的唯一途径》The Only Way to Learn Astrology

以下内容摘自玛丽昂·马奇Marion March与琼·麦克埃弗斯Joan McEvers合著的一套同名教材之第一册。该系列自二十世纪七十年代起广受欢迎，至今仍在印行，也很容易买到。本书的进路完全是心理化、关键词驱动的。描述一开头便赋予土星符号一个象征含义；在传统文献中，我们没有见过这种做法。原书 p.74
This is excerpted from the first book of a series of textbooks with this title by Marion March and Joan McEvers. This was a very popular series in the 1970's and after, and is still in print and widely available. The approach in this book is thoroughly psychological and keyword driven. The description starts with line giving a symbolic meaning to the glyph of Saturn, something we did not see in the traditional texts.
符号：时间之神克罗诺斯的镰刀。
glyph; sickle of Chronos, the God of Time.
不同作者会把土星神话追溯到不同人物。在希腊神话中，克洛诺斯是瑞亚之夫、宙斯／木星之父；他与跟时间有关的克罗诺斯本是两位不同神祇，但在谈到土星时，两者后来混为一谈。这是我唯一见过把土星符号说成收割者镰刀的地方。作者没有提到，镰刀通常也与死神形象相联；据我所见，她们也没有谈土星与死亡的联系。在现代占星学的大部分领域里，土星已经基本失去了与死亡的联系。
The mythology of Saturn gets traced back to different figures by different people. In Greek mythology, Kronus, husband of Rhea and father of Zeus/Jupiter, is a different god than Chronos, related to Time, and the two were conflated when referring to Saturn. This is the one place that I have seen the Saturn glyph described as the sickle of the Reaper. They do not mention that the sickle is also typically associated with the figure of Death as the Grim Reaper, and as far as I can tell they do not mention the connection of Saturn with death. Saturn has largely lost its association with death in much of modern astrology.
背景克洛诺斯、克罗诺斯与镰刀
克洛诺斯（Kronos/Cronus）是泰坦神，克罗诺斯（Chronos）是时间的拟人；两者因名字相近，古代后期已经发生混同。镰刀本是克洛诺斯阉割乌拉诺斯及农耕收获的器物，进入「时间老人／死神」图像后又获得收割生命之义。作者关注的正是现代教材只保留「时间」，却淡去「死亡」这一筛选过程。
解剖：皮肤、骨骼系统（包括牙齿）、韧带、膝盖、左耳与听觉器官、胆囊、甲状旁腺、体内蛋白质。
anatomy: the skin, the skeletal system (including the teeth), ligaments, knees, the left ear and auditory organs, gall bladder, parathyroid glands, body protein.
代表：追求保障与安全的冲动
represents urge for security and safety
请注意，她把土星的含义浓缩成一种单一的心理冲动。回想各部传统文献中广泛的征象，真正与保障、安全直接相关的其实并不多。这里取了土星的一个面向——界线、边界——把它化为一种心理冲动：想要一个安全的地方、一条安全的界线、一处可以藏身的安全庇护所。
Notice she takes the meaning of Saturn and encapsulates it as referring to a single psychological urge. If you think back on the wide range of associations we saw in the various traditional texts, there really aren't that many that are directly related to security and safety. This is taking a single aspect of Saturn - as boundary, border - and turning it into a psychological urge to have a safe place, a safe boundary, a safe refuge to hide in.
关键词：老师
keyword the teacher
这用一个词概括土星的作用，而它与保障、安全是非常不同的概念。它抓住的是土星作为经验智慧的一条线索：在人生这所严酷学校里受教。原书 p.75
This sums up the effect of Saturn in a single word, and it is a very different concept from security and safety. This is picking up a thread of Saturn as the wisdom of experience, learning in the school of hard knocks.
土星主管形式、纪律、责任、组织、抱负、发展事业的能力、限制、悲伤与延迟。它主管理论与科学定律、老年人、深度、耐心、时机、传统、惯例、正统以及对时间的有效利用。土星代表真理、收缩、凝固、智慧与老化的原则。它的作用缓慢而持久。土星是本命盘中的监工。早先它被称为大凶星。本命盘中土星所在之处，就是你最缺乏安全感、因而倾向于过度补偿之处。
Rules form, discipline, responsibility, organization, ambition, capacity for a career, limitations, sorrows and delays. Saturn rules theories and scientific law, older persons, depth, patience, timing, tradition, conventionality, orthodoxy and productive use of time. Saturn represents the principles of truth, of contraction, of solidification, of wisdom and aging. Its action is slow and lasting. Saturn is the taskmaster of the horoscope. In earlier times it was known as the greater malefic. Where you find Saturn in the horoscope is where you feel least secure and tend to overcompensate.
这里的土星显示出对安全的欲求——但它所在之处，也是你感到不安全之处。言下之意是：只有在感到不安全的地方，你才会遭遇坏运；事实并非如此。另请注意二十世纪的特色：把行星描述为心理特质，也就是你的行为与感受方式。她没有提到腐败、腐朽、疾病与死亡，也没有提到欺骗或隐匿。土星象征中许许多多的传统面向都被省略了。
Saturn here shows a desire for security - but it is where you feel insecure. The implication here is that you only experience bad fortune where you feel insecure - and that is just not the case. Also note the 20th century characteristic of describing the planets as psychological traits, ways you act and feel. Notice that she did not mention corruption, decay, disease and death, or deceit, or hiddenness. There are many, many traditional aspects of Saturn symbolism that were omitted here.
提示“安全感”是取样，不是总纲
边界确实能导出庇护与安全，但不能自动导出矿山、铅、衰老、死亡或隐秘等整簇传统征象。作者的批评对象不是「安全感」这项含义本身，而是以它作为唯一心理钥匙，反过来筛掉无法纳入的含义。
下一节延续二十世纪后期的一项主题：让天王星与土星对立。我在此摘引天王星的含义，因为赋予天王星的这些含义，最终会深刻改变赋予土星的含义。
This next section continues the late 20th century theme of setting Uranus over against Saturn. I am quoting from the meanings attributed to Uranus as these end up profoundly changing the meanings attributed to Saturn.
（天王星）
(Uranus)
代表：追求自由的冲动（「神圣的不满」）
represents freedom urge ("divine discontent")
关键词：唤醒者
keyword the awakener
它面向未来、关怀人类、重视智识……它主管革命、反叛与自主……天王星是传统的破除者。原书 p.76
It is futuristic, humanitarian, intellectual... it governs, revolution, rebellions and autonomy... Uranus is a breaker of traditions.
天王星所反抗的传统、那些古老而邪恶的限制，最后都与土星联系起来。这组配对在现代占星学中无处不在。
The traditions that Uranus rebels against, the old evil restrictions, end up being associated with Saturn. This pairing is ubiquitous in modern astrology.
提示土星／天王星对立会反向改写土星
一旦天王星固定承担自由、未来、革命与破旧，土星便被推到另一端，承担束缚、过去、惯例与压迫。作者后面会论证：这不是仅给新行星添加含义，而是借新行星重新分配了旧行星原有的含义范围。

罗纳德·戴维森Ronald Davison

罗纳德·C. 戴维森Ronald C Davison（1914—1985）是英国著名占星师。他那些广受欢迎的占星教材，旨在采取极其简明实用的进路。原书 p.77
Ronald C Davison (1914-1985) was a prominent British astrologer. His popular astrology textbooks were designed to be very concise and practical in approach.
这里不再像传统文献那样罗列各种征象归属，而是按行星的功能来描述它们，也就是说明它们执行什么任务。还请注意，这种功能指向性格演化或完善的过程。另外，土星被解释成外在的力量，与作为内在力量的天王星相对。
Instead of the list of various attributes we saw in traditional texts, the planets are here described by their function, what job are they preforming. Also note that the function refers to a process of evolution or perfection of character. Note also Saturn is interpreted as being external, which is opposed to Uranus as internal.
校记preforming 当作 performing
原书作 what job are they preforming，依句义应为 performing「执行什么任务」。这是拼写层面的手民之误，中文径按正确词义翻译。
请注意，以下引文里所有首字母大写的词，均照原文保留。
Note that all the Capitalized Words in the Following Quotes are as in the Original Text.
土星是「考验者」（撒但），其功能是借不断的试炼完善人的品格。土星环象征土星作用强加的限制；这些限制以严厉的外在纪律发生作用，直到我们学会约束自己。
Saturn is the Tester (Satan) whose function is to perfect character through constant trials. The rings of Saturn symbolize the limitations imposed by Saturnian action that operate as a harsh external discipline until we have learned to discipline ourselves.
这里再次给一颗行星规定称号与功能。这也是我最早见到赋予土星环象征含义的地方之一。还要注意：我们会把土星体验为严厉的力量，「直到」学会自律为止。它预设，凡严酷之事都是我们的过错，起因在于我们缺乏纪律。
Again, note defining a planet as having a title and a function. This is one of the first places I have seen where Saturn's rings are given any symbolic meaning. Also note the implication that we experience Saturn as harsh UNTIL we learn to discipline ourselves. The assumption is that whatever harsh happens is our fault, due to our lack of discipline.
这是二十世纪中后期的典型进路。按这种进路，星盘中的潜能全是好的；只要做得正确，我们就注定获得好运与成功——而在做错之处，我们便受苦、遭遇坏运。公平地说，若当面用如此直白的话质问戴维森，我不确定他本人是否会这样表述；但我要坚持的是，他的描述确实含有这层意思。
This is a typical mid to late 20th century approach. In this approach the potential in the chart is all good, and we are destined for good fortune and success IF we do it right - and we suffer, and experience bad fortune, where we do it wrong. Now to be fair I question whether Davison himself would put things quite that bluntly if you confronted him with it, but I maintain that is indeed the implication of his description.
它代表「收缩」「结晶」「专注」的原则，以及施加「限制」的能力；还代表抱负、自我保存、因循惯例、谨慎、悲观、匮乏感、正直、责任、正义Justice、坚忍、稳定与耐久。
It represents the principle of Contraction, Crystallization, Concentration, the ability to impose Limits; Ambition, Self-Preservation, Conventionality, Caution, Pessimism, Sense of Lack, Integrity, Responsibility, Justice, Perseverance, Stability, Endurance.
它代表作为「永恒之结晶」的时间。因此，土星或撒但便显为上帝的敌人；上帝居于永恒之中。原书 p.78
It represents Time as a crystallization of Eternity. Saturn, or Satan, is thus shown as the enemy of God, who dwells in Eternity.
如前所述，直到二十世纪，我们才看到土星与撒但——魔鬼、上帝之敌——被画上等号。还请注意，这句话暗示时间与永恒彼此对立、互为仇敌。在传统占星学中，两者确属不同存在层级；但我认为，你不会在那里看到时间与永恒被设为敌人，而这里确有这种暗示。
As we noted previously it is only in the twentieth century that we get the equation of Saturn and Satan, the Devil, enemy of God. Also notice that the implication is that Time and Eternity are opposites, enemies. They are indeed different levels of existence in traditional astrology, but I do not think you would find time and eternity set up as enemies, and I think that is implied here.
提示“结晶”不等于“敌对”
若时间是永恒的「结晶」，两者本可理解为上下层级或显现关系；戴维森又借 Saturn／Satan 把这种差异解释成敌对。奥伯特在此抓住的，正是隐喻之间的跳步：由「不同」并不能直接推出「互为敌人」。
土星是老年之星；到了老年，生命过程便慢下来。它象征老人或严肃的人、父亲、身居负责职位者、农民、建筑工人与公务员。
Saturn is the planet of old age, where the life processes are slowed up. It signifies old or serious people, the father, those in responsible positions, farmers, builders, civil servants.
它的作用是限制、保存、考验、深化、完善、抑制、延迟、约束。
Its action is to limit, conserve, test, deepen, perfect, inhibit, delay, restrict.
它守护阴性的摩羯座，在那里，物质组织与现实抱负达到顶峰；也守护阳性的水瓶座，在那里，超然最为重要。它在巨蟹座落陷fall，在那里变得过度敏感；在狮子座也落陷，在那里其抱负漫无边际。它擢升于天秤座，显示精确的正义达到完美平衡。它在白羊座失势，在那里，急躁是「稳定」之敌。它的符号显示物质十字高悬于接受性的弯月之上，在象征意义上置于天底（同化、过去的牵引）。
It rules the negative Capricorn, where material organization and practical ambition reaches its peak, and the positive Aquarius, where detachment is paramount. Its fall is in Cancer, where it becomes hypersensitive, and Leo, where its ambition is boundless. It is exalted in Libra, showing exact Justice perfectly balanced. It is in detriment in Aries, where impetuosity is the enemy of Stability. Its glyph shows the cross of matter elevated above the crescent of receptivity, symbolically placed at the Nadir (assimilation, pull of the past.)
校记落陷与失势被对调
引文把土星说成「落陷于巨蟹、狮子，失势于白羊」，技术上对调了两类弱势：传统上土星在巨蟹与狮子为失势，在白羊为落陷。这是观点陈述中的事实错误，正文照译，不替原作者暗改。
（天王星一节）这颗行星的作用是打破土星的结晶。它代表「原创性」「灵感」「动态的自我表达」「独立」……
(Section on Uranus) This planet acts to break up the crystallizations of Saturn. It represents Originality, Inspiration, Dynamic Self-Expression, Independence...
我们的英雄天王星又来了。土星的含义根据外侧紧邻的这颗行星而被重新定义。这是含义上的深刻转变，后面的若干篇文章还会进一步讨论。原书 p.79
Here is our Hero Uranus again. The meanings of Saturn have been redefined based on this next planet out. This is a profound shift in meaning, one we will discuss further in some of the later essays.

伊莎贝尔·希基Isabel Hickey

詹姆斯·霍尔登James Holden称伊莎贝尔·希基Isabel Hickey（1903—1980）为「美国占星师、作家与教师，在波士顿地区颇有名望」。我从她的《占星学：一门宇宙科学》中摘引文字，把它作为二十世纪灵性占星学一个出色而典型的范例。原书 p.80
James Holden describes Isabel Hickey (1903-1980) as an "American astrologer, author and teacher, well-known in the Boston area." I am quoting from her book Astrology: A Cosmic Science as a good and characteristic example of 20th century spiritual astrology.
灵性占星学spiritual astrology体现了一种范围更广的二十世纪现象：不同形式的占星学与其他学科或主题结合起来。例如，有灵性占星学；有心理占星学，其中包括极受欢迎的荣格占星学；还有原型占星学、天王星占星学、演化占星学、谐波占星学。近来又有酷儿占星学、女性主义占星学、异教占星学，大概还有社会正义占星学，等等。
Spiritual astrology is an example of a wider 20th century phenomenon where we see different forms of astrology which are combined with other disciplines or subjects. For instance, there is spiritual astrology; there is psychological astrology, including the very popular Jungian astrology; there is archetypal astrology, and Uranian astrology, and evolutionary astrology, and harmonic astrology. More recently you have queer astrology, and feminist astrology, and pagan astrology, and probably social justice astrology, and so on.
在这些不同流派中，占星学本身作为一门学科的独特性消失，变成了推进其母体理论议程的工具。例如，心理占星学把荣格心理学理论置于首位，从荣格框架内部理解和解释占星学。这种做法的一个结果，是压缩占星符号，使它们适合荣格范畴；而两者并不总能妥帖相合。在本节其余部分考察若干占星师时，我们会看到，每个人都依自己的框架与议程来解释符号。
With these different schools astrology itself loses its distinctness as a discipline, and it becomes a vehicle to be used to further the agenda of its parent. For instance, psychological astrology puts the Jungian theory of psychology first, and understands and interprets astrology from within the Jungian framework. One of the things this does is to collapse the astrology symbols down so that they fit Jungian categories, and it is not always a good fit. We will see, with some of the astrologers we look at in the rest of this section, that each interprets the symbols in terms of their own framework and agenda.
提示“母体理论”如何筛选占星含义
作者并不是说跨学科结合必然无效，而是在追问解释权属于谁：若先规定一套心理或政治框架，再只保留能装入框架的征象，占星符号就不再检验理论，而只是替理论换一种语言。
灵性占星学预设一种形而上学框架，把所有行星等因素都解释为灵魂走向完全觉悟之旅的各个阶段。伊莎贝尔·希基正处在二十世纪神智学／新时代／正向思考这股潮流的中央；她的解释会清楚显示这一点。她也与后来的演化占星学和原型占星学同属一股潮流或同一条线索。
Spiritual astrology assumes a metaphysical framework, and all of the planets and so on are interpreted as phases of the soul's journey to complete enlightenment. Isabel Hickey is squarely in the middle of the theosophical/new age/positive thinking current in the 20th century, and we will see that in her interpretations. She is also in the same current or thread as the later evolutionary and archetypal astrology.
下面是她对土星符号含义的说明。请注意，为符号本身赋予象征意义，是神智学中特别鲜明的发展，很可能可以追溯到布拉瓦茨基。
Here is her description of the meaning of the glyph for Saturn. Note that giving the glyph itself a symbolic meaning is a distinctly theosophical development and likely goes back to Blavatsky.
这个符号显示显化的十字位于弯月之上；月亮或人格被十字压在下面。我们身上唯一可能被钉上十字架的部分，是黑暗的自我——人格。土星无法触及「你」——只能触及你戴着的面具。土星施加的一切约束与限制，其实都在个体自身之中。表象世界是一面镜子，我们在其中看见自己本性的倒影。原书 p.81
The symbol shows the cross of manifestation above the crescent; the Moon or personality held down by the cross. The only portion of ourselves that can be crucified is the dark self - the personality. Saturn can not touch YOU - only the mask which you wear. All restraints and limitations imposed by Saturn are really in the individual himself. The world of appearance is a mirror in which we see reflected our own nature.
这把神智学对印度教神学与正向思考的混合推进到了逻辑上的极端结论。整个世界都是个体「真我」创造的表象，所以你经历的一切限制也都是自己创造的。请留意神智学特有的明暗自我、高低自我之分：低等自我是幻象而自私，高等自我是真实而无私。吊诡的是，真实自我是无我之我；当你彻底无私时，便会获得无限自由，表达真实自我。
This is carrying the Theosophical mix of Hindu theology with positive thinking to its logical and extreme conclusion. All the world is appearance created by the individual Self, so all the restrictions you experience are created by you. Note the distinctly Theosophical division into a dark and light self, a lower and higher self, where the lower self is illusion and selfish, while the higher self is real and selfless. Paradoxically, the real self is a selfless self, and you will have limitless freedom to express your real self when you are completely unselfish.
术语YOU 与 Self 的大写
引文以大写 YOU、作者按语以大写 Self 指超越日常人格的高等「真我」，并非普通代词的强调。中文分别用引号与「真我」标出这层神智学用法；小写 personality 则译「人格」。
土星被称为诸神中最古老者；统治大地（人格）的权柄交给了这位天使。
Saturn is called the oldest of the gods and to this angel was given the rulership of the earth (the personality).
人格是黑暗自我或低等自我的另一个名称。
The personality is another name for the dark self or lower self.
……土星毫不留情地把个体自己的创造带回给他……它收集每一段经验的果实，将其筑成灵魂力量。土星在时间中建造，为永恒作准备。理解土星的使命，便是解开生命奥秘的答案。土星在自我周围筑起高墙，直到它强大得足以无需墙垣而站立。它是一股约束、收缩的影响，直到灵魂强大得足以打破人内在的自私与分离，获得自由。
...Saturn relentlessly brings back to the individual his own creation....It gathers the fruit of every experience and builds it into soul power. Saturn builds in time for eternity. In understanding the mission of Saturn lies the solution to the mystery of life. Saturn builds walls around the self until it is strong enough to stand without them. It is a constricting, contracting influence until the soul is strong enough to break down the selfishness and separateness in him and go free.
这里再次出现「你创造自己的现实」：你收获自己思想的结果。土星的限制由自私与分离造成；墙壁、规则和法则的目的，是让人最终超越它们。一旦越过，便能打破一切限制，变得无限而自由。请认真想想，猜猜是哪颗行星代表破墙与自由。
Again, you create your own reality, you harvest the results of your own thought. The limitations of Saturn are caused by selfishness and separateness. The purpose of the walls, the rules, the laws, is to eventually outgrow them. Get past those and it breaks all restrictions and you become limitless and free. Think very hard, and see if you can guess which planet represents breaking the walls and freedom.
下一段引文来自后面的一节；该节为每颗行星各给一个核心概念。原书 p.82
This next quote is from a later section where a single key concept is given for each planet
土星——考验者
SATURN - THE TESTER
土星的目标是完美。人经由考验、悲伤、延迟与匮乏的磨炼，学会生命的目的不在享乐，而在获得经验、耐心、谦卑humility、智慧与慈悲……在土星的静默里，在回顾、冥想meditation、专注、向内寻求指引以及甘愿等待之中，土星帮助我们承受自己的业力、通过人生的考验，最终使我们在演化过程中达到人人都必须达到的完美。
Saturn's goal is perfection. Through the chastening process of testing, sorrow, delay, privation, man learns the purpose of life is not pleasure but to gain experience, patience, humility, wisdom and compassion....In Saturn's silence, in retrospection, meditation, concentration, looking within for guidance, and being willing to wait, Saturn helps us to bear our karma and pass the tests of life and finally enables us to reach the perfection we all must attain in the process of evolution.
请注意这里鲜明的混合：达尔文与演化、西方进步论、神智学与印度思想及轮回，全部汇成一个朝向完美、永无止境的进步演化过程。
Note the distinct mix of Darwin and evolution, western progress, Theosophy with Hindu thought and reincarnation, all mixing to make an endless process of progress and evolution towards perfection.
到这里，希基还列出土星的基本、正面与负面特质。它们全是心理上的恶习与美德，都是人格品质；没有任何一项含义涉及人类心灵之外的事物。
At this point in the book she also gives lists of basic, positive and negative traits of Saturn. All of them are psychological vices and virtues, qualities of personality. There is no mention of any meaning regarding anything outside of the human psyche.
希基在这里谈到的观念有一部分确实成立，并且与土星处在时间与永恒的边界相连。在这个意义上，她的灵性视角确实接上了土星传统含义谱系的某些面向。后面的文章会重新拾起并发展她提到的一些主题。
There is some validity to the concepts that Hickey talks about here, and they tie in with Saturn's position at the border between time and eternity. In that sense, her spiritual perspective does connect up with some aspects of the traditional spectrum of Saturn's meaning. In later essays we will pick up and develop some of the themes she mentions.
提示作者的批评不是全盘否定
奥伯特一面反对把外部苦难一律化成内在业力，一面承认「在时间中建造、为永恒作准备」触及了本书的核心。关键区别在于：这可以是土星含义谱系中的一面，却不应吞并全部外部、物质与命定层面的征象。

齐波拉·多宾斯Zipporah Dobyns

齐波拉·波廷杰·多宾斯Zipporah Pottenger Dobyns（1921—2003）是美国现代占星师。她对小行星很感兴趣，参与编制了第一部小行星星历。她的儿子马克·波廷杰Mark Pottenger则以《美国星历》闻名。原书 p.83
Zipporah Pottenger Dobyns (1921-2003) was a modern American astrologer. She was very interested in asteroids, and was involved in the development of the first asteroid ephemeris. Her son Mark Pottenger is well known for the American Ephemeris.
我把齐波拉·多宾斯收入这篇巡礼，是因为她把自己称为十二字母体系12 Letter Alphabet、又称「邮政编码」（Zip Code）的系统编定成形；在这个系统中，星座、行星与宫位彼此等同，可以互换。它对现代占星学的影响极其广泛，几乎无处不在。现代占星师一想到第一宫，往往立刻联想到白羊座与火星。也部分由于她的影响，许多乃至大多数现代占星书都会把白羊座、火星与第一宫合为一节，继而把金牛座、金星与第二宫合为一节，依此类推。传统占星学并不建立这些联系，而是分别赋予行星、宫位与星座各自独立的含义集合。这种星座—宫位—行星对应是二十世纪的发展；像我这样从现代占星学转向传统占星学的人，往往很难打破这种自动联想。
I am choosing to include Zipporah Dobyns in this survey since she is the astrologer who codified what she called the "12 Letter Alphabet", also known as the "Zip Code", where the signs, planets and houses are identical and interchangeable. The effect of this system on modern astrology is very broad and pervasive. Almost all modern astrologers, as soon as they think of the first house, immediately think Aries and Mars. It is partly due to her influence that you will see many if not most modern astrology books organized with sections combining Aries, Mars and the First House, then Taurus, Venus and the Second House, and so on. Traditional astrology does not make these connections and gives the planets, houses and signs each distinct sets of meaning. That association of sign, house and planet is a 20th century development, and people who move from modern to traditional astrology, like myself, often have a hard time breaking that automatic association.
术语“十二字母体系”与“邮政编码”
十二组对应关系像字母表一样依次排列，故译「十二字母体系」；Zip Code 是多宾斯借本人昵称 Zip 所作的双关，也指一套快速定位含义的「编码」。为保留这一文字游戏，正文按字面译「邮政编码」，并附原文。
在多宾斯的系统中，土星只与摩羯座相联；现代占星学把这一个星座留给它守护。下一个星座水瓶座的守护权如今交给天王星；这次转换，使水瓶座与土星两者的含义都发生了深刻变化。
In Dobyns' system, Saturn is associated with Capricorn, the one sign it rules in modern astrology. The next sign, Aquarius, is now given to Uranus for rulership, and that switch makes a profound difference in meaning both for the sign Aquarius and the planet Saturn.
正如现代占星学的典型做法，多宾斯为十二字母体系中的每一个「字母」各配一个核心概念与原则，并从该原则推导行星的含义；这颗行星的其他联系则往往脱落。对多宾斯而言，土星是「法则」之星，尤其是物质法则之星。我们将会看到，现代占星学赋予土星的「法则」内涵，比传统占星学更单薄，范围也更有限。
As is typical of modern astrology, Dobyns associates each "Letter" of the 12 Letter Alphabet with a single core concept and principle. She derives the planet's meanings from that principle, and other associations with the planet tend to fall away. For Dobyns Saturn is the planet of Law, especially material law. We will see that the connotations of Law associated with Saturn in modern astrology have gotten thinner and more limited than in traditional astrology.
下面是她对这颗行星核心含义的基本说明。强调之处出自多宾斯。请注意，她说的是「土星原则」——土星如今成了核心概念或原则，不再是一颗拥有广泛、间或彼此矛盾之征象的行星。这同样是极其常见、无处不在的现代进路。土星要在她的整体系统中扮演一个角色，它的全部含义都从该角色生出；征象之间的复杂性消失了。
Here is her basic description of the planet's core meaning. Areas of emphasis are by Dobyns. Note that she speaks of 'The Saturn principle' - Saturn is a core concept or principle now rather than a planet having a wide set of sometimes contradictory associations. Again, this is a very common and pervasive modern approach. Saturn has a role to play in her overall system, and the meanings of Saturn all come out of that role. The complexity of associations is gone.
依我们目前对占星学原则的理解，土星（以及它的星座摩羯座、它在本命盘中的「自然」宫位第十宫）是各层面「法则」的钥匙。其中包括重力与时间这类「自然法则」，它们构成我们在物质宇宙中生活的框架。土星原则也包括文化规章，例如红灯停车、绿灯通行，以及按限速驾驶。它还包括执行法律的权威人物：父母、警察、工作中的上司……土星原则也包括一个人的良知、内在法则，以及未能依其限制生活时产生的罪疚感；此外还包括我们过去如何应对这些法则所带来的后果。简而言之，土星象征：若想在物质世界中生存，我们能做什么、不能做什么，以及必须做什么。原书 p.84
Based on our current state of understanding of the principles of astrology, Saturn (and its sign, Capricorn, and its 'natural' house in a horoscope - the tenth) are keys to the Law on all levels. They include 'natural laws' like gravity and time which provide the framework in which we live in this material universe. The Saturn principle also includes cultural regulations such as stopping for red lights and going through green ones and driving the speed limit. It includes authority figures who enforce the law: parents, the police, the boss on the job...The Saturn principle also includes one's conscience, one's inner law, and guilt when we fail to live according to its limits. Plus it includes the consequences of how we have handled the laws in the past. In a nutshell, Saturn symbolizes what we can do, what we can't do, and what we have to do if we want to survive in this material world.
校记“自然第十宫”属于现代对应体系
引文特意给 natural 加引号。把第一宫依次等同白羊／火星、第二宫等同金牛／金星，直至第十宫等同摩羯／土星，正是十二字母体系的现代配置；传统占星学不把星座序号直接变成宫位的「自然」守护关系。正文保留引号，避免把它写成无争议的古典规则。
这些说法都很好，确实也是土星含义中非常重要的一部分。然而，与我们对传统占星学的巡览相比，许多其他类型的含义已经丢失——例如寒冷潮湿之地、衰老与死亡，以及关节炎之类的疾病。这些都无法与「法则」概念建立联系，所以大多被舍弃。就连与土星相联的法则概念本身也收窄了，仅限于物理、文化与物质法则；土星与宗教、道德或灵性法则的联系，则不受重视或已经消失。
Now all of this is very good, and this indeed a very important part of Saturn's meaning. However, compared to our survey of traditional astrology, many other sorts of meanings have been lost - for instance, the association with cold damp places, with aging and death, with diseases like arthritis. None of those have any associations with the concept of Law, so they have been mostly dropped. Even the concept of Law associated with Saturn has become narrower and is limited to physical, cultural and material laws. The connection of Saturn with religious or moral or spiritual laws is unemphasized or lost.
这里我想摘引她对第十一字母与天王星含义的说明，因为它展示了一种现代占星学中极常见、传统占星学中却没有对应物的解释。它也会影响赋予土星的含义。
Here I want to quote from her description of the meaning of letter 11 and Uranus, since it illustrates an interpretation that is very common in modern astrology and has no parallel in traditional astrology. This also impacts the meaning given to the planet Saturn.
第十一字母包括天王星、水瓶座与第十一宫，显示超越法则、抗拒一切限制的驱力。作为生命中属风的一面，我们可以发明新技术来扩展知识；也可以支持民主、平等、自由与普遍人权。原书 p.85
Letter eleven with Uranus, Aquarius, and the eleventh house shows the drive to go beyond the laws, to resist any limits. As an air side of life we may expand knowledge by inventing new technology. We may support democracy, equality, freedom, and human rights in general.
土星、摩羯座与第十宫代表法则；天王星、水瓶座与第十一宫则驱使人超越这些法则。这里又出现我在〈旧宇宙与新宇宙〉一章谈过的对立：土星成了限制，天王星则以自由之名将其击碎。这也是新与年轻取代旧与年老，并汇入一种强调自由、成长、无限扩张与进步的世界哲学。土星的含义范围缩小了；与传统占星学相比，它的征象谱系单薄得多。这里赋予土星的含义确实良好而有效，但也遗失了很多。
Saturn, Capricorn and the tenth house represent the laws that Uranus, Aquarius and the eleventh house now drive to go beyond. Here we have the opposition I talked about in the Old and New Cosmos chapter, of Saturn being the limitations that Uranus smashes through in the name of freedom. This is also the new and young replacing the old, and it plays into a world philosophy emphasizing freedom, growth, limitless expansion and progress. Saturn's meaning has gotten smaller, its range of associations is much thinner than in traditional astrology. The meanings attributed to Saturn here are good and valid, but much has been lost.
提示单一原则的收益与代价
十二字母体系让星座、行星与宫位能用一套简洁关键词互相索引，教学上很高效；代价是三类符号原本不同的层次被压平，而无法从「物质法则」推导的传统土星征象也被判作外围信息而脱落。奥伯特在此评价的是系统化造成的选择，而非否认「法则」本身。

丽兹·格林Liz Greene

丽兹·格林Liz Greene生于 1946 年，是英国荣格派心理学家与占星师。她的著作在现代人本占星学与心理占星学领域影响深远。她的观点是：整个占星学、行星的全部象征，乃至整个人类生活，都应被视为心理过程的一部分。这一切都发生在心灵内部；外部事件只有在反映心灵内部正在发生之事时，才具有意义。人的意识是万物的尺度。原书 p.86
Liz Greene, born in 1946, is a British Jungian psychologist and astrologer. Her books have been influential in the areas of modern humanistic and psychological astrology. Her viewpoint is that all of astrology, all of the symbolism of the planets, and actually all of human life, are to be viewed as being part of a psychological process. This all takes place inside the psyche, and external events only have meaning in so far as they reflect something going on in the psyche. The human consciousness is the measure of all things.
格林的《土星：新观点与老恶魔》是她最著名的作品之一。与传统占星学相比，心理学框架在此以全新方式得到充分发展；在心理这一领域，它的确使含义更加丰富而灵活。我会大段引用并加以评论，因为我认为，这本书清楚铺陈了现代人本占星学与灵性占星学的许多预设，既展示其长处，也显示其局限。我主要批评格林的一点，是她在我看来不公允地把传统占星学画成漫画，然后一笔带过。
Greene's book, Saturn: A New Look at an Old Devil, is one of her best known works. The psychological setting is fully developed here in a new way compared to traditional astrology, and it does add a richness and flexibility of meaning in that one area. I will quote this at length and comment on it, since I think it clearly lays out many of the assumptions of modern humanistic and spiritual astrology, both in good ways and in limiting ways. The one area that I am going to be critical of Greene is where I think she unfairly caricatures and dismisses traditional astrology.
在《美女与野兽》的故事里，野兽尽管丑陋、严厉、足以激起恐惧，最后却变成英俊王子并迎娶女主人公；这似乎不知怎的很自然、很熟悉，也很合宜……野兽永远是英俊王子的黑暗面。
In the tale of Beauty and the Beast, it seems somehow right, familiar, and fitting that the Beast, for all his ugliness, his sternness, and his capacity to inspire fear, should at the end turn into the Handsome Prince and marry the heroine...The Beast is always the dark face of the Handsome Prince.
这种悖论似乎是生活中显而易见的一面……然而，这种二元并存的性质，似乎丝毫没有渗入我们的现代占星视角。依然有全然坏的坏行星与全然好的好行星……
This kind of paradox seems to be an obvious facet of life... however, this quality of duality does not seem to have permeated our modern astrological viewpoint to any degree. There are still bad planets which are wholly bad and good planets which are wholly good...
我相信，我们在此前巡礼中已经考察了足够多的传统材料，足以说明最后一句并不正确。传统占星学里确有许多极端描述，但同样经常见到混合与矛盾；所有行星都有一整套从极为正面到极为负面的表现。在传统或现代占星学中，都不存在全然坏或全然好的行星。传统行星之中，土星的含义或许最稳定地偏于负面；然而我们已经看到，即便谈土星，所考察的每一部文献也都提到一些非常强烈而正面的特征。原书 p.87
I trust we have looked at enough traditional sources in our survey to show that last sentence is incorrect. It is true that you do see a lot of extreme descriptions in traditional astrology, but just as often you see mixes, contradictions, and all of the planets have a gamut of expressions from very positive to very negative. There are no planets that are either wholly bad or wholly good, in traditional or in modern astrology. Saturn is probably the most consistently negative in meaning of the traditional planets, yet we have seen that even with Saturn there are some very strong and positive features that are also mentioned through all the texts we looked at.
现代占星学常把传统材料描绘成一幅简化的漫画，再显示现代观点如何更优越；这很不幸，却又极具代表性。它仍属于我们的现代预设：进步与演化不可避免，我们永远向前，今日的启蒙会取代过去的黑暗与迷信。这是现代世界的一个盲点。
It is unfortunately very characteristic of modern astrology to paint a simplified caricature of traditional sources and then show how the modern viewpoint is superior. Again, this is part of our modern assumption of the inevitability of progress and evolution, that we are always moving forward, and the enlightenment of the present supersedes the darkness and superstition of the past. It is a blind spot of our modern world.
提示“凶星”不等于“全然坏的行星”
传统把土星归为凶星，是在判断其通常更易造成损害，并非宣称它在每个条件下都只有负面表现。格林所批评的善恶二分确实可能存在于简化教材中；奥伯特的反驳是，她把这种简化误投射给了整个传统。
土星既象征一种心理过程，也象征某种性质或经验类型。它不只代表痛苦、限制与纪律；它还象征人人天然具有的一种心理过程：个体可以借此把痛苦、限制与纪律的经验，转化为更大的意识与成就。心理学已经表明，个体心灵内部存在一种趋向完整或完满的动机或推动力。
Saturn symbolizes a psychic process as well as a quality or kind of experience. He is not merely representative of pain, restriction and discipline; he is also a symbol of the psychic process, natural to all human beings, by which an individual may utilize the experiences of pain, restriction, and discipline as a means of greater consciousness and fulfillment. Psychology has demonstrated that there is within the individual psyche a motive or impulsion towards wholeness or completeness.
这是一个很好的洞见，也是理解土星的一条途径。它同时采取了现代心理学的立场：人生的目的是心理完整。
This is a good insight, and this is one way of understanding Saturn. It also takes the modern psychological stance that the purpose of life is psychological wholeness.
依土星所在的星座与宫位，它标示个体最可能感到自我表达受阻的生活领域，也就是最容易受挫或遭遇困难之处。
By his sign and house position Saturn denotes those areas of life in which the individual is likely to feel thwarted in his self-expression, where he is most likely to be frustrated or meet with difficulties.
请注意这里的现代预设：人生的目的在于自我表达。人类文化史如此强调个体，是非常晚近的发展，也是现代世界极具代表性的特征。
Note the modern assumption that the purpose of human life is self-expression. This emphasis on the individual is a very recent development in human cultural history, an emphasis that is very characteristic of our modern world.
许多情况下，土星似乎对应某些痛苦处境；它们看似与当事人自身的任何弱点或缺陷无关，只是「碰巧发生」，因此使这颗行星获得「业力之主」的称号。原书 p.88
In many instances Saturn seems to correspond with painful circumstances which appear not to be connected with any weakness or flaw on the part of the person himself but which merely 'happen', thereby earning the planet the title, 'Lord of Karma'."
格林把两个概念揉在了一起，我想把它们拆开来分别讨论。
Greene is merging two concepts into each other that I want to sort out and discuss separately.
业力通常含有这样的意思：某件事之所以发生在你身上，是因为过去的某项行为；这与事情只是碰巧发生截然不同。我们用命运Fortune一词来描述「事情就是发生了」这一整套观念；它确实像是人类经验的一部分。现代占星学很大一部分主张，发生在人身上的一切都是他自己的错；我认为这种观念有一些严重问题。命运观念已经基本丢失。我认为它在占星学中有其用处，后面一节还会进一步考察。
Karma usually has the connotation of something happening to you because of some past actions, and that is very different from the idea of something just happening. That whole concept of things just happening we describe with the word Fortune, and that does seem to be a part of human experience. Much of modern astrology takes the stance that everything that happens to a person is their own fault, a concept which I find has some serious problems. The concept of Fortune has largely been lost. I think it has a useful place in astrology, and I will be examining Fortune further in a later section.
术语Fortune 译作“命运”
依全书既定的 fate／fortune／providence 三分，Fortune 指不由主体意志或道德过往充分解释、却实际降临且起落无常的外部遭逢，译「命运」；不用容易被读成好兆头的「幸运」，也不并入预定边界意义较强的「命定」。第三部〈命运〉将系统展开这一词。
这种颇为消沉的评价仍附着于土星，尽管有一项最古老、始终延续的教导告诉我们：它是「门槛上的居者」，是大门钥匙的保管者；只有通过它，我们才能借自我理解最终获得自由。
This rather depressive evaluation remains attached to Saturn despite a most ancient and persistent teaching which tells us that he is the Dweller of the Threshold, the keeper of the keys to the gate, and that it is through him alone that we may achieve eventual freedom through self-understanding.
她所说的这项「最古老、始终延续的教导」，其实来自十九世纪中后期，也就是神智学兴起的时代。「门槛上的居者」一语由维多利亚时代小说家Sir Edward Bulwer-Lytton在 1842 年出版的秘学启蒙小说《扎诺尼》中创出，后来被神智学及同一时期的其他秘学著作采用。它是神智学式灵性—心理模型的典型术语；现代占星学很大一部分的理解框架正由这种模型提供。我没有发现此前存在「门槛上的居者」的用例。
This Most Ancient and Persistent Teaching that she describes comes from the mid and late 1800's, the era of Theosophy. The term 'Dweller of the Threshold' was coined by the Victorian era novelist, Sir Edward Bulwer-Lytton, in a novel called Zanoni, a story of occult initiation, which was published in 1842. The term was later taken up into Theosophy and other occult writing from that era. It is characteristic of the sort of Theosophical spiritual and psychological model that provided the framework of understanding for much of modern astrology. I have not found any reference to Dweller of the Threshold prior to that.
背景“门槛上的居者”并非古典占星称号
《扎诺尼》中的 Dweller of the Threshold 指启蒙者必须面对的可怖灵性存在，后被神智学心理化。格林把这套十九世纪秘学语言称为「最古老」教导；奥伯特在此作的是历史年代纠正，并不否定这一意象作为现代象征的解释力。
请留意这里的断言：人生以自由为目的；它是一个心理过程，并且通过自我理解来完成。这是现代占星学大部分流派的典型立场。下一段谈土星经验中的痛苦。原书 p.89
Note here the assertion that the purpose of life is freedom, it is a psychological process, and it is achieved through self-understanding. This is characteristic of much modern astrology. This next piece is referring to the pain of Saturnian experiences.
这种经验中也可能有喜悦，并不容易得到承认；然而，土星助长的并不是享受痛苦，而是获得心理自由时的振奋。
That there can be joy in this kind of experience is not so easily recognized; however, it is not enjoyment of pain which Saturn fosters, but rather the exhilaration of psychological freedom.
人生的目标再次成了自由，也就是心理自由。下一段讨论如何利用土星经验。
Again, the goal of life is freedom, psychological freedom. This next section is talking of how Saturnian experiences can be used.
对这个问题，通常的回答若不是「偶然」这种全无用处的答复，就是同样无用的业力观念：这些经验代表个体的业力，是过去某一世开始的行动或周期在今生完成……
The ordinary answer to this question, when it is not the wholly useless reply of chance, is the equally useless idea that because these experiences represent the individual's karma, the present completion of an action or cycle begun in a past incarnation..."
这句话很好地体现了一种令人遗憾的现代倾向：尚未透彻理解传统概念的意义与用处，便把它们弃置。本书后面会用专节说明，「全无用处」的偶然或命运观念实际上非常重要。把它丢掉，剩下的便是一个发生在你身上的一切都是你自己过错的世界；这种进路会造成严重问题。我也认为，「同样无用」的业力观念自有其位置，后文同样会探讨。
This statement here is a good example of the unfortunate modern tendency to take traditional concepts and dismiss them without thoroughly understanding their meaning and usefulness. I will devote a later section of this book to showing how the 'wholly useless' concept of chance or fortune is actually very important. Discard it and you are left with a world where everything that happens to you is your own fault, an approach that can cause serious problems. I also think there is a place for the 'equally useless idea' of karma, and we will explore that later also.
他并非有意识地创造这些处境；真正的推动能量是更大的自我，是完整的心灵，它在个体的展开背后发挥作用。
He does not consciously create these circumstances; it is the larger self, the total psyche, which is dynamic energy behind the individual's unfoldment.
言下之意是，发生在我们身上的一切都由自身心灵从内部驱动；因此，我们创造自己的现实，而发生的一切也都是自己的创造，于是就是自己的过错。对现代占星学的很大一部分来说，完整心灵与宇宙整体在暗中被视为同一回事。
The implication is that everything that happens to us is driven from within our own psyches, hence that we create our own reality, and thus everything that happens to us is our own creation and hence our own fault. For much of modern astrology the total psyche and the totality of the universe are implicitly identical.
我们大多数人还没有达到这样一个阶段：稠密的物质分子会听从思想号令，即刻移动……原书 p.90
The majority of us are not yet at that stage where the dense molecules of matter move instantaneously at the bidding of our thoughts...
这指的是神智学所主张的扬升大师Ascended Masters：一些高度演化、超越常人的人类存有，已经达到某种演化与觉悟阶段，世界会立即响应其思想。这种思想非常具有十九世纪末至二十世纪初的特色，而占星学现代世界观的大部分正是在那一时期成形。按这个模型，我们都会创造自己的现实；同时也在不断演化，终有一天能完全有意识地控制这一过程。这里暗示，我们正在趋向的心理自由与完整，实际上包括让现实听从我们的思想与欲望而被创造出来。
This is referring to the Theosophical claim of the existence of Ascended Masters, highly evolved human super beings, who have reached the point of evolution and enlightenment that the world instantly responds to their thought. This is very characteristic of the thinking of the late 1800s and early 1900s when much of the modern worldview of astrology took shape. In this model we all create our own reality, but we are evolving to the point where we can do it with full conscious control. The implication here is that the psychological freedom and wholeness we are evolving towards literally includes creating reality at the bidding of our thoughts and desires.
大多数人看见自己的创造通过迂回渠道，以物质现实的形式返回；表面看来，那仿佛是别人的过错……
Most people see their creations return to them as physical reality through indirect channels which appear to be someone else's fault...
她在这里直截了当地说，我们人人都从自己的心灵出发、由内向外创造现实。因为缺乏有意识的觉察与控制——因为我们尚未高度演化——这一过程往往牵涉负面事件与受苦。坏事发生时，我们看见的是自己的创造回到自己身上。
Here she flat says that we all create our own reality from our psyches, from inside out. Because of lack of conscious awareness and control - because we are not yet highly evolved - it often involves negative events and suffering. When something bad happens to us we are seeing our own creations come back to us.
提示从“心理意义”跳到“现实由心创造”
「外部事件可以映照心理过程」是解释原则；「外部事件由完整心灵制造」则是更强的因果命题。奥伯特批评格林时，主要针对后一跳及其伦理后果：它可能把受害、疾病或偶然灾祸重新归责于经历它的人。
而人一旦承担责任，通常又把它涂成黑色，称为罪；这同样是一种毫无用处的态度。
And when responsibility is taken, it is usually colored black and called sin, which is an equally useless attitude.
我认为，这是又一个把传统概念画成漫画再予以否定的例子。她误解了人类之罪的概念；在她的心理模型中，也没有任何位置可以容纳这一概念的用处。我知道罪的观念如今并不受欢迎，但人类之罪这一思想中有值得思考的有效洞见。一个不包含罪的人性观，可能陷入狂妄，夸大人类心灵的力量与价值。意识到我作为人拥有作恶的一切潜能，而我的行为有时确实出于这种恶——不论我是否意识到、是否加以控制——可以发挥非常有用、使人谦卑的作用。
I think this is another example of taking a traditional concept, caricaturing and dismissing it. She misunderstands the concept of human sin, and there is no place in her psychological model for any way it could be useful. I realize the concept of sin is out of favor these days, but there is valid insight in the idea of human sin that is worth considering. A concept of humanity that does not include sin runs the risk of hubris, inflating the power and value of the human psyche. There can be a very useful and humbling purpose in the awareness that I as a human being hold all the potential for evil inside me, and that sometimes my actions come out of that evil, whether I am aware of it or not, whether I control it or not.
人通常只看见其中的破坏性。它经常被称为邪恶，并被人格化为一种外在能量或一个名叫撒但的存在——当然，撒但与土星十分接近，连山羊摩羯座的蹄子与犄角也一应俱全。原书 p.91
Usually all that is seen is the destructiveness. It has often been termed evil and given personification as an external energy or person known as Satan - who of course is very close to Saturn, complete with the hoofs and horns of Capricorn the Goat.
把土星与撒但视为同一，是非常典型的二十世纪现象；把土星与它守护的摩羯座视为同一，也是如此。事实上，土星更常见的形象是时间老人；格林自己这本书的封面用的正是它。我想不起任何传统实例，把土星画成基督教魔鬼的标准漫画形象，带着犄角、蹄子、尾巴、干草叉和红色紧身裤。山羊与黄道星座摩羯座相联；在传统占星学中，摩羯座与土星并不等同。
The identity of Saturn and Satan is very much a 20th century phenomenon, as is identifying Saturn with the sign Capricorn that it rules. In fact, a more common image for Saturn, the one that she has on the cover of her own book, is as Father Time. I cannot think of any traditional instances where the planet Saturn is pictured as the standard caricature of the Christian Devil, with horns and hoofs, tail, pitchfork and red tights. The goat is associated with the zodiac sign Capricorn, and in traditional astrology Capricorn and Saturn are not equated.
这种意识与无意识、黑暗与光明之间的冲突，本性上既非善也非恶；它是成长所必需的，因为最终的整合与更大的意识由此产生。
The nature of this conflict between conscious and unconscious, dark and light, is neither good nor evil; it is necessary for growth because out of it comes eventual integration and greater consciousness.
请注意，这套心理模型实际上没有为道德之恶留下位置。这里不存在恶，只有无意识与黑暗；处理恶，就是整合对立面，由此扩展意识。目标是心理完整、觉察与自由。它似乎暗示：一旦我们完整、不再有任何无意识的黑暗，恶也就不复存在。
Notice that this psychological model really has no place for moral evil. There is no evil, there is only unconsciousness, darkness. Dealing with evil involves the integration of opposites leading to expanded consciousness. The goal is psychological wholeness, awareness, and freedom. The implication seems to be that once we are whole, once there is no more unconscious darkness, that there will be no more evil.
提示心理整合与道德判断是两套问题
荣格式语言询问的是：被意识排斥的内容如何整合；道德语言询问的是：行动是否伤害他人、是否应受责备。两者可以相交，却不能自动互相替代。作者认为，若把恶完全改写成「尚未整合的黑暗」，责任与外部伤害便可能被吸收到心灵成长叙事里。
丽兹·格林的著作确有一些有用洞见。对土星作用的心理解释有其效力；但放回整个传统脉络来看，它并未穷尽土星的含义。这种解释固然有用，但我认为这里严格的心理模型存在一些严重缺陷与失衡，而它们非常能代表现代文化。她在这里所作的许多预设，是我们整个文化共有的；它们已深深嵌入我们观看世界的方式，因而大多变得不可见。
There are some useful insights in Liz Greene's work. The psychological interpretation of the effect of Saturn does have validity, but as we have seen in the context of the overall tradition, it does not exhaust the meaning of Saturn. Useful as it is, I think the strictly psychological model here has some serious inadequacies and unbalances, that are very characteristic of our modern culture. A lot of the assumptions she makes here are common to our entire culture, and they are so much a part of the way we see the world that they are mostly invisible.

史蒂芬·福里斯特《内在的天空》Steven Forrest - The Inner Sky

如果你是现代占星师，一定听说过史蒂芬·福里斯特Steven Forrest。他的第一本书《内在的天空》初版于 1982 年，是最受欢迎的占星学入门教材之一。福里斯特从业多年，出版过许多著作，也通过自己的学校训练并影响了许多现代占星师。原书 p.93
If you are a modern astrologer you have heard of Steven Forrest. His first book, The Inner Sky, first published in 1982, is one of the most popular introductory textbooks of astrology. Forrest has had a long career and has published many books, and through his school he has trained and influenced many modern astrologers.
他的占星学进路完全是时代的产物，彻底现代，也彻底心理化。其中隐含着一种信念：整个宇宙、所有行星的作用都是正面的，没有一颗是来伤害人的。这非常典型地体现了二十世纪的判断，也与现代文化强烈偏向正向思考相接。
His approach to astrology is very much a product of his times and is thoroughly modern, and thoroughly psychological. There is the implicit belief that all the cosmos, all the planets, are positive in effect, none are there to hurt. That is very much a 20th century judgment, and it ties into the strong positive thinking slant of our modern culture.
请注意，下面整段引文在原书中全部受到强调。
Note that this next quoted section is all emphasized in the original text.
功能：发展自律。发展自尊。发展对自身命定的信念。与独处和解。
Function: The development of self-discipline. The development of self-respect. The development of faith in one's destiny. Making peace with solitude.
功能失调：抑郁、忧郁、犬儒、冷漠、无反应、趋时附势、苦役般的劳作、缺乏想象力、压抑情感、物质主义。
Dysfunction: Depression, melancholy, cynicism, coldness, unresponsiveness, timeserving, drudgery, lack of imagination, suppression of emotions, materialism.
关键问题：我必须在哪个生活领域学会独自行动？缺乏自律会在哪里最快给我带来悲伤？
Key Questions: In what area of life must I learn to act alone? Where will a lack of self-discipline lead most quickly to sorrow?
这些土星征象都很扎实，也与我们在传统文献中看到的相近。独处与自律是典型的土星长处，抑郁、忧郁等则是典型弱点。福里斯特把说明组织成这样一个框架：如何运用土星的长处，避免不得不经历这颗行星最糟糕的负面作用。
These attributions of Saturn are all solid and are similar to what we saw in traditional texts. Solitude and self-discipline are characteristic Saturnine strengths, and depression, melancholy and so on are characteristic weaknesses. Forrest frames the description in terms of how to use the strengths of Saturn to avoid having to experience the worst negative effects of the planet.
我梦想与抱持信念的能力，将在哪里受到最严峻的考验？
Where will my ability to dream and have faith be most severely tested?
逆行Retrograde时：根深蒂固的自给自足。可能表示一个「独来独往者」。内在力量储备极其庞大。情感上自律。可能很难说「不」。原书 p.94
When Retrograde: Deeply rooted self-sufficiency. May indicate a "loner". Enormous reserves of inner strength. Emotional self-discipline. May have a hard time saying "no".
提示从征象判断改成自我提问
福里斯特把土星写成「功能／失调／关键问题」，读者由此被引导去发展能力，而不是先问外部事件会发生什么。作者认为这些关键词本身大体可靠；他要指出的是，整个框架预设了负面结果可以靠正确的内在工作来避免。
在土星这一章稍后列完主要征象之后，我们读到下面这段总述。
A little later in the chapter on Saturn, after the listing of main attributes, we see this general description.
土星——古代占星师有时称它为撒但。大凶星。即使如今，这颗带环行星仍常被视为宇宙中的弗兰肯斯坦怪物。
Saturn - the old astrologers sometimes called it Satan. The Greater Malefic. Even now, the ringed planet is often viewed as the cosmic Frankenstein.
校记Frankenstein 所指是怪物形象
日常英语常以 Frankenstein 代称小说中被造出的怪物，严格说弗兰肯斯坦是创造它的科学家。此处语义显然是「可怖的人造怪物」，中文补出「怪物」，不把它误译成人名式比喻。
重申我前面的观点：把土星说成撒但，是二十世纪的征象归属。请想想「即使如今」这几个字：它暗示，哪怕进入现代，我们仍在犯这个古老错误；照理说，我们早该成长并越过它了。这预设进步信念，认定现代新时代优于以往诸时代；也暗示早期时代的土星含义全然负面，而「凶星」一词意味着只有伤害。
Repeating my earlier point, Saturn as Satan is a twentieth century attribution. Consider the phrase, 'Even now', which implies that even in our modern era we still make this old mistake. You would think that we would have grown past that by now. This assumes the belief in progress and that our new modern era is superior to previous ages. It also implies that, in earlier ages, the meanings of Saturn were entirely negative, and the term Malefic meant being only harmful.
它与抑郁和忧郁、失败、孤独和挫折相联。这些都是真的；若我们使土星不悦，它便以这些方式显现，描述十分准确。但那不是它的目的。没有一颗行星是来伤害我们的。
It is associated with depression and melancholia, with defeat, with loneliness and frustration. All that is true. It is an accurate description of the way Saturn manifests if we displease him. But that is not his purpose. No planet is there to hurt us.
这里暗示，土星只有在我们使它不悦时才会伤害我们。若体验到土星之怒，那是自己的过错。进一步说，只要做对事情、成功取悦土星，我们便不会遭遇那些负面作用。
The implication here is that Saturn only hurts us if we displease him. If we experience the wrath of Saturn it is our fault. This implies that, if we do things right and manage to please Saturn, that we will not experience those negative effects.
土星所教的美德，有一项高于其他一切：自律。这是理解这个符号的钥匙。土星与另一颗所谓凶星火星一样，试图使意志聚焦。
Saturn teaches one virtue above all others: self-discipline. That is the key to understanding the symbol. Like Mars, the other so-called malefic, Saturn seeks to focus the will.
为的是教会我们那门最难掌握的艺术：如何完全随心所欲。
To teach us that most elusive of arts: how to do exactly what we please.
我承认，第一次读到这句话时，我倒吸了一口气。这是一字不差的原话：生活的艺术，就是完全随心所欲。我们来到世上，是为了做任何自己想做的事。这极具二十世纪后期美国的特色，尤其带有本书问世的七十年代末、八十年代初的时代印记。据我所知，更早的历史文化没有哪个会作出这种宣言。原书 p.95
I confess that this sentence took my breath away when I first read it. This sentence is an exact quote - the art of life is to do exactly what we please. We are here on earth to do whatever we want to do. That is very characteristic of late 20th century America, and especially of the period of the late 1970's and early 1980's when this book appeared. I am not aware of any previous historical cultures that would make that statement.
如何让我们的意图支配恐惧、懒惰与情绪。
How to make our intentions dominate over our fears, our laziness, our emotions.
土星常以隐士象征，这是非常恰当的形象。它是独处之星。本命盘中土星所在之处，就是我们必须独自行动的生活领域；在那里，自给自足就是一切，我们除了自己谁也不能依靠，甚至连奖赏也必须独力给予自己。
Saturn is often symbolized by the hermit, which is a very appropriate image. This is the planet of solitude. Wherever it lies in the birth chart, we find an area of life in which we must act alone. An area where self-sufficiency is everything. An area in which we can count on no one but ourselves. Even our rewards must be supplied by us alone.
福里斯特这些引文对个体、个体的重要性与价值有着强烈强调；传统占星学中没有与之相当的东西，它却十分典型地体现了我们的现代时代。
In these quotes from Forrest there is an emphasis on the individual, and the importance and worth of the individual, that is without parallel in traditional astrology, but is characteristic of our modern era.
提示自律在此服务于“随心所欲”
传统语境也赞扬克制与坚忍，但常把它们置于义务、秩序或共同善之下；福里斯特则把自律定义成个人意图战胜内在阻力的技术。奥伯特震惊的不是「自律」本身，而是它最后所服务的人生目的被表述为「完全做自己想做的事」。

詹姆斯·希尔曼与原型占星学James Hillman and Archetypal Astrology

詹姆斯·希尔曼James Hillman（1926—2011）是一位荣格派心理学家，后来走出了自己的道路；借用维基百科页面的话，他转向了如今所谓的原型心理学archetypal psychology。希尔曼本人并非占星师，却影响极大；这里他把土星的含义作为一种心理原型来讨论。本节我想集中说明：希尔曼的进路如何鲜明展示现代世界的许多预设。他在此运用土星含义的方式，也很能代表现代占星学的大部分。原书 p.96
James Hillman (1926-2011) was a Jungian psychologist who eventually went his own direction and, to quote from the Wikipedia page, moved towards what is now called archetypal psychology. Hillman was not an astrologer himself, but he was very influential, and here he is talking about the meanings of Saturn as a psychological archetype. In this section I want to focus on how Hillman's approach vividly illustrates many of the assumptions of our modern world. The way he uses the meaning of Saturn here is characteristic of much of modern astrology.
下面这段出自Richard Tarnas所写文章的导言第 11 页，谈的是希尔曼。
This is from the introduction to the essay by Richard Tarnas p 11, talking about Hillman.
「希尔曼……运用他对土星与老者原型的洞见，揭露并颠覆那些在他看来既塑造又束缚我们文化想象力的正统。
"Hillman ...(is) using his insights into Saturn and the senex to reveal and subvert the orthodoxies he perceives as both structuring and constraining our cultural imagination.
希尔曼巧妙解构的不只是心理学领域，而是西方文明的整个宗教—哲学元结构。
Hillman deftly deconstructs not just the field of psychology but the entire religious-philosophical meta-structure of Western civilization.
无论身处何种环境，他始终是无所畏惧的异议者。」
He was always the fearless dissident, whatever the setting."
请注意这里的反叛姿态。解构意味着摧毁、拆除；颠覆也有相似意味。这里预设，「西方文明的整个宗教—哲学元结构」需要受到批判并被摧毁；它之所以邪恶，是因为它构成束缚，限制想象力。
Notice the stance of rebellion. Deconstruct means destroy, tear down. Subvert has a similar connotation. The assumption here is that "the entire religious-philosophical meta-structure of Western civilization" needs to be criticized and destroyed. It is evil because it is constraining, limiting the imagination.
校记deconstruction 不宜直接等同 destruction
这里是奥伯特对「解构」的批评性释义，正文照译其判断；但作为术语，德里达式 deconstruction 并非简单主张把对象物理摧毁，而是分析文本或概念内部赖以成立又难以稳定的对立与层级。后文的切斯特顿检验，批评的是这类技术语言在实际修辞中可能淡化「拆除旧结构」的力度。
这里预设限制是邪恶的，结构也是邪恶的；「无所畏惧的异议者」希尔曼承担起反抗它的任务。这就是反叛者天王星位于土星环之外，与作为束缚结构的土星相对。历史上，这种「无所畏惧的异议者」姿态非常流行，已成为一种现代规范；至少从十九世纪后期起，扮演无畏异议者便大受欢迎。
The assumption is that limits are evil, structure is evil. And the "fearless dissident" Hillman is taking on the task of rebelling against it. This is Uranus the rebel outside the ring of Saturn the constraining structure. Historically this stance of the "fearless dissident" is very popular and is a kind of modern norm, and being the fearless dissident has been popular since at least the late 1800's.
这其实是异端与正统相对立的语言。「异端」是一个严格的宗教术语，只有置于传统基督教脉络中才真正有意义。基督教会的阴影以如此强大的力量笼罩整个讨论，以至尼采宣告上帝已死一百多年后，人们仍在反抗它。希尔曼成了现代勇敢的马丁·路德，把九十五条论纲钉上教堂大门。原型占星学呈现为一种新教式异端——而且请注意，Protestant 一词就是「抗议者」之意；这是我们文化典型的姿态。原书 p.97
This is actually the language of heresy over against orthodoxy. Heresy is a technical religious term, and only really has meaning in the context of traditional Christianity. The shadow of the Christian church towers over this whole discussion with so much force that it is still being rebelled against over a century after Nietzsche proclaimed that God is dead. Hillman is a modern day brave Martin Luther nailing his 95 Theses to the church door. Archetypal astrology is in the form of a Protestant heresy - and note that the word Protestant just means Protester, and it is a characteristic stance of our culture.
现代文化中有一股强烈倾向，始终采取反叛、摧毁旧结构的姿态。在某些方面，我们依然生活在后基督教世界，也就是反抗那套秩序的世界。我们生活在抗议的世界。旧结构是一种有待解构、颠覆、拆除和摧毁的邪恶——而土星常被用来象征旧结构。焦点在于站到一个待摧毁结构的对面与上方。新的事物并未得到清楚界定；我认为这是有意的。未来被有意设想成尚未成形、不确定、开放无尽之物，只能被暗示、隐含、提议或迂回接近。
There is a strong streak of modern culture that is in a stance of rebellion, of destroying old structure. In some ways we still live in a post-Christian world, a world in rebellion against that order. We live in a world of protest. The old structure is an evil to be deconstructed, subverted, torn down, destroyed - and Saturn is often used as a symbol for the old structure. The focus is on being over and against a structure to be destroyed. The new is not clearly defined, and I think that is intentional. The future is intentionally conceived as unformed, indefinite, open-ended. It is hinted at, implied, suggested, approached sideways.
这是「摆脱」限制的自由。这是现代外行星以及土星之外无限空间所代表的开放无尽、没有形式的世界。它是天王星——土星之外紧邻的行星——的姿态；我们在现代占星学里一再看见这组含义配对。若用原型来谈，我会把它看成反叛的儿童与青少年，站到年长父母和祖先的对面。
This is freedom FROM restriction. This is the open-ended, formless world of the modern outer planets and infinite space past Saturn. It is the stance of Uranus, the planet just past Saturn, and we see that pairing of meanings repeatedly in modern astrology. If you want to talk of archetypes, I think of it as the stance of the rebellious child and teenager over against the elder parent and ancestors.
下一节中，希尔曼谈到 senex 与 puer 两种原型。用普通话说，senex（老者）senex指很年长的人，puer（少年）puer指儿童。为使含义清楚，我在评论中会使用普通对应词，而不用拉丁技术术语。下一段中的强调为我所加。
In this next section Hillman talks of senex and puer archetypes. In plain English, senex means very old person, and puer means child. In my comments I will use the plain equivalents rather than the Latin technical terms to clarify the meanings. The emphasis in this next paragraph is mine.
「我们文化中上帝的主要形象：全知、全能、端坐而蓄须，依正义、道德与秩序的抽象原则实施统治……我们文化的至高神是一位老者之神……」原书 p.98
"the main image of God in our culture: omniscient, omnipotent, seated and bearded, a ruler through abstract principle of justice, morality and order...The high God of our culture is a senex god..."
希尔曼认出了西方犹太—基督教文明的传统结构，也认出了传统基督教上帝与世界观的整个传统结构；他把它概括为老者原型：一位衰老、年迈而垂死的人。请注意，这是我唯一见过把土星说成全知全能的地方。我认为，全知全能指的是他在此等同于老人的基督教上帝，而不是把这两项属性直接赋予土星。
Hillman is identifying the traditional structure of Western Judeo-Christian civilization, and all of the traditional structure of the traditional Christian God and world view, and summing it up by identifying it with the senex, a senile, old and dying man. Note that this is the first and only reference I have ever seen to Saturn as being omniscient and omnipotent. I think that reference is to the Christian God he is identifying with an old man here, rather than relating that to Saturn.
「因为这一原型表达一切古老、有序、已经确立之物，所以它与我们的文化，以及文化中那位据称已死或垂死的上帝，尤其相关。」
"Because this archetype expresses all that is old, ordered and established, it has particular bearing upon our culture and its supposedly dead or dying God."
「结构的崩溃，就是这一特殊结构的死亡；它本身便是『结构原则』。」
"The breakdown of structure is the death of this particular structure, who is the Principle of Structure".
如今，土星与一切结构的崩溃、与「结构原则」本身的崩溃等同起来。结构是土星的核心含义之一。
Now Saturn is identified with the breakdown of all structure, of the Principle of Structure itself. Structure is one of the core meanings of Saturn.
「若我们宗教意识的中心执念已经老化，退入遥远的超越领域，在那里枯萎死去，那么，映照这位主神、我们自己曾被铸成的形象也正在消逝……文化的『解—构』与个人生命的崩溃，都源于老者原型居支配地位的阶段正在过渡……」
"If the central fixation of our religious consciousness has aged into remote transcendence, there to wither and die, then the image in which we have been cast, reflecting this main god, is also passing away... The 'de-struction' of culture and the breakdowns in individual lives result from the transition of the senex dominant..."
「希腊人称为克洛诺斯、罗马人称为萨图尔努斯者，我们的传统则将其作为『我们在天上的父』来敬拜。但它已离开天国，天国本身也已消失；如今，老者原型最好经由心理现象学、以间接方式遭遇。」
"What the Greeks called Kronos, and the Romans Saturn, our tradition has worshiped as 'Our Father which art in Heaven.' But gone from Heaven, and Heaven gone too, the senex now can be best encountered indirectly, through psychological phenomenology."
传统占星师并不把土星等同于基督教的父神形象。我认为，他是在说西方文明的结构正在崩解死亡，或许已经枯萎而死。「结构原则」本身正在坍塌；固定真理不复存在，一切都是变化与流动。原书 p.99
Traditional astrologers do not equate Saturn with the Christian father God image. I think he is saying that the structure of Western civilization is breaking down and dying, or may already be withered and dead. The Principle of Structure itself is collapsing. There are no fixed truths anymore, and all is change and flux.
从隐喻上说，希尔曼提出了一个重要观点：我们已经砸碎父亲的房屋。静止秩序与天上大教堂已经消失，混沌与变化之风正从外面灌入。我们生活在后基督教世界的废墟上。我认为，他在这里抓住了现代世界极为重要的一面。
Metaphorically Hillman is making an important point - we've smashed the house of the Father. The static order, the cathedral of the heavens, is gone, and the winds of chaos and change are blowing in from the outside. We are living in the ruins of a post-Christian world. I think he catches something very important about our modern world here.
至此，希尔曼的引文结束。下面我想谈谈，他在这里表现得如此鲜明清楚的世界观转变。
That is the end of the quotes from Hillman. I want to talk about the change in worldview that Hillman is so vividly and clearly illustrating here.
我们文化所采取的隐喻姿态已经改变。哥特式大教堂高耸入天的尖顶，变形为摩天大楼，带着一种宏伟、削尽装饰的矩形朴素。哥特式教堂仰望上帝；摩天大楼却是人类升向天际，不承认高于自身的任何东西，更像一座巴别塔。
The metaphoric stance of our culture has changed. The spire of the Gothic cathedral soaring up into the heavens has morphed into the Skyscraper, with a sort of magnificent stripped down rectangular plainness. The Gothic church aspires to God, the skyscraper is humanity ascending to the heavens, acknowledging nothing higher than itself, more like a tower of Babel.
让我们在这里应用切斯特顿检验。「destruction（摧毁）」被拆写成「de-struction（解—构）」；一方面是为了显示它意味着拆解结构，另一方面也为了削弱这个词的具体含义与情感冲击。它看起来比原词更抽象、更不具威胁——仿佛在说：我不是指 destruction，我是指 de-struction。说「解构一座建筑」，听起来远没有「摧毁它」那么吓人。
Let's apply the Chesterton Test here. The word "destruction" is written as "de-struction" partly to illustrate it means to break down structure, and also because it defuses some of the concrete meaning and emotional charge of the word. It looks more abstract and less threatening than just using the word itself - something like, I didn't mean destruction, I meant de-struction. To Deconstruct a building sounds a lot less threatening than to destroy it.
若「结构原则」已经枯萎而死，留下的就是开放无尽的无秩序，以及新事物的潜能。若真正审视他所用的那些富于召唤力的技术术语，他似乎是在说：传统西方文明该被摧毁、该坍塌了。被解—构、被解构，就是被拆倒、被撕成碎片。
If the Principle of Structure is withered and dead then what remains is open-ended lack of order, a potential for the new. If we really look at the evocative technical terms he uses, he seems to be saying that it is time for traditional western civilization to be destroyed, to collapse. De-structed, deconstructed, means torn down, ripped to pieces.
上帝已不在天国，天国本身也消失了。「结构原则」已经消失。剩下的是心智中的意象——原型。这些心中意象没有外部指涉物，也没有裁判标准；它开放无尽、充满潜能，却没有固定结构。既然宇宙中外在的「结构原则」已经坍塌——换句话说，既然上帝已死——唯一能寻找意义之处、诸神唯一仍然居住之处，就是我们心内。与现代唯物科学相同，外在的物质宇宙没有价值，也没有心智强加于它之外的固有意义。原书 p.100
God is no longer in Heaven, and Heaven itself is gone. The Principle of Structure is gone. What we are left with, is images in the mind - archetypes. And, these are images in the mind with no external referent or judging standard. It is open ended, it has potential, there is no fixed structure. Now that external Principle of Structure in the Universe has collapsed - or, in other words, now that God is dead - the only place to look for meaning, the only place where "the gods" still live, is within our minds. As with modern materialistic science, the external universe of matter is value-free, and has no inherent meaning other than what our minds impose on it.
这套模型在解释占星学为何可能有效时存在一个问题。若诸神只存在于心智之中，周围天上物质天体的运动便没有真正理由会与人生中任何有意义之事相关。如此一来，除个人投射外，占星学含义便失去了立足之地；世俗占星学或世界占星学为何还能具有任何意义，也变得很难讲通。
There is a problem here with this model in explaining how astrology can be valid. If the gods exist only in our minds, then there is really no reason why there should be any correlation between the movement of physical bodies in the heavens around us, and anything at all of meaning in our lives. It cuts the ground out from under any meaning to astrology other than as personal projection. It also makes it very difficult to make sense of mundane or world astrology having any significance at all.
提示心理原型模型的“相关性难题”
若原型只是个体或集体心中的意象，天体运动与人生事件为何同步，仍需另一个连接原则。原型占星学也可以主张心灵与宇宙共享同一结构；奥伯特此处批评的是把原型完全内置于心智、又继续使用外部天象时产生的解释缺口。
希尔曼说，我们文化的至高神是一位「老者之神」。既然 senex 指老人，无论男女，那么若我们生活在老者文化中，它就应当高度尊崇老者价值；这意味着老年受到尊重，前代传统也受到尊重并传承下来。
Hillman speaks of the high god of our culture as being a "senex god". Given that senex means old man or woman, if we live in a senex culture then it should be a culture in which senex values are highly honored and respected. This would mean that old age is honored and respected, and that the tradition of previous generations is respected and passed down.
我要坚持，我们当下的文化恰恰与老者文化相反。我们的文化崇拜青春而非年老，崇拜新而非旧。希尔曼远非勇敢地反叛当下文化；我认为，他非常典型地体现了它。
I maintain that our current culture is the exact opposite of a senex culture. Our culture worships youth and not age, the new rather than the old. Far from being in brave rebellion against our current culture, I think Hillman is very typical of it.
我们的文化崇拜青春、崇拜新事物，轻蔑贬低旧事物，并相信永恒进步。当下文化很大一部分采取一种总体上的青春反叛姿态，反对被视为过时、压迫、僵化、枯萎而垂死的传统。我们文化的一般姿态珍视自由，而这是一种「摆脱」一切限制的自由，是走向无限扩张的运动：打破一切限制，隐喻地扩展到无边的外太空，或无边的内在心理空间。
We are in a culture that worships youth, that worships the new, that dismisses and denigrates the old, and that believes in perpetual progress. Much of our current culture is in a general stance of youthful rebellion over against a tradition that is perceived as outmoded, repressive, structured, withering and dying. The general stance of our culture values freedom, and it is freedom FROM all restriction, a movement towards limitless expansion. It is a breakdown of all restriction to expand metaphorically into limitless outer space, or limitless inner psychological space.
本章最后补充一点：我在 2019 年 9 月写完此书。写作此刻，新闻中有几个非常醒目的例子，都是年幼儿童成为发言人、权威或重要讯息的传递者。还有一些儿童成群参与示威与集会，也有几位年轻人获得报纸头版的突出报道，就当前议题发表意见。未说出口的预设是：我们恰恰因为他们是孩子，所以应该听从。他们是未来，是新的权威，因此他们的声音远比老人更有分量；老人的时代已经过去，现在应当让位，让年轻人接管。原书 p.101
As a final note to this chapter - I am finishing up this book in September 2019. As I write this, there are a couple of very prominent examples in the news of young children being spokespeople, or authorities, or bearers of important messages. There are some examples of children gathering as groups in demonstrations and rallies, and there are a few young people being given prominent front page news coverage to have their say on current issues. The unspoken assumption is that we are supposed to listen to them precisely because they are children. They are the future, they are the new authorities, so their voice weighs far heavier than that of old people, whose time is past and should now step aside and let the young take over.
我们并不生活在老者文化中，恰恰相反。我们生活在崇拜年轻与新事物、贬低老旧过时之物的文化中。勇敢的年轻反叛者成了新常态，异端与抗议者成了新的正统。
We do not live in a senex culture; quite the opposite. We live in a culture that worships the young and the new, and devalues the old and outmoded and old fashioned. The brave young rebels are the new normal, and the heretics and protesters are the new orthodoxy.

当代原型占星学Current Archetypal Astrology

这里的引文出自伦恩·巴特勒Renn Butler的一部当代著作《通往完整之路：原型占星学与超个人旅程》。他采用一种称为超个人心理学Transpersonal Psychology的模型，把荣格原型同对「非常意识状态」及神秘合一状态的探索结合起来。他承接斯坦尼斯拉夫·格罗夫Stanislaus Grof等人的工作；而背景中还有一位未明说、未注明的幽灵——六十年代的内在空间探索者蒂莫西·利里Timothy Leary。原书 p.102
The quotes here are from a contemporary book by Renn Butler named, Pathways to Wholeness: Archetypal Astrology and the Transpersonal Journey. He is working in a model called Transpersonal Psychology which integrates Jungian archetypes with exploration into "non-ordinary states of mind", mystical states of oneness. He is building on the work of people like Stanislaus Grof, and unstated and uncredited in the background is the specter of Timothy Leary, the explorer of inner space from the Sixties.
校记Stanislaus Grof 的姓名形式
原书作 Stanislaus Grof；这位超个人心理学家的通行姓名是 Stanislav Grof，中文常译「斯坦尼斯拉夫·格罗夫」。正文中文按通行名，英文栏保留原书字样。利里则因迷幻药与意识探索而成为六十年代反文化象征。
本书非常明确地说明了整体宇宙论；它基本沿用同一组预设：从丹恩·鲁伊尔、神智学，经丽兹·格林与荣格占星学，一直延伸到较晚近的演化占星学。
This book is very explicit about the overall cosmology, and it is basically the same set of assumptions we see from Dane Rudhyar and Theosophy, through Liz Greene and Jungian astrology, to our more recent Evolutionary astrology.
在知觉领域，土星通过「趋物」的意识模式显现，也就是「朝向物质」；它是「趋整」或「走向完整」原则的自然补足。
In the realm of perception, Saturn manifests through the hylotropic or "moving toward matter" mode of awareness - the natural complement to the holotropic or "moving toward wholeness" principle.
术语hylotropic／holotropic
hylotropic 由希腊语 hylē「物质」与「转向」构成，译「趋物」；holotropic 取「趋向整体／完整」之义，译「趋整」。二者是格罗夫意识模型中的成对用语：前者聚焦日常物质现实与分立个体，后者指超越个体边界、趋向整体的非常意识经验。
既然完整被定义为善，是整个过程的终极目标，那么土星按定义便恰好站在它的反面。
Since wholeness is defined as the good, the ultimate goal of the process, then Saturn is by definition the exact opposite.
为了创造物质宇宙，「绝对意识」把自身的一些部分分离出来，成为时空中彼此分立、界限清楚的实体……随着「绝对者」化身并细分为越来越多样的实体，分离与自我遗忘的过程也随之发生。
In order to create the physical universe, the Absolute Consciousness splits off parts of itself into separate, distinct entities in time and space...As the Absolute incarnates and subdivides into more and more diverse entities a process of separation and self-forgetting occurs.
土星代表这种划分与分离原则。它是收缩之力，使物质世界中许多彼此分立的意识单位忘记自己的真实身份。宇宙中的每一种形式，无论是原型、无生命物体还是生物，都曾经是、如今也仍是更大宇宙场域的一部分——因此，灵性旅程常被称为觉醒或真我实现……
Saturn represents this dividing and separating principle. It is the force of contraction that causes the many separate units of awareness in the material world to forget who they really are. Every form in the universe, whether an archetype, an inanimate object, or a living being was once, and still is, a part of this greater cosmic field - which is why the spiritual journey is often referred to as awakening or Self-realization...
请注意，原型被列为一个「意识单位」；我认为，这意指具有意识的活存有。原型一词实际上承担了过去所谓神、天使或魔灵的位置。旧宇宙被占星学的主观宇宙取代——然而，他又在这里强烈暗示，原型以某种方式拥有自身独立的意识存在。过去是天使居于上帝心中，如今则是原型居于「绝对意识」场域。原书 p.103
Notice that archetype is listed as a "unit of awareness", which I think means a conscious living being. The term archetype effectively means what used to be called a god, an angel, or a demon. The old cosmos has been replaced with the subjective cosmos of astrology - and yet he is strongly implying here that archetypes have some kind of conscious existence in their own right. Instead of Angels in the mind of God, we have Archetypes in the field of Absolute Consciousness.
下一组引文中的强调为我所加，因为它概括了现代占星学的许多特点。
The emphasis in this next section of quotes is mine, as it sums up a lot of modern astrology.
化身的过程如此痛苦，以至我们忘记了自己的来处；灵魂最高的渴望，就是重新获得这份失落的完整，记起自己的真实身份。这种觉醒过程由天王星、海王星与冥王星三种原型以不同方式象征。
The process of incarnation is so painful that we forget where we came from, and the highest yearning of our soul is to regain this lost wholeness and remember who we really are. This awakening process is symbolized in different ways by the archetypes Uranus, Neptune, and Pluto.
太阳系作为人类觉醒过程的地图而存在。我们个人的人类心理，以放大形式写在天上。细想便会发现，这是一种高度以人为中心的宇宙论；它把人类意识演化投射到苍穹的形状中。不再是人类依上帝形象受造，而是宇宙依人类心理过程的形象受造。
The solar systems exists as a map of the human awakening process. Our personal human psychology is writ large in the heavens. When you think of it, this is a very human-centered form of cosmology, and it projects our human conscious evolution out into the shape of the heavens. Instead of humanity being created in the image of God, we have the cosmos created in the image of human psychological process.
宇宙依人类心灵的形象存在。我们把自己向外投射到天上；人的意识扩展到无限，吞没整个宇宙。
The cosmos exists in the image of the human psyche. We project ourselves out into the heavens. Human consciousness expands to infinity and engulfs the entire cosmos.
提示“如上在下”被倒转成人类中心论
传统宇宙论常说人对应宇宙秩序；这里则把太阳系直接说成人类觉醒过程的地图。奥伯特认为方向发生了倒转：不再是人的心灵参与更大的宇宙格局，而是宇宙成为人的心理历程之放大投影。
整合良好的土星原则，会赋予人责任感、谦卑与耐心。他们会安然生活在自然的物质边界之内，同时认识到意识本身没有边界……［土星的］功课帮助人培养纪律、耐力与坚毅，最终使他们摆脱土星对人类心智无意识的压迫。
A well-integrated Saturn principle confers in people feelings of responsibility, humility, and patience. They will live comfortably within nature's material boundaries while recognizing that consciousness itself is without boundaries...[Saturn's] lessons help people to cultivate the discipline, endurance, and fortitude that can ultimately liberate them from Saturn's unconscious oppression over the human mind.
我们再次遇到极具现代特色的语言：通过越过限制性结构，从压迫中获得解放。最终目标是认识到意识没有边界。任何结构或边界都是无意识压迫的来源，会把心智围困起来。我认为，这组引文清楚说出了现代占星学大部分内容背后的许多预设。原书 p.104
Again we have that characteristically modern language, talking of liberation from oppression by getting past restricting structures. The final goal is a realization of consciousness without boundaries. Any sort of structure or boundary is a source of unconscious oppression, it hems in our minds. I think this series of quotes clearly spells out a lot of the assumptions behind much of modern astrology.

天王星占星学及其后继者Uranian Astrology & its Descendants

这里我要把三位拥有共同传承与进路的占星师合在一起讨论。他们都高度强调数学、几何与相位，也都强调占星学科学、预测的一面。原书 p.105
I'm going to group three astrologers together here who share a common heritage and approach. All heavily emphasize mathematics, geometry and aspects, and all emphasize the scientific and predictive side of astrology.
此外，这几位占星师都把每颗行星提炼成一个核心概念或原则，再从这些原则如何组合来作解释。他们不用元素性质，也不用星座守护权或任何神话性质。解释大多涉及具体事件与人物。所有这些含义体系都非常适合现实的星盘解读，值得学习与深思。与传统征象对应表相比，它们缺少广泛的具体联系；但也有一种简洁优雅，能依脉络生成特定含义。根据我的经验，这些体系全都非常好用。
Also, each of these astrologers has distilled each of the planets down to a core concept or principle, and the interpretation comes from how these principles combine. The elemental qualities are not used, nor are sign rulerships, nor are any mythical qualities. The interpretations are mostly concrete, specific events and people. All of these systems of meaning work very well with realistic chart reading, and they are well worth learning and pondering. While they lack the broad range of concrete associations of the traditional correspondence lists, they have a kind of elegance that can generate specific meanings by context. I know from experience that all of these systems work very well.
这种赋予行星少数核心概念、关键词与原则的含义进路，在现代占星学中已经非常流行。天王星学派及其后继者的特点在于，其解释往往扎实、具体、明确，不像人本或心理占星师的关键词清单那样含糊，或只提供心理暗示。
This kind of approach to the meanings of planets, giving a few core concepts, along with key words and principles, has become very popular in modern astrology. What marks the Uranian school, and their followers, is that they tend to be solid, concrete, specific, and are not as vague or psychologically suggestive as keyword lists by humanistic or psychological astrologers.
提示同是“关键词”，组合规则不同
前几节的关键词主要把行星化为心理功能；这里的核心原则则像运算元，与另一颗行星、点位或几何结构组合，生成事件层面的具体判断。作者肯定的正是这种可组合性，同时提醒它仍舍弃了传统对应表的部分名物丰富度。

阿尔弗雷德·维特——天王星占星学Alfred Witte - Uranian Astrology

天王星占星学Uranian astrology是三者中最早的学派，二十世纪二十年代兴起于德国。阿尔弗雷德·维特Alfred Witte是一位天才；他想出用刻度盘——尤其是九十度盘——来处理星盘，从而轻易聚焦于重要的硬相位。
Uranian astrology, the earliest school of the three, arose in Germany in the 1920's. Witte is the genius who came up with working with charts on dials, especially the 90 degree dial, as a way to easily focus in on important hard aspects.
术语九十度盘与硬相位
九十度盘把黄道四个九十度区段折叠到同一圆盘，合相、四分相与对分相因而可在相近刻度直接比较；这些通常造成较强张力的相位统称「硬相位」。这是一种几何检索工具，不是另立一套星座。
他的主要参考教材《行星图式规则》，依据几何配置，把两颗或三颗行星的含义相互组合。
His main reference textbook, Rules for Planetary Pictures, combines the meanings of the planets in twos and threes with each other based on geometric configurations.
这里摘录他赋予土星的一些含义。他先给出独立定义，再详述土星如何与其他传统行星组合。维特先取这些行星对，然后说明它们又如何同第三颗行星或点位组合。行星与点位名称的缩写是他文风的特色之一，我会原样保留。原书 p.106
I am here giving an excerpt of his meanings of Saturn. First he gives a stand-alone definition, and then details how Saturn combines with the other traditional planets. Witte takes these pairs and then shows how they in turn combine with a third planet or point. I am keeping his abbreviations of the planet and point names as they are a characteristic part of his style.
Sat（土星）——限制。套上缰绳。妨碍。分离。损失。
Saturn - Restriction. To bridle. To hinder. Separations. Losses.
Sat–Asc（土星—上升点）——与他人分离。疏远。遭到拒绝。受到冷遇。
Sat - Asc - To be separated from other people. Estrangement. To experience refusal. Given the cold shoulder.
Sat–MC（土星—天顶）——拖慢或妨碍他人。感到被遗弃。感到负担。接手一项职责。义务。抑郁、受抑。
Sat - MC - To slow down or hinder others. To feel deserted. To feel a burden. To take over a duty. Obligations. Depression, inhibition.
Sat–Sun（土星—太阳）——告别。身体分离。老人及老年。严肃的人。个人与身体上的阻碍及困难。
Sat - Sun - To take leave. Bodily separations. Old man and old age. The serious man. Personal and physical hindrances and difficulties.
Sat–Moon（土星—月亮）——自我保留。避开女性。不满。与公众分离。约束、分开。绝望。心情抑郁。年老、单身、分居或不幸福的女性。寡妇。不得不独处的时辰。傍晚时分。
Sat - Moon - Self-reserve. To avoid women. Discontented. To be separated from the public. Restrain, separate. Despair. Depressed mind. Old, single, separated or unhappy woman. Widow. The hours one has to be alone. Evening hours.
Sat–Merc（土星—水星）——分离之念。旅行。逻辑。哲学思考。形成意见、作出判断的能力。思量。斟酌。
Sat - Merc - Thoughts of separation. Travels. Logic. Philosophical thinking. Competence to form an opinion, to pass judgment. To ponder. Deliberation.
Sat–Ven（土星—金星）——爱情分离。和谐中断。情意受阻。短暂爱意。爱情的悲伤。非婚生子女。
Sat - Ven - Separation of love. Interrupted harmony. Hindered inclinations. Passing affection. Love's sorrow. Illegitimate birth.
Sat–Mars（土星—火星）——葬仪（丧葬礼拜）。葬礼。周期性工作。（火星代表劳动、工作。）工作中断。疾病。分离行动。死亡。
Sat - Mars - Obsequies (Funeral services). Funeral. Periodic work. (Mars as labor, work.) Interruption of work. Sicknesses. Action of Separation. Death.
Sat–Jup（土星—木星）——没有好运。金钱损失。房地产损失。「高寿」。成功无常。成功分离。与好运分开。建筑物。变更住所。原书 p.107
Sat - Jup - To have no luck. Money losses. Losses of real estate. "Ripe old age." Inconstant success. Successful separation. Separated from luck. Buildings. Changes of residence.

莱因霍尔德·埃伯廷——宇宙生物学Reinhold Ebertin - Cosmobiology

同为德国人的莱因霍尔德·埃伯廷Reinhold Ebertin原是维特的同事，后来另创体系；这可说是天王星占星学的精简版本，保留了对相位与刻度盘技术的强调。埃伯廷还大量运用中点，将其视为行星能量相互结合的位置。他的主要参考著作《星体影响组合》流传甚广、影响深远，也是我个人最喜欢的占星著作之一；我认为书中的含义与组合非常实用、合乎逻辑且富于启发。
Ebertin, also German, was originally a colleague of Witte's who went off and formed his own system, which is a kind of streamlined version of Uranian, keeping the emphasis on aspects and dial work. Ebertin also worked heavily with midpoints as places where the energies of planets combined. His book, The Combination of Stellar Influences, is his main reference work and has been widely influential. It is one of my personal favorite astrology texts, and I find its meanings and combinations to be very useful, logical and suggestive.
与维特的天王星占星学教材一样，埃伯廷先把行星成对考察，再用表格说明这一对如何与第三颗行星互动。我先列土星本身的主要定义，再举土星加水星为例。埃伯廷用加号与减号（＋、－）表示正面与负面解释，C 则表示介于两者之间。
As in the Witte's Uranian textbook, Ebertin looks at the planets in pairs, and then gives tables of how those pairs would interact with a third planet. I will give the main definitions of Saturn by itself, then an example of Saturn plus the planet Mercury. The plus and minus signs (+ and -) are used by Ebertin meaning positive and negative interpretations, with the C meaning somewhere in between.

土星Saturn

原则——抑制、专注。
Principle - inhibition, concentration.
心理对应
Psychological Correspondence
＋ 专注、巩固、坚忍、严肃、从经验中学习的能力、节俭。
+ Concentration, consolidation, perseverance, seriousness, the ability to learn from experience, economy.
－ 受抑、忧郁、拘谨寡言、日益孤独、隔绝、离心、猜疑、吝啬、缺乏适应能力。
- Inhibition, melancholy, reserved and taciturn, increasing loneliness, isolation, eccentration, distrust, stinginess, lack of adaptability.
术语eccentration
原文的 eccentration 不是常见日常词，在埃伯廷体系中指从中心或共同体偏离、退向孤立位置的倾向，译「离心」。它与前面的 isolation 相邻，却更强调偏出中心而非单纯独处。
生物对应——骨骼结构、硬化过程、结石形成、器官丧失、老年。原书 p.108
Biological Correspondence - The bony structure, the process of hardening up, stone formation, the loss of organs, old age.
社会对应——勤劳、受抑或悲伤的人。农业、采矿业、房地产。
Sociological Correspondence - Hard working, inhibited or sad people. Agriculture, mining, real estate.

水星／土星Mercury/Saturn

原则——思想深度、脑力劳动。
Principle - Depth of thought, mental work.
＋ 逻辑思考、彻底、专注、直击要点的能力、爱整洁、组织能力、勤奋、运用方法、哲学思考。
+ Logical thinking, thoroughness, concentration, ability to come straight to the point, love of tidiness, ability to organize, industrious, the application of method, philosophical thinking.
－ 心智能力发展受抑（有时由言语缺陷造成）、猜疑。害羞、心胸狭窄、态度保守、任性或固执，性情不善交流而拘谨。
- An inhibited mental development (caused sometimes by defective speech), distrust. Shy, narrow-minded, conservative attitude, self-willed or obstinate, an uncommunicative and reserved disposition.
C 固执、顽强或耐久、笨拙或沉重、勤奋、忍受或承受痛苦而不屈服的状态。
C Obstinate, tenacity or endurance, clumsiness or heaviness, industrious, the state of enduring or suffering without yielding.

生物对应Biological Correspondence

神经系统阻滞、传导痛觉的神经。神经系统与言语及听觉器官之间的功能关系。
Blocking of the nervous system, pain-conducting nerves. The functional relationship of the nervous system to the organs of speech and hearing.

可能表现Probable Manifestations

＋ 人生进展缓慢但稳妥；获得经验的机会；深入专注于哲学问题的能力；旅行机会。
+ A slow but sure advancement in life, opportunities to gain experience, ability to concentrate deeply on philosophical problems, opportunity to travel.
－ 婴幼儿期与青春期的艰难岁月；人生上升过程困难而辛苦；疏远；频繁分离。原书 p.109
- Hard times during infancy and adolescence, a difficult and laborious rise in life, estrangements, frequent separations.

大卫·科克伦——振动占星学David Cochrane - Vibrational Astrology

大卫·科克伦David Cochrane如今仍十分活跃，影响力正处顶峰——本文写于 2019 年。他的振动占星学vibrational astrology体系把行星及其互动视为能量模式，会以各自典型的方式显现。他承接约翰·阿迪John Addey与谐波占星学Harmonic Astrology的工作；后者研究用不同数字或谐波分割星盘圆周所形成的相位，每一个数字都有自己的典型含义与表现。科克伦在含义上强调各种谐波，因此行星主要通过其谐波或振动互动而获得意义。
David Cochrane is active and at the peak of his influence today - I am writing this in 2019. His system of vibrational astrology views the planets and their interactions as energy patterns that manifest in characteristic ways. He is building on the work of John Addey and Harmonic Astrology, which examines aspects formed by dividing up the chart wheel by different numbers or harmonics, with each number having its own characteristic meaning and expression. For meanings, Cochrane emphasizes the various harmonics as giving the meanings, so the planets have most meaning in terms of their harmonic or vibrational interaction.
科克伦的工作再次很好地展示了：如何把一颗行星的诸种联系提炼成一个核心概念或原则，并在实践中取得极佳效果。它仍然缺少传统清单的某些具体丰富性、变化与不整齐；但它简洁优雅、精炼而实用。谐波占星学与科克伦振动占星学的含义复杂度和丰富度，来自以不同数字分割星盘圆周所得相位的含义。行星象征相当直接、简化；相位与数字象征则得到高度发展。
Cochrane's work is another good example of taking a planet's associations and distilling it down to a core concept or principle that works out very well in practice. Again, it lacks some of the concrete richness, variation and untidiness of the traditional lists, but it is elegant, streamlined and functional. The complexity and richness of meaning in Harmonic astrology and in Cochrane's vibrational astrology comes from the meanings of the aspects based on different numeric divisions of the chart wheel. The planet symbolism is fairly straightforward and simplified, and the aspect and number symbolism is highly developed.
提示复杂度从行星转移到数字与相位
科克伦并未简单削减整个体系，而是把解释负荷重新分配：行星只保留少数核心驱力，细腻差异交给圆周分割数及相位组合产生。因此，衡量其丰富度不能只数土星关键词，还要看数字—相位层的结构。
以下取自科克伦第一本书中论土星核心含义的一节。
This is the section on the core meaning of Saturn in Cochrane's first book.

土星Saturn

与木星一样，土星有许多联系，其中一些是：超然、僵硬、成熟、智慧、责任、挫折、抑制、孤立、分离、失败、死亡、干燥、沉重与权威。
Like Jupiter, there are many associations with Saturn, a few of which are: detachment, rigidity, maturity, wisdom responsibility, frustration, inhibition, isolation, separation, failure, death, dryness, heaviness, and authority.
我把土星视为一种驱力：除去多余、脂肪与一切轻浮之物，同时保存本质之物。土星倾向于消除铺张，有时甚至消除简单的快乐，使注意力集中到责任上。土星不喜欢铺张与分心之物；它珍视本质与重要之物，把其余一切丢到一旁。几乎所有传统土星征象，都可以看成土星聚焦本质这一特点的显现或后果。原书 p.110
I view Saturn as a drive to remove excess, fat, and anything frivolous, while preserving what is essential. Saturn tends to remove extravagances and sometimes even simple pleasures and focus our attention on our responsibilities. Saturn dislikes extravagances and distractions. Saturn values what is essential and important, and casts everything else aside. Virtually all of the traditional associations of Saturn can be viewed as manifestations or consequences of Saturn's focus on what is essential.
