I think that Ruperti's use of the word resistance carries connotations of its use during the Nazi era, as referring to people in Europe attempting to fight totalitarian oppression. This is the stance of the lone individual fighting for identity against a hostile system and a hostile universe.
鲁佩蒂与鲁伊尔都完成了一些精彩工作；鲁佩蒂用周期显示人类潜能展开，非常有价值。但我认为，他们的工作被这种内外断裂与敌意损害了；这也误解了周期与行运。他们两人离看见全貌都只差一步。
Ruperti and Rudhyar both did some marvelous work, and Ruperti's work on cycles as showing unfolding of human potential is very valuable, but I think their work is marred by this inner-outer disconnect and hostility. I also think it is a misunderstanding of cycles and of transits. They are both so very, very close to seeing the whole picture.
我想提出另一种看待行运的方式。我最初在劳里·埃弗林Laurie Efrein的《如何校正本命盘》中见到这个概念；开篇章名概括了它：〈一生的占星结构如何在出生时内置〉。
I want to present a different way of viewing transits. I first saw this concept in a book called How to Rectify a birth chart by Laurie Efrein, and the concept is summed up in the title of the initial chapter, How a Lifetime of Astrology is Built-In at Birth.
请回想，我们说过，给定任意日期、时间与地点，无论过去或未来几千年，都能绘制本命盘。依同样逻辑，在出生时，你便可以排出一生全部行运。根据我们对行星位置与行星运动法则的认识，它们已经在那里。
Recall that we talked about how you can draw up a birth chart for any given date, time and location thousands of years into the past and future. Given that same logic, at the time of birth, you can draw up the transits for the entire lifetime. They are already there, given what we know of the planetary positions and the laws of planetary movement.
想想这一点：出生之时，整套将要展开的行运、次限推运、流年盘，以及你愿意使用的任何其他预测技术，都已经在那里；它们隐含于本命盘，也隐含于周期在时间中的结构。一生经验的整个框架，在出生那一刻就已经潜在地绘制出来。
Consider that: at birth, you have the entire unfolding set of transits, progressions, solar returns and any other predictive technique you care to use, already there, implicit in the birth chart and the structure of cycles through time. The whole framework of life experience is already potentially mapped out at the moment of birth.
我认为，说行运与次限推运一样，都是本命盘潜能的一部分，要合理得多。你无法在任何地方划开内在与外在；二者共同属于无缝整体。次限推运与行运都由同一套数学逻辑系统产生——而且别忘了，二者使用的还是同一部星历！
I think it makes far more sense to say that the transits are every bit as much a part of the potential of the birth chart as are progressions. There is no place at which you can draw the line between inner and outer. They are together all part of a seamless whole. The same logical system of mathematics produces both progressions and transits - and remember, both progressions and transits use the same ephemeris!
术语progression 与 profection 分开
此处 progressions 指以出生后天数象征人生年数等方法推进星盘的次限类技术，译「次限推运」；不得与 profection「小限推运」混同。前文第 62 页一处笼统译作「小限推运」已同步回改。
人的一生不只是一张静止本命盘，而是本命盘加次限推运，加行运，再加实际人生经验的整个过程，一切都在时间中展开。完整身份由整个周期显示，不只由出生时刻显示。完整的人类身份属于更大整体，并不站在它的对面。原书 p.144
The human life is not just a static birth chart. The human life is the entire process of birth chart, plus progressions, plus transits, plus actual life experience, all unfolding through time. The full identity is shown by the entire cycle, not just the moment of birth. Full human identity is part of the larger whole, not over against it.
这就是鲁伊尔犯下的关键错误。人的身份并未冻结在本命盘的出生时刻。更合理的看法是，把本命盘视为种子点或格局：它是进一步发展的起始框架，不是一个必须抵抗变化来保卫的静止不变身份。人生的目的不在停止时间，而在时间中工作、与时间合作。
This is the critical error that Rudhyar made. Human identity is not frozen at the moment in time of the birth chart. It makes more sense to view the birth chart as a seed point or pattern that is a starting framework for further development rather than a static unchanging identity to be defended against change. The purpose of human life is not to stop time, but to work with and in time.
孩子出生到世界上，不是为了与世界战斗。孩子来到世界，是为了成为世界的一部分，变化、成长，走完从出生到死亡——也许还越过死亡——的整个周期。
A child is not born into the world to fight the world. A child comes into this world in order to be part of the world, to change and grow, to go through the entire cycle from birth to death, and perhaps beyond.
孩子作为世界的一部分出生于世界，并非出生来对抗世界。
Children are born into the world as part of the world, not over against the world.
人的身份永远不是静止冻结的。我们成长、变化、经历并回应各种事情，作出选择并处理后果。本命盘、次限推运、行运与处境共同构成人生上演其中的动态框架。这个框架在整体有机结构中包含自由意志的一项成分。
Human identity is never static and frozen. We grow, we change, we have experiences and respond to them, we make choices and deal with the consequences. The birth chart, plus progressions, plus transits, plus circumstances, together all form the dynamic framework within which human life is played out. It is a framework that includes an element of free will within an overall organic structure.
柏拉图体系的核心原则同样适用于人生：永恒秩序与身份在整个时间周期中显现。这里，永恒秩序显示于所有星盘与预测技术贯穿时间的结构，包括行运、次限推运、定向推运、小限推运，或你愿意使用的任何其他预测技术。我们必须观看整个周期在时间中展开，才能把握在时间之外的永恒秩序里一举隐含的一切。
The core principle of the Platonic system, that the eternal order and identity is revealed in the entire temporal cycle, applies equally to human life, and here the eternal order is shown in the structure through time of all of the charts and predictive techniques, including transits, including progressions, including primary directions or profections or any other predictive technique you care to use. We need to see the entire cycle play out in time in order to grasp what is implicit all at once in the eternal order outside of time.
鲁伊尔与鲁佩蒂关于周期与展开的观念，离这一真理如此接近。与其说个体对集体、内在对外在，不如说两者都属于一个包含二者的更大体系；而这个更大体系映照宇宙的永恒结构原理。永恒映现在时间的展开中。若要映照完整周期，土星的结构必须同时包含时间与空间；土星的许多含义也与时间流逝有关。原书 p.145
Rudhyar and Ruperti come so very close to this truth in their concepts of cycles and unfolding. Instead of it being individual over against collective, inner over against outer, it is far more true to say that both are part of larger system including both, and that the larger system mirrors the eternal structural principles of the universe. Eternity is mirrored in the unfolding of time. Saturn's structure has to include time as well as space to be able to mirror entire cycles, and many of the meanings of Saturn have to do with the passage of time.
永恒映照于时间中的完整周期，土星代表永恒与时间之间的那条边界。土星的许多含义都从这一核心概念生长出来。
Eternity is mirrored in entire cycles in time, and the planet Saturn represents that boundary between eternity and time. Many of Saturn's meanings grow out of this central concept.

土星与乌拉诺斯Saturn and Ouranos

这里正适合讨论一个现象对现代占星学造成的巨大影响：外行星天王星运行在土星轨道之外。原书 p.146
This is a good place to address the enormous impact in modern astrology of having the outer planet Uranus be in an orbit beyond that of Saturn.
本节大部分地方，我会把希腊神的名字拼作乌拉诺斯Ouranos，而不用 Uranus，以避免读者立刻联想到同名的现代行星。这个词在希腊原文中意为「天」或「天空」。乌拉诺斯与盖娅——也就是天与地——共同生下许多神，包括克洛诺斯；后者更广为人知的名字，是其罗马名萨图尔努斯／土星。
For most of this section I will spell the name of the Greek god as Ouranos rather than Uranus. I want to avoid immediate association with the modern planet of that name. The word in the original Greek means the Heavens or the Sky. Ouranos and Gaia, or Heaven and Earth, together gave birth to a large number of gods, including Kronus, better known by his Roman name of Saturn.

神话中的乌拉诺斯／天王星Ouranos/Uranus in Mythology

在原始希腊神话中，乌拉诺斯是克洛诺斯／土星之父。土星阉割父亲，推翻其统治；土星与妻子瑞亚又生下其他诸神，包括宙斯，也就是木星。宙斯继而推翻并阉割克洛诺斯，正如克洛诺斯曾推翻并阉割乌拉诺斯。
In the original Greek mythology Ouranos is the father of Kronus/Saturn. Saturn overthrew the rule of his father by castrating him, and Saturn and his wife Rhea gave birth to other gods, including Zeus, also known as Jupiter. Zeus in turn overthrew and castrated Kronus as Kronus had overthrown and castrated Ouranos.
校记宙斯并未在赫西俄德叙事中阉割克洛诺斯
原书把两次权力更替都写成阉割。赫西俄德《神谱》的标准叙事是：克洛诺斯以镰刀阉割乌拉诺斯；宙斯则迫使克洛诺斯吐出被吞的子女，继而在泰坦之战中将其推翻，并非再次阉割。此属神话事实陈述的错误，正文照译并志疑，不替作者改写论证。

传统占星学中的乌拉诺斯／天王星Ouranos/Uranus in Traditional Astrology

若思考传统宇宙的象征，乌拉诺斯或「天」就是环绕诸行星的恒星球。克洛诺斯是乌拉诺斯之子，也是最外侧行星，离乌拉诺斯最近。克洛诺斯之子宙斯或木星，则是序列中的下一颗行星。在原始神话中，土星由此占据一个独特位置，位于恒星领域与永远运动的行星领域之间的边界。土星处在时间与永恒之间；神话也映照了这一点。
If you consider the symbolism of the traditional cosmos, Ouranos, or the Heavens, is the sphere of the fixed stars that surround the planets. Kronus, the son of Ouranos, is the outermost of the planets, the planet closest to Ouranos. The son of Kronus, Zeus or Jupiter, is the next planet in order. Saturn here in the original mythology has his distinctive place at the border between the realm of the fixed stars and the realm of the perpetually moving planets. Saturn is at the border between time and eternity, and that is reflected in the mythology.
行星宙斯或木星，是工匠神（德穆革）Demiurge在较低层级的形象或代表；这位工匠神塑造我们居住的显现宇宙，仰望土星以取得建造宇宙的格局或范型。在这套象征中，土星把永恒世界的理型与格局向下映入时间和物质世界。
The planet Zeus or Jupiter is a lower level image or representative of the Demiurge, the artisan god who crafted the manifest cosmos we inhabit, looking to the planet Saturn for the patterns or templates on which the cosmos is built. Saturn in this symbolism mirrors the forms and patterns of the eternal world down into the world of time and matter.

现代占星学中的天王星Uranus in Modern Astrology

现代占星学把土星与天王星的象征彻底颠倒。现代宇宙中，天王星是土星之外紧邻的行星；它被安排成勇敢自由战士与反叛者的角色，与土星战斗并将其推翻；土星则代表一切古老、压迫、过时而限制人的事物。原书 p.147
In modern astrology the symbolism of Saturn and Uranus has been flipped on its head. The modern planet Uranus, the next planet out from Saturn in the modern cosmos, is cast in the role of the brave freedom fighter and rebel who fights and overthrows Saturn, and Saturn represents everything old, oppressive, outmoded and restricting.
原始神话被反转了。不再是土星推翻父亲天王星，而是自由战士天王星推翻土星。按传统神话来说，这讲不通。
The original mythology has been reversed. Instead of Saturn overthrowing his father Uranus, we have Uranus the freedom fighter overthrowing Saturn. In terms of the traditional mythology it makes no sense.

土星含义的转换Switch of Meanings of Saturn

可以看出，这种神话变化如何深刻影响土星含义，乃至整个体系。在传统体系中，土星把「天」或乌拉诺斯的永恒法则与结构向下映入易变世界。土星与永恒法则、传统及世代相传的规则相联；人们尊重、敬畏传统法则与结构。
You can see how this change in mythology has profound effects on the meanings of Saturn, and on the system as a whole. In the traditional system Saturn mirrors the eternal law and structure of The Heavens or Ouranos down into our mutable world. Saturn is associated with eternal law and with tradition, with rules that have been passed down over time. There is a respect and reverence for the traditional laws and structures.
现代占星学引入天王星之后，土星改而代表一切陈旧、过时、限制人并且不再有用的事物。永恒反叛者天王星推翻土星，代表新、年轻与现代。传统遭到贬值，旧事物与老人也一样；所有新而年轻之物则受到崇拜与高度评价。
With Uranus in modern astrology Saturn now represents all that is outmoded, outdated, restrictive and no longer useful. Uranus the eternal rebel overthrows Saturn and stands for the new, the young, the modern. Tradition is devalued, as is anything old, as are old people, and everything new and youthful is worshiped and highly valued.
现代占星学贬低并抛弃了占星学原本依赖的整个传统体系，用新事物与现代事物取而代之。本书第一部考察现代占星师著作时，我们已经看到这是贯穿其中的共同线索。
In modern astrology the entire system of tradition on which astrology is originally based is devalued and thrown away, to be replaced by the new and modern. We saw that as a common thread running through the writings from modern astrologers that we examined in the first part of this book.
外行星的发现，不只是给太阳周围增加了几个运行天体；它彻底改变了宇宙结构的象征含义。本书主张，这次含义转换抛弃了许多宝贵之物；我也主张找回许多传统含义，包括宇宙整体的秩序，尤其包括土星的含义。原书 p.148
The discovery of the outer planets did not simply add a couple of new moving bodies orbiting around the Sun. It completely changed the symbolic meaning of the structure of the cosmos. In this book I am arguing that much of value has been thrown away with this switch of meaning, and I also argue for reclaiming much of the traditional meaning, of the order of the Cosmos in general, and of the meaning of Saturn in particular.
提示新行星改变的是整幅空间图
在传统地心图式里，土星紧邻恒星天，承担把永恒格局送入时间的边界功能；天王星被置于土星之外后，「外侧」改而象征超越限制。因此，新发现不仅给词典添一颗行星，也改变了「越过土星之后是什么」这一空间隐喻。

占星学中的一致性Consistency in Astrology

我们的论证认为，宇宙存在一套永恒秩序与永恒结构原理，人的心智能够把握并映照它。这里我想再增加一个维度：占星学许多面向的含义历经时代，仍具有惊人的一致性，这是一项事实。原书 p.149
I want to add another dimension to our argument that there is an eternal order, an eternal set of structural principles to the universe, and that our minds can apprehend and mirror that order. That dimension is the fact of the very amazing consistency of meaning in many of the aspects of astrology over the ages.
尽管我一直强调上个世纪土星及其他行星的含义变化，象征含义仍有一种惊人的一致性。土星仍然一眼可认，绝不会被错认成金星。若查看瓦伦斯、菲尔米库斯·马特尔努斯与其他古典作者最早赋予土星的含义，那些规则与征象归属如今仍然适用、仍然有效，有时准得令人害怕。再看现代赋予土星的含义，它们与最早那组含义仍有明显的家族相似，都指向同一项底层核心实在。
For all that I am emphasizing the changes in meaning of Saturn and the other planets in the last century, there is still an astounding consistency of meaning to the symbolism. Saturn is still recognizably Saturn. You could never mistake Saturn for Venus. If we look at the earliest meanings for Saturn, from Valens, Firmicus Maternus and other classic authors, these rules and attributions still apply, they still work, sometimes frighteningly well. If you look at the modern meanings attributed to Saturn they still all have a close family resemblance to the earliest set of meanings. They point to the same underlying core reality.
我要坚持，这是一项非常有力的论据，说明占星体系映照了宇宙实际结构中的某种东西，而不只是人创造的构造物。它也有力提醒我们：传统的吉星与凶星benefic and malefic、尊贵与弱势等概念，仍有许多值得学习；即便现代文化不喜欢它们，我们宁愿认为占星学中的一切按定义都必须得到正面解释。事实上，我认为恰恰因为现代占星学如此偏向正面，我们才更需要向传统概念学习。它们提供平衡与另一种视角。
I maintain that this is a very strong argument for saying that the system of astrology mirrors something in the actual structure of the universe. It is not simply a human created construct. This is also a very strong argument for remembering that we still have much to learn from traditional concepts like benefic and malefic, and dignity and debility, even if such concepts are unpopular in our modern culture and we would prefer to think that everything in our astrology must by definition be interpreted positively. In fact I think it is precisely because modern astrology has such a positive slant that we have much to learn from the traditional concepts. They provide a balance and an alternate perspective.
提示一致性不是“从未改变”
作者刚用整整一部讨论含义如何变薄、心理化与重组，因此这里的一致性不是字面清单保持不动，而是变化后的含义仍能认出同一家族核心。他的推论是：若不同年代、流派仍反复触及相似结构，占星象征便可能不只是任意发明；这是论证，不是实验性证明。
占星学会随时间变化发展，但这并不会使传统失效；相反，新发展建立在传统之上。我们传承的是一项神圣传统。我所主张的是维护传统的完整丰厚——而这样做，本身就是一件非常土星式的事。
Astrology changes and develops over time, but this does not invalidate the tradition. Rather, it builds on it. We are passing down a sacred tradition, and I am arguing for maintaining the fullness of the tradition - and that is a very Saturnian sort of thing to do.

土星天球与占星学The Sphere of Saturn and Astrology

下面一段意大利作家但丁·阿利吉耶里Dante Alighieri的话，为我们打开另一扇窗，让人看见传统世界中的土星含义。但丁最著名的身份是《神曲》作者。在《飨宴》中，他把七艺与七颗行星相比，并作如下分配：原书 p.150
Another window into the meaning of Saturn in the traditional world is shown in the following quote from Dante Alighieri, the Italian writer best known as author of the Divine Comedy. In the Convivio Dante compares the seven liberal arts to the seven planets, assigning them as follows.
文法对应月亮；
Grammar corresponds to the Moon,
逻辑对应水星；
Logic to Mercury,
修辞对应金星；
Rhetoric to Venus,
算术对应太阳；
Arithmetic to the Sun,
音乐对应火星；
Music to Mars,
几何对应木星；
Geometry to Jupiter,
占星学对应土星。
and Astrology to Saturn.
背景七艺与七行星
中世纪七艺分为三艺（文法、逻辑、修辞）与四艺（算术、音乐、几何、天文）。但丁在《飨宴》第二篇依次把它们配给七行星天，并把最高、运行最慢的土星天配给天文／占星知识；当时 astrologia 尚未按现代方式与 astronomy 严格分家。
下面是但丁的原话。
Here is the quote from Dante.
土星天有两项性质，可据以同占星学相比：一是它穿行十二星座的运动缓慢；依占星师著作，它完成一周需要二十九年有余；二是它高居其他一切行星之上。占星学也具有这两项性质：完成它的圆周（也就是掌握这门科学）要经过极漫长的时间；一方面因为它的侍女学科比上述任何科学都多，另一方面因为作出正确判断需要经验。此外，它远高于其他一切学科；正如亚里士多德在《论灵魂》开篇所说，一门科学的高贵，来自其研究对象的高贵及其确定性。而占星学比上述任何学科都崇高、高贵，因为它崇高高贵的研究对象涉及天体运动；它也因自身的确定性而崇高高贵。这种确定性毫无瑕疵，因为它来自一个最为完美而有规律的原则。若有人认为其中存在错误，那错误不属于这门科学；正如托勒密所说，它源于我们的疏忽，必须归因于此。
The heaven of Saturn has two properties by which it may be compared to Astrology: one is the slowness of its movement through the 12 signs, for according to the writings of the astrologers, a time of more than 29 years is required for its revolution; the other is that it is high above all the other planets. And these two properties are found in Astrology: for in completing its circle (that is to say, to master this science) a very great span of time passes, both because of its handmaids, which are more numerous than those of any of the above-mentioned sciences, and because of the experience required in it for making proper judgments. Furthermore, it is far higher than all the others, since, as Aristotle says at the beginning of On the Soul, a science is high in nobility by virtue of the nobility of its subject and by virtue of its certainty; and this one, more than any of those mentioned above, is high and noble because of its high and noble subject, which regards the movement of the heaven, and high and noble because of its certainty, which is flawless, as coming from a most perfect and regular principle. And if anyone believes that there is a flaw in it, it does not pertain to the science, but as Ptolemy says, it results from our negligence, and so must be attributed to that.
这段话把占星学这门技艺与科学归于土星，与我们逐步展开的土星整体含义非常契合。占星学是一门居于顶端、统合其他学科的科学，显示永恒秩序世界如何在时间世界中展开。它符合土星处于时间与永恒边界的核心含义，也符合土星与时间周期的联系。原书 p.151
In this quote the art and science of Astrology is attributed to the planet Saturn. This fits well in the context of Saturn's overall meaning that we have been developing. Astrology is a crowning and integrating science showing how the world of eternal order plays out in our temporal world. It fits in with the core meanings of Saturn given its position on the border of time and eternity, and Saturn's association with the cycles of time.
提示“确定性”首先属于天体秩序
但丁的论证把天体运行所依据的规则性，与占星判断这门实践连在一起；判断若错，责任落在人之疏忽，而非天体原则。现代读者可以质疑这一步，但本章借它说明的是传统宇宙观：最高的时间科学因长期经验、统合众学并接近恒常秩序而属于土星。

生活在两个世界Living in Two Worlds

为了完成我们的柏拉图宇宙模型，还需要谈谈人的本性，以及我们如何处于这个世界中。原书 p.152
In order to complete our Platonic model of the cosmos, we need to talk about the nature of the human being, and how we fit in this world.
宇宙有永恒维度，也有时间维度。两者共享一条法则、一组共同结构原理，柏拉图称之为理型Forms或理念。这些结构理型也包括硬接入宇宙的基本道德标准，也就是我们用正义Justice等词描述的标准。
The cosmos has an eternal dimension and a temporal dimension. There is a common law to both, a common set of structural principles that Plato called the Forms or Ideas. These structural Forms also include basic moral standards hard-wired into the universe, standards that we describe with words like Justice.
作为活着且具有理性的受造物，我们人类同时生活在两个世界中。我们有一个处在时间中的身份，也有一部分始终生活在永恒中，有时称为灵魂。这种人类模型是传统宇宙的共同特征，也反映在我们考察过的大多数乃至全部现代占星师身上。谈到死后生命、轮回与多生，就意味着我们有一部分处于生死世界之外，会在两世之间继续存在。
As living and rational creatures we as human beings live in both worlds at the same time. There is our identity here in time, and there is the part of us that always lives in eternity that is sometimes described as our Soul. This model of the human is a common feature of the traditional cosmos, and we also see it reflected in most if not all of the modern astrologers we have looked. When you speak of life after death, or of reincarnation and multiple lives, this implies that there is a part of us which is outside of the world of life and death and that continues to exist between lives.
柏拉图模型的独特之处在于：人身上为永恒与时间两个世界架起桥梁的部分，是我们的理性Reason能力。
What is distinctive about the Platonic model is that the part of us as human beings that provides the bridge between the eternal and temporal worlds is our ability to Reason.
这里我把「理性」首字母大写，是为了指明一种首要的人类官能：它使我们独具人性，并区别于其他动物。Reason 与 Ratio 一词相关，后者有度量与权衡之意。理性使我们能够度量、评价、选择与判断；若要正确这样做，理性就需要一项标准、一把判断所依据的量尺。那项标准扎根于永恒世界，因此，正确运用的理性会统合两个世界，把永恒标准带入我们在时间中的生活。
I put Reason with a capital letter here to point to a primary human faculty, that which makes us distinctly human and distinguishes us from other animals. Reason is related to the word Ratio which means to measure and balance. Our reason is what gives us the ability to measure, evaluate, choose and judge, and to do that correctly Reason needs a standard, a measuring stick to judge by. That standard is rooted in the eternal world, so Reason correctly used integrates both worlds and brings the eternal standards into our life in time.
术语Reason 仍译“理性”
本书用大写 Reason 指逐步度量、比较、判断的心智能力，译「理性」；它向上连接一举把握整体的 Intellect「智性」。二者若都译「理智」，第 153 页的层级关系就会消失。拉丁 ratio 还含计算、比例、说明等义，作者在此突出「量尺」隐喻。
我认为，这种对理性的强调，与「灵魂」一词的许多现代联想很不一样。如今人们更可能把灵魂看成感受问题——我们之所以不朽，是因为不知怎的感觉它是真的。类似地，现代灵性在设想与神圣相连时，常把它理解成搁置忙碌的理性心智，调谐到没有言语的感受，以此建立连接。
I think this emphasis on reason is very different from many modern connotations of the word soul. Soul is much more likely to be thought of as a matter of feeling - we are immortal because we somehow feel that is true. Similarly, in order to connect with the divine it is characteristic of modern spirituality to conceive that as involving setting aside the busy rational mind and tuning in to our wordless feelings to make the connection.
在柏拉图世界模型中，永恒维度并不温暖朦胧、没有形式；它拥有永恒结构。心智中与这种永恒结构相连的部分，就是我们所谓的智性Intellect，也就是整全之知Unitive Knowing、一举而知All-At-Once knowing。我们的理性连接智性；理性的任务，是依永恒理型塑造我们的心智与判断。原书 p.153
In the Platonic model of the world, the eternal dimension is not warm, fuzzy and formless. It has an eternal structure. The part of our minds that connects to this eternal structure is what we call Intellect or Unitive Knowing, All-At-Once knowing. Our Reason connects to the Intellect, and the job of reason is to shape our minds, our judgments, along the lines of the eternal Forms.
术语Unitive Knowing／All-At-Once knowing
两词都是作者为 Intellect「智性」提供的白话释义：不是逐步推演，而是把结构作为整体一举把握。依既有 Unitive Thought「整全之思」词族，这里译「整全之知／一举而知」，保留 knowing 与 thought 的差别。
按此模型，人生最重要的目的，是作出判断，并依硬接入宇宙及心智结构的「永恒法则」行动。这是许多柏拉图对话的主题，也是《理想国》的中心议题。因此，我们必须对宇宙内置永恒法则拥有强烈而鲜活的感受；这一点极其重要。没有它，整个占星学与全部生活，都只剩没有形状的感受，像云烟中来去无常的变幻图像。
In this model the most important purpose of human life is to judge, and to act in alignment with the Eternal Law that is hard wired in the cosmos and in the structure of our minds. That is the theme of many of the Platonic dialogues, and is the central subject of The Republic. This is why it is so very important for us to have a strong, living sense of the cosmos having built-in eternal laws. Without that, all our astrology, and all of our lives, are just a matter of shapeless feelings, shifting images in clouds and smoke that come and go.
在传统占星学模型中，土星是两个世界之间的桥梁。土星把永恒结构向下映入不断变化的时空世界。因此，我们把土星与法则、正义、审判等概念相联。土星常涉及处理自身选择与行动的后果。我们用土星指向的永恒法则并不温暖朦胧、没有形式；它们坚硬、有结构而明确。
In the model of traditional astrology Saturn is the bridge between the two worlds. Saturn mirrors the eternal structures down into the changing world of space and time. That is why we associate Saturn with concepts like Law, and Justice, and Judgment. Saturn often relates to dealing with the consequences of our choices and actions. The eternal laws we point to with Saturn aren't warm, fuzzy and formless, they are hard, structured and definite.
我们的一切行为都有后果，因为生命每一刻，我们都受到这些永恒法则的判断。我们在生时受判断，死后也受判断。在我见过的每一种死后生命或轮回模型中，死后的命定都取决于今生所作选择的后果；来生由今生决定。
All of our acts have consequences because we are judged by these eternal laws every moment of our lives. We are judged during our lives, and we are judged after death. Every model of life after death or reincarnation that I have ever seen includes the idea that our fate after death is a consequence of the choices we make in this life. Our next life is determined by the present life.
位于时间与永恒边界的土星提醒我们：永恒法则始终在那里，死亡之时我们将面对它。用一个常见传统隐喻来说，我们死时面对审判。我相信，这种审判包含怜悯，「爱」与「怜悯」概念也指向永恒理型。我们需要怜悯与审判同在。
Saturn at the border of time and eternity reminds us that the eternal law is always there, and that we face that law at death. Using a common traditional metaphor, at death we face judgment. I believe that this judgment includes mercy, and that the concepts of Love and Mercy also point to eternal forms. We need Mercy and Judgment together.
传统宇宙的全部元素现在已经各就各位；我们因此拥有一个脉络，土星的完整含义可以在其中对现代世界讲得通。基于这套结构，我们已经准备好考察土星含义中几个不同的重要维度。我想集中讨论现代占星学或是强调不足、或是通常不予处理的概念。我要追求的是平衡——这本身就是非常土星式的事。原书 p.154
We now have all the elements of the traditional cosmos in place, so we have a context within which the full meaning of Saturn can make sense in our modern world. Given this structure, we are ready to look at some of the different important dimensions of the meaning of Saturn. I want to concentrate on concepts that are either under-emphasized or not usually dealt with in modern astrology. I am going for balance, which is a very Saturnian thing to do.
提示第二部的落点
第二部建立了三层连接：永恒理型提供标准，智性整全把握它，理性把标准带入时间中的判断；土星则在宇宙象征层面承担同样的桥梁功能。第三部将不再搭模型，而按「平衡、传统、凶吉、命运、衰老、受苦、死亡」等主题检验这套模型。
PART THREE

第三部　土星主题诸论

Part Three: Essays on Saturn Themes

导言Introduction

这一部分不讨论如何解释星盘中的土星，而讨论处理土星相关事件时会出现哪些主题。我们现在处于观念与哲学层面，而不是释盘层面。原书 p.155
This section is not about how to interpret Saturn in a chart; it is about the kinds of themes that come up when you are dealing with Saturn related events. We are at the level of ideas and philosophy rather than interpretation.
与土星相联的经验十分复杂。它比任何其他行星都更深入地探测恶的深处，也探测善的深处；两者都必须面对。现代世界倾向否认或隐藏土星可能带入人生的那些令人不快的经验真相。土星的一部分含义与阴影、盲点、隐而不见、遭人回避和令人恐惧之物有关。我们会把其中一些经验带出阴影，并考察它们如何使人生更加丰富。
The experiences associated with Saturn are complex. More than any other planet it plumbs the depths of evil, and the depths of good. We need to deal with both. Our modern world has a tendency to want to deny or hide the unpleasant truths of experience that Saturn can bring into our lives. Part of Saturn's meaning has to do with shadows, blind spots, things hidden, shunned and feared. We will bring some of those experiences out of the shadows and look at how our lives are richer because of them.

平衡Balance

在持续成长、扩张与改善的文化中，平衡观念已经基本丢失。我们不再用彼此平衡的概念思考，而只抓住单个失衡概念，力图把它推向极端。土星完整的传统含义在这里尤其有用，因为土星按定义就是对立、反作用，也是事物向某个极端失控时的平衡力量。原书 p.156
The concept of balance has been largely lost in our culture of continuous growth, expansion and improvement. We no longer think in terms of balanced concepts but of single unbalanced concepts we seek to push to an extreme. Here the full traditional meaning of Saturn is particularly useful, as it is, by definition, the opposition, the reaction, the balance to things that are out of control in an extreme direction.

核心概念Key Concepts

有几个核心概念可以解释赋予土星之含义的核心；它们也是整个占星体系的核心结构概念。
There are a few key concepts that explain the core of meanings attributed to Saturn, and these are also core structural concepts for all of the astrology system.
首先是对立与平衡。土星的一些核心含义，与它形成对分、站在对面、阻挡或使之平衡有关。
There is the concept of oppositions and balance. Some of the core meanings of Saturn are related to its being in opposition, or over against, or blocking, or balancing.
还有时间与永恒这组配对概念：时间映照永恒结构，土星位于两者之间的枢轴点。土星的一些含义指向法则与审判等永恒之物，一些指向时间的作用；还有一些把两者结合起来，于是产生时间中的审判或清算。
There are the paired concepts of time and eternity, with time mirroring the structure of eternity, and Saturn being at the pivot point between the two. Some of the meanings of Saturn point to eternal things like law and judgment, and some point to the effects of time, and some meanings combine the two, so that you have judgment or reckoning in time.
把这些概念放在一起，便得到相反事物随时间运动、交替出现的周期。只有完整周期才让时间映照永恒。它与土星其他性质相连：老年的智慧、从经验学习、从历史学习；凭完整周期的制高点，平衡当下风尚的盲点，等等。时间周期中的单独时点很少完全平衡，但整个周期会表达平衡。钟摆在运动，来回摆动。本节的一部分目的，是让人意识到这种失衡，再集中考察我们文化中若干薄弱失衡的具体领域。
Take these concepts together and you have cycles of alternating opposites in motion over time. Time mirrors eternity only in the entire cycle. This connects to other Saturnian qualities: the wisdom of age, learning from experience, learning from history, the vantage point of the entire cycle balancing the blind spots of the current fads, and so on. Few points in cycle of time are completely balanced, but the cycle as a whole expresses balance. There is a pendulum in motion, swinging back and forth. Part of the purpose of this section is to bring this lack of balance to awareness, and then to focus on some specific subject areas that are weak and unbalanced in our culture.
提示平衡不等于静止在中点
作者的钟摆比喻很关键：周期中某一刻可以强烈偏向生长或衰退，平衡却由完整往返表达。因此，「平衡」并非每时每刻各取一半，而是让上升、下降、行动、反思各有其时，并从全周期判断其位置。

单边极端的文化A Culture of One-Sided Extremes

我们的文化一贯看重单边极端，并按「更多」与「更少」来判断。面对相反事物，我们的文化总想拥抱并聚焦其中一面，而忽视、贬低、否认，或以其他方式看不见另一面。我们只重视一边，不重视另一边。我们生活在「越多越好」的数量文化里；我们知道如何处理更多，却不善于面对更少。原书 p.157
We have a culture that consistently values one-sided extremes and judges in terms of More and Less. In terms of opposites, we consistently see our culture wanting to embrace and focus on one side of a pair, and ignore or denigrate or deny or otherwise not see the other side. We value one side and not the other. We live in a culture of More is Better, a quantity culture. We know how to deal with more, we don't deal well with less.
我们考察过的现代占星师都认为，人生目的是充分实现潜能。我们来到这里，是要尽可能幸福、富有、强大、成功而长寿；一切都用最高级来表述。
The modern astrologers we looked at share the view that the purpose of life is to do fulfill our potential. We are here to be as happy and wealthy and strong and successful and long lived as we possibly can, all phrased in terms of superlatives.
下面这些例子显示，这种失衡如何像一条金线贯穿整个文化。
These examples show how this lack of balance runs like a golden thread all through our culture.
我们重视青春，不重视老年。
We value youth and not age.
我们能安然面对出生，却不能安然面对死亡，往往轻描淡写或否认死亡。我们知道如何好好出生、成长，却失去了好好衰老、好好死亡的艺术。
We are comfortable with birth and not comfortable with death, which we tend to gloss over or deny. We know how to be born and grow well, and we have lost the art of aging and dying well.
我们擅长扩张，不擅长收缩；擅长成长，不善于面对腐朽。
We are good at expanding and not at contracting. We are good at growing and not good at dealing with decaying.
我们知道如何面对上升途中，却不知道如何驾驭下降途中。我们能够应付成功，却不善于面对失败。
We know how to deal with being on the way up and do not know how to navigate being on the way down. We can cope with success and don't deal well with failure.
我们善于处理健康，却不善于应对疾病；能够接受舒适，却不能接受受苦；我们不懂得如何「善处苦难」这门从容优雅的艺术。
We are good at dealing with health and not with handling disease. We can handle comfort and not suffering; we don't know the graceful art of how to suffer well.
我们知道如何骄傲，却忘记了如何谦卑。
We know how to be proud, and we have forgotten how to be humble.
我们重视自由，却忘记约束的价值。原书 p.158
We value freedom and we have forgotten the value of constraint.
人人都想富裕殷实，却忘记贫穷与资财微薄也有价值。
We all want to be rich and affluent, and we have forgotten the value of being poor and of humble means.
我们重视成名，却失去了默默无名的力量。
We value being famous, and we have lost the power of being anonymous.
我们知道如何取胜，却不懂如何失败。我们期待事情顺遂己意；一旦事与愿违，便不知如何从容面对。
We know how to win and don't know how to lose. We expect things to go our way, and we don't know how to deal with it gracefully when things don't go our way.
我们知道如何开始，却不知道如何结束。
We know how to start things and not how to end them.
校记suffering well 定译“善处苦难”
结合第 202–205 页〈苦难〉专章的完整语境，suffer well 定译为「善处苦难」：不是歌颂痛苦或主动求苦，而是问不可避免的痛苦来临时，人能否以合宜、有德性且不增加第二层痛苦的方式回应。

周期的亏减半程The Waning Side of the Cycle

从周期角度思考，我们已经失去对周期亏减半程之意义与用处的感受。渐盈半程用来成长与行动，亏减半程则用来后退、反思与学习。渐盈半程关乎做事，亏减半程关乎理解。
Thinking in terms of cycles, we have lost a sense of the meaning and usefulness of the waning side of cycles. The waxing side is where grow and act, the waning side is where we pull back, reflect and learn. The waxing side is about doing, the waning side is about understanding.
我们重视为了行动而学习，却忘记了学习本身的价值；一项行动直到转化为学习才算完成。我们阅读却不反思，就像进食却不消化。全部重点都放在做事／建造／扩张上，而处理／学习／收获／提炼的艺术已经失传。
We value learning in order to act, and we have forgotten the worth of learning for its own sake, that action is not completed until it is transmuted into learning. We read without reflecting, which is like eating without digesting. Our entire emphasis is on doing /building /expanding, and we have lost the art of processing /learning /harvesting /distilling.
我们知道如何保持活跃，却不知道如何停下来反思。行动与反思交替，是生命中非常重要的节律。我们会行动，却不会停止行动并加以反思。
We know how to be active and not how stop to reflect. There is a very important rhythm to life of alternating action and reflection. We know how to act but not how to stop acting and reflect.
我们会在论时间的一章再次拾起失衡这个主题。
We will pick up this topic of unbalance again in the chapter on time.
我们也忘记了我所谓的「对立面悖论」；某些宗教传统特别强调它：想做众人之主的人，必须做众人之仆；最高者取了最低者的形态。我们忘记基督教传统中马利亚的力量：她回答天使加百列说，「我是主的使女，情愿照你的话成就在我身上。」我们忘记，做一个好仆人与做一个好主人同样宝贵、同样有分量；而且，好主人或好领袖应当成为下属的仆人。
We have also forgotten what I think of as the paradox of opposites, highlighted in some religious traditions, where the one who would be Master of all must be Servant of all, where the Highest takes the form of the Lowest. We have forgotten the power of Mary in the Christian tradition where she answers the angel Gabriel by saying, behold the slave of the Lord, be it done unto me according to thy word. We have forgotten that it is equally valuable and worthy to be a good servant as a good master - and, that a good master or leader should be the servant of those under them.
出处马利亚答加百列
见《路加福音》1:38。中文引文采用和合本「我是主的使女，情愿照你的话成就在我身上」；原书英语把传统 handmaid 改说 slave，强调最低者的服从。这里的悖论还呼应福音书中「为首者作众人仆人」的教导。
最重要的是，我们的文化需要找回最佳点、中间位置与平衡的价值，把它视为最好、最可欲、最有力量的位置。我们忘记了：若要有一种强有力的品质观与德性观，就需要平衡概念。原书 p.159
Most important of all is our cultural need to recover the value of the sweet spot, the balance, the middle position, as being the best, the most desirable and the most powerful. We have forgotten that we need the concept of balance to have a powerful concept of quality and of virtue.

永恒价值Eternal Values

这番讨论回接本书整个第二部，那里谈了永恒法则，包括永恒道德律。
This discussion ties back to the entire second section of this book where we discussed eternal law, including eternal moral law.
我们已经忘记如何提出意义问题，因为没有一项可以衡量意义的标准。
We have forgotten how to ask questions of meaning because we don't have a standard to measure meaning against.
这里我想回到亚里士多德及《尼各马可伦理学》，回到中道golden mean观念。在亚里士多德思想中，一切德性都关乎正确平衡；每一种德性都有一对相应恶习，分别向相反方向失衡。我们已经失去了平衡、德性以及何为「善」的整个观念。德性、道德律与平衡三个概念彼此补足，共同完整。
Here I want to look back to Aristotle and the Nicomachean Ethics, and the concept of the golden mean. In Aristotle's thinking all of the virtues are a matter of correct balance, and each virtue has a pair of corresponding vices which are unbalanced in opposite directions. We have lost that whole notion of balance, of virtue, of what the Good is. The concepts of virtue, of moral law, and of balance all complement and complete each other.
术语golden mean 译“中道”
亚里士多德的 mesotēs 不是把任何两端机械平均，而是「相对于我们」在具体情境中由实践智慧判断的适度；例如勇敢处在怯懦与鲁莽之间。中文伦理学通行译「中道」，这里不作容易让人想到统计数值的「黄金平均」。
我们的文化奉行「越多越好」，是数量文化，因此已经完全忘记：人生之善在于品格与德性，也就是与永恒法则对齐。放在时间与永恒的讨论中看，品格聚焦于我们死后带入永恒生命的东西。在柏拉图思想、基督教及许多其他传统中，我们最终只按自身德性受判断——不是银行账户，不是一连串成功，不是健康身体，不是活了多久，不是多么富有，也不是多么出名。
Given our culture of More is Better, a culture of quantity, we have completely lost the notion that the good of life is Character and Virtue, which is aligning with eternal law. Thinking in terms of our discussion of time and eternity, character focuses on what we bring forward after death into eternal life. In Platonic thought, and in Christian and many other traditions, the only things we are ever judged by is our virtues - not our bank account, not our string of successes, not our healthy body, not how long we lived, not how rich we were, not how famous we were.
本文前面列出的许多平衡问题，都可追溯到我们的文化缺乏土星德性，包括耐心与谦卑；也包括年龄、时间与经验带来的眼界，以及停下来缓慢、深入思考的能力。我们的文化大多缺乏深度，而这又是一项鲜明的土星性质。原书 p.160
Many of the balance problems I listed earlier in this essay trace back to our culture lacking the Saturnian virtues. This includes patience and humility. This also includes the perspective that comes with age, time and experience, the ability to stop and think slowly and deeply. Much of our culture lacks depth, another distinctly Saturnian quality.

土星与时间Saturn and Time

土星的许多含义都与时间及时间作用有关。与土星相联的一些负面作用——事物老化、腐朽、瓦解、衰退——全都是时间的直接作用。从周期来看，鲜明的土星作用出现在周期后段。土星与衰退而非成长、老年而非青春、腐朽而非生长、下降而非上升相联。
Many of the meanings of Saturn have to do with time, and the effects of time. Some of the negative effects associated with Saturn - things getting old, or decaying, or falling apart, or declining - are all directly the effects of time. In terms of cycles the distinctly Saturn effects come in late in the cycle. Saturn is associated with decline rather than growth, age rather than youth, decaying rather than growing. falling rather than rising.

考验Testing

时间的一部分作用是检验事物、试用事物，看看它们建造或组合得多么牢靠；它也检验老化的作用。即便建造良好的结构最终也会瓦解。
Part of the effect of time is to test things, to try things, to see how well they are built or put together. It also tests the effects of age. Even well built structures eventually fall apart.
土星也与后果有关。第一次土星回归first Saturn return常常令人意识到自己已经建造的事物、已经作出的选择，以及自己如何在时间中处理这些选择的后果。
Saturn also has to do with consequences. The first Saturn return is often about becoming aware of the things we have built and the choices we have made, and how we deal with the consequences of those choices through time.
背景第一次土星回归
土星约二十九年半绕黄道一周；因此出生后约二十九岁前后，行运土星第一次回到本命土星位置。现代占星常把这段时期与成年责任、事业关系结构及既往选择的后果联系起来。
选择与行动都和结构有关。一个人选择某项事业或某段婚姻，便选择了一个结构。它从好的意义上塑造人生，也会限制我们。一旦结构开始产生作用，我们就被限定在必须处理这些作用后果的位置上。
Choices and actions are related to structures. If a person chooses a particular career, or a particular marriage, that is a structure. It gives shape to our lives in a good sense, and it also confines us. Once it starts to have effects we are confined to dealing with the consequences of those effects.
选择也与惯性有关，而惯性同样属土星。进入一项事业与工作后，你会积累惯性——技能组合、薪水、声誉；一旦在某领域立足，就很难改变事业方向。尝试转行可能像从行驶中的火车跳下：职业生活的全部惯性都朝一个方向运动，转入新事业不只是从头开始，还要逆着整股惯性行动。你的工作履历是简历的一部分，会跟随你余生。
Choices also relate to momentum, which is also Saturnian. Once you get into a career and a job, you build up momentum - a skill set, a salary, a reputation - and it is difficult to change career areas once you are established in one area. Trying to change careers can feel like stepping off of a moving train; all of the momentum of your career life is moving in that one direction, and switching to a new career isn't just starting over, it is moving against all that momentum. Your job history is part of your resume, and it will follow you for the rest of your life.
习惯也一样，无论好坏。若你已经习惯睡前再从冰箱拿出巧克力冰淇淋吃最后一碗，明天冰淇淋就更可能自己从冰箱跳出来扑向你。越频繁地朝同一方向选择，下一次选择同一方向就越容易、越可能。建立新习惯，也包括一场对抗旧习惯惯性的斗争。原书 p.161
It is the same with habits, both good and bad. If you get used to repeatedly grabbing the chocolate ice cream out of the freezer for one last dish before you go to bed, it is more and more likely that the ice cream will jump out of the freezer at you tomorrow. The more often you choose in a single direction, the easier and more likely it is that your next choice will be in that same direction. Building a new habit also includes a struggle against the momentum of the old habit.
这与时间的单向运动及历史的恒久性有关。一件事一旦做过，就是做过，无法撤销。互联网与社交媒体无处不在，使我们尤其强烈地意识到这种作用。一旦把东西放上网络——帖子、照片、视频——它就永远在那里，任何人都可能发现。令人遗憾的是，翻旧账常被用作摧毁名誉的政治手段；你高中时一句欠考虑或不成熟的评论，四十年后仍会回来成为攻击你的证据。历史可能冷酷无情，时间也冷酷无情；人们，尤其身处没有面孔的群体时，也可能极其严酷无情地利用时间。这样严苛地判断人，正是土星作为凶星的一个好例子。
This is related to the one-way movement of time, and to the permanence of history. Once you have done something you have done it, and you cannot undo it. We are made aware of this effect in an intensified way with the ubiquity of the internet and of social media. Once you put something out on the web - a post, a picture, a video - it is out there forever for anyone to discover. It is unfortunate that digging up old dirt is often used as a political tactic to destroy reputations, so that a thoughtless or immature comment you made in high school can come back 40 years later to be held against you. History can be relentless, and Time is relentless - and people, especially in faceless crowds, can be very harsh and relentless in how they use time. Judging in this harsh way is a good example of Saturn as malefic.
移动的手指写下；写完，便继续向前：
你全部的虔诚与才智，也不能诱它折返，抹去半行；
你全部的泪水，也洗不掉其中一字。
——奥马尔·海亚姆，爱德华·菲茨杰拉德译
The Moving Finger writes; and, having writ,
Moves on: nor all thy Piety nor Wit
Shall lure it back to cancel half a Line,
Nor all thy Tears wash out a Word of it.
- Omar Khayyam, translated by Edgar Fitzgerald
出处《鲁拜集》的“移动手指”
这段出自菲茨杰拉德英译《鲁拜集》的著名四行诗，以写过便不停回返的手指比喻时间与历史不可撤销。中文在这里依本段论证直译，保留「虔诚、才智、泪水皆不能改字」的三重无力感，不借用可能版本不一的旧译。

结构的良好作用Good Effects of Structure

上一节主要把结构与习惯说成限制；但反面同样成立。随时间建起结构与惯性，也是成就有价值之事的一种方式。
In the previous section I talked mainly about structure and habits as being confining, but the opposite is true also. Building structure and momentum through time is also a way to accomplish worthwhile things.
建立一项事业需要时间，需要学习、经验、工作与品格。所有这些共同创造一个有价值的结构，并形成自身的正向惯性。原书 p.162
Building a career takes time, it takes learning, it takes experience, it takes work and character. All of those together create a worthwhile structure that has its own positive momentum.
好好养育一个孩子需要时间；稳定的家庭结构和牢固稳定的关系会支持这个过程。好好养育孩子需要预先考虑、规划、谨慎决定，也需要有意识地反复行动；这些全是土星式技能。
Raising a child well takes time. That process is aided by a stable family structure to support it, a strong stable relationship. Raising a child well takes forethought, planning and careful decision, and deliberate repeated action, and all of those are Saturnian sorts of skills.
掌握占星学这样的体系需要时间。若认真学习占星学，就应预备至少扎实投入十年学习、研究与实践，把体系吸收到足以熟练运用的程度。学习过程会随时间聚焦无数细节，直到你能对体系一举而知，形成整全理解。那种综合会赋予你眼界，使你知道应在特定情境中应用哪些含义。
Mastering a system like astrology takes time. If you are serious about astrology you should count on at least a good ten years of study, research and practice to assimilate the system to the point that you can do it well. The process of learning focuses on myriad details over time, until you reach the point of having all-at-once knowledge of the system, a unitive understanding. That synthesis gives the perspective to know what meanings to apply to a given situation.
提示从细节积累到整全之知
这里把第二部的智性／理性区分落实到学习过程：理性逐项处理规则、案例与细节；长期熟练后，判断者可以一眼把握整体格局。「十年」不是证书门槛，而是作者用来强调土星式时间、纪律与经验不可被速成替代。
学习并掌握任何有价值之物，都需要大量时间、工作与持续纪律，也会发展出精通所带来的理解深度；还需要许多耐心与坚忍。所有这些都是格外土星式的性质。
The process of learning and mastering anything worthwhile takes a great deal of time, of work and sustained discipline, and it develops a depth of understanding that comes with mastery. It also takes a lot of patience and of perseverance. All of those are particularly Saturnian qualities.

历史History

时间在历史中运动，另一个正面作用是产生历史意识，让人向时间与经验学习。现代文化基本失去了历史感，也失去了认识历史、尊重历史、向历史学习之价值感。
Another positive side to the movement of time through history is historical awareness, learning from time and experience. Our modern culture has largely lost a sense of history, the value of knowing it, of respecting it, and of learning from it.
与此相关的，是留意历史传下之物的价值，是把传统当作人类累积经验而心怀敬意。传统传递的是经受住时间考验之物。
Related to this is the value of paying attention to what has been passed down through history, of reverence for tradition as a cumulative human experience. Tradition passes on what has stood the test of time.
这与土星按周期运动的联系有关。历史意识教导我们：直到看见整个周期，才会理解整体的意义；直到看见一个行动在时间中充分展开，才会理解它。这又与时间映照永恒秩序相关：我们会学到，行动结果依循法则。只有看到整体秩序时才能看见这一点，而那需要时间。土星是空间与时间合在一起的结构；历史意识会增强对整个结构的觉察。原书 p.163
This relates to the connection of Saturn with moving in cycles. Historical awareness teaches us we don't understand the meaning of the whole until we see the entire cycle. We don't understand an action until we see it play out in time. This in turn is related to time as mirroring eternal order, learning that the results of our actions follow laws. We can only see that when we can see the whole order, and that takes time. Saturn is the structure of space and time together. An awareness of history increases awareness of the whole structure.

时间与年龄的智慧The Wisdom of Time and Age

这是一个非常丰富的概念，把土星含义的许多维度连接起来。
This is a very rich concept, and it ties in a lot of the dimensions of Saturn's meaning.
老年智慧的一部分，来自活得够久，见过风尚流行、消退，又再次归来；也活得够久，亲眼看见权力与名誉的兴衰。你会学到，历史不是一条直线式进步；它包含上下起伏、来回往复的周期。政党乘着声望浪潮经选举执政；随后浪潮消退，对立政党的浪潮上升并达到顶峰，它又在日后被投票赶下台。这里有一种来回节律，是相反事物的舞蹈与游戏。意识到时间中的完整周期，便会获得这种智慧。
Part of the wisdom of age comes with having lived long enough that you have seen fads and fashions come and go, and then return again. You've lived long enough to have watched rises and falls in power and reputation. You learn that history is not a straight line of progress; it involves cycles of up and down, back and forth. Political parties ride waves of popularity and are voted into power, only to be voted out of power down the road when their wave ebbs, and the opposite party's wave is rising and cresting. There is a rhythm back and forth, a dance and play of opposites. That is the wisdom that comes with awareness of whole cycles in time.

周期与“进步”Cycles and "Progress"

下面引文出自《传道书》。飞鸟乐队The Byrds把这段文字谱成热门单曲《转！转！转！》，使它进入流行音乐史。阅读时请想想：其中有多少内容逆着我们文化的习惯而行，这段周期文字又如何与前面的平衡一节共鸣。
The following quote is from Ecclesiastes. This passage made its way into pop music history when The Byrds made it a hit single with their song Turn, Turn, Turn. As you read it over, consider how much of this goes against the grain of our culture, and how this passage on cycles resonates with the earlier section on balance.
《传道书》第三章（英文本据 KJV；中译据和合本）原书 p.164
Ecclesiastes, Chapter 3, King James Version
1　凡事都有定期，天下万务都有定时。
2　生有时，死有时；栽种有时，拔出所栽种的也有时；
3　杀戮有时，医治有时；拆毁有时，建造有时；
4　哭有时，笑有时；哀恸有时，跳舞有时；
5　抛掷石头有时，堆聚石头有时；怀抱有时，不怀抱有时；
6　寻找有时，失落有时；保守有时，舍弃有时；
7　撕裂有时，缝补有时；静默有时，言语有时；
8　喜爱有时，恨恶有时；争战有时，和好有时。
1 To every thing there is a season, and a time to every purpose under the heaven:
2 A time to be born, and a time to die; a time to plant, and a time to pluck up that which is planted;
3 A time to kill, and a time to heal; a time to break down, and a time to build up;
4 A time to weep, and a time to laugh; a time to mourn, and a time to dance;
5 A time to cast away stones, and a time to gather stones together; a time to embrace, and a time to refrain from embracing;
6 A time to get, and a time to lose; a time to keep, and a time to cast away;
7 A time to rend, and a time to sew; a time to keep silence, and a time to speak;
8 A time to love, and a time to hate; a time of war, and a time of peace.
出处《传道书》3:1–8
中文采用和合本，并保留原书所引 KJV 英文。经文不是把两端都判为道德上同等，而是描写受造生活在时间中的不同季节；作者据此反对只承认成长、建造、欢笑与和平，却否认另一半周期。
请注意，这段经文怎样在成对的相反事物间来回运动。这正是我们文化之永恒进步理想的反题。周期性的相反配对，描述人类历史实际上如何随时间展开——不是应该发生什么，也不是我们希望发生什么，而是真正会发生什么。
Notice how this passage moves back and forth, in pairs of opposites. This is the antithesis of our culture's ideal of perpetual progress. Cyclic pairs describe how human history actually works out across time - not what should happen, or what we would like to happen, but what actually does happen.
我们的文化很大一部分建立在永恒直线成长与进步的预设上，期待改善无止境地延伸到未来。土星教导我们，现实并非如此运作。现实不是直线形，而是波浪形：随时间上下起伏、来回往复、周而复始。
A lot of our culture is built on the assumption of perpetual straight line growth and progress, unending improvement going on into the future. Saturn teaches us that reality doesn't work out like that. Reality isn't shaped like a straight line; it is wavy, going up and down, back and forth, around and around through time.
认识到这一点，会带来智慧与成熟。能够辨认自己处在周期哪个阶段——渐盈还是亏减、成长还是退收——并相应调整态度与行动，其中自有智慧。你所支持的政党赢得选举很容易接受，对立政党上台却不那么容易。好好获胜很容易，从容认输则非常困难。胜负都属于政治过程的来回往复，人生许多其他事情也是如此；能够同时面对两者，便是成熟。
There is a wisdom and maturity that comes with recognizing that. There is wisdom in being able to recognize what phase of a cycle you are in - waxing or waning, growing or pulling back - and adjusting your attitude and your actions accordingly. It is easy to deal with the political party you support winning an election, and not so easy to deal with the opposing party coming to power. It is easy to win well, but it is very difficult to concede defeat gracefully. Winning and losing are both part of the back and forth of the political process, as of so much else in life, and there is maturity in being able to deal with both.
商业世界里，大多数最终失败的公司，在生命周期的成长阶段都表现良好。多数公司崩溃在成长放缓时；那原本应是巩固阶段。典型的失败反应，是把早期成长与扩张阶段投射到不定的未来，期待成长继续。随后，当成长减速，仍在扩张的产量与库存和吸收这些产品的市场之间日益失配，企业便像过度充气的气球一样濒临爆裂。原书 p.165
In the business world, most companies that end up failing do just fine during the growth part of the life cycle. Where most companies crash is during the phase where growth slows down, a period that should be a consolidation phase. The typical failing response is to project the early phase of growth and expansion forward into an indefinite future, expecting that growth to continue. Then, when growth slows down, there is an increasing mismatch between expanding output and inventory, and the market for that output. This becomes a setup for a business collapsing like an over-inflated balloon.
用数学语言说，这就是把短期趋势外推成一条向前延伸的直线，假定它会无限期朝同一方向运动。因此，我们才会看到那么多可怕预言，宣称若不立刻改变做法，不久的未来将发生什么。我一生所见的末日预言已经绰绰有余：若不立即做某件事，文明将在多少年后终结。我写作此刻，新闻标题中也有一些这样的预言，主角是惊恐的年轻人；他们深信若现在不行动，世界将在十年后终结。
In mathematical terms, that is taking a short term trend and extrapolating it forward as a straight line, assuming it will continue to move in the same direction indefinitely. That is why we see so many dire predictions about what will happen in the near future if we don't immediately change our ways. I have seen more than my share of apocalyptic predictions in my lifetime on how civilization will end in x years if we don't start doing y immediately. There are some of those in the news headlines today as I write this, featuring terrified young people who are sure the world will end in ten years if we don't do something now.
然而，生活继续。
Instead, life goes on.
这是最好的时代，也是最坏的时代；这是智慧的时代，也是愚蠢的时代；这是信仰的纪元，也是怀疑的纪元；这是光明的季节，也是黑暗的季节；这是希望之春，也是绝望之冬；我们面前无所不有，我们面前一无所有；我们全都直上天堂，我们全都直奔另一个方向——简而言之，这个时期与当今时期如此相似，以至其中一些最喧嚷的权威坚持认为，无论说它好还是坏，都只能使用最高级。
——查尔斯·狄更斯
It was the best of times, it was the worst of times, it was the age of wisdom, it was the age of foolishness, it was the epoch of belief, it was the epoch of incredulity, it was the season of Light, it was the season of Darkness, it was the spring of hope, it was the winter of despair, we had everything before us, we had nothing before us, we were all going direct to Heaven, we were all going direct the other way—in short, the period was so far like the present period, that some of its noisiest authorities insisted on its being received, for good or for evil, in the superlative degree of comparison only.
- Charles Dickens
出处《双城记》开篇
狄更斯以一连串相反最高级描写法国大革命前夕；重点不在折中抹平，而在同一时代会同时容纳彼此冲突的真实。它与本章的周期观点相呼应：只抓「最好」或「最坏」的一端，都会失去全貌。
认识并应对完整周期与不同阶段，会带来真正的智慧。它随时间、眼界以及看见整个过程而来——也就是知觉中的土星式成长。原书 p.166
There is a real wisdom that comes with recognizing and dealing with whole cycles and phases. That comes with time, with perspective, with seeing the whole process - with Saturn sorts of growth in perception.

轮回的周期Cycles of Reincarnation

现代占星学与流行灵性模型中有一个有趣的反讽。流行的多生轮回观念有东方哲学根源，主要经由神智学的影响传入。有趣的是，今天的典型观念却把它理解成不断改善的一连串生命，是把进步放大到多世。这是轮回加演化。
There is an interesting irony in the model of modern astrology and popular spirituality. The popular notion of multiple lives and reincarnation has eastern philosophical roots, mostly by way of their influence through Theosophy. What is interesting is that the typical concept today is of an ever improving series of lives, progress writ large across multiple lifetimes. This is reincarnation plus evolution.
实际的东方轮回体系根本不是进步式或演化式的；它完全关乎周期，是经过难以想象之漫长时间的巨大周期。业力是一只最终只是旋转、哪儿也不去的轮。佛陀对业力与受苦之轮的回答，不是改善它，而是脱离轮转。从哲学上说，这几乎直接否定了现代永恒进步与扩张观。
The actual Eastern system of reincarnation is not progressive or evolutionary at all. It is all about cycles, enormous cycles through mind-boggling lengths of time. Karma is a wheel that ultimately just revolves and goes nowhere. The Buddha's answer to that wheel of karma and suffering is not to improve things, but to get off the wheel. Philosophically it is very nearly the direct negation of the modern notion of perpetual progress and expansion.
校记“东方体系”是概括性说法
作者用单数概括「实际的东方轮回体系」，便于与神智学进步论对照；但印度教、佛教、耆那教内部对轮回、解脱与修行进路并不完全相同。这里可保留的共同点是：轮回本身通常被视为有待解脱的循环，而不是无限升级即为最终目的。
把轮回理解为演化进步，也与柏拉图《理想国》末章厄尔神话所阐述的西方轮回模型大不相同。（本书附录有一篇长文讨论该章及其含义。）在柏拉图模型中，生命倾向交替：正面生命通常遭到滥用，导致两世之间的纠正期；困难或负面生命则通常激起正面回应，带来两世之间的休息与奖赏期。它来回往复、上下起伏，没有简单的线性进步；世与世之间的推进本身是周期性的。即使存在持续成长，也很缓慢，伴随许多起落。
The concept of reincarnation as evolutionary progress is also very different from the Western model of reincarnation spelled out in the Platonic Myth of Er from the final chapter of Plato's Republic. (I include a long essay on that chapter and its meaning in the appendix to this book.) In Plato's model the lives tend to alternate, with positive lives typically being abused and leading to periods of correction between lives, while difficult or negative lives typically bring about positive responses that lead to periods of rest and reward between lives. It is back and forth, up and down, with no simple linear progress. The progression between lives is itself cyclic. If there is an ongoing growth it is slow, with many ups and downs
这正是土星作为周期意识的观念进入之处。原书 p.167
Here the notion of Saturn as awareness of cycles comes in.
凡上升者必会下降
旋转的轮，转个不停
What goes up must come down
Spinning wheel spinning round
它是一架跑步机、一方打谷场，也是一座磨坊，让麦子在旋转的磨石之间被碾碎——神的磨石转得缓慢，却磨得精细无比。
It is a treadmill, a threshing floor, and also a mill that grinds wheat between revolving stones - millstones that grind slowly but grind exceeding fine.
背景轮、打谷场与磨石
三个意象都以重复运动产生结果：跑步机让人劳作却不前进，打谷场借踩踏分离谷粒，磨石在缓慢旋转中把谷物磨细。末句还借用「神的磨坊磨得慢，却磨得极细」的古老谚语，把周期同迟来而精确的后果连接起来。
它也是伟大、有节律、有周期的「呼吸」：扩张之后是收缩，吸气之后是呼气；成长之后是衰退。文化通常很容易面对周期的成长部分，却难以面对衰退部分；面对后者，典型反应是困惑、难以置信与否认。看看四周吧。
It is also the great rhythmic and cyclic BREATH where expansion is followed by contraction, in breath by out breath. Growth is followed by decline, and cultures typically have a much easier time dealing with the growth part of a cycle than dealing with the decline part, which typically is reacted to with confusion, disbelief and denial. Look around.

客观性Objectivity

这种周期意识与客观立场相关：人能够从当下时代后退一步，批评性地审视它。只重视新、当下与时尚，有一项巨大弱点：这种立场不给你任何外部视角，无法借此意识到当下风尚的失衡与盲点。因此，历史感与完整周期感非常重要，也与客观性相联。
This awareness of cycles is related to a stance of objectivity, being able to stand back from one's current age and critique it. There is a great weakness in valuing only the new, the current, the fashionable. Such a stance gives you no outside perspective from which to become aware of the unbalances and blind spots of the current fads. This is where a sense of history and of entire cycles is so important and is also related to a sense of objectivity.
可以看到，这也与其他土星主题相接。周期意识与历史意识，同接受命运变迁、接受短暂易逝、接受衰退与死亡的不可避免相连。土星与历史意识也涉及对立的含义。客观性就是站到当下风尚的对面，以取得眼界。
You can see that this also ties in with other Saturnian themes. Cyclic & historic awareness is connected with accepting changes of fortune, accepting ephemerality, accepting the inevitability of decline and death. Saturn and historical awareness also is related to the meaning of opposition. Objectivity is standing over against the current day's fads to get perspective.

传统Tradition

写作本书的一项主要目标，是批评现代文化中反对保存传统的偏见，以及总是喜新厌旧的倾向。它会造成可怕损失，使极其丰富宝贵的资源消失。轻率抛开传统的人，失去一切向传统学习的机会，人生也因此变得贫乏。原书 p.168
One of my main goals in writing this book is to critique the modern bias in our culture against preserving tradition, the tendency to always favor the new over the old. This leads to a horrible loss of very rich and valuable resources. It impoverishes the lives of those who brush off tradition too lightly, as they lose all chance of learning from it.
占星学历史的大部分时期里，人们对传统的一般态度都是尊重。传统教导的整体被视为有价值的成果，应当传承，也应当向它学习。我们已经看到，这种传统态度与现代世界多么不同；后者极度重视进步，重视新胜于旧。我们的世界高度面向未来，预设古老就是过时，旧真理会被新真理取代。
The general attitude throughout most of astrology's history has been one of respect for tradition. The body of traditional teaching is viewed as a valuable work to be passed down and to be learned from. We have seen how very different this traditional attitude is from our modern world which so highly values progress and the new over the old. Our world is very much future oriented, assuming that old means outmoded, and that old truths are superseded by new truths.
这种态度差异会造成传统与现代占星师之间的沟通问题，因为双方使用十分不同、而自己多半没有意识到的参照框架。一方面，重视旧有内容的传统占星师会认为，现代占星学创新太快，或尚未透彻理解传统含义便将其抛弃。另一方面，现代占星师从不同视角出发，会把传统占星师看成害怕改变创新、困在过去。
This difference in attitude causes communication problems between traditional and modern astrologers, since they are using very different frames of reference, likely without being conscious of it. On the one hand, a traditional astrologer who values the old would find a problem with modern astrology innovating too quickly, or discarding traditional meanings without thoroughly understanding them first. On the other hand, a modern astrologer, coming from a different point of view, sees the traditional astrologer as afraid of change and innovation, and stuck in the past.
我们对传统的态度与对时间的态度相关，而这些都是非常土星式的问题。若要向土星学习，就必须向时间、历史与传统学习。值得留意传统，不可轻率抛弃。这套教导之所以世代相传而基本不变，自有原因：人们一再发现它有价值，它经受住了时间考验。
Our attitude towards tradition is related to our attitude towards time, and these are very Saturnine sorts of issues. To learn from Saturn we need to learn from time, from history, from tradition. It is worth paying attention to tradition and not discarding it lightly. There is a reason that this body of teaching has been passed down essentially unchanged for generations. People have repeatedly found it valuable, and it has stood the test of time.
这种对传统的正面态度，会尊重前代人的智识与判断；我有时觉得，现代态度缺少这种尊重。作为传统占星师，我承认，看到现代著作高谈「扫除中世纪迷信的蛛网」时，我很受伤，因为所谓中世纪迷信正是我实践的占星学风格。我不知道这种态度是否有意居高临下，但读来确实如此，也带着一种格外现代的傲慢。
This positive attitude towards tradition shows respect for the intelligence and judgment of the generations that have gone before, and I sometimes find that respect lacking in modern attitudes. As a traditional astrologer I confess it hurts when I see modern books talking about "sweeping away the cobwebs of medieval superstition" when that medieval superstition is the style of astrology I practice. I do not know if that attitude is deliberately condescending, but it definitely comes across as condescending, and as having a peculiarly modern sort of arrogance.
提示双方使用的时间标尺不同
传统占星师问的是「这套方法经历多少代检验」；现代占星师问的是「它是否适合当下、能否继续创新」。同一句「古老」，在前者那里可表示可靠传承，在后者那里却可表示过时。先看见这组参照系差异，才可能真正讨论某项旧法是否仍有用。
重视传统的另一个好处，是让人有所仰望。现代世界强调进步与未来；你不向上仰望，只向外扩张。带着这种态度，就没有高于自身的标准，可以纠正当下一刻的想法。你需要历史感与传统感，才能从现代世界的当下风尚后退一步，取得审视它们的眼界。原书 p.169
Another one of the good things about valuing tradition is that it gives you something to look up to. In our modern world, with its emphasis on progress and the future, you don't look up, you look out to expand. With such an attitude there is no standard greater than yourself to be a corrective to your current thoughts of the moment. You need a sense of history and tradition to be able to stand back from the modern world's current fads and get a sense of perspective on them.
这里有几项鲜明的土星德性在起作用。传统传递经受住时间考验的材料；而时间非常善于把值得之物与无价值之物筛分开来。再深入一步：若永恒法则与结构确实存在，而我们试图从永恒到时间去把握或模拟它们，那么经受住时间与传统考验之物，最可能与那些永恒法则相应。这些材料能存续，恰恰因为它们拥有真实价值。
There are distinctly Saturnian virtues at play here. Tradition passes on the material which stands the test of time, and time is very good at sorting out what is worthy from what is worthless. Going a bit deeper than that, given the thesis that there are eternal laws and structures that we are attempting to apprehend or model from the eternal to the temporal, then that which stands the test of time and tradition most likely corresponds with those eternal laws. This material survives precisely because it has real value.
希望占星世界如今已经来到一个节点，可以开始统合、综合现代与传统各自的精华。今天的一项任务，就是判断现代占星学——二十世纪的占星学——有哪些部分可能经受住时间考验。
Hopefully we are at a point in the world of astrology that we can start to look at integrating and synthesizing the best of modern and traditional. One of our tasks today is to see what of modern astrology, the astrology of the 20th century, is likely to stand the test of time.
我提出一项检验现代占星特色之价值的有效方法：看新特色如何与既有传统互动。它是否建立在传统之上、扩展传统，或进一步照亮传统？还是削弱、忽视、违背传统？我们现在可以让传统与现代占星学各自最好的部分展开对话、彼此学习。
I propose that a useful test of the value of modern astrology features would be to consider how the new feature interacts with the existing tradition. Does a new feature build on, extend, or shed further light on the tradition? Or does it diminish it, ignore it, contradict it? We can now look at the best of traditional and modern astrology dialoging and learning from each other.

天王星与土星Uranus and Saturn

前面我提出一段称为「切斯特顿检验」的文字：把抽象概念或含混短语，改用一两个音节的简单具体词语重述。下面是同一位伟大作家、思想家提出的另一项检验，我见过有人把它称作：原书 p.170
Earlier I laid out a passage I called the Chesterton Test, restating concept or vague phrases in simple concrete words of one or two syllables. This is another test from the same the same great writer and thinker, that I have seen referred to as,

切斯特顿的栅栏Chesterton's Fence

「谈到改革事物——这与使事物变形不同——有一条清楚而简单的原则；它多半会被人称为悖论。
"In the matter of reforming things, as distinct from deforming them, there is one plain and simple principle; a principle which will probably be called a paradox.
在这种情形里，某个制度或法则已经存在；为简单起见，假设道路上横着一道栅栏或大门。较现代的改革者兴高采烈地走上前，说：『我看不出这有什么用；把它清掉吧。』
There exists in such a case a certain institution or law; let us say, for the sake of simplicity, a fence or gate erected across a road. The more modern type of reformer goes gaily up to it and says, 'I don't see the use of this; let us clear it away.'
较聪明的改革者最好这样回答：『正因为你看不出它有什么用，我才不会让你清掉它。回去想一想。等你回来，能告诉我你确实看懂了它的用处，我也许会允许你摧毁它。』」
To which the more intelligent type of reformer will do well to answer: 'If you don't see the use of it I won't let you clear it away.''Go away and think. Then, when you can come back and tell me you do see the use of it, I may allow you to destroy it.'"
这个悖论建立在最基本的常识上。
This paradox rests on the most elementary common sense.
大门或栅栏不是自己从那里长出来的，也不是梦游者睡着时建起来的。它由逃出疯人院、因为某种原因在街上游荡的疯子安放在那里的可能性，也微乎其微。
The gate or fence did not grow there. It was not set up by somnambulists who built it in their sleep. It is highly improbable that it was put there by escaped lunatics who were for some reason loose in the street.
总有某个人出于某种理由，认为它会对某人有好处。在弄清理由之前，我们实在无法判断那个理由是否合理。若一个和我们一样的人设立的东西，看起来全然无意义、神秘莫测，那极可能是我们漏掉了问题的整个一个面向。
Some person had some reason for thinking it would be a good thing for somebody. And until we know what the reason was, we really cannot judge whether the reason was reasonable. It is extremely probable that we have overlooked some whole aspect of the question, if something set up by human beings like ourselves seems to be entirely meaningless and mysterious.
有些改革者假定自己的祖辈全是傻瓜，以此越过这项困难；但若果真如此，我们只能说愚蠢看来是一种遗传病。事实却是：在真正把一个社会制度看作历史制度以前，任何人都没有资格摧毁它。原书 p.171
There are reformers who get over this difficulty by assuming that all their fathers were fools; but if that be so, we can only say that folly appears to be a hereditary disease. But the truth is that nobody has any business to destroy a social institution until he has really seen it as an historical institution.
若他知道制度如何产生、原本要服务哪些目的，他或许真能判断：那些目的本来就坏，后来变坏，或制度已不再服务那些目的。但若他只是瞪着它，把它看成不知怎的冒到路上的荒诞怪物，那么陷入幻觉的是他，而不是传统主义者。」
——G. K. 切斯特顿
If he knows how it arose, and what purposes it was supposed to serve, he may really be able to say that they were bad purposes, that they have since become bad purposes, or that they are purposes which are no longer served. But if he simply stares at the thing as a senseless monstrosity that has somehow sprung up in his path, it is he and not the traditionalist who is suffering from an illusion.”
- GK Chesterton
提示栅栏检验不禁止改革
检验要求的是先恢复制度的历史功能，再决定保留、改造或拆除。看懂用处之后，切斯特顿明确说「也许允许你摧毁」；所以这不是「凡旧皆对」，而是反对把「我没看出用处」误当作「它从来没有用处」。
这些文章一直强烈批评一种常见刻板描述：天王星与土星象征开明青年冲破古老过时结构。这已经是我们文化延续一百多年的陈词滥调。正统不再流行，至少自十九世纪末起，异端与反叛便一直是潮流。「新优于旧」如今本身已成一个古老观念，一道思想车辙。
In these essays I have been quite critical of the usual stereotyped description of Uranus and Saturn as signifying the enlightened youth smashing through old and outmoded structures. This has been a cliche of our culture for well over a hundred years. Orthodoxy is out of fashion, and heresy and rebellion have been the trend since at least the late 1800's. The idea that the new is better than the old, is now an old idea, a mental rut.
在我们这个易变世界里，没有任何结构是绝对的，也没有有限形式能永远存续。没有国家永存，没有组织永存；物质世界的一切结构最终都会瓦解。
Here within our mutable world no structure is absolute and no finite form lasts forever. No country lasts forever, no organization lasts forever. All structures in the material world eventually fall apart.
这意味着，地上的宗教结构没有任何一个能永远垄断全部真理；地上的教会没有任何一个能完全体现并容纳唯一真正的教会。任何教会传统都面临一项挑战：在一条细线上行走，一面守护传下来的真理传统，一面避免把自己看成唯一、完整、最终而无谬之真理的唯一守护者。我们建造的任何结构，都只能指向真理，无法完全容纳它。
This means that no earthly religious structure has an eternal monopoly on the entire truth. No earthly church can ever completely embody and contain the one true church. The challenge of any church tradition is to walk the fine line between being the guardians of a tradition of truths that have been passed down, whil avoiding thinking of themselves as the only guardians of the only complete, final and infallible truth. Any structure we build can only point to truth without completely containing it.
校记whil 当作 while
原书 whil avoiding 显为 while avoiding 的脱字。中文径按「同时避免」翻译。
这也意味着，传统占星学不会只因为是传统就更好；最古老的文献也不会仅凭年代最早便按定义更优越。它们值得尊重与学习吗？当然。它们是否含有许多有价值、甚至受神启发而具永恒价值的内容？我深信如此。最早文献是否在所有时代都完整而具有最终权威？不是。原书 p.172
This also means that traditional astrology is not better just because it is traditional. The oldest texts are not by definition superior just because they are oldest. Are they worth respecting and learning from? Goodness, yes. Do they contain much of value, even of divinely inspired and eternal value? I am convinced that is true. Are the earliest texts complete and definitive for all time? No.
这是问题的一面。
That is one side of the question.
另一面则有这样的危险：仅仅因为某个体系古老，便认为它过时、应该摧毁；仅仅因为某物崭新，便按定义认为新优于旧。还有一种危险：认为一个确实应当拆除的旧结构，内部便没有任何有价值之物值得保存。
On the opposite side, there is the danger of thinking that any system is outmoded and ready to be destroyed just because it is old, and that the new is better by definition just because it is new. There is also the danger of thinking that an old structure that is ready to be destroyed has nothing of value in it to be preserved.
土星不总是坏人，天王星也不总是好人。
Saturn is not always the bad guy, and Uranus is not always the good guy.
天王星摧毁并扰乱过时形式，也会摧毁并扰乱美丽、有价值、有用的事物。炸弹不区分好人坏人，只会爆炸；雷击也不会依有用与否、价值高低选择目标。
Uranus destroys and disrupts outmoded forms, but it also destroys and disrupts things of beauty and value and usefulness. Bombs don't discriminate between good and bad people, they just blow up. Lightning strikes don't choose their targets on the grounds of usefulness or value.
天王星的另一面——心理作用——是可能表现成极端个人主义，只因权威是权威便采取反叛一切权威的姿态。
The other side of Uranus, the psychological effect, is that it can manifest as an extreme individualism, a stance of rebellion over against any authority just because it is authority.
外行星的危险恰恰在这里：它们是「外在的」，超出我们通常现实的边界。与它们作个人认同存在危险；那是狂妄、自我膨胀的危险。
The danger of the outer planets is just that - they are outer, beyond the bounds of our usual reality. There is a danger with personally identifying with them, and it is the danger of hubris, ego inflation.
天王星似乎确实与年轻及创新相连，也对它们具有特殊吸引力；但夸大重要性、只因某物新便认为它比实际更好更重要，是年轻人典型的失败，也可能造成严重失衡。这同样是整个青春导向文化的典型失败。
Uranus does seem to have a connection with, and a particular attraction for, the young and with innovation - and this tendency of inflated importance, to think the new is better and more important than it really is just because it is new, is a characteristic failing of youth, one that can be a source of serious unbalance. It is also a characteristic failing of our youth-oriented culture as a whole.
举一个极端例子：对天王星的迷恋可能表现成一个自以为正义的恐怖分子，投掷炸弹摧毁巨型建筑——同时杀死数百人，毁掉无价书籍与艺术品。它可能为了扰乱而扰乱，为了反叛而反叛。原书 p.173
In an extreme example, an obsession with Uranus could play out as a self-righteous terrorist hurling a bomb to destroy a massive building - and, in the process, killing hundreds of people and destroying priceless books and art works. It can be disruption for the sake of disruption, rebellion for the sake of rebellion.
天王星、海王星、冥王星三颗现代外行星都有这种迷恋与自我膨胀的危险，只要你与它们作过于私人、过于紧密的认同。它们不受通常人类人格边界约束。认同它们，就像认同一位神或天使一样危险：你把神的力量误认成自己的。你可能陷入幻想，以为自己已经成神；实际上，却只是一个以人类情感与欲望为食的低阶魔灵之傀儡。
All three of the modern outer planets, Uranus, Neptune and Pluto, have this danger of obsession and ego inflation if you identify with them too personally and closely. They are not bound by the usual limits of human personality. Identifying with them has the same kind of hazard as identifying with a god or angel, and you mistaking the god's power for your own. You can get caught up in imagining you have become a god yourself, while in actuality you are the puppet of a low level demon that feeds off of human emotion and desire.
术语此处 demon 不译“代蒙”
柏拉图语境的 daemon 是中性的神人中介，锁定译「代蒙」；本段 low level demon 明指以情欲为食、操纵人的低阶有害灵体，故译「魔灵」。若仍译「代蒙」，就会把前文黄金时代的治理者与此处危险形象混成一类。
所有外行星都可能令人迷醉振奋，让人感到被带出通常人类人格的边界；它们会使你觉得强大、无敌、无边、灵性、觉悟、无限而不朽。这就是它们的吸引、魅惑，也是它们的危险。
All of the outer planets can be intoxicating and exhilarating. All of the outer planets can give the sensation of being carried beyond the limits of the usual human personality. They can make you feel powerful, invincible, boundless, spiritual, enlightened, infinite, immortal. That is their attraction, that is their fascination, and that is their danger.
天王星、海王星与冥王星被发现时，人类并没有立刻演化得更高；正如原子弹发明时，人类也没有立刻变得更成熟、更负责任。
Humanity did not immediately become more evolved when Uranus, Neptune and Pluto were discovered, any more than humanity immediately became more mature and responsible when the atomic bomb was invented.
现代行星似乎确有作用，但作用处于另一层级；它们若要在显现中作用于人，只能通过传统七曜发挥。我的朋友兼老师本·戴克斯有一种说法，我很喜欢：它们还不完全是行星，却又比恒星多些什么；若与一颗传统行星紧密对齐，就值得留意。把自己限制在传统行星内，也提醒我们：在地上这个易变时空现实里，需要尊重人生的边界。
The modern planets do seem to have effects, but they are on another level, and they can work on us in manifestation only by acting through the traditional seven. I like the description of my friend and teacher Ben Dykes - they are not quite planets, but they are something more than fixed stars, and are worth paying attention to if they are in close alignment with a traditional planet. Limiting ourselves to the traditional planets reminds us that we need to respect the limits of human life here on earth in the mutable reality of time and space.
提示“通过传统七曜作用”是作者的技术立场
这里不是现代天文学分类：天王、海王、冥王当然是太阳系天体。奥伯特提出的是占星解释层级——传统七曜构成显现世界的基本语法，外行星只在紧密接触七曜时加以调制。这属于他的实践主张，正文照译，不当作普遍占星共识。
所以，作为传统占星师，我把太阳系结构的界线画在传统七曜。占星宇宙的传统秩序两千多年基本原样传承；我不会轻率丢弃这样的传统。本书的一部分目的，就是在现代科学对太阳系之发现的脉络中，以现代人可以理解的方式重新思考这一传统。原书 p.174
That is why, as a traditional astrologer, I draw the line of the structure of the solar system with the traditional seven planets. The traditional order of the astrology cosmos was passed down essentially unchanged for over two thousand years. I do not discard such a tradition lightly, and part of the purpose of this book is to re-think that tradition in ways that are relatable to us in the modern world in the context of modern scientific findings about the solar system.
用切斯特顿的话说，那道栅栏之所以设在那里，自有原因。我想我已经理解原因，并希望在这里保存其中最好的部分。
In Chesterton's terms, that fence was put there for a reason. I think I understand that reason, and I wish to preserve the best of that reason here.
一旦陷入「新按定义优于旧」的刻板态度——我们的文化非常强烈地向此倾斜——便有一种危险：以变化与进步之名，不加思索地抛弃传统中真正有价值的部分。
When you fall into the stereotyped attitude that new is better than old by definition - and our culture very heavily leans in that direction - there is a danger of thoughtlessly discarding aspects of the tradition that have real value, in the name of change and progress.
年轻人可以通过学习传统累积的智慧而受益。有些事物之所以数百、数千年都被当作宝贵之物传承，自有原因；这是累积经验，是一代又一代人检验并发现珍贵的材料。轻率抛弃它，是青春典型的狂妄。
Youth can benefit by learning from the accumulated wisdom of tradition. There is a reason some things were passed down for hundreds or thousands of years as having value. This is accumulated experience, this is material that generation after generation has tested and found precious. To discard that lightly is the characteristic hubris of youth.
今天，在青春导向的文化里，土星的角色是后退一步，站到当下风尚之外的客观位置，劝人谨慎。当现代占星学的讯息与当前潮流过于完全、紧密地对齐——当我们把占星学视为推动社会变革的工具，把它等同于最新流行政治纲领——土星便在那里发出警告。
Today, in our youth oriented culture, it is the role of Saturn to stand back, in an objective position, outside of the fad of the moment, and advise caution. When the message of modern astrology aligns too completely and closely with the current fad - when we think of astrology as a tool for social change and identify it with the latest popular political platform - Saturn is there to give a warning.
这一切也会过去。
This too shall pass.

土星与冥王星Saturn and Pluto

把天王星、海王星、冥王星三颗现代行星引入占星体系，远不只是给家族增加三个新成员；它还彻底改变了传统七曜的含义。原书 p.175
Introducing the 3 modern planets, Uranus, Neptune and Pluto, to the system of astrology, did much more than just adding 3 new members to the family. It also drastically changed the meanings of the traditional 7 planets.
外行星从传统的神圣七曜Sacred Seven取走了一部分含义。在这个过程中，传统七曜的含义不再那么丰富复杂，变薄，也不再那么多维。这部分含义变薄，还来自一种现代倾向：想把行星含义精简化、概念化。我们常看到每颗行星只获得一个核心关键词或概念，作为把握其含义的方法；甚至有些传统占星学教师也这样做。
The outer planets have taken some of their meanings away from the traditional Sacred Seven planets. In the process, the meanings of the traditional seven have become less rich and complex, thinned down, less multi-dimensional. Part of this thinning down of meaning comes from a modern tendency to want to streamline and conceptualize the meanings of the planets. You often see that each planet is given a single core keyword or concept as a way to grasp its meaning. This is done even by some teachers of traditional astrology.
旧文献中的行星含义更广、更具体，也远远更杂乱——我甚至想称它们为轶事式。例如，现代占星学会说土星象征你在哪里感到恐惧；传统文献则会列举：土星与墓地、黑暗处、地下、皮肤、骨骼与牙齿、老人、关节炎之类的疾病、黑色、冬季等等相联。土星的含义以一组具体对应或特定脉络中的含义给出；你必须逐渐体会这些不同项目如何彼此契合。从概念而非具体对应出发，往往会使过程与含义变薄。
In the older texts the meanings of the planets are broader, more concrete, and much, much messier - I'm tempted to call them anecdotal. For instance, instead of modern astrology saying that Saturn signifies where you have fear, a traditional text would list how Saturn is associated with graveyards, dark places, underground, the skin, the bones and teeth, old people, diseases like arthritis, the color black, winter, and so on. The meaning of Saturn is given as a set of concrete associations or specific contextual meaning, and you have to feel your way into how these different items all fit together. Starting with concepts rather than concrete associations tends to thin out the process and the meaning.
提示“杂乱”也是信息
传统清单里的墓地、牙齿、黑色与冬天不能全由一个心理关键词直接推出，却会在不同问题中提供具体判断。作者并不否认核心概念便于教学；他提醒，先从抽象词出发，容易把暂时解释不出的名物当成冗余删除。
除了变得更抽象、更单维之外，原本与传统行星相联的一些含义还被转移给现代外行星，进一步造成含义丰富度的损失。
In addition to becoming more abstract and one-dimensional, some of the meanings originally associated with the traditional planets were transferred to the modern outer planets, which in turn led to a loss of richness of meaning.
为说明这一点，我想看看如今与冥王星相联的多少含义，原本都属于土星。
To illustrate this, I want to look at how many of the meanings now associated with Pluto were originally associated with Saturn.
首先列出一些通常与冥王星相联的概念与关键词：原书 p.176
First, let's list some of the common concepts and keywords that are associated with Pluto.
死亡；
death
转化；
transformation
迫使人改变的强制压力；
a compulsive pressure to change
摧毁旧形式，让新形式得以诞生；
destroying old forms so that new forms can be born
需要放下旧事物；
a need to let go of old things
压力；
pressure
随时间发生的转化过程——纯粹的时间流逝就是冥王星压力的一部分。
a process of transformation over time - sheer passage of time is part of the Plutonian pressure
上述每一项含义原本都与土星相联。下面进一步思考。
Every one of those meanings was originally associated with Saturn. Let us consider that further.
土星就是死亡。死亡的形象是一位极老的人或一具骷髅，穿宽松黑色兜帽长袍，手持收割镰刀；这是土星的象征。土星是骷髅、骨骼、牙齿，也是老人。镰刀作为收获工具，属于死亡象征的一部分。
Saturn is death. The figure of death, a very old person or a skeleton, in a loose black hooded robe, carrying a scythe for the harvest, is a symbol of Saturn. Saturn is skeleton, bones, teeth. Saturn is old people. The scythe is part of the symbolism of death as a harvest.
土星是克洛诺斯、时间老人，代表物质现实中、随时间展开的一生之限制与结构。土星是时间的作用。
Saturn is Kronos, Father Time, representing the limitations and structure of a life within material reality and over time. Saturn is the effects of time.
旧文献把土星与地下场所、死亡及死亡领域——冥界——相联；而这恰恰是我们如今理解冥王星、死亡与冥界之主的方式。
In older texts Saturn is associated with places underground, with death, and with the realm of death, the underworld, which is precisely how we think of Pluto, lord of death and the underworld.
土星行运以施加改变压力著称。它是考验者；任何建造不良或已经过了下架期限的结构，都会被摧毁并转化。想想你的土星回归！这正是如今归给冥王星的那种改变压力。
Saturn transits are notorious for applying pressure for change. It is a tester, and any structure that is poorly built or is past its pull date will be destroyed and transformed. Think about your Saturn return! It is precisely that sort of pressure to change that we now associate with Pluto.
术语pull date
pull date 是商品到期应从货架撤下的日期，近于「下架期限」。作者把结构比作过期商品：不是只因古老就要拆，而是已无法安全发挥原有功能。
土星行运还有一种冷酷诚实的压力。你不能靠任何伪装或虚假蒙混过关；一切被剥到核心本质，然后接受考验。它照亮弱点与脆弱处，改变压力正从那里驱使你。若与改变合作，它可以是渐进成长；若抗拒或忽视改变，就可能骤然发生、带来灾变。事物会在土星无情压力下破裂。原书 p.177
There is also a sort of ruthless pressure of honesty with Saturn transits. You can't get away with any pretense or sham, and you are stripped down to the core essentials and tried. It illuminates your weak points, your vulnerabilities, and that is where you are driven to change. If you work with the change it can be gradual growth. If you resist or ignore the change it can be abrupt, cataclysmic. Things break under the relentless pressure of Saturn.
现在重读上一段，把土星换成冥王星。你一个字也不必改。
Now take that previous paragraph and re-read it, substituting Pluto for Saturn. You would not need to change a single word.
我还想考察现代土星含义以另一种方式被简化、变成单维，用来衬托或对比外行星。现代占星学常把土星与现状、既有结构及抗拒改变相联。
I want to look at another way that the modern meaning of Saturn has been simplified and made one-dimensional, to provide a contrast or foil to the outer planets. In modern astrology Saturn is often associated with the status quo, with existing structures, and with resisting change.
然而，土星也象征随时间改变的过程。土星的确是旧结构与旧格局；但土星既是时间，也就是改变的压力，是打破旧格局、为新事物让路的力量。土星是骨骼，也是年龄压断骨骼的压力；土星还是腐朽过程。旧文献把土星与正在腐烂之物相联。
However, Saturn also signifies the process of change over time. Saturn is indeed old structures and old patterns - and yet, being time, Saturn is also the pressure for change, that which breaks down old patterns to make way for the new. Saturn is the bones, and the pressure of age that breaks bones. Saturn is also the decaying process. In older texts Saturn is associated with things that are rotting.
现代占星学常建立一种二分：土星在一边，代表现状、抗拒改变；外行星在另一边，是改变与革命之力。我们在此前考察的一些现代占星师那里见过明白表述。这是虚假二分，来自对土星含义变薄、单边的解释。
Modern astrology often sets up a dichotomy, with Saturn on the one side as the status quo, resisting change, and the outer planets as the forces for change and revolution. We saw that explicitly stated in some of the modern astrologers we looked at. It is a false dichotomy, and it comes from a thinned out, one sided interpretation of the meaning of Saturn.
现代占星学可能把土星—冥王星相位解释成：土星是抗拒改变之力，冥王星是不可抗拒的转化之力，两者相互对抗。它建立了一种善恶二元论：土星是阻碍改变的坏人，天王星至冥王星等现代外行星则是好人，是新事物、革命与启蒙之力。
In modern astrology a Saturn-Pluto aspect might be interpreted as Saturn being the forces resisting change, and Pluto being the forces of irresistible transformation. It is one against the other. This sets up a good-evil dualism where Saturn is the Bad Guy standing in the way of change, while the modern outer planets Uranus through Pluto are the Good Guys, the forces for the new, for revolution and enlightenment.
土星的一部分作用恰恰是向需要改变之物施压。把这一含义维度从土星分离出来，我们便把一个复杂过程拆成相互对立、各自较简单的力量，建立了虚假二分。原书 p.178
Part of the effect of Saturn is precisely the applying of pressure on something that needs to experience change. By separating that dimension of meaning from Saturn we have split a complex process into opposing, less complex opposite forces. We have set up a false dichotomy.
改变是非常复杂混合的过程；保留土星传统含义的复杂性，有助于认识这一点。改变不总是正面或可欲；伴随看似正面的改变，也可能有许多东西丢失或毁坏。记住人生不是成长与演化的直线进军，会对我们有所帮助。人生有峰有谷，有升有降，有些事物存续，有些则瓦解。
Change is a very complex and mixed process, and keeping the complexity of the traditional meaning of Saturn helps us realize that. Change is not always positive or desirable and along with a seemingly positive change much can be lost or destroyed. It helps us here to remember that life does not come in the form of a straight line march of growth and evolution. It has peaks and valleys, ups and downs, things which survive and things which fall apart.
土星的传统含义体现了时间与改变之义的许多复杂面向。再想想镰刀：它象征收获，也象征把麦粒与糠秕分开。这是除草与考验的过程，保留有价值之物，抛弃无价值之物。镰刀是土星作为改变之力、也作为经改变而成长之力的核心象征之一；因为在那里被收获的，往往是意识改变。
The traditional meaning of Saturn embodies much of the complexity of the meaning of time and change. Think of the scythe again, symbol of the harvest, of separating the wheat from the chaff. This is a process of weeding and of testing, keeping the valuable and discarding what is worthless. That scythe is one of the key symbols of Saturn as the forces of change and of growth through change, for often what is harvested there is a change of awareness.
值得花时间找回传统神圣七曜含义的更多丰富性与复杂性。它们现在是、也永远会是我们占星学的核心。
It is worth the time to reclaim more of the richness and complexity of the traditional meaning of the Sacred Seven planets. They are, and always will be, the core of our astrology.

吉星与凶星Benefic and Malefic

传统占星学把土星视为大凶星Greater Malefic。要理解凶星，就必须把它与配对概念吉星一起考察；这会调用并联结我们已经研究过的一些核心概念。原书 p.179
In traditional astrology Saturn is considered the Greater Malefic. To understand malefic we need to look at it with its partner concept benefic. This will draw on and tie together some of the key concepts we have already examined.
反对使用凶星概念，是我们反复指出的现代文化奇特失衡的一部分。现代对凶星的反对包括以下论点。
The objection to using the concept malefic is part of the curious unbalance of our modern culture that we have repeatedly noted. The modern objections to malefic include some of the following arguments.
首先，现代占星师主张没有行星全然坏，却认为传统占星师把土星视为道德上全然邪恶，把「凶星」理解为只有坏处。丽兹·格林提出过这种说法；史蒂芬·阿罗约及其他现代人本占星师也会作类似陈述。我们已经确定，这种立场是对传统占星学的漫画化；也一再看到，传统占星学中的土星含义非常复杂混合。
First, modern astrologers claim that no planet is completely bad, while traditional astrologers view Saturn as completely evil, and Malefic as meaning only bad.
